### 1. [Self-Conditioning Enables Deep Sequential Reasoning in Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19628v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19628

**作者：** Zeyang Zhang、Linxiang Gao、Meiqi Gu、Xingyan Chen、Minrui Luo、Kaifeng Lyu、Tianxing He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19628v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19628v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从电路复杂度角度审视「用图像生成做视觉推理」，证明在温和正则条件下 vanilla flow matching 的采样轨迹可被常数轮顺序的并行模型评估模拟，故增加采样步数无法提升常深度骨干的表达力；随后指出 self-conditioning（以上一步对最终图像的估计为条件）能解除该瓶颈，受控实验显示其准确率与推理深度更高且随步数持续提升，并证明此时常深度骨干可达空间有界图灵机的表达能力。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及 LVR 中「采样／迭代步数是否等价于推理深度」与「步骤间应传递多少信息」这两条主线，其理论结论可能反驳「迭代采样步≈推理步」的直觉；self-conditioning 作为步骤间信息传递机制，可供核查 latent 状态传播、信息坍缩与动态计算的收益来源。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Conditioning Enables Deep Sequential Reasoning in Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Recent work has explored visual reasoning through image generation, where models solve visual-reasoning problems by directly generating solution images. Intuitively, iterative samplers such as flow matching are well suited to this task, since each sampling step may resemble one reasoning step. However, in this work, from a circuit complexity perspective, we show that this intuition is misleading. We prove that as long as the model outputs satisfy certain mild regularity conditions, the trajectory of the vanilla flow-matching sampler can be simulated by a constant number of sequential rounds of parallel model evaluations. Consequently, additional sampling steps cannot increase the expressive power of a constant-depth backbone model under the standard complexity-theoretic model of transformers. We then reveal that self-conditioning, which conditions the model on the previous step's estimate of the final image, plays a previously unrecognized role in removing this bottleneck. Controlled experiments on diverse visual-reasoning tasks show that flow matching with self-conditioning achieves higher accuracy and greater reasoning depth than vanilla flow matching, and its performance improves with more sampling steps. Theoretically, we prove that with self-conditioning, a constant-depth backbone can achieve expressive power equivalent to that of space-bounded Turing machines. Our study suggests a design principle for future visual reasoning research: carry more information between sampling steps, so that additional steps translate into deeper reasoning.

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Learning to Compute Is Not Learning to Retain or Reuse in Looped Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19768v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19768

**作者：** Peilong Zhou、Hongtao Zhang、Yulong Huang、Cangyuan Li、Mingyu Chen、Ying Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19768v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19768v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在上下文 Mealy 机执行任务上研究 looped transformer：模型在训练循环深度内完全正确，循环加倍后仅保留 22% 答案；通过只监督随机位置、随机化训练循环数可把保留率提到 99.8%，而梯度截断无效；并用隐藏状态替换证明位置 k 的计算精确但对齐复用有限。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 latent 循环推理的状态更新、过思考（overthinking）与停止/复用，明确区分「算得对」「留得住」「可复用」三类失败，与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益成本、支持组合及循环长度泛化关注高度相关，其监督位置影响保留的结论可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Compute Is Not Learning to Retain or Reuse in Looped Transformers

**abstract/A1：** Looped transformers compute by applying one block repeatedly, so they can run for more loops than they were trained with. Recurrent reasoners are known to overthink: iterating past the trained depth can turn a correct answer into a wrong one. Such a late error can reflect failed computation, loss of retention, or a state that later computation cannot reuse. We separate these cases in in-context Mealy-machine execution, where a simulator supplies intermediate targets and exact counterfactuals for hidden-state interventions. Models trained to answer at loop 32 are fully correct there but keep only 22% of these answers at loop 64. Retention depends on what training asks for: supervising one randomly chosen position per example, instead of always the last, keeps 99.8%, and randomizing the number of training loops also helps; truncating gradients, as in progressive training against overthinking, does not. The computation itself is exact: position $k$ is computed at loop $k$, and swapping its loop-$k$ hidden vector for the one from another input redirects every later position to that input's exact continuation (237/237 pairs in each of ten models). Retention, however, is not reuse. Even in models that keep nearly all answers, holding each position $k$ fixed from loop $k$ drops accuracy to 4--6%, whereas holding it from loop $k+4$ is harmless. A released recurrent maze network trained to avoid overthinking behaves the same way. Learning to compute is thus not learning to retain, and avoiding overthinking does not make a state reusable when its answer first appears.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages

</details>

### 3. [Riding the Fixed-Point Curve: Image Classification in the Continuous Thought Machine](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19220v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19220

**作者：** Thomas Hannagan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19220v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19220v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以 ImageNet 为例分析 Continuous Thought Machine（CTM）这类循环网络如何分类图像，刻画每个思考步骤的神经状态动力学：每步是吸引但强非正规的映射并伴随平衡放大，存在随步移动的局部唯一吸引不动点，思考轨迹即这些不动点勾勒的曲线，而该曲线大部分运动跨图像共享，注意力调度也近乎通用（打乱各图查询仅损失约 7% 精度）。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及 LVR 关注的循环 latent 状态是否真正承载图像证据：若思考轨迹与查询调度在不同图像间高度一致，可能提示 latent 状态的信息坍缩或泛化策略单一，可供核查 latent 内容使用、动态计算与停止决策的收益来源。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Riding the Fixed-Point Curve: Image Classification in the Continuous Thought Machine

**abstract/A1：** The Continuous Thought Machine (CTM) is a recurrent network that attends to inputs and makes predictions based on the synchronization between its neurons. On tasks spanning many domains, it displays emergent thought processes that have not been explained. We report a number of findings about how the CTM classifies images, focusing on ImageNet. Each thinking step is an attracting but strongly non-normal map exhibiting balanced amplification (Murphy and Miller, 2009); while attraction is already present at CTM initialization, training highly promotes non-normality. By Banach's theorem, the neural state has a locally unique attracting fixed point, which moves at every step, and the thought trajectory is the curve these fixed points trace, followed one tick behind. Most of that curve's motion is shared across images, and visible as a previously reported traveling wave of activity across neurons. The attention schedule along the thought is largely generic: shuffling queries across images at every tick costs only 7 percent of classification accuracy, while image-independent increments predict the next query with R^2 = 0.98 (held out). Our results suggest that the CTM has found a single strategy to process all ImageNet images.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables. Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Symmetry and Geometry in Neural Representations (NeurReps), extended abstract track (non-archival)

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [Integrating Simulation and Chain-of-thought Reasoning in Multimodal-Language Models For Physical Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19441v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19441

**作者：** YingQiao Wang、Eric Bigelow、Tomer Ullman、Yujin Tang、Sebastian Risi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19441v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19441v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 系统比较物理推理任务中生成式模拟与语言 CoT 两类方法的适用边界，并提出受人类资源理性启发的混合模型，在测试时自行决定采用哪种方式解题，以在精度与计算成本间取得平衡，且可用 4B 小模型训练后迁移提升超大模型的表现。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 关注的动态计算分配及 THINK/LOOK/STOP 收益—成本权衡直接相关，其「先判断该模拟还是该推理」的路由机制可供核查是否依赖任务对视觉细节的需求；但论文不涉及内部 latent 状态或视觉证据保留，具体决策信号需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Integrating Simulation and Chain-of-thought Reasoning in Multimodal-Language Models For Physical Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal language models often need to reason about the physical dynamics of the world in order to answer questions appropriately. Various methods have emerged for solving physical reasoning tasks, and in particular, two leading approaches are Chain-of-Thought reasoning and physics-grounded simulation. However, relatively little is known about which particular problems can be solved by each approach. Some tasks may benefit from computationally expensive generative video simulations, whereas others may be solved equally well (or even better) with language-based reasoning. Our work addresses this by systematically comparing simulation and language reasoning across a variety of multimodal tasks involving physical reasoning, such as answering questions about fluid dynamics and elasticity. We draw a parallel with this to resource-rational theories of human cognition, which propose that people optimally trade off simulation and reasoning depending on task demands. We present a novel cognitively-inspired hybrid model which optimally trades off simulation and reasoning to improve model performance and computational efficiency. Our model is able to decide the appropriate method to solve a physical reasoning problem at test time, and finds an optimal balance between accuracy and cost. Further, our model can be trained on a smaller vision-language model that is only 4B parameters and deployed to improve performance on much bigger base models with $&gt;$3T parameters like Gemini 3 Pro.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [Frame Evidence:A Benchmark for Reference Frames and Visual Evidence in Multi-Image Spatial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19564v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19564

**作者：** Chunxi Luo、Qishuai Ding、Xingchen Peng、Hengwei Ye、Ke Zhang、Wenxin Lian、Tianyi Lu、Chunlong Yu、Zhenya Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19564v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19564v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Frame Evidence 基准，基于 320 个匹配三元组与 960 道中文选择题，在多图空间推理中联合考察指称参考系与视觉证据的充分/必要关系，并报告 20 种配置下的项目准确率与三帧联合正确率。

**相关性简评：** 其联合指标与「图像删除标注为不充分／答案保持的添加」配对设计，可供核查 VLM 推理是否真正依赖视觉证据、答案是否随参考系与证据变化而稳定，与研究兴趣中的视觉证据筛选、因果作用与评价问题直接相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frame Evidence:A Benchmark for Reference Frames and Visual Evidence in Multi-Image Spatial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** A spatial answer depends on the reference frame specified in language and its supporting visual evidence. Item accuracy alone obscures whether correctness persists when either changes. We introduce Frame Evidence, a benchmark of 320 matched triplets and 960 Chinese multiple-choice questions from captured and rendered scenes. One family compares three grounded reference frames over fixed images. The other pairs image deletion annotated as insufficient with answer-preserving addition. Joint metrics require correct answers across all three frames and paired evidence variants. Across 20 configurations on 288 held-out questions, GPT three-shot reaches 50.00\\% item accuracy, but the best three-frame group accuracy is 5/48 (10.42\\%), reached by GPT two-shot. Few-shot prompting and task-specific adaptation improve item accuracy without consistently improving joint correctness. Input controls show where images support matched success, while repeated executions reveal answer instability beneath similar scores.

</details>

### 6. [LED-BENCH: A Benchmark for Large-format Engineering Drawing Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19571v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19571

**作者：** Ke Li、Tianyi Wang、Enning You、Chaoqun Wan、Zhen Huang、Shuxian Liang、Xian-Sheng Hua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19571v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19571v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LED-BENCH，一个覆盖机械、建筑与交通领域的多学科基准，用于评测大尺寸工程图纸上的细粒度理解与跨区域、跨视图的复杂推理，并系统性测试了 11 个多模态大模型。

**相关性简评：** 其关注跨区域证据整合与细粒度视觉信息保留，评测显示模型在需要更广证据整合的问题上普遍更差，可为 visual evidence 筛选、信息坍缩与 THINK/LOOK 分配等 LVR 问题提供诊断线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LED-BENCH: A Benchmark for Large-format Engineering Drawing Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Real-world engineering drawings are often large-format and information-dense. Answering practical engineering questions therefore requires both fine-grained understanding of engineering information and complex reasoning over objects, relations, and evidence distributed across different regions or views. Existing benchmarks cover a range of engineering-drawing understanding tasks involving local regions, individual views, specific elements, or more constrained visual settings. However, systematic evaluation of understanding over realistic, large-format engineering drawings remains limited. To address this gap, we introduce LED-BENCH, a multidisciplinary benchmark designed to evaluate fine-grained understanding and complex reasoning over large-format engineering drawings, spanning the mechanical, architectural, and transportation domains. Extensive evaluation of 11 representative multimodal large language models, including 8 proprietary models and 3 open-source models, reveals substantial remaining difficulty: GPT-5.6 Sol achieves 52.65% average accuracy, performance varies across engineering domains, and all evaluated models perform worse on questions requiring broader evidence integration. These results highlight fine-grained understanding, evidence integration, and complex reasoning over large-format engineering drawings as persistent challenges for current multimodal models.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages,5 figures,10 tables

</details>

### 7. [What Breaks in Multi-Image Schema Application? Factorial Oracle Interventions Reveal an Instance-Information Bottleneck](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19611v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19611

**作者：** Yiliu Xu、Xinjin Li、Yu Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19611v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19611v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建一个受控的多图 schema 应用任务（在 16 张图中定位两个目标字符、读取视觉属性、查物品专属 codebook 并合成有序代号），用 within-item 2×2 Oracle 干预（提供已验证文本 schema 或有序目标外观表示）分析 72 个条目、1188 条回答，定位多图流程的主要失败环节。

**相关性简评：** 材料支持的 Oracle-B 增益远超 Oracle-A，说明瓶颈在「有序实例信息恢复」这一上游环节，直接关联 LVR 关注的视觉细节与实体信息是否保留在内部状态、以及贡献分配诊断；其 Oracle 因子设计可供核查是否可迁移为 latent 内容因果归因工具，但任务为合成 codebook 场景，外推需谨慎。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Breaks in Multi-Image Schema Application? Factorial Oracle Interventions Reveal an Instance-Information Bottleneck

**abstract/A1：** Modern vision-language models can accept multi-image contexts, but aggregate accuracy rarely reveals where a multi-image reasoning pipeline fails. We study a controlled schema-application task in which a model must locate two target characters in a 16-image sequence, identify their visual attributes, consult an item-specific visual codebook, and compose two ordered nonce code names. We introduce a within-item 2-by-2 factorial Oracle design: Oracle-A supplies a verified textual schema, Oracle-B supplies a verified ordered target-appearance representation, and both interventions retain the original images and final question. Across 72 new items and 1,188 analyzed model responses, Oracle-B increased strict exact accuracy by 54.6 percentage points (95% confidence interval: 46.3 to 63.0) and outperformed Oracle-A by 60.6 points (Holm-adjusted p-value no greater than .0002). An auxiliary retrieval diagnostic reached only 49.1% ordered-appearance accuracy even after removing code-name generation. Providing both Oracle sources yielded 100% accuracy across all models. These results localize the dominant recoverable deficit to upstream ordered instance recovery rather than primarily to downstream schema use and composition.

</details>
