### 8. [Mitigating Video Hallucinations via Attention-Guided Representation Perturbation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19728v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19728

**作者：** Zhixin Cai、Ruoqi Yuan、Xinchen Yang、Zhuofan Chen、Jun Bai、Chuantao Yin、Wenge Rong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19728v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19728v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的对比解码框架 ARP-CD：以答案预测位置对视觉 token 的注意力质量为引导，对输入视觉隐状态做有界梯度扰动以削弱视觉证据，构造负分支并与原分支对比生成，缓解视频 LLM 幻觉。

**相关性简评：** 直接触及视觉证据筛选与视觉旁路问题；其用注意力引导扰动、控制生成期视觉依赖的做法，可供核查 VLM 推理中视觉信息被使用或绕过的机制，以及注意力结构对视觉状态的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mitigating Video Hallucinations via Attention-Guided Representation Perturbation

**abstract/A1：** Video large language models (Video LLMs) often generate responses unsupported by the input video. Contrastive decoding (CD) can mitigate these hallucinations, but its effectiveness depends on how the negative branch weakens visual evidence. Existing methods rely on predefined perturbations or masking guided by question reconstruction, offering limited control over the model's visual reliance during answer generation. We introduce Attention-guided Representation Perturbation for Contrastive Decoding (ARP-CD), a training-free framework for constructing contrastive negatives directly in representation space. ARP-CD uses the attention mass assigned to visual tokens at the answer-prediction position to guide bounded gradient updates of the language model's input visual hidden states. These updates reduce visual attention and produce a negative input, whose predictions are contrasted with those of the original branch throughout generation. Experiments across four Video LLMs and four benchmarks show that ARP-CD outperforms representative training-free baselines in most model--benchmark settings, reducing hallucinations while improving general video understanding. Further analyses show that attention-guided perturbation directions improve negative quality and that the resulting negatives provide effective contrastive references for correcting the original predictions.

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [DeltaStreamer: Towards Human-Like Agentic Streaming Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19849

**作者：** Boyuan Sun、Deshui Miao、Wenzhao Gao、Shaoyong Jia、Shaohui Jiao、Chi Liu、Yan Shu、Derek Li、Bryan Dai、Shengsheng Qian、Qibin Hou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19849v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DeltaStreamer 这一 agentic 流式视频理解框架:查询前用变化感知的结构化记忆持续记录视觉变化,查询到来时由 query-driven planner 判断所需证据并引导记忆检索与定向回放历史帧,以补齐观察阶段被压缩掉或遗漏的视觉细节,在 OVO-Bench 与 StreamingBench 上报告提升。

**相关性简评：** 与「获取证据、动态计算、视觉细节是否保留」直接相关:其变化感知记忆加按需回放的机制,可看作对外部证据筛选与补全的具体探索,可供核查缺失视觉证据如何被重新取回并影响答案定位。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeltaStreamer: Towards Human-Like Agentic Streaming Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** We propose DeltaStreamer, a novel agentic framework for streaming video understanding inspired by how humans observe, remember, and investigate. Streaming video understanding requires a system to retain evidence before knowing which details future queries will need. Existing approaches often obscure event transitions and omit relevant details when compressing video history, and cannot recover missing visual evidence. DeltaStreamer addresses these limitations by combining change-aware memory with query-driven planning and targeted evidence gathering with replay. Before a query arrives, it continuously observes the stream and explicitly records visual changes in structured memory, preserving how events unfold rather than only what is present. When a query arrives, a query-driven planner identifies the required evidence and guides memory retrieval and targeted replay. By revisiting historical frames, the agent can recover visual details omitted during observation and ground its answer in evidence beyond the retained descriptions. This connects continuous observation with active investigation tailored to each question. Experiments on OVO-Bench and StreamingBench demonstrate improvements over representative streaming video understanding methods, with overall scores of 66.07% and 59.53%, respectively. Project page: https://bbbbchan.github.io/DeltaStreamer/

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [On the Limits of Representation-Based Test-Time Adaptation for Multimodal LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19892v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19892

**作者：** Mikołaj Piórczyński、Wojciech Masarczyk、Damian Sójka、Sebastian Cygert、Bartłomiej Twardowski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19892v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19892v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文考察生成式 MLLM 上基于表示的 test-time adaptation，实验（甚至在离线使用目标数据、违反标准假设的条件下）显示样本盲的特征对齐仅带来边际提升，并将失败归因于 MLLM 视觉 token 序列的域偏移近乎正交、散布于整个嵌入空间，而非共享的单一方向。

**相关性简评：** 该失败分析直接触及「视觉证据是否保留在内部状态」以及表示空间单点、位置共享干预的可行边界，可供核查其对视觉旁路与信息坍缩诊断的借鉴；结论偏向负结果，但也提示 LVR 中表示层修正可能不足。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Limits of Representation-Based Test-Time Adaptation for Multimodal LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models suffer significant accuracy drops under common corruptions. While test-time adaptation (TTA) successfully mitigates these shifts in CLIP-like contrastive models, its application to generative multimodal large language models (MLLMs) remains underexplored and uniquely challenging due to their sequential token generation. In this work, we demonstrate that representation-based TTA fails to recover performance in MLLMs. Our experiments reveal that even when violating standard TTA assumptions by leveraging target data offline, sample-blind feature alignment recovers only marginal accuracy. We attribute this failure to the nature of corruption shifts: unlike CLIP-like architectures, which benefit from a shared domain-shift direction within a single pooled classification embedding, MLLM visual token sequence shifts are nearly orthogonal and span the full embedding space.These findings bound what any sample-blind, position-shared correction at a single encoder site can achieve, establishing that representation-space interventions alone might be insufficient to ensure corruption robustness in MLLMs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Grounded and Faithful Vision-Language Models for Real-World Deployment (VLM4RWD) workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [ChemArgus: Automated Scientific Diagnostics for Lab-Grade Multimodal Chemistry Reasoning: A Frontier Benchmark](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20012v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20012

**作者：** Hanyao Guo、Mo Cui、Zihan Tan、Leixin Sun、Yichun Wang、Yinan Shen、Guojun Zhu、Jia Jiang、Erhao Chen、Zui Chen、Wenzhe Gao、Zhihao Yu、Qijun Wang、Yetian Zhu、Jiande Chen、Chuhang Pei、Yao Jiang、Yichen Fan、Pinhan Wang、Xiaorui Pan、Qianyi Zhu、Yang Liu、Mang Ye、Guancheng Wan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20012v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20012v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** ChemArgus 是一个竞赛级化学多模态基准，用逐步 rubric 部分给分、图像与受控文本描述配对、以及只改变上下文构造的配对指标，来定位模型推理链在整合、传递还是局部推理环节出错。

**相关性简评：** 其「同一任务上图像与文本配对打分」及「配对指标差异隔离单一原因」的诊断设计，可供核查 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据、视觉信息在何处丢失或坍缩，并与推理链失败定位这一主线直接相关；但它是化学领域基准，未涉及 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ChemArgus: Automated Scientific Diagnostics for Lab-Grade Multimodal Chemistry Reasoning: A Frontier Benchmark

**abstract/A1：** Chemistry evaluation has moved past multiple-choice probes toward open-ended expert problems, yet the benchmarks that define the current frontier still score answer endpoints alone. Such scoring conflates memorized facts with stepwise derivation, treats visual and textual inputs as interchangeable, and confounds long-horizon planning with stepwise execution. These blind spots prevent any diagnosis of where the reasoning chain breaks in a model. We present ChemArgus, a competition-level chemistry benchmark drawn from chemistry competition material, textbooks, and research papers, whose scoring replaces the answer endpoint with three measurement properties. Fine-grained scoring grades every subquestion point by point with partial credit, so a score lands at the level of the step rather than the outcome. Multimodal capability is priced on every problem containing a real image, paired with a controlled text description, so visual understanding is measured against text on identical tasks. Diagnostic attribution reads paired metrics that share one scoring unit and differ only in context construction, so a gap between two scores isolates a single cause, namely integration, propagation, or local reasoning. GPT-5.6-Sol scores 23.2% on unassisted whole-problem solving against 28.8% and 30.4% under the two sequential metrics, a gap measuring the integration burden. Through rubric partial credit and these cause-isolating score gaps, ChemArgus shows not only whether a model answers chemistry correctly but where its reasoning chain fails, so that every score it reports doubles as a diagnosis, a readout of the chain rather than a verdict, aiming to guide language models step by step on their path toward chemistry expertise, one corrected step at a time, each step priced by the rubric, point by point.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [VisualWorldBench: A Fine-Grained Multi-Task Benchmark for Evaluating Visual World Knowledge in MLLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20083v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20083

**作者：** Chen Chen、Zheming Liang、Jiaqi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20083v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20083v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VisualWorldBench：以 21,291 个叶节点类别、8 个视觉领域的层级分类体系为基础，构建含 32,541 个实例的闭集识别、对比选择、精确定位与开放命名四任务基准，并配套 5 万类别的训练资源；实验显示最强闭源模型平均仅 80.4%，在其资源上微调可提升逾 21 个百分点。

**相关性简评：** 基准用分类学邻近类别强制细粒度判别，直接针对模型是否依赖粗粒度线索或排除法而非掌握精确视觉概念，可供核查视觉细节与实体信息在内部状态中的保留，以及训练监督对推理证据使用的作用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VisualWorldBench: A Fine-Grained Multi-Task Benchmark for Evaluating Visual World Knowledge in MLLMs

**abstract/A1：** Visual world knowledge is a core capability of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), underpinning knowledge-intensive visual question answering and fine-grained visual reasoning. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in two key respects. First, their category coverage is narrow: most benchmarks span only a small number of categories across a few domains, falling far short of the open-ended scale of real-world visual entities. Second, their task construction is often insufficiently rigorous: models can answer correctly using coarse-grained cues, vague associations, or elimination strategies without truly mastering precise concepts, making it difficult to accurately assess their knowledge of fine-grained entities. We introduce VisualWorldBench, a taxonomy-grounded multi-task benchmark built on a hierarchical taxonomy of 21,291 leaf-node classes across 8 visual domains, with 32,541 evaluation instances whose ground-truth labels cover 15,479 categories across four complementary tasks: closed-set recognition, contrastive selection, precise localization, and open-world naming, covering different directions of knowledge retrieval and varying degrees of answer openness to comprehensively evaluate visual world knowledge. Most questions are constructed from taxonomically proximate categories, forcing precise discrimination among fine-grained entities. Experiments reveal that the strongest closed-source model achieves only 80.4% average accuracy, exposing capability gaps hidden under conventional benchmarks. We further construct VisualWorldBench-50K, a training resource covering 50K+ categories. Fine-tuning on VisualWorldBench-50K yields over 21 percentage points improvement on VisualWorldBench, with consistent transfer to public fine-grained recognition and visual knowledge benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [Reflect After Failure: Brain-Inspired Self-Evolving Agents for Embodied Spatial Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20312v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20312

**作者：** Xin Li、Xiaohuan Gu、Yifan Wang、Shaobin Ling、Tianli Shi、Jingao Xu、Zhimeng Yin、Dapeng Wu、Yun Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20312v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20312v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 HiMeco，一个受元认知监控与海马记忆巩固启发的自演化具身智能体，通过「感知-探索-规划」失败归因的反思机制与基于证据契约的技能筛选，在冻结参数、轮间更新记忆的条件下提升具身空间推理表现。

**相关性简评：** 其跨感知-探索-规划的失败归因、从部分视觉证据中定位错误来源及技能保留验证，与 LVR 关注的影响贡献分配、视觉证据筛选和 THINK/LOOK/STOP 决策有具体联系，可供核查其诊断是否仅依赖执行轨迹而非内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reflect After Failure: Brain-Inspired Self-Evolving Agents for Embodied Spatial Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have made substantial progress in spatial reasoning, and self-evolving agents further extend these capabilities to embodied environments by learning from interactions. However, current self-evolution remains particularly challenging in embodied spatial tasks, complex and multi-step decisions from partial visual evidence making it difficult to determine why an execution fails. The failure may stem from misperception, insufficient exploration, or incorrect planning, yet execution feedback alone rarely reveals which component is responsible. As a result, simply accumulating interaction experience can reinforce ineffective strategies. To address this, we propose HiMeco, a brain-inspired self-evolving agent for embodied spatial intelligence, inspired by metacognitive monitoring and hippocampal memory consolidation. Through Hippocampal-Metacognitive Reflection, HiMeco diagnoses failures in perception-exploration-planning (PEP), then compares execution attempts to derive reusable strategies and guidance for avoiding recurring errors. It also abstracts interaction programs into parameterized skills, using evidence contracts and execution-based validation to determine their retention and reuse. These strategies and skills guide subsequent interactions, with memory updated only between rounds and model parameters kept frozen. HiMeco achieves state-of-the-art performance on ESI-Bench, and it also improves accuracy on EmbSpatial-Bench by 3.94 percentage points without evolution.

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [ReferFlow: Beyond Final-Target Localization for Complex Referring Expressions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20352v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20352

**作者：** Tianyi Gao、Han Fang、Xin Wei、Yaoyao Zhong、Zhaoyan Pan、Weijie Zhu、Hongbo Sun、Zhiyu Lin、Kaicheng Yang、Jinglin Xu、Ye Yuan、Hao Li、Hao Sun、Jingmin Xin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20352v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20352v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 trajectory-level REC 设定与 ReferFlow-Bench（Refinement、Shift、Reset、Mixture 四类轨迹），要求模型按有序 grounding 步骤定位中间区域；并给出 ReferFlow 方法，用 Stateful Region Latent Reasoning 以连续 latent 建模带对象索引的区域状态并加视觉与空间监督，再由轨迹感知并行解码分四轮恢复全部坐标块，配合 Grounding-Aware GRPO 提升跨步一致性。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及多模态推理中的 latent 状态表示与生成方式：其「中间参考、逐步 grounding」设定和带视觉/空间监督的区域 latent，可供核查 latent 是否保留任务必要视觉细节、是否被后续定位真正使用，并行坐标解码与轨迹监督亦与动态计算和贡献分配问题相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReferFlow: Beyond Final-Target Localization for Complex Referring Expressions

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models have made significant progress in referring expression comprehension (REC), yet most REC benchmarks still focus on final-target localization. Complex queries often involve intermediate visual references with different dependencies on prior grounding, but final-only evaluation leaves this process unexamined. To address this gap, we formulate trajectory-level REC, in which models receive an ordered sequence of grounding steps constructed from a single complex query and localize the target regions associated with each step. We instantiate this setting in ReferFlow-Bench, which characterizes structured region transitions through four trajectory types, namely Refinement, Shift, Reset, and Mixture. Experiments show that current models struggle to consistently ground intermediate visual references across successive steps. We further propose ReferFlow, which decouples interleaved text-latent trajectory generation from coordinate decoding. Specifically, Stateful Region Latent Reasoning represents each target region as an object-indexed state composed of continuous latents, with explicit visual and spatial supervision to preserve region information for subsequent grounding. Trajectory-Aware Parallel Decoding then recovers all coordinate blocks in parallel through four geometry-constrained refinement rounds, with each block conditioned on its corresponding trajectory prefix, thereby reducing within-box sequential dependencies and eliminating cross-box autoregression. We further apply Grounding-Aware GRPO to promote consistent grounding across steps and learn adaptive trajectory generation from complex REC queries. ReferFlow achieves leading performance on general REC benchmarks and ReferFlow-Bench.

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [ActiveEvidence: Decision-Focused Evidence Graphs with Verifiable Acquisition Gaps](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20532v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20532

**作者：** Miaobo Hu、Shuhao Hu、Xiaobo Guo、Xin Wang、Bokun Wang、Peng Zhang、Daren Zha、Jun Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20532v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20532v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多模态 agent 在免费元数据与付费观察边界下，如何判断视觉证据是否值得获取、查询哪个候选或分辨率、以及何时停止交互，提出反事实证据交互图 CEIG 与 ε-决策证书，并配以成对续跑、成本过滤和独立验证器。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的收益—成本权衡与视觉证据筛选；其决策加权包络、间隙导向精化与可验证停止证书可供核查 latent 推理的停止判据与成本核算是否具备决策可认证性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ActiveEvidence: Decision-Focused Evidence Graphs with Verifiable Acquisition Gaps

**abstract/A1：** A multimodal agent must decide whether visual evidence is worth acquiring, which candidate or resolution to query, and when further interaction can no longer change its decision. We study this problem under a strict free-metadata/paid-observation boundary, stochastic vector costs, and a frozen execution trace, and treat it as decision-certifiable active evidence acquisition. A Counterfactual Evidence Interaction Graph (CEIG) represents unary evidence value, signed second-order interaction, and directed conditional harm while maintaining explicit score enclosures for unresolved interactions; a decision-weighted enclosure objective tightens the bounds that can change action identity. Gap-directed refinement evaluates only interactions that can displace the incumbent acquisition and stops once an $ε$-decision certificate shows that unresolved terms cannot change the selected action beyond a declared tolerance. The certificate is model-relative: it verifies policy-consistent optimality under the pinned learned score, and semantic quality is measured separately through post-freeze labels. Paired continuation rollouts learn the downstream value of paid previews, an online operational cost filter is separated from independent finite-policy-family trajectory certification, and an acquisition monitor with an independently implemented verifier binds every certificate to the frozen trace. On 640 cases split into 384/128/128 training, validation, and test cases, the controller reaches task score 0.842, grounding F1 0.816, evidence recall 0.873, 1.74 accepted calls, 286.5K visual tokens, and 742ms p95 latency. Dense replay of issued certificates shows 0.992 action agreement and a 0.988 certificate rate while evaluating 19.6 instead of 191.4 expensive interaction terms per decision.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-16"></a>

### 16. [GeoThinker-$F$: Endogenous Predictive World Representations for Fast Spatial Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20592v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20592

**作者：** Haoyuan Li、Qihang Cao、Huanyuan Zhou、Xin Hu、Sijia Chen、Zihan Guo、Kun Xiang、Yunshuang Nie、Tao Tang、Xinlong Zheng、Jia-Wang Bian、Xianming Liu、Xiaodan Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20592v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20592v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GeoThinker-F：用两阶段训练把 MLLM 隐状态转成预测性 latent 世界表示，第一阶段冻结主干、由轻量 LFP 头从离线 3D 几何专家和下一帧语义目标学习，第二阶段移除外部专家并用 SGT Fusion 选择性整合自预测先验，在空间智能基准上保持性能的同时去掉推理期外部 3D 编码器、获得最高约 3.6× 加速。

**相关性简评：** 直接落在「内部状态是否保留几何与任务必要信息并真正被使用」这条主线上，其用外部专家蒸馏 latent 先验、以预测目标塑造工作区、以及用自预测替代视觉旁路的做法，可供核查 latent 内容对答案的因果贡献与收益成本权衡。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoThinker-$F$: Endogenous Predictive World Representations for Fast Spatial Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** Existing geometry-aware Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) increasingly rely on external 3D encoders to provide structural priors, but executing these additional encoder paths at inference introduces substantial latency and computational overhead. We propose GeoThinker-$F$, an efficient framework that internalizes spatiotemporal perception by transforming causal MLLM hidden states into predictive latent world representations. Our key insight is that intermediate semantic representations contain sufficient information to predict useful representations of current geometry and future semantics, without requiring an exact reproduction of features from a dedicated 3D encoder. To realize this idea, GeoThinker-$F$ adopts a decoupled two-stage training strategy. In the first stage, the MLLM backbone is frozen while lightweight Latent Frame Prediction (LFP) heads learn from an offline 3D geometry expert and next-frame semantic targets. In the second stage, the external expert is removed, and the model is jointly optimized to generate and selectively integrate its self-predicted priors through a specialized Spatial-Grounded Temporal (SGT) Fusion module. Extensive evaluations show that GeoThinker-$F$ eliminates runtime dependence on external 3D geometry encoders while maintaining competitive performance across diverse spatial intelligence benchmarks. It achieves up to a $3.6\\times$ inference speedup, reducing latency from $1.45\\,\\mathrm{s}$ to $0.40\\,\\mathrm{s}$ while retaining strong spatial reasoning performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [Inverse Reasoning Models for Efficient Post-Training and Verification from Answer-Only Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18878v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18878

**作者：** Eric O. Korman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18878v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18878v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究仅用「答案数据」后训练推理 LLM：把 CoT 当作隐变量，训练一个以答案生成推理轨迹的「逆向推理模型」，用于离线自蒸馏，并兼作正确性验证器做 best-of-n 选择。

**相关性简评：** 将 CoT 视为 latent variable、用逆模型从答案反推推理轨迹，可能为 LVR 中缺少 latent 监督的轨迹训练与推理内容验证提供线索，可供核查；但属纯文本工作，无视觉证据或内部状态分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Inverse Reasoning Models for Efficient Post-Training and Verification from Answer-Only Data

**abstract/A1：** Post-training reasoning LLMs often requires making a tradeoff between costly supervision and compute inefficiency: supervised finetuning typically requires reasoning traces, which is data inefficient since in many applications one only has prompt-answer tuples and lacks the groundtruth chain-of-thought (CoT) sequence. On the other hand, reinforcement learning (with verifiable rewards, for example) can operate without CoT data but it is much more compute-inefficient since it requires multiple autoregressive completions per optimization step, none of which are guaranteed to give a non-zero policy gradient. In this work we explore a middleground, a self-distillation technique that uses answer-only data, treating CoT as latent variables. This relies on training a partner \`\`inverse reasoning LLM" that generates the original's reasoning traces conditioned on the answer. The inverse model is trained once and reused across incoming batches of answer-only data, with all generation happening offline and up front, making the approach particularly suited to continual post-training. Using Qwen3-0.6B on the Countdown and GSM8K tasks, we show that after GRPO training has saturated, a single offline distillation round from the inverse model roughly doubles pass@1 on hard prompts and, on Countdown, outperforms rejection-sampling self-distillation on every hard-tail metric at every mix ratio (with smaller gains on GSM8K); rejection sampling by construction has no signal on problems the model never solves on its own. Continued GRPO on the fresh data retains an edge at hard pass@1 but trails distillation at pass@8 and pass@32 while regenerating rollouts at every step. As an added bonus we see that the inverse model has a second application as an accurate verifier of correctness. When used for best-of-$n$ selection at test time, it raises selection accuracy on Countdown from 0.753 (majority voting) to 0.969.

**comment/C1：** Published at the COLM 2026 Workshop on Efficient Reasoning

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [Backpropagating Verifier Rewards Through Continuous Flow Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19080v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19080

**作者：** Xu Han、Jiajing Hu、Li-Ping Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19080v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19080v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为「连续流」语言模型提出 RL 后训练框架：模型在多个步骤中逐步去噪出连续 token 嵌入，仅在最后一步解码为离散 token，并训练一个 critic 估计中间嵌入的价值，以绕过不可微的解码环节、用低方差的路径梯度把 verifier 奖励反传回连续动作，在四个推理任务上验证其优于同类基线。

**相关性简评：** 论文虽属纯文本而非视觉多模态，但其「对中间连续表征估值 + 逐步奖励反传」的机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的训练监督、中间步贡献分配与信用分配提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Backpropagating Verifier Rewards Through Continuous Flow Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially improved the reasoning of autoregressive and discrete-diffusion language models. Language models that generate continuous token embeddings are emerging as a strong alternative, but RL post-training for them remains understudied. We propose \\method{}, a post-training framework for continuous flow language models. Such a model denoises token embeddings over multiple steps and decodes them into discrete tokens only at the last step. To obtain pathwise gradients, which backpropagate through continuous actions and have much lower variance than REINFORCE, we introduce a critic that estimates the value of intermediate token embeddings, bypassing the non-differentiable decoding step. The critic must also keep track of the value function as the policy changes; to this end, we design a critic that predicts a value from every token representation and improves policy optimization. Across four reasoning tasks, \\method{} reliably improves a continuous flow language model through RL post-training and outperforms baseline methods of the same type.

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Demystifying On-Policy Distillation: Where Does the Teacher's Advantage Live?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19145v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19145

**作者：** Yiping Li、Fan Zhao、Zhibo Hou、Zhiyu An、Wan Du

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19145v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19145v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从知识蒸馏视角重新审视 on-policy distillation，在师生分歧的位置做受控 token 翻转，量化教师纠正带来的准确率增益，并比较按 mass、KL 与随机方式选择纠正点的效果，还分析了 OPD 之后干预是否仍有价值及其与 argmax 一致性的关系。

**相关性简评：** 其「翻转—增益」的因果式贡献测量与选择性监督设计，可能可供核查 LVR 训练中轨迹监督的价值分配、教师信号筛选与何时停止纠正，但属纯文本蒸馏场景，需确认能否迁移到 latent 状态监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Demystifying On-Policy Distillation: Where Does the Teacher's Advantage Live?

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) is increasingly studied as a cheaper alternative to reinforcement learning, yet it remains a form of knowledge distillation (KD) that relies on teacher supervision. We revisit OPD from the KD perspective by asking where the teacher's advantage lies along student-generated trajectories. Controlled token flips at teacher-student disagreements directly measure the accuracy gained from teacher corrections. Single-token flips yield a modest average gain on selected failed trajectories, whereas repeated flips raise accuracy from 28.4% to 74.5%, close to the teacher's 76.9%. Gains continue to accumulate with more flips, but mass- and KL-based selection outperform random flips by 15-18 percentage points at counts of similar magnitude. Selective intervention remains useful after OPD, but greater argmax agreement does not consistently improve accuracy across model pairs. We advocate training schedules that learn more valuable teacher corrections while maintaining distributional stability. Controlled flips and fork-resolution analysis evaluate OPD beyond accuracy by measuring which teacher preferences the student acquires and what further correction can recover.

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [Test-Time Forecasting: Recurrent Depth Enables In-Context Dynamics Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19552v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19552

**作者：** Jeffrey B. Lai、John Quinn、Anthony Bao、William Gilpin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19552v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19552v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文建立 linear attention 与（扩展）动态模式分解的联系，说明 looped transformer 可在上下文中模拟动力系统传播算子，并提出 Test-Time Forecasting 框架：把一步预测器的梯度下降迭代当作 recurrent depth，在 ODE/PDE 上训练后于自回归 rollout 中泛化到分布外动力学。

**相关性简评：** 「迭代即深度」的 recurrent depth 设计、仅用一步预测监督却在多步 rollout 中泛化的现象，可能为 LVR 中循环式 latent 状态生成、动态计算与轨迹贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索，但材料无视觉或多模态成分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Test-Time Forecasting: Recurrent Depth Enables In-Context Dynamics Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Foundation models for dynamical systems can forecast unseen partial differential equations (PDEs), ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and time series during inference. However, it remains an open question whether these foundation models truly learn generalizable in-context forecasting strategies. We show that looped transformers are capable of emulating propagators for dynamical systems in-context by first establishing a link between linear attention and (extended) dynamic mode decomposition. We then generalize this insight into the $\\textit{Test-Time Forecasting}$ (TTF) framework in which a one-step forecaster leverages iterations of gradient descent as recurrent depth. We demonstrate that lightweight TTF models trained on ODEs and PDEs generalize to out-of-distribution system dynamics during autoregressive rollout after being trained for one-step prediction. Our results reinforce TTF as a theoretically motivated framework for designing recurrent depth models capable of forecasting unseen dynamical systems at test-time.

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Divergent Paths: Learning an Outcome-Predictive Representation of LLM Agent Trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19614v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19614

**作者：** Alexander Gill、Guram Gogia、Francisco Guzmán

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19614v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19614v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 LLM agent 轨迹视为动作与环境观测共享嵌入空间中的路径，用逐任务判别器及元学习的 Precog-RM 在终止前预测 rollout 成败，并作为 GRPO 的早停控制器把训练 FLOPs 降低最多 47.3%。

**相关性简评：** 其「由潜在轨迹表示预测结果并据此提前终止」的机制，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策与推理成本—收益权衡、轨迹监督提供可核查线索，但研究对象是文本 agent 轨迹而非视觉推理状态，迁移需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Divergent Paths: Learning an Outcome-Predictive Representation of LLM Agent Trajectories

**abstract/A1：** In this work, we reframe agentic trajectories as paths through a shared embedding space of actions and environmental observations. We show that successful and failed rollouts of the same task separate in this space, with simple per-task discriminators predicting held-out rollout outcomes at up to 0.95 AUROC across three agent benchmarks and three policy models. To exploit this signal across tasks, we introduce Precog-RM, a transformer-based model that meta-learns a representation in which outcome prediction can be performed in a few-shot setting. We show that Precog-RM generalizes to unseen tasks and models, and gives confident predictions of trajectory success long before termination. Deployed as an early stopping controller for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on Tau2-Bench, Precog-RM cuts training FLOPs by up to 47.3\\% while maintaining performance comparable to baseline GRPO.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 8 pages appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [Model What Matters: Decision-Guided Selective World Modeling for Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19642v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19642

**作者：** Maolin Ran、Minda Chen、Di Huang、Wei Xiong、Fei Huang、Weiwen Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19642v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19642v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DGS-WM：在语言智能体的策略与世界模型联合训练中，依据观测单元对后续策略回答的支持程度筛选具决策价值的世界建模目标，并按模型预测置信度自适应调整监督强度，形成决策感知的辅助损失，在多跳问答与交互式网页任务上优于全观测世界建模。

**相关性简评：** 其「按决策价值筛选观测单元 + 置信度自适应加权」的辅助监督思路，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、latent 状态监督的贡献分配相关，可供核查是否迁移到多模态观测单位的选择与加权。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Model What Matters: Decision-Guided Selective World Modeling for Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning provides a direct mechanism for optimizing task objectives in large language model-driven agents, but task rewards are typically sparse trajectory-level signals that only evaluate task success, without directly supervising action-conditioned environment transitions, leaving the model with limited explicit understanding of action consequences and environment dynamics. Recent work introduces auxiliary supervision through policy and world modeling co-training, yet typically treats complete environmental observations uniformly as world modeling targets, overlooking the varying decision relevance of observation content. We propose DGS-WM (Decision-Guided Selective World Modeling), a decision-guided policy-world modeling co-training approach for language agents. DGS-WM employs a Decision-Guided Observation Selection component that filters observation units with decision value as world modeling targets based on their support for subsequent policy responses, and a Confidence-Adaptive Weighting component that adaptively adjusts supervision strength according to the model's prediction confidence on selected targets. Together, these components form a decision-aware auxiliary world modeling loss, jointly optimized with the RL loss on the same policy model. Across multiple benchmarks covering single-hop/multi-hop question answering and interactive web tasks, DGS-WM achieves consistent improvements under different model scales and RL algorithms, demonstrating more stable and effective performance compared to full-observation world modeling methods.

**comment/C1：** Core implementation included as ancillary files

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [Learning When to Trust Medical Knowledge: Regression-Safe Routing for Clinical Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19663v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19663

**作者：** Hui Zhang、Yujia Zeng、Yuhao Xin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19663v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19663v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建覆盖检索证据与推理链的医学知识投毒基准，并把「是否使用外部知识」形式化为逐实例路由；提出冻结求解器的轻量控制器 Med-TrustRouter，依据无泄漏运行时信号估计各动作的期望正确性增益与不确定性，仅在其他动作更有把握时改变答案，从而提升最差情形准确率。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本临床推理，其「采纳外部证据 vs 保留基线答案」的置信路由、期望增益与不确定性估计，可能为 LVR 的 LOOK/STOP 决策和视觉证据筛选提供借鉴；该机制能否迁移到多模态运行时信号尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning When to Trust Medical Knowledge: Regression-Safe Routing for Clinical Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Clinical reasoning LLMs need external medical knowledge, but retrieved evidence and reasoning chains can be outdated, poisoned, or hallucinated. Harmful knowledge can cause regression: changing a correct answer into an incorrect one. We construct a medical knowledge-poisoning benchmark covering retrieved evidence and reasoning chains, with seven attack families filtered by a three-judge "subtle-but-wrong" vote and paired clean exams that separate poisoning effects from question difficulty. No fixed knowledge-use strategy is robust across these settings: strong RAG methods trade robustness to poisoned evidence against accuracy when evidence is useful. We formulate knowledge use as per-instance routing and introduce Med-TrustRouter, a lightweight controller that keeps the solver frozen. From leakage-free runtime signals, it estimates each action's expected correctness gain and uncertainty, preserving the base answer unless another action is confidently better. Across seven medical reasoning settings, Med-TrustRouter achieves 78.9 macro and 69.2 worst-case accuracy, matching the strongest competitor on macro accuracy while substantially improving the worst-case floor. Its gains transfer across solvers and evidence judges, unlike a hand-coded router using the same signals. Similar performance across linear, tree, and neural action-value heads suggests that the benefit comes from routing rather than estimator complexity. These results support learning when to trust external knowledge for robust clinical reasoning.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 8 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Teaching Agents in their Own Style: Blackbox Teacher Distillation for Web Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19834v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19834

**作者：** Md Farhan Ishmam、Kenneth Marino

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19834v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19834v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WebKD：黑盒教师充当学生失败轨迹的编辑者，定位首个错误点后以学生自身风格最小改写推理痕迹与动作，并在目标上按推理对动作的贡献加权、用角色非对称 KL 锚点让纠正后的动作可自由移动而推理保持接近学生先验，在 Web 基准上优于行为克隆等基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「推理对动作的贡献加权」与「推理贴近先验、动作自由更新」的角色非对称 KL 锚点，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配与状态监督提供可核查线索，尤其在区分推理状态与答案输出应受多强约束这一问题上。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Teaching Agents in their Own Style: Blackbox Teacher Distillation for Web Agents

**abstract/A1：** Frontier web agents using closed models have high inference and computational costs. Smaller open models can be an alternative, but to be performant, they require training supervision from a human or teacher model. Token-level teacher distillation is not an option for closed teachers as it needs access to the model's logits. The standard approach is to imitate teacher demonstrations offline, but it trains the student on states it has never visited, and in a style it does not write in. We introduce WebKD, where the teacher is the editor of the student's failures. Given a failed episode, the teacher finds the first point of mistake, and rewrites the student's reasoning traces, harness states, and actions from that point onward. The editing reveals that the teacher's corrections can be more surprising to the student than the student's own text. WebKD instead aims to edit minimally. In the data space, the teacher writes each correction in the student's style. In the objective space, the reasoning is weighted by its contribution to the action. A role-asymmetric KL anchor allows the corrected action to move freely while keeping the reasoning near the student's prior. On the TimeWarp benchmark, WebKD students gain about 5.5-10.7 pp over behavior cloning, outcome-reward RL, and teacher editing baselines. The performance transfers to unseen versions and to the live web in the OnlineMind2Web benchmark. WebKD opens a new way of distilling black-box models to improve the capabilities of open-weight web agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [Explorer-Zero: Improving Self-Play for Long-Horizon Tasks with Decomposable Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19868v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19868

**作者：** Tianyu Liu、Davide Belli、Bence Major、Arash Behboodi、Tribhuvanesh Orekondy

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19868v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19868v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Explorer-Zero 自博弈框架：由可学习的 proposer 在环境中探索并生成任务，同时记录中间与最终状态作为可验证检查点，从而给出细粒度的部分完成奖励，并交替训练 proposer 与 solver 以自适应调整课程。

**相关性简评：** 其用中间状态检查点构造可验证子任务奖励、缓解不同完成度 rollout 坍缩的思路，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的密集监督与贡献分配提供方法线索；但论文面向纯文本交互环境（ALFWorld 等），与视觉推理的关联及迁移可行性仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Explorer-Zero: Improving Self-Play for Long-Horizon Tasks with Decomposable Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Training LLM agents on long-horizon interactive tasks with reinforcement learning typically relies on human-annotated tasks and reliable verification, both expensive to construct for stateful environments. Self-play offers an alternative, but must generate achievable, compositional tasks and provide feedback beyond binary outcomes. We propose Explorer-Zero, a self-play framework that jointly generates tasks and fine-grained, verifiable rewards through environment interaction. A learned proposer explores the environment, constructs tasks, and records intermediate and final states as verification targets. These checkpoints reward partial completion, while alternating proposer--solver training adapts the generated curriculum. We show theoretically that subtask rewards reduce collapse among unsuccessful rollouts when they complete different numbers of subtasks. Without human-annotated training tasks, Explorer-Zero outperforms the evaluated self-play baselines across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and AppWorld; on AppWorld, our 30B agent reaches 59.67% task success, approaching 62.65% for RL on human-annotated tasks. Further analyses support task-relevant composition and benefits on complex tasks, while self-play initialization improves subsequent human-data RL across all three benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [LatentLoop: Scaling Looped MoE Models with Asymmetric Compute Allocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19975v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19975

**作者：** Zefeng He、Yunkai Chai、Xiaoyang Chen、Tong Zhu、Zongliang Wu、Yun Luo、Yafu Li、Yu Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19975v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19975v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 looped MoE 中不同循环访问的角色差异：首轮访问的残差尺度更小且压缩它不损性能，据此提出 LatentLoop——压缩第一轮序列、第二轮保留全分辨率，节省的算力用于扩大隐藏维度，在 1.3B–7B 多配置下降低验证 NLL 并提升九个基准成绩。

**相关性简评：** 其「同一权重下不同循环访问贡献不对称」的观察、首轮序列压缩与可学习的首轮更新强度系数，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理步骤的算力分配、状态压缩与细节保留之间的权衡提供可供核查的方法线索，但原文为纯语言建模设定，需人工判断迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LatentLoop: Scaling Looped MoE Models with Asymmetric Compute Allocation

**abstract/A1：** Looped Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models combine parameter reuse with sparse computation to improve performance under matched budgets. Prior work demonstrates that different loop visits can play different roles even when they share parameters. To probe the contributions of different loop visits, we learn separate residual coefficients in a model that applies a shared block twice, observing a smaller scale in the first visit. Fixed coefficient experiments show that reducing the first visit's scale preserves or improves performance, whereas the same reductions to the second hurt it. This asymmetry motivates us to explore asymmetric compute allocation. We propose \\textbf{LatentLoop}, which compresses the first visit's sequence, retains full resolution in the second, and reallocates saved computation to increase the hidden size. We further learn how strongly the first visit updates the full sequence, and share MoE weights across visits while using separate attention parameters to accommodate their different sequence resolutions. Across 15 configurations spanning 1.3B--7B parameters, with the largest budget exceeding $10^{21}$ FLOPs, LatentLoop consistently lowers validation NLL. At a fitted NLL of 1.988, predicted compute savings are 19.0\\% and 7.9\\% relative to vanilla MoE and the looped baseline. It also improves average scores on nine benchmarks at all sizes in 0-shot and 5-shot settings. These results suggest asymmetric compute allocation is a promising direction for looped MoE models.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Beyond Correctness: Compute-Responsive Data Selection for Reasoning Model Post-Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20057v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20057

**作者：** Liang He、Xiangyu Li、Yuxin Wang、Hanbin Zhang、Mingyu Cao、Zhaomiao Guo、Xilu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20057v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20057v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出按「额外推理计算能否把初始失败转为成功」筛选后训练数据（CRDS），在 Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B 上用 SFT 与 on-policy 蒸馏验证：用 compute-responsive 样本训练可提升低算力失败恢复率并改善 512–4096 token 的算力—准确率前沿。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本任务、不涉及视觉，但以「对额外推理的边际效用」作为样本选择与监督信号，可能可供核查 LVR 训练中如何挑选能从 latent 推理步骤获益的样本、以及如何评估推理步骤的贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Correctness: Compute-Responsive Data Selection for Reasoning Model Post-Training

**abstract/A1：** Reasoning language models increasingly rely on test-time compute scaling, yet most existing work studies how to allocate inference computation while treating the model itself as fixed. We ask a complementary question: can reasoning models be post-trained to make better use of a given amount of test-time compute, and which examples are most useful for this goal? Our key observation is that reasoning problems differ substantially in their response to additional computation. Some are already solved under limited compute, others remain unresolved even after further reasoning, while a distinct subset changes from complete initial failure to success when additional reasoning is provided. We call these compute-responsive (CR) examples and introduce Compute-Responsive Data Selection (CRDS), a post-training data-selection principle that prioritizes examples with empirically demonstrated marginal utility from additional reasoning. On Qwen3-8B, SFT on CR examples recovers 45.3\\% of held-out low-compute failures, compared with 40.6\\% when training on already-solved examples. The advantage extends beyond the selected CR split: SFT-CR reaches 51.8\\% overall accuracy on an independently sampled test distribution, with the gain concentrated on compute-responsive problems. CR training also shifts the compute--accuracy frontier across inference budgets from 512 to 4,096 tokens, outperforming matched non-CR training throughout and reaching 48.1\\% recovery at the largest budget. The same pattern is supported across multiple data sources, remains visible under on-policy distillation, and extends to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct when CR sets are reconstructed separately for that model. Better test-time compute utilization can therefore be learned through post-training, with recoverable responses to additional reasoning providing a particularly informative signal for selecting training examples.

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [From Plato to Aristotle: Toward a General Theory of Representation Hypotheses](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20286v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20286

**作者：** Dai Shi、Andi Han、Xiaoyu Li、Luke Thompson、José Miguel Hernández-Lobato

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20286v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20286v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Lattice Representation Hypothesis，把邻域重叠、CKA、生成树重叠等表示对齐判据统一到同一格结构上，指出 Platonic 表示假说的收敛与证据分处不同层次，并分析校准如何削弱全局对齐，在大量视觉与语言模型及两个理论模型上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其对不同相似度判据所处层次、邻域重叠与 CKA 差异及校准影响的讨论，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度的度量选择与结果解读提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Plato to Aristotle: Toward a General Theory of Representation Hypotheses

**abstract/A1：** The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that increasingly capable models converge toward a common representation. However, recent studies have shown different views on aligning representations via their neighborhoods, spanning trees, and distances. We show that these views can differ because the alignment criteria behind them sit at different points of one lattice, and we formalize this explanation as the Lattice Representation Hypothesis (LRH). Under the LRH, the convergence claimed by the PRH is agreement at the top of the lattice, whereas its evidence comes from neighbor overlap, a criterion near the bottom. The Aristotelian Representation Hypothesis (ARH) restricts convergence to local neighborhoods because calibration keeps the growth of neighbor overlap with model size but largely removes that of centered kernel alignment. We show that this removal can come from calibrating a maximum over layers, so global convergence remains open. Back into Plato's Cave (BPC) infers coarse shared structure from the collapse of neighbor overlap on larger evaluation sets, whereas we trace it at least partly to a fixed number of neighbors covering shorter distances. We show that the spanning-tree overlap used by Structure over Geometry (SOG) mostly repeats one-nearest-neighbor overlap. The LRH also extends to independently trained models, whose training procedure and objective decide the criteria met reliably across runs. We prove this claim for the training procedure in a linear autoencoder and for the objective in a finite contrastive Gibbs model. Experiments on dozens of pretrained vision and language models and thousands of runs of both theoretical models support these conclusions. We hope this work paves the way toward a general theory of representation hypotheses, with a framework to analyze them and compare their evidence. Project page: https://eethanshi.github.io/LRH

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [TreeFlex: Agents Navigating Their Own Search Trees](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20334v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20334

**作者：** Dongzhuo Li、Fangzhou Cheng、Weiwei Li、Kevin Han、Haixu Ma、Zhongchang Sun、Ruiyi Li、Serena Li、Xiangjun Fan、Lizhu Zhang、Yuandong Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20334v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20334v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** TreeFlex 让 agent 自身策略在 harness 维护的搜索树上导航：模型分 Reflection 与 Execution 两个角色，自主决定深化、分支、回溯、验证或停止，并用树感知的 GRPO 联合训练导航、任务动作、检查点与方案评审，在 deep-research 与 SWE-bench Verified 上超过 ReAct 和 FoldAgent。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时回溯/验证/停止」的学习式决策、节点摘要式状态检查点与上下文压缩，可能为 LVR 的推理状态更新、证据筛选和停止决策提供可核查的方法线索，但属纯文本 agent 场景，需确认能否迁移到多模态 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TreeFlex: Agents Navigating Their Own Search Trees

**abstract/A1：** A ReAct-style agent accumulates observations, intermediate results, and failed attempts in a single context. As this context grows, stale and off-path information consumes its limited capacity and obscures relevant evidence, making focused reasoning increasingly difficult. We introduce TreeFlex, a framework in which the agent's own policy decides where to search. The agent records each intermediate state, with a short summary, as a node in a search tree maintained by the harness. The policy is a single language model that acts in two roles. In the Reflection role, it has a global view of the tree through these summaries and picks a node to resume, together with an instruction. In the Execution role, the harness restores the path that led to that node, and the model carries out the instruction with the usual task tools until it saves a new node or proposes a solution, which Reflection then approves or sends back to another node. Through these choices, the policy decides whether to deepen the current line, branch from or revisit an earlier state, verify a proposal, or stop, without a fixed traversal order or a score for each node. We train navigation, task actions, checkpointing, and proposal review jointly with a tree-aware adaptation of GRPO, using task-level outcome rewards augmented by process rewards. After small-scale RL of a 4B model, with the same active-context limit for all methods, TreeFlex reaches 48.8% accuracy on a challenging deep-research benchmark, versus 41.5% for flat ReAct and 42.8% for FoldAgent, and its Pass@8 is about ten points higher than either. On SWE-bench Verified, with all three systems trained with RL from the same 9B model, TreeFlex resolves 54.9% of tasks, versus 43.3% for FoldAgent and 45.2% for its step-wise variant.

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [Look Before You Restore: Learning to Predict Before Acting for Agentic Image Restoration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20384v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20384

**作者：** Xu Zhang、Jun Wan、Lefei Zhang、Liangpei Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20384v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20384v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PreActIR：一个混合退化图像复原的 agent 框架，用工具条件化的 world model 在执行前预测候选工具对残差退化、画质与保真度的影响及风险与不确定性，由独立 verifier 决定提交或回滚后重新规划，并在固定候选池与预算下用更少真实工具调用获得更好指标。

**相关性简评：** 其「先预测后果再调用」的世界模型、风险与不确定性排序、verifier 提交–回滚以及区分选择器与候选空间的归因审计，或可供核查 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的收益成本权衡与视觉证据筛选，但任务为图像复原，迁移线索尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Look Before You Restore: Learning to Predict Before Acting for Agentic Image Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Agents for mixed-degradation image restoration typically learn what a tool does to an image only after running it: failed calls waste computation and harmful outputs go unnoticed until too late. We propose PreActIR, an agentic restoration framework that predicts tool consequences before execution. For each candidate tool, a tool-conditioned world model forecasts changes in residual degradation, image quality, and fidelity, together with the risk of harm and the uncertainty of the prediction. The agent executes the top-ranked candidate, and an independent verifier commits or rolls back its output before the agent replans from the observed state. PreActIR also audits whether performance is limited by the selector or by the candidate space, and studies targeted tool adaptation as a controlled way to expand that space. In a controlled evaluation that fixes the candidate pool, verifier, and budget and employs reference-annotated degradation states, PreActIR uses 42.5% fewer real tool calls and 63.4% fewer rejected calls than reactive, execute-then-evaluate trial and error, while improving Y-PSNR by 0.92 dB.

**comment/C1：** Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [Training Language Models to Fail Gracefully](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20401v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20401

**作者：** Daman Arora、Xuecheng Liu、Himanshu Gaurav Singh、Fahim Tajwar、Abitha Thankaraj、Ruslan Salakhutdinov、Haiwen Feng、Yuda Song、Andrea Zanette

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20401v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20401v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出训练语言模型「优雅失败」：在可能成功时保留成功行为，在行动不可靠时改用安全回退策略；方法 GracefulRL 通过重塑优化景观缓解策略梯度的脆弱性，仅凭结果级奖励即可让该行为自发出现，无需回退时机标注或推理期验证器。

**相关性简评：** 该文研究的正是「何时不该继续行动」的选择性回退，与 LVR 中 STOP 决策和能力边界判断直接呼应；其「仅用结果奖励诱导回退、不依赖额外推理期机制」的训练思路可供核查能否迁移到 VLM 推理的停止与弃答监督上。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training Language Models to Fail Gracefully

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning makes language models better at solving tasks, but the same mechanism also rewards guessing. When success is the only way to earn reward, opting for a safe fallback is always worse than a long-shot attempt. When a wrong action can corrupt a database or ship broken code, that incentive can be costly. Therefore, we propose training models to fail gracefully: to preserve successful behavior whenever possible while learning to execute a safe fallback policy when acting would be unreliable. However, naively using policy gradients with intermediate reward for fallback behavior is brittle due to unfavorable optimization dynamics. We address this challenge with GracefulRL, which reshapes the optimization landscape to promote graceful failure. Importantly, this behavior emerges directly from outcome-level reward signals, without annotations specifying when to fall back or additional inference-time machinery such as a verifier. Across a range of tasks and scales, we train models that largely preserve their task-solving capability while selectively falling back when acting would be unreliable. More broadly, our results show that reinforcement learning can not only incentivize models to succeed, but also to reason about the limits of their capabilities and change course when they determine that success is out of reach.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Hindsight-Privileged RLVR: Single-Rollout Token Credit Without Group Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20497v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20497

**作者：** Wei Chen、Yubing Wu、Zhou Xu、Haoyue Zhang、Delu Zeng、Jiahao Wang、Junmei Yang、Pan tiewen、Jingfeng Liu、Zhiheng Zhou、Bo Li、Min Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20497v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20497v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HP-RLVR：一种单次 rollout 的 RLVR 方法，用同一轨迹的因果历史视角与「事后视角」（含已实现未来和验证器反馈）之间的归一化 Jensen–Shannon 散度，为每个 token 生成有界信用权重，无需参考解或 critic，在数学、代码和 ALFWorld 上以更少 rollout 达到与 GRPO 相当的准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其 token 级信用分配与「失败轨迹也有梯度信号」的设计，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的贡献分配、奖励稀疏和 GRPO 全同结果导致的零信号问题提供可核查的训练线索；但原文为纯文本任务，未涉及视觉状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hindsight-Privileged RLVR: Single-Rollout Token Credit Without Group Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains language models from checked task outcomes, but assigning a terminal reward to individual reasoning tokens or actions remains difficult. Group-relative methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) sample several attempts per prompt, but lose their reward-based learning signal when all attempts receive the same outcome. We propose Hindsight-Privileged RLVR (HP-RLVR), a single-rollout method that turns a completed trajectory's own future into token-level update weights. The generating policy snapshot scores each eligible token under its causal history and under a hindsight context containing the realized future and verifier feedback. A normalized Jensen--Shannon divergence between the two views weights the reward relative to a historical baseline, yielding bounded token credit for both successful and failed attempts, with no reference solution or learned critic. In paired Qwen3-8B runs over a shared $100$-step schedule, HP-RLVR trains $1.21\\times$, $1.46\\times$, and $2.30\\times$ faster than GRPO on mathematics, code generation, and ALFWorld, using one eighth the rollouts per prompt. Accuracy is comparable overall, with HP-RLVR ahead on mathematics at all three sizes and GRPO ahead on code and ALFWorld. In ALFWorld, $43.2\\%$ (4B) and $49.0\\%$ (8B) of GRPO's gradient-norm records are zero, versus none for HP-RLVR.

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [Agentic Self-Awareness: Aligning Knowing, Acting, and Holding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19226v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19226

**作者：** Qinglin Chen、Hiroaki Hayashi、Jiaxin Zhang、Chien-Sheng Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19226v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19226v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 agentic self-awareness（ASA）框架，区分「任务是否可行」与「智能体是否可解」，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 与零售 τ²-bench 上构造成对的可行/阻塞任务，并用 M0–M3 分级标准分离「识别阻塞」与「以正确理由采取延迟行动」；结果显示识别率 84.4% 但严格 SA 仅 40.6%，不更新权重的 harness 搜索可将其提升至 93.8%。

**相关性简评：** 其「知道却未行动」的差距刻画与 M0–M3 评分，可能为 LVR 的停止/弃权决策及「latent 内容是否真被答案使用」的评估提供可核查的参照线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agentic Self-Awareness: Aligning Knowing, Acting, and Holding

**abstract/A1：** Frontier AI agents increasingly act in environments where seemingly solvable tasks may lack prerequisites, contain conflicting requirements, or reveal blockers only during execution. We formulate agentic self-awareness (ASA) as the alignment of task-grounded feasibility judgments (know), warranted behavior (act), and responses to reconsideration (hold), distinguishing what a task permits from what an agent can solve. We build paired feasible and blocked tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.1 and retail $τ^2$-bench and introduce an authorship-aware M0-M3 rubric that separates recognizing a blocker from taking the certified deferral action for the correct reason. Separate probes assess stated feasibility beliefs and responses to a single challenge. In a Terminal-Bench protocol baseline, blocker recognition reaches 84.4%, but strict self-awareness (SA) reaches only 40.6%. Harness search without model-weight updates increases strict SA from 45.3% to 93.8% on reused Terminal-Bench search tasks; selection for abstention alone, however, can reduce success on feasible tasks. Independently initialized joint-objective searches improve both blocked-task SA and feasible-task solve on their own search sets. Retail held-out gains remain limited, and Terminal-Bench blocked-task transfer is not measured. This evaluation pipeline makes the recognition-action gap measurable and sets a two-sided target for improving agent decisions under changing task evidence.

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Robust Explanatory Analysis for Structured Optimization Networks (REASON) in Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19378v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19378

**作者：** Vahid Jebraeeli、Hamid Krim、Keisuke Fujii

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19378v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19378v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 REASON 两环路机制：快速环先用现有证据消解可解残差，残余的认知残差再触发中速环主动获取额外证据，并以残差下降衡量其信息价值。

**相关性简评：** 其「残差触发追加计算、按信息价值决定是否继续取证」的元推理框架，可能与 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的动态计算分配与停止决策形成概念类比，可供核查；但论文未涉及视觉语言模型或 latent 状态，属远域启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Explanatory Analysis for Structured Optimization Networks (REASON) in Learning

**abstract/A1：** We seek a hierarchical resolution of residual error under a composite learning objective as a principled mechanism for identifying and exploiting additional information, particularly under distribution shift and perturbation. Inspired by the System 1/System 2 paradigm, we formulate this principle as a two-loop mechanism serving a common inference goal. A fast, System 1-like loop resolves the residual addressable by the available evidence; the remaining epistemic residual triggers a medium speed, System 2-like loop that actively acquires additional evidence and measures its informational value through residual reduction. Over nested solution spaces, the medium-loop optimum is provably no worse than the fast-loop optimum. Experiments show that the resulting two-loop model consistently improves upon the fast loop at approximately half the computation of the medium loop, including under unseen corruptions, and, when provided a cleaner observation, outperforms staged classifiers and learning-to-defer methods at equal computational cost.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [Adaptive Observation Control for Active Visual Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19624

**作者：** Dikai Li、Weijia Wang、Yuan-Gen Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19624v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为主动视觉识别提出可控制的观察接口：预测每次注视的尺度以决定视野，配合尺度门控 foveation 与高分辨率细节 token 分支细化裁剪，并用标签监督的 stop head 实现输入相关的提前停止，使同一 checkpoint 可在不同阈值下调节准确率与计算量。

**相关性简评：** 其自适应注视次数、尺度选择与停止头对应 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 和动态计算预算的问题，可作为证据筛选与停止决策评价目标的参考；但工作面向图像分类而非多模态推理，能否迁移到 latent 推理需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Observation Control for Active Visual Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Visual recognition models usually process a fixed view under a fixed computation budget, although images often benefit from different scales and amounts of spatial evidence. Active vision can adapt by observing a coarse scene and then selecting informative fixations, but the public AdaptiveNN-DeiT-S pipeline still fixes both field of view and observation count. We extend this fixed-step recognizer with a controllable observation interface that adapts both the field of view and the number of fixations to the input. Specifically, the model predicts a per-fixation scale that sets its field of view, refines each selected crop with optional scale-gated foveation and a residual high-resolution detail-token branch, and uses label-grounded stop heads for input-dependent early termination. Experiments across fine-grained benchmarks, full ImageNet-1K, and ImageNet-A/R-derived classification splits show that the interface remains competitive with fixed-step references while exposing a controllable observation budget. The same checkpoint enables threshold-controlled accuracy-compute trade-offs without per-threshold retraining. On sequential ImageNet-1K, the learned stop rule outperforms fixed-step truncation of the same checkpoint, sustaining 81.9% top-1 using 2.45 of the four focus steps and exceeding three-step truncation at lower cost. On each benchmark, a variant combining scale control and detail-token fusion achieves the highest accuracy among the tested component-added non-exit variants. Our code is available at https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/LI-Dikai.

**comment/C1：** This paper has been accepted for presentation in ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [Towards a Data-Centric Understanding of Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19108v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19108

**作者：** Mahdieh Mallahnezhad、Masoud Jafaripour、Mohamadali Tofigh、Sheila Schoepp、Osmar R. Zaiane

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19108v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19108v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 一篇综述，从数据视角梳理为空间推理任务微调预训练 MLLM 的监督到强化学习工作，按数据集设计、构建与评测三类组织，归纳规则模板、LLM 提示、程序化生成、人在回路四类标注构建方法，并指出动态标注、高效空间推理、评测标准化等方向。

**相关性简评：** 空间推理依赖几何与实体关系是否保留在内部状态中，属主线关注的能力；其数据集构建方式与评测差异（评分、容差、基准类型）可供核查 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与评测设计，但原文未直接考察 latent 状态或推理过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards a Data-Centric Understanding of Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been widely applied in embodied agents and robotics for vision tasks. However, MLLMs suffer from limitations in spatial reasoning and perception. Post-training techniques have shown promise for improving these capabilities; yet, there are limited data-driven surveys of such approaches. In this survey, we consider approaches to fine-tuning pretrained MLLMs for spatial reasoning tasks, ranging from supervised to reinforcement learning, with an emphasis on the datasets used. We investigate themes of dataset construction, data modality and annotation, and evaluation of the resulting models. Based on these themes, we categorize research into three groups: dataset design, dataset construction and evaluation. We find four main methods for constructing fixed spatial annotations: rules and templates, LLM prompting, procedural generation, and human-in-the-loop. We also observe substantial variation in evaluation approaches, including different scoring methods, tolerance metrics, and benchmark types. Finally, we identify opportunities for future research in unified dynamic annotation, efficient spatial reasoning, standardization of evaluation, expanded spatial range, and human-centered spatial interactions.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables, Under review at Artificial Intelligence Review

</details>
