### 37. [Seeing, Maintaining, and Learning: The Evolution of Multimodal Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19366v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19366

**作者：** Minghao Guo、Qingyue Jiao、Xiujin Liu、Jianhong Pang、Ruimeng Yang、Naihao Xu、Liwei Che、Haochen Xue、Ziqi Wang、Xinying Cai、Jiaxuan Luo、Jiaxin Deng、Xuesong Ye、Jintao Qu、Wenyao Gao、Jingwen Gu、Chenyang Zhao、Shichun Liu、Guibin Zhang、Zefang Liu、Siyin Wang、Wujiang Xu、Danrui Li、Zeru Shi、Woongyeong Yeo、Shuang Liu、Wei-Chieh Huang、Weizhi Zhang、Tianyu Yang、Kangsan Kim、Yebowen Hu、Bowen Wen、Bowen Jiang、Haohao Su、Ming Yin、Sheldon Yu、Mian Zhang、Yuyan Chen、Hanrong Zhang、Kai Mei、Zihao Zhang、Mingyu Jin、Xi Zhu、Hengyi Wang、Tianxin Wei、Linyong Nan、Zhenwen Liang、Zhendong Chu、Ziqi Huang、Yongye Su、Sixun Dong、Shujian Liu、Kaiqu Liang、Jiaojiao Han、Zhaoye Fei、Kun Wang、Shihan Dou、Zhichun Guo、Xingjian Diao、Zeqi Gu、Heng Wang、Tao Feng、Cheng Qian、Hamid Palangi、Silei Xu、Xi Yun、Yi Zhu、Shilong Liu、Qingsong Wen、Sung Ju Hwang、Zuxuan Wu、Yu-Gang Jiang、Yiyu Shi、Dimitris N. Metaxas、Jiaxuan You、Julian McAuley、Mengdi Wang、Jiawei Han、Philip S. Yu、Ziyi Ye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19366v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19366v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该综述提出多模态智能体记忆的双轴分析框架：轴一关注多模态信息如何形成与表示（模态嵌入、文本与结构化记录、latent 及参数化表示），轴二将记忆管理分为静态、动态与自演化三层，并归纳设计模式、权衡原则与未来挑战。

**相关性简评：** 其关于保留哪些信息、如何编码、以及让记忆使用锚定于可恢复证据的讨论，与 LVR 关心的内部状态表示、视觉证据筛选和跨步更新有交集，latent/参数化表示与记忆策略演化等线索可供核查；但作为综述，它不提供推理期 latent 机制或实验证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Seeing, Maintaining, and Learning: The Evolution of Multimodal Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal models and agents have emerged to enable AI systems to learn from and interact with the real world more like humans by integrating information across multiple modalities. However, enabling multimodal agents to continually acquire useful information from complex, heterogeneous multimodal data remains a significant challenge as they move toward long-horizon tasks and lifelong settings. Addressing this challenge requires effective memory mechanisms that can maintain continuity across sessions, adapt to changing users and environments, and refine memory-management policies through accumulated experience. We introduce a two-axis analytical framework for multimodal agent memory structured around two key questions: how multimodal information is formed and represented (Axis I, Formation and Representation), and how its management adapts with accumulated experience (Axis II, Memory Management). Axis I, Formation and Representation, characterizes what information is retained and how it is encoded (e.g., modality-specific embeddings, textual and structured records, latent and parametric representations) to support subsequent reasoning and action. Axis II, Memory Management, distinguishes three tiers: T1 Static Memory reuses fixed memory; T2 Dynamic Memory updates memory through a stable policy; and T3 Self-Evolving Memory updates memory through an evolving policy. Based on this framework, we synthesize recurring design patterns and derive principles for balancing source fidelity with abstraction, maintaining temporal validity and provenance, grounding memory use in recoverable evidence, and evolving memory policies over time. Furthermore, we discuss the challenges and potential future directions for the memory mechanism of multimodal agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [Holi-Spatial 360: Generative Reconstruction from Sparse Panoramas for Spatial Rendering and Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19502v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19502

**作者：** Yuanyuan Gao、Zhirui Liu、Yuning Gong、Yifei Liu、Yifan Zhan、Kuixuan Jiao、Xiaolong Zhou、Yifan Yang、Hongjie Zhang、Xue Yang、Dan Xu、Zhihang Zhong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19502v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19502v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Holi-Spatial 360 框架，把稀疏全景图重建为带标注的多房间 3DGS 室内环境，并据此构建含 1K+ 场景、10 万余实例标注、200 万空间问答对的 Holi360 数据集与 VLM 空间理解基准，显示微调可提升空间推理表现。

**相关性简评：** 其空间问答基准与多房间环境可用于核查 VLM 内部状态是否保留几何与实体关系信息，并可能作为视觉证据筛选与长度泛化评测的载体，但论文本身不研究 latent 推理状态生成，与 LVR 主线距离待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Holi-Spatial 360: Generative Reconstruction from Sparse Panoramas for Spatial Rendering and Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** Spatial intelligence requires scalable 3D environments that are both photorealistic and semantically grounded, yet existing indoor datasets are dominated by room-scale captures and costly LiDAR acquisition. Online real-estate platforms offer abundant panoramas of diverse multi-room homes, but sparse viewpoints and texture-poor regions make reconstruction severely under-constrained. We introduce Holi-Spatial 360, a generative reconstruction and data curation framework for converting these panoramas into annotated multi-room 3D environments. Because reconstruction quality bounds subsequent generation, our Correspondence-Guided Geometric Optimization converts feed-forward reconstruction priors into reliable cross-view constraints and builds a coherent 3D Gaussian Splatting representation as 3D spatial memory. Adapted single-image and video priors then restore appearance and complete continuous trajectories while preserving observed content. The reconstructed scenes support arbitrary RGB-D rendering and an automated curation pipeline that combines end-to-end 3D grounding with VLM-based verification. Holi360 contains over 1K multi-room 3DGS scenes, more than 100K instance annotations, and 2M spatial question-answer pairs. We also introduce the Holi360 benchmark to evaluate the spatial understanding of VLMs in real-world, multi-room indoor environments. Experiments demonstrate superior reconstruction and curation quality under sparse panoramic inputs, while fine-tuning VLMs on Holi360 substantially improves spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [Lotus: Learning to Understand Time-series by Learning to Forecast](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18877v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18877

**作者：** Van Dai Do、Huu Hiep Nguyen、Minh Hoang Nguyen、Manh Nguyen、Hung Le

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18877v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18877v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「forecast grounding」预训练原则并实现 LOTUS：把时间序列按通道分块并映射进 LLM 嵌入空间（叠加频谱表征），用 LLM 隐藏状态预测下一 patch，在三种骨干、六个下游数据集上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其让语言对齐表示保持「对未来演化可预测」的辅助目标，以及用隐藏状态预测下一 patch 的做法，可能与 LVR 中防止 latent 表示信息坍缩、对内部状态施加监督与贡献分配的线索相关，可供核查，但跨模态迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lotus: Learning to Understand Time-series by Learning to Forecast

**abstract/A1：** Time-series language models typically align numerical signals with language using labels, captions, or question-answer pairs. Although such supervision encourages linguistic representations, it does not explicitly require them to preserve information about how the underlying time-series signal evolves. We introduce forecast grounding, a pretraining principle that requires language-aligned time-series representations to remain predictive of the future evolution before they are adapted to downstream tasks. We instantiate this principle in LOTUS, which divides each time-series channel into non-overlapping patches and maps them into the LLM embedding space. It then predicts the next patch from the LLM hidden states of the time-series tokens, encouraging the learned representation to retain temporal information. To capture both temporal changes and periodic patterns, we also propose a temporal-spectral patch representation that combines each raw time-series patch with its spectral representation before mapping it into the LLM embedding space. Across three language model backbones and six downstream evaluation datasets, LOTUS achieves the best average performance with minimal trainable parameters, reaching 59.91% accuracy and outperforming the strongest matched baseline by 3.36%. Our representation analyses further show that LOTUS uses the numerical input and preserves temporal structure in its learned patch representations. These results establish forecast grounding as a promising pretraining principle, enabling compact time-series language models to outperform models 25 to 50 times their size on TSRBench.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Token Utility for On-Policy Distillation via Local Trust-Region Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18883

**作者：** Narayanan Sadagopan、Yilan Chen、Vijay Lingam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18883v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从 on-policy 蒸馏目标本身推导每个 token 的重要性，即 logit 梯度与 categorical Fisher 矩阵构成的 utility s\_t，并实例化为连续的 Fisher Utility Weighting，在多个数学推理基准的蒸馏中优于均匀加权。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本蒸馏工作，不涉及视觉证据或 latent 推理状态；但其用训练目标闭式推导 per-token 效用并按重要性分配监督权重的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 步骤的贡献分配与监督权重设计提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Token Utility for On-Policy Distillation via Local Trust-Region Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Token-selection methods for on-policy distillation (OPD) prioritize student-visited positions using diverse signals, including uncertainty, teacher-student disagreement, and mismatch direction. We instead derive token importance from the distillation objective itself: how much can each position's loss decrease under an equal local KL budget on the student's predictive distribution? The answer is the utility $s\_t=g\_t^\\top F\_t^\\dagger g\_t$, where $g\_t$ is the logit gradient and $F\_t$ is the categorical Fisher matrix. For reverse-KL it equals the student-weighted variance of the student-teacher log-probability ratio, and for forward-KL it equals the Pearson $χ^2$ divergence, so the appropriate utility follows from the choice of objective. On shared training checkpoints, several independently motivated criteria, including student entropy, KL disagreement, and TIP, concentrate their importance on nearly the same positions as the derived utility at a 50\\% selection fraction, while diverging at stricter fractions and for direction-specific criteria. We instantiate the utility as Fisher Utility Weighting (FUW), a continuous, mean-normalized token weighting. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three Qwen3 student-teacher pairs, FUW improves over matched uniform OPD under both reverse-KL (1.35-4.0 macro points) and forward-KL (1.08-2.71 macro points), and is competitive with existing selective methods using a single closed-form score derived from the training objective.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, Accepted to Pre-to-Post workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [How Many Ranks Does a Token Need? A Noise-Floor Squelch for Rank-One LoRA Experts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19033v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19033

**作者：** Mei Okonkwo、Owen Castellanos、Ingrid Solberg、Yusuf Demirel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19033v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19033v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SQUELCH：一种无路由器的秩一 LoRA 专家混合，按每 token 自身稳健噪声水平对响应做软阈值，使激活秩随 token 变化，并用 Stein 无偏风险估计选阈值、用 value Gram 矩阵上的 explaining-away 抑制冗余激活，附有 oracle 分配与风险一致性证明，在多任务上超过 LoRA 与最强混合基线。

**相关性简评：** 该文的「按 token 自适应决定激活多少组件」与 explaining-away 抑制冗余、招募互补分量，可能为 LVR 的动态计算/停止决策与 latent 分量筛选提供可核查线索，但它属纯文本 PEFT，与视觉推理无直接联系，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Many Ranks Does a Token Need? A Noise-Floor Squelch for Rank-One LoRA Experts

**abstract/A1：** Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) applies every rank-one component of its update to every token, and mixture-of-experts variants replace this with a learned router that activates a fixed number of components. We argue that the number of components a token needs is a detection question with a classical answer. A token's responses to the rank-one components of a trained LoRA consist of a few large values on a Gaussian noise floor whose width matches that of an untrained adapter, and the floor carries cross-talk rather than adaptation. We introduce SQUELCH, a router-free mixture of rank-one experts that (i) soft-thresholds each token's responses at a multiple of its own robust noise level, so the active rank varies per token; (ii) chooses that multiple for each module by Stein's unbiased risk estimate, so no rank budget is specified; and (iii) replaces the resulting marginal keep-or-drop decisions by one explaining-away step on the value Gram matrix, which suppresses redundant co-active components and recruits complementary ones. SQUELCH adds no trainable parameters to LoRA. We prove that the squelch is within a logarithmic factor of the oracle input-dependent allocation, that the selected threshold is risk-consistent, and that explaining away is a descent step toward the joint sparse code whose error is controlled by the coherence of co-active components. On commonsense reasoning, knowledge, math, code, and GLUE with LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen3, and DeBERTaV3, SQUELCH improves over LoRA by 2.2--3.2 points and over the strongest mixture baseline by 0.8--1.2 points while activating 8.6 of 32 components per token on average.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Tracing Morphology, Semantics, and Divergence in Language Acquisition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19041v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19041

**作者：** Forough Shirin Abkenar、Haoshuo Fu、Liang Feng、Ning Yan、Masood S. Mortazavi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19041v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19041v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 利用 Pythia、OLMo 等开源模型的中间 checkpoint，在 teacher-forcing 下考察形态结构获取、embedding drift、重复主导与共现效应随训练步数与模型规模的演变，发现形态结构早于语义表示稳定。

**相关性简评：** 其「追踪中间 checkpoint 表示漂移」的分析范式可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节何时进入并稳定于内部状态，embedding drift 亦可能作为信息坍缩的诊断线索；但论文为纯文本、无多模态与 latent 推理内容。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tracing Morphology, Semantics, and Divergence in Language Acquisition

**abstract/A1：** Understanding the dynamics of language acquisition in Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for uncovering how statistical representations evolve into structured linguistic capabilities. However, most existing studies focus primarily on fully trained models, leaving the developmental trajectory across intermediate training stages largely underexplored. In this work, we leverage intermediate checkpoints from open-source models (e.g., Pythia and OLMo) under teacher-forcing evaluation to systematically investigate language acquisition across training steps and model scales. We focus on four core characteristics: morphological structure acquisition, embedding drift, repetition dominance, and co-occurrence effects. Our findings reveal that models acquire morphological structures well before semantic embeddings stabilize. Furthermore, we show that training dataset size has a positive impact on divergence recognition, while co-occurrence during pre-training and repetition during inference have a negative impact on divergence recognition and associative link generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [Loss Curvature as a Sampling-Free Proxy for Memorization in Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19042v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19042

**作者：** Shashank Sule、Xinyue Yu、Boonam Shin、Yuxuan Liu、Abolfazl Hashemi、Hayden Schaeffer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19042v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19042v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从模型层面与样本层面考察扩散模型为何记忆训练数据，提出用训练损失 Hessian 的 Frobenius 范数（输入损失曲率）作为免采样的记忆度量：训练/验证样本的平均曲率可指示记忆起始时刻，并引入反事实记忆定义，证明其受留一模型曲率控制，再用实验验证该界。

**相关性简评：** Hessian 曲率与留一（leave-one-out）影响量构成的免采样样本级归因线索，可能可供核查迁移到 VLM 推理轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督评估；但原文面向图像扩散的记忆机制，与视觉 latent 推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Loss Curvature as a Sampling-Free Proxy for Memorization in Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Understanding the mechanism behind why diffusion models memorize their training data remains an open question in generative modeling. In this paper, we examine this question from two perspectives: (a) the model-level perspective concerning the onset of memorization in a model during training, and (b) the sample-level perspective concerning the proclivity of an individual sample to be memorized by a given model. We show that the so-called input loss curvature, or the Frobenius norm of the Hessian of the diffusion model's training loss, is an effective metric for quantifying both perspectives on memorization. In particular, we empirically demonstrate that input curvatures averaged across training and validation samples can identify when a model is likely to start regurgitating its training data when tracked over training time. However, this metric is not sufficient for measuring an individual sample's likelihood of being memorized. To address this perspective, we introduce a counterfactual definition of memorization in diffusion models, extending the analogous notion from supervised learning proposed by \[Feldman, 2019\]. We prove that this counterfactual memorization is controlled by the input curvature of the leave-one-out model, thus establishing a direct theoretical link between a sample's local loss landscape and its generation likelihood. We validate our derived bounds through several empirical experiments and show that input-dependent curvature provides a proxy for memorization.

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [Why Do VLMs Fail at Remote Physiological Measurement? Rethinking with Physiological Grounding and Numerical Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19052v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19052

**作者：** Xiao Yang、Zekun Zhang、Yuxuan Fan、Duoxun Tang、Ziyang Liu、Dengbo He、Kaishun Wu、Jiyao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19052v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19052v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文实证分析 VLM 在视频远程生理测量（RPM）上失败的两个瓶颈：通用视觉语义表示难以保留细粒度生理线索、离散 token 自回归生成与连续数值测量不对齐，并提出 VITAL 双通路 token 对齐框架与结构化数值对齐。

**相关性简评：** 其「细粒度视觉线索未被表示保留」的诊断与 LVR 关注视觉细节是否留在内部状态相呼应，独立生理通路可能提供视觉旁路与信息路由的核查线索，数值对齐对生成式连续输出也有参照；但任务是 rPPG 估计而非推理主线，迁移需核实。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why Do VLMs Fail at Remote Physiological Measurement? Rethinking with Physiological Grounding and Numerical Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Video-based remote physiological measurement (RPM) has broad applications, while Vision-Language Models (VLMs) provide a promising opportunity to enable end-to-end physiological monitoring for future human-aware intelligent systems. However, existing RPM methods rely on task-specific models and cannot be seamlessly integrated with the multimodal understanding and interaction capabilities of VLMs. We revisit RPM with VLMs and provide empirical evidence for two major bottlenecks: generic visual semantic representations fail to preserve fine-grained physiological cues; while discrete token prediction in autoregressive generation is inherently misaligned with continuous numerical measurement. To address these challenges, we propose VITAL (Visual-physiological Token Alignment for Language Models), a visual-physiological token alignment framework for RPM and preserving general vision tasks. VITAL retains the original visual pathway for semantic understanding and introduces an independent physiological pathway to jointly model semantic and physiological information. Moreover, we propose a structured numerical alignment approach to improve the reliability of generative heart rate estimation by enhancing numerical relationship modeling. Extensive experiments show that VITAL improves cross-domain estimation performance on multiple rPPG datasets while preserving the general visual understanding capability of VLMs, demonstrating its effectiveness as a unified visual-physiological model. Code will be released.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [RLPE: Teaching LLMs to Prompt with Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19062v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19062

**作者：** Rahul Singhal、Karime Maamari、Pradyumna Tambwekar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19062v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19062v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RLPE：用强化学习训练一个模型读取任务模型的推理轨迹、针对观察到的失败写出提示词，奖励仅取自提示带来的下游任务表现；提示生成一次即可跨任务复用，并在多个困难基准及跨规模、跨模型家族的任务模型上验证了泛化。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本提示优化，与视觉/多模态推理无直接关系，但其「仅以任务表现为奖励、训练模型读取另一模型轨迹并生成条件干预信号」的做法，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹训练的信号设计与贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RLPE: Teaching LLMs to Prompt with Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Prompting is a widely used method for adapting general-purpose LLMs to domain-specific tasks. Effective prompts can be tedious to create manually, so prompt writing is increasingly handed to LLMs, with recent work training LLMs to write prompts for other models. These techniques either write prompts without observing how the task model behaves, blind to the failure modes the prompt should address, or must run alongside the task LLM at inference time, adding overhead to every query. Neither reflects how prompt engineering is often practiced. To close this gap, we introduce Reinforcement Learned Prompt Engineering (RLPE), which trains a model to analyze traces of a separate task model and write a prompt targeting the failures it observes. RLPE rewards the trained model strictly on prompt performance, so it learns the prompting strategies that improve the task model. The prompt is generated once and reused across the task, so inference does not require the trained model. Since RLPE needs only query access to the model it prompts, it is also compatible with closed-source models. We apply RLPE to Enigmata, BIG-Bench Extra Hard, and PlanBench. We train a 9B-parameter model that writes better prompts than state-of-the-art frontier models (gpt-5.6-sol and claude-opus-5) and prompt optimizers (GEPA and TextGrad) on 2/3 benchmarks. Although the model is never explicitly trained to iterate on prompts, this capability emerges at a level that matches or beats frontier models. The learned prompting capability also generalizes, with the trained model improving task models 6x larger than the one it trained with, including ones from entirely different model families, and matching claude-opus-5 on a fourth benchmark held out from training.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [Plan or Retrieve First? A Scale Study of Tool Discovery Architectures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19068v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19068

**作者：** Mingfei Zhang、Amit Surana、Ziauddin Majid、Chenxi Wang、Michael Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19068v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19068v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 ToolBench（约 4.7 万 API）上系统比较两种工具发现架构：主流的「先检索再规划」(RtP) 与替代性的「先规划再检索」(PtR，让 LLM 先生成抽象工具桩再作为检索查询)，发现两者在单工具与多工具查询上存在随复杂度变化的交叉，并刻画「桩幻觉」失败模式、token 开销与复杂度自适应路由。

**相关性简评：** PtR「先生成抽象假设、再据此获取证据」的流程可能与 LVR 中先形成抽象 latent 状态再检索视觉证据的思路同构；桩幻觉的双刃剑（抽象掉专名信号 vs 分解复合意图）可供核查 latent 抽象程度与细节保真间的权衡，基于 top-1 检索分数的零成本路由也可能为 STOP／动态计算提供线索，均属推测性迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Plan or Retrieve First? A Scale Study of Tool Discovery Architectures

**abstract/A1：** Tool-augmented LLM agents discover relevant APIs from large registries before planning execution, yet finite context windows prohibit injecting all tool definitions into a single prompt. The dominant approach, which we term Retrieve-then-Plan (RtP), embeds the user query and retrieves similar tools via approximate nearest-neighbor search. We study an alternative Plan-then-Retrieve (PtR) paradigm in which the LLM first generates Abstract Tool Stubs (hypothesized function signatures describing needed capabilities) which then serve as retrieval queries against the registry. Evaluating on ToolBench (46,984 unique API endpoints, 50 categories) across single-tool (G1), intra-category multi-tool (G2), and cross-category multi-tool (G3) query subsets, we find a clear complexity-dependent crossover: RtP outperforms PtR on single-tool queries while PtR outperforms RtP on multi-tool queries. This crossover holds at every retrieval budget (k=1 to 20), is robust across registry sizes from 1k to 47k APIs, and is consistent across model families (Gemini 2.5 Flash and gpt-oss-120b). We characterize a stub-hallucination failure mode (the LLM invents plausible but non-existent API names) that acts as a double-edged sword: on simple queries PtR's stubs abstract away proper-noun signals that RtP matches directly, yet on complex queries the same mechanism decomposes multi-faceted intent into functional sub-needs. We further analyze token overhead and show that PtR's extra discovery call roughly doubles inference cost, which pays off on multi-tool queries but hurts on single-tool ones, motivating complexity-adaptive routing as a practical direction, one that can be driven by a zero-cost signal already present in the pipeline (RtP's top-1 retrieval score).

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [Contextual Credit Policy Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19103v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19103

**作者：** Huiwen Han、Ziyao Zeng、Zihao Zhu、Sisong Bei、Hao Wang、Hongyu Xu、Yan Han、Degui Zhi、Zhiwen Fan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19103v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19103v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CCPO：用冻结 LLM 表示与累积轨迹统计计算上下文相似度，对跨轨迹的「观测匹配」同侪基线加权，并把历史信用与短期实际延续上的未来信用配对，实现无需价值 critic 的步级信用分配，在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上优于 GRPO。

**相关性简评：** 其无 critic 的步级信用分配和「历史+未来」双信号，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中 latent 步骤的贡献分配与监督提供线索；基于冻结表示的上下文加权也可供核查 latent 相似度中内容、位置与缓存的影响，但论文为纯文本 agent 场景，迁移到视觉推理需另行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Contextual Credit Policy Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Group-based reinforcement learning enables large language model agents to learn from sparse outcome rewards in long-horizon tasks. Recent methods derive step-level credit by comparing observation-matched experiences across trajectories. However, identical observations can occur in different contexts, and matched peers are not equally informative for a given decision. Moreover, favorable terminal outcomes can obscure redundant actions or detours within successful trajectories. To address these two challenges, we propose Contextual Credit Policy Optimization (CCPO). For the first, CCPO weights observation-matched peers from other trajectories by contextual similarity, computed from frozen LLM representations and accumulated trajectory statistics, allowing peers from similar contexts to contribute more to a step's baseline. For the second, CCPO pairs historical credit, which compares the realized return against the weighted peer baseline, with future credit, which measures the change in contextual outcome value over a short realized continuation. A step is thus credited for the progress it makes, not only for its trajectory's eventual outcome. Both credits are computed in hindsight from rollouts already collected for training, enabling CCPO to address these challenges without an auxiliary value critic. Across two Qwen2.5-Instruct backbones (1.5B and 7B), CCPO achieves up to 97.4% success on ALFWorld and 79.4% on WebShop and outperforms GRPO by up to 21.6 and 18.5 points. Ablations show that contextual weighting outperforms uniform weighting, and that historical and future credit serve as complementary learning signals.

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [Genesis Steering: Better Activation Steering Through Training Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19114v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19114

**作者：** Tora Bodin、Eric Aubinais、Ilaria Torre、Chih-Hong Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19114v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19114v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 genesis steering：从模型自身微调数据中的对比样例导出「genesis 向量」用于推理期激活引导，数据不可得时借助成员推断（MIA）筛选疑似训练样本构造代理数据，并给出关于最优决策边界位移与代理估计偏差的两条理论结果，在 Tülu-3-8B 与 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「用对比样例与分布内数据构造方向向量来干预内部激活」的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的引导、对比式监督数据选择与视觉证据筛选提供可核查线索，但论文为纯文本且针对安全引导，与多模态推理状态的关联尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Genesis Steering: Better Activation Steering Through Training Data

**abstract/A1：** Activation steering controls large language models at inference time, but its effectiveness depends on the choice of reference data. We introduce genesis steering, which derives a genesis vector from contrastive examples in the model's own fine-tuning data. When these data are unavailable, we draw on the membership inference literature to identify likely training examples and construct a proxy dataset. We establish two theoretical results on optimal displacement to the decision boundary and reduced proxy-estimation bias. We evaluate genesis steering and membership-based proxy construction on Tülu-3-8B, whose fine-tuning data are publicly available, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as a control. We find that safety data from Tülu-3-8B's training mixture yield stronger steering than external datasets on both models. On Tülu-3-8B, membership scores largely agree in identifying training examples, and vectors built from top-ranked examples achieve competitive performance against random subsets of the same size. In contrast, the results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct hint that a MIA method will only have a positive effect if the mean direction shift with MIA-filtered in-distribution data is much less severe than the direction shift with OoD data.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [Unlocking Structured Pruning for Reasoning Language Models via Attention-Preserving MLP Trimming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19121v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19121

**作者：** Woosang Lim、Haneul Yoo、Vincent-Daniel Yun、Sungjoo Yoo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19121v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19121v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LayerTrim，一种免训练的结构化剪枝方法：识别冗余解码层并只裁剪其 MLP 特征，保留注意力与层深，以维持长链条推理能力，在多个推理模型与基准上兼顾精度与推理解码效率。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留注意力、裁剪 MLP」的经验结论可能提示推理相关计算更依赖注意力路径，且其压缩后生成长度缩短的观察与长推理成本问题相关，可供核查；但论文不涉及 latent 状态生成或多模态视觉证据，与 LVR 主线仅有间接线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Unlocking Structured Pruning for Reasoning Language Models via Attention-Preserving MLP Trimming

**abstract/A1：** Structured pruning reduces LLM inference costs by removing model components to produce smaller dense models. However, existing methods can cause reasoning performance to collapse on long-form reasoning tasks such as AIME. We propose \\textit{LayerTrim}, a training-free structured pruning method that identifies redundant decoder layers and selectively trims their MLP features while preserving attention and decoder depth. LayerTrim is the first training-free structured pruning method designed to preserve long-form reasoning without gradients or recovery training. Across three reasoning language models and five benchmarks, LayerTrim outperforms existing structured pruning methods in most evaluated settings, improving average accuracy by up to 21.4 percentage points over RESP. On \\texttt{Qwen3-14B}, LayerTrim achieves 1.24$\\times$ faster prefill and 19.7\\% lower peak GPU memory than the dense model, while better preserving reasoning ability. It also achieves the shortest average generation length among evaluated pruned models, while retaining the highest average accuracy. These results demonstrate that LayerTrim improves reasoning efficiency through both structural compression and reduced token generation.

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [Plant Memory: Technical Language Processing and a Verified Maintenance Ontology for Agent-Accessible Failure Knowledge from SAP PM Work Orders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19131v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19131

**作者：** Akash Raj

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19131v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19131v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作面向 SAP PM 维修工单，用规则优先的技术语言处理抽取故障信息，并可选地让 LLM 充当「第二读者」，构建带置信度、证据出处和核验状态的 ISO 14224 对齐维护本体，通过审核队列与 MCP 服务器向人和智能体提供可引用证据的答案。

**相关性简评：** 规则抽取与 LLM 复核的分歧不覆盖事实、只提升复核优先级，以及每条事实绑定证据与核验状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中多假设/多读取器的证据筛选与监督信号聚合提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Plant Memory: Technical Language Processing and a Verified Maintenance Ontology for Agent-Accessible Failure Knowledge from SAP PM Work Orders

**abstract/A1：** Maintenance work orders are the largest written record of how industrial equipment fails, yet in most plants they are close to unusable for analysis. Short texts are abbreviated and misspelt, asset identity is buried in free text, work-type labels contradict the work described, and the fields that matter most for reliability (symptom, root cause, action taken) are usually empty. As a result, diagnostic knowledge stays with senior technicians and leaves when they do. We present Plant Memory, a system that applies technical language processing (TLP) to SAP Plant Maintenance (PM) exports and builds an ISO 14224-aligned maintenance ontology in which every extracted statement is a reified fact carrying a confidence score, the evidence it was read from, and a verification state. Extraction is rule-first, with an optional large language model (LLM) acting as a second reader whose disagreements raise a fact's review priority rather than overwrite it. Technicians verify facts through a review queue, answer targeted knowledge-gap questions where failures repeat without a recorded cause, and approve or reject ontology drift proposals when new terms appear. The resulting graph is served to people through a web application and to AI agents through a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, so both query the same verified knowledge and every answer cites the work orders behind it. On a synthetic 12-month export of 400 work orders from a parboiled-rice plant, rule-based extraction reaches 96.2% component accuracy, 88.9% failure-mode accuracy and 100% root-cause precision at 62% coverage; on 28 diagnostic questions, ranked root causes from the ontology are correct at top-1 in 82.1% of cases against 53.6% for the raw SAP root-cause column.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables. Code: https://github.com/AkashRajSingh/Plant-Memory

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [TRACER: Trace-Regularized Training Against Indirect Prompt Injection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19133v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19133

**作者：** Kshitij Mishra、Salem Lahlou、Nils Lukas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19133v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19133v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究间接提示注入防御，发现攻击成功率依赖注入所处通道，提出通道感知对抗训练 TRACER：采样多种呈现包装，并用答案锚定、证据门控的奖励与 ATRC 将受攻击推理对齐到干净证据。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据门控奖励」与把受攻击推理轨迹对齐干净证据的监督设计，可能为 LVR 的推理轨迹训练、证据筛选与贡献分配提供可核查的线索，但属纯文本安全场景，具体联系尚需确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRACER: Trace-Regularized Training Against Indirect Prompt Injection

**abstract/A1：** Indirect prompt injection uses untrusted retrieved content, tool outputs, or agent messages to override an LLM's intended behavior. We show that defense robustness is channel-conditioned: the same attack succeeds 18% of the time as a follow-up message but 53% when placed in retrieved context. We propose TRACER (Trace-Regularized Adversarial Channel Exposure with Reward-shaping), a channel-aware adversarial training method that samples presentation wrappers and combines an answer-anchored, evidence-gated reward with ATRC, which aligns attacked reasoning with clean evidence when an injection is invalid. On GSM8K with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, TRACER reduces RAG-framed ASR from 65.5% to 6.1% while preserving 78-82% clean accuracy. Joint RAG+tool training achieves 13.9% average ASR across framings at 3B scale, while a single epoch at 7B reaches 9.4%. These results demonstrate that robust defenses must explicitly train and evaluate across deployment channels.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 13 figures, 12 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026

</details>

### 52. [Graph-Based Orchestration of Heterogeneous AI Models: A Control Node Approach to Composite Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19136v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19136

**作者：** G. Michael Youngblood、Filip Dvořák、Slavo Švancár、Tomáš Balyo、Michal Ficek、Martin Dousek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19136v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19136v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出基于图的 Composite AI 框架，用专门的控制节点配合有向无环图与行为树，编排规划、机器学习、约束编程和 LLM 等异构模型的组合与执行顺序，并给出数据连接器、计算服务与容器化部署方案，支持分层、顺序、并发组合，以案例说明可行性。

**相关性简评：** 其控制节点与分层/顺序/并发编排模式可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等推理步骤与视觉证据获取的调度、交替和组合提供架构层面线索，可供核查是否涉及执行状态更新或停止判据；但该文不涉及 latent 表示与多模态推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph-Based Orchestration of Heterogeneous AI Models: A Control Node Approach to Composite Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** We present a graph-based framework for Composite AI systems that integrate multiple AI techniques to address complex problems. We describe an architecture using specialized control nodes to orchestrate diverse models including planning, machine learning, constraint programming, and large language models. The system employs directed acyclic graphs and behavior trees to manage model relationships and execution sequencing. Our methodology encompasses data connectors, computational services, and a containerized deployment framework supporting hierarchical, sequential, and concurrent model composition patterns. Through case studies, we demonstrate how this architecture enables the creation of composite systems that leverage the strengths of multiple AI techniques within a unified operational schema, overcoming the limitations of single-model approaches.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 4 figures, Citation: G. Michael Youngblood, Filip Dvořák, Slavo Švancár, Tomáš Balyo, Michal Ficek, and Martin Dousek. Graph-Based Orchestration of Heterogeneous AI Models: A Control Node Approach to Composite Intelligence. IJCAI Workshop on Composite AI (CompAI). 2025

</details>

### 53. [Compiling Expert Procedures into Verified Programs: When a Small Model Is Enough](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19148v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19148

**作者：** Vishvesh Bhat、Asad Aali、Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19148v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19148v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把教师模型（Kimi-K3）的推理轨迹编译成显式类型化程序 PLVR：聚类轨迹片段定位重复操作，由前沿模型为每个操作编写原语，再用 A\* 搜索在规则库上组装程序；推理时程序或生成学生本应自产的思维链、或对学生提出的动作做检查、修复与重试，在 tau2-Bench Retail/Telecom 与 LiveMCPBench 上用未改权重的 8B 等小模型验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「在冻结小模型之外用程序检查/修复/重试候选动作并生成推理链」的机制，与 LVR 中推理状态的外部校验、贡献定位及停止/重试决策可能存在方法联系，可供核查；材料无视觉或多模态内容，故不按主线入选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Compiling Expert Procedures into Verified Programs: When a Small Model Is Enough

**abstract/A1：** In procedural tool-use domains, how much of the gap between base models is procedure rather than capability? We build an explicit program from a teacher's traces and run it in front of unchanged small models, including an 8B model. The construction, PLVR , takes the reasoning traces of a teacher, Kimi-K3, clusters their segments to locate recurring operations, has a frontier model author one typed primitive per operation, each run by a small frozen model and assembles the program by A\* search over a mined rule library, under a reference-action objective. At inference the program takes one of two forms: for troubleshooting and tool search it produces the chain of thought the student would otherwise generate for itself, and the student acts on it; for multi-turn tool matching the student proposes each action and the program checks, repairs, or retries it before it goes out. On the official tau2-Bench Retail and Telecom test sets and on LiveMCPBench, with three Nemotron base models and the same program for each, the answer depends on the domain. On Telecom, Granite-4.2-8B as the student goes from 43.0% to 91.2% under the program, and every Nemotron student reaches 93.3-94.4%, against at most 58.2% for fine-tuning on the same traces, prompt optimisation of the student, or two token-matched baselines. With Granite-4.2-8B as the student, the program is within 2 to 3 points of the Nemotron students on Telecom and within 3 on Retail, and 3 to 13 points below them on LiveMCPBench: where the domain is procedural an 8B student is enough, and on tool search the student still matters. On Retail and LiveMCPBench the program is competitive with the best baseline. The spread between base models shrinks from about 7 points to about 2, averaged over the suites, with no weights changed.

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Verification Turns Capability Gaps into Routing Decisions: Small Language Models for Clinical Documentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19169

**作者：** Nina Fatehi、Meysam Ghaffari、Animesh Agarwal、Anushka Swarup、Carlos Morato

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19169v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在临床对话生成病历场景中固定四评估器验证栈与精化策略，仅替换生成器（4B QLoRA 模型 vs 前沿基线），并用验证结果作路由：默认小模型、被拒时升级，另提出按小节范围的精化以保留未标记内容。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本、无视觉或 latent 状态，但「验证器判定→升级/路由」与按范围精化的做法，可供核查 LVR 中验证筛选、STOP/升级决策与局部状态更新的分工问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verification Turns Capability Gaps into Routing Decisions: Small Language Models for Clinical Documentation

**abstract/A1：** Clinical note generation from doctor--patient dialogue is high-volume and hallucination-intolerant: a fabricated laboratory value can create patient-safety risk, not a quality regression. We ask whether an agentic \\emph{verification} architecture, rather than generator scale, can make a small language model viable here. Holding a four-evaluator verification stack and refinement policy fixed and swapping only the generator between a 4B-parameter model with a QLoRA adapter and a frontier baseline, the small model reaches $0.984$ claim-level factual entailment against $0.992$ and comes within $3.3$ points on completeness, generating entirely on-device. Its deficit is confined to \\emph{inter-section structural fidelity}---arrangement rather than clinical comprehension, and the axis on which it also falls furthest below the corpus's reference notes. Because that deficit is an explicit verifier decision rather than a silent quality difference, it becomes a routing policy: run the small model by default, escalate what the verifier rejects. Measured end to end, that it achieved a similiar verified-rate acceptance to the frontier-only acceptance in this test set ($99.6\\%$ vs.\\ $98.4\\%$ over $250$ encounters) while routing $62$, documenting three quarters without a frontier \\emph{generation} call. Ablating the loop separates architecture from scale: unrefined, the small model is accepted on $26.8\\%$ of encounters and the frontier model on $74.8\\%$, so verification does the most work where the generator is weakest. We also contribute section-scoped refinement, which preserves unflagged sections by construction rather than instruction, and find the baseline is not uniformly stronger---its provider refused two trauma-related transcripts the on-device model handled and certified, one facet of a provider dependency a served adapter does not carry.

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [Learning from Self-Generated Responses: Reliability--Uncertainty Self-Distillation for Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19176v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19176

**作者：** Jinhao Pan、Bowen Wei、Yuqing Zhou、Ziwei Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19176v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19176v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出可靠性—不确定性自蒸馏 RUSD：同一模型兼任教师与学生，教师输入问题与已完成的解答尝试、学生仅凭问题匹配其 token 级预测，并按自评得分与置信度得到的可靠性及学生预测不确定性加权蒸馏损失，在 11 个推理基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本自蒸馏，不直接研究 LVR；其按可靠性、不确定性为监督信号加权的做法，可能为 latent 轨迹训练与贡献分配提供可核查线索，但能否迁移到视觉证据筛选尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning from Self-Generated Responses: Reliability--Uncertainty Self-Distillation for Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Reference solutions and question-specific rubrics are not always available to guide the teacher in self-distillation. A large language model's own solution attempts offer an alternative, but their quality is difficult to assess in open-ended tasks without automatic correctness checks, and even correct attempts can involve uncertain token predictions. We propose Reliability--Uncertainty Self-Distillation (\\method{}) to learn from these attempts. The same model serves as teacher and student: the teacher receives the question and completed attempt, and the student matches the teacher's token-level predictions using a question-only prompt. \\method{} weights each attempt's distillation loss by additively combining judged reliability, estimated from a self-judge's score and confidence, with student predictive uncertainty. Across eleven reasoning benchmarks, \\method{} outperforms all five baselines in average accuracy, reaching 70.47\\% on Qwen3-4B and 76.69\\% on Qwen3-8B (8.90 and 2.10 percentage points above the base models). Data and code are available at https://github.com/JP-25/RUSD.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [Multi-System Cognitive Steering for Generative Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19177v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19177

**作者：** Eric Lacosse、Pablo Tano、Daniel C. McNamee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19177v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19177v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Cognitive Steering 框架，用环境指称、情绪状态和沟通动机等多模态上下文来约束 LLM，把高度缩略的输入扩展为完整句子以重建用户意图，并在模拟缩略语基准上报告约 88% 的 top-5 扩展准确率。

**相关性简评：** 文中发现不同上下文需不同控制方式——物理对象适合显式文本提示，情绪等抽象因素更适合直接修改内部激活——这一线索可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据与抽象信息应写入 prompt 还是编码进内部状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-System Cognitive Steering for Generative Communication

**abstract/A1：** A fundamental bottleneck for translating thoughts to speech is the high physical cost of motor input forcing a strict tradeoff between communication speed and expressivity. Generative AI offers a promising solution by expanding highly abbreviated text input into fluent sentences, significantly reducing the motor burden on the user. However, sparse inputs are inherently ambiguous; without sufficient context, AI models frequently misinterpret these abbreviations and generate outputs that fail to align with the user's true cognitive intent. We propose \\textit{Cognitive Steering}, a framework that can be leveraged to reconstruct user intent by grounding Large Language Models (LLMs) in multimodal context and applying AI control methods. We demonstrate that aligning frontier models with selected environmental referents, emotional states, and communication motives on simulated abbreviation benchmarks achieves up to $\\sim$88\\% top-5 expansion accuracy, doubling no-context baselines. We also reveal that different types of context require distinct AI control techniques: while physical objects in a scene are best provided through explicit text prompts, abstract factors like emotion may be more effectively managed by directly altering the AI's internal activations. These findings suggest a hybrid architecture for future interfaces that leverage Cognitive Steering to help users communicate both quickly and accurately, recruiting multiple AI control methods simultaneously to perfectly capture their intended communication goals.

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Into the LidarVerse: A Real-Time Unified LiDAR World Model Across Tasks and Sensors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19201v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19201

**作者：** Henry Che、Xuanchi Ren、Sanja Fidler、Shenlong Wang、Laura Leal-Taixé、Shengyu Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19201v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19201v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LidarVerse：用通用 tokenizer 将 LiDAR 距离序列映射到共享 latent 空间，教师以 LiDAR 前缀、HD 地图、空间缓存与文本为条件，并蒸馏为四步自回归因果学生，配合滚动时序 K/V 缓存实现长时程实时推演。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线不直接相关，但其持久空间缓存与滚动时序 K/V 缓存、以及「改变可用场景证据即切换生成／预测／重仿真」的条件接口，可能为 latent 状态的缓存与注意力结构、视觉证据筛选提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Into the LidarVerse: A Real-Time Unified LiDAR World Model Across Tasks and Sensors

**abstract/A1：** We present LidarVerse, a real-time unified LiDAR world model for sensor simulation across tasks and sensors. Existing LiDAR simulators largely follow one of two complementary paradigms: reconstruction-based methods preserve the geometry of a captured scene but require lengthy per-scene optimization, whereas generative methods amortize learning across data but lack the consistency needed for long-horizon rollouts. LidarVerse brings both paradigms into a single, unified conditional generative framework. A universal tokenizer maps LiDAR range sequences into a shared latent space, where a bidirectional teacher learns a common interface for conditioning on a LiDAR prefix, an HD-Map, a persistent spatial cache, and optionally, text. Varying the available scene evidence turns the same model into a generator of new worlds, a point-cloud forecaster, or a generalizable resimulator of observed scenes without per-scene optimization. We distill the teacher into a four-step autoregressive causal student and maintain both a rolling temporal K/V cache and a world-aligned spatial cache, enabling persistent long-horizon rollouts with real-time inference at 11 FPS on a single H100 GPU. LidarVerse achieves state-of-the-art performance on most reported metrics across all three evaluation protocols, while its tokenizer supports 32-, 64-, and 128-beam LiDAR and generalizes to an unseen scanning pattern. These results demonstrate that generative and reconstruction-grounded LiDAR simulation can be realized within one model, providing the core capabilities for future closed-loop sensor simulation.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 17 figures. Project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/lidarverse

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [Decoupled Drift-Diffusion World Models for Consistent Future Frame Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19212v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19212

**作者：** Anton Arapin、Aditya Singh、Yixin Wang、Jun Gao、Bernadette Bucher

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19212v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19212v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 D3WM，用 transformer 以高斯负对数似然同时回归确定性下一帧预测与空间异方差不确定性图，再以该不确定性图作为条件扩散模型的空间门控，把世界模型状态演化分解为确定性漂移与随机扩散，并在桌面操作与相机运动两类动作空间上验证。

**相关性简评：** 原文「预测不确定性图决定哪些区域锚定、哪些区域需扩散采样」的门控式状态更新，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态更新与视觉证据筛选提供线索，可供核查能否用预测不确定性决定哪些区域值得重新观察或重采样。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decoupled Drift-Diffusion World Models for Consistent Future Frame Prediction

**abstract/A1：** World models of visual information predict future image frames conditioned on given action sequences. This predicted scene evolution is driven by deterministic changes such as controlled motion on a robot platform, as well as stochastic changes such as rendering of manipulated objects or previously occluded regions. Our key insight is that the spatial location of these stochastic changes is itself learnable. We leverage this insight by jointly regressing both the deterministic next-state prediction and the spatial uncertainty map, then using the uncertainty map to guide a conditional diffusion process. We propose Decoupled Drift-Diffusion World Model (D3WM), which uses a transformer trained with a Gaussian negative-log-likelihood objective to produce both the deterministic next-state prediction and a spatial map of heteroscedastic aleatoric uncertainty. The learned uncertainty map then provides a structured spatial gate for a conditional diffusion model: deterministic regions are anchored to the predicted state, while uncertain regions, such as disocclusions or dynamic textures, are sampled by the diffusion process. We frame this decomposition under a variance-gated stochastic differential equation that separates deterministic drift from stochastic diffusion. D3WM shows improved results over competitive baselines in two distinct types of action spaces: robotic tabletop manipulation and camera motion.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 18 figures, 7 tables. Project page: https://anton-arapin.github.io/d3wm-site

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Definition Grounding in Theory-Level Auto-formalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19223v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19223

**作者：** Leyan Pan、Christian Szegedy、Frederick Rajasekaran、Robi Bhattacharjee、Gregory Constantine、Wenke Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19223v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19223v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「definition grounding」，在 LLM 自动形式化（Lean）中持续评判与修正定义，并构建 ReuseBench 基准衡量定义能否被后续命题复用：原始定义兼容率仅 25%–50%，澄清非形式定义后平均升至 77.5%，仅陈述少量未证明的基本性质即可用约 11% 成本提升模型判别兼容问题的能力。

**相关性简评：** 定义能否被后续命题复用的评判—修正流程，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态是否仍可被后续推理步骤使用的检测提供借鉴线索；其低成本「陈述性质」式判别信号能否用于发现不可用的内部表示，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Definition Grounding in Theory-Level Auto-formalization

**abstract/A1：** LLM-based auto-formalization systems can now generate formally verified Lean code at unprecedented rates. However, a formalization advances mathematical research only when further mathematical results can be built on it, especially when its definitions and helper lemmas are general enough to be imported and used idiomatically. We propose definition grounding, where definition formalizations are continuously judged and revised, as a method to ensure formalization quality. We find that agents formalizing definitions from textbook text often produce definitions that later statements cannot use, and only 25%-50% of their definition sets are compatible with all later statements. However, clarifying the informal definitions can improve this rate up to 77.5% averaged over all models. Furthermore, stating a small set of basic properties without proving them can improve model judges' capability to detect definitions with compatibility issues, at only about 11% of the definition formalization cost. These results are verified with ReuseBench, a benchmark we curated for formalizing new mathematical fields and theories.

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [LLMs program using Reusable and Composable Abstract Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19238v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19238

**作者：** Casper L. Christensen、Kyle J. Ray、Dmitry Manning-Coe、Adam Shai、Paul Riechers

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.PL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19238v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19238v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究LLM生成代码时中间层激活是否形成可复用、可组合的抽象表示，借助activation steering分别操纵函数、编程语言与实现策略，并在合成数据上训练最小模型探究这类表示的形成。

**相关性简评：** 其可独立操纵且可组合的latent表示线索，可供核查LVR中推理状态能否按视觉证据、实体关系等因子分别控制与组合，但研究面向纯文本代码，能否迁移到多模态推理状态仍需查证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLMs program using Reusable and Composable Abstract Representations

**abstract/A1：** When writing code, the same computation can be expressed in different programming languages and through different implementation strategies. We show that the activations of LLMs capture this shared structure in reusable and composable abstract representations when they generate code. These representations emerge in the middle layers, are reusable across programming languages, and afford the model an impressive amount of generalization ability when generating code under various interventions. Using activation steering, we show that function, programming language, and implementation strategy can be independently manipulated. The independent interventions on language and function can be flexibly composed to control code generation, even in cases when the prompt contains no information about language and function. We additionally find that finetuning on examples of arbitrarily defined functions in one programming language transfers to other languages, consistent with the models reusing abstract features of code generation that are shared across different examples. Finally, to study how such abstract representations come to be, we implement a minimal model of shared, reusable, and composable structure in a synthetic setting, and show that transformers trained on such data learn representations that reuse these parts flexibly across contexts.

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [Refined Topology-Pass Scheduling: Damped Trust-Region Compensation for Dynamic k-NN Graphs in SE(3)-Equivariant Structure Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19246v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19246

**作者：** Jiankai Huang、Hanwen Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19246v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19246v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对等变蛋白质结构预测中 kNN 图重建的开销问题，把图构造基的选择建模为阻尼不动点迭代，推导非翻转证书、BLUE 回放增益、ℓ∞ 置信域投影与近邻间距律，并在 C++ 中实现 L1–L3 三级补偿，报告约 1.5×/1.76× 加速且无明显损失。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结状态复用 vs 全面重建」的折中、判断缓存何时失效的非翻转证书与阻尼置信域，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的刷新时机、缓存复用及动态计算收益/成本权衡提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Refined Topology-Pass Scheduling: Damped Trust-Region Compensation for Dynamic k-NN Graphs in SE(3)-Equivariant Structure Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Equivariant structure-prediction pipelines alternate coordinate updates with the construction of a k-nearest-neighbour (kNN) graph whose membership is a discrete, piecewise-constant function of the coordinates. Rebuilding that graph at every block boundary (PB) is the expensive part of an epoch, whereas reusing one frozen graph (F) is fast but leaves a topology gap. We study a two-pass design in which the cheap pass records a small amount of state (margins, kNN radii, measured per-boundary displacements) and a second pass compensates conservatively. We formulate the choice of the graph-construction basis as a damped fixed-point iteration whose unique fixed point is exactly PB, and derive (i) exact non-flip certificates with the two-sided constant $m\_i/4$ and one-sided constant $m\_i/2$, (ii) the minimum-variance (BLUE) replay gain $γ^\*$ and the resulting feasibility crossover, (iii) an $\\ell\_\\infty$ trust-region projection that preserves convergence, and (iv) an analytic near-tie spacing law $E\[m\] \\approx r\_k/(3k)$ that matches measurements to 1\\%. Three compensation levels are implemented in C++ (L1: frozen set with live geometry; L2: margin-gated local repair; L3: damped, projected basis) and validated under a matched-conditions protocol (identical seed, MSA depth and trajectory family, only the topology mode differs). L2 reproduces the PB edge set exactly ($D = 0.0000$ at every boundary), L1 stays within 0.6% and L3 interpolates monotonically: L2 reproduces the PB edge set exactly when the trust region is removed, and L3 interpolates between the frozen and the PB topology. The recorded trajectory itself yields a measured $1.5\\times$ Pass-1 and $1.76\\times$ epoch speed-up with no measurable loss gap. An open-source implementation of the runtime and of all the experiments reported here is available at https://github.com/AlbertHuang96/ProteinPredictMachineLearning.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [Long-horizon Video Generation with Slot Memory Addressed By Region Tokens](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19257v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19257

**作者：** Eren Cetin、Julius Erbach、Vukasin Bozic、Hyojun Go、Zixiang Zhao、Nikolai Kalischek、Federico Tombari、Konrad Schindler、Dominik Narnhofer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19257v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19257v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SMART：一种由区域 token 寻址的 slot 记忆，用于长时程视频生成。写入由观察相机寻址、读取由渲染相机查询，在不建显式 3D 表示、不增长缓存的前提下保持常数大小状态，并输出读取置信度来条件化 chunk-causal 扩散 transformer；在 Memory Maze 与 Minecraft 上实现超出训练时域的回忆。

**相关性简评：** 其「常数大小 slot 状态＋按查询几何寻址读写＋用置信度决定是否采信检索内容」的机制，可能为 LVR 中 latent 工作区的状态更新、视觉证据筛选与 LOOK/STOP 类决策提供方法线索，能否迁移到语言推理状态尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Long-horizon Video Generation with Slot Memory Addressed By Region Tokens

**abstract/A1：** Memory for long-horizon video generation must satisfy two competing requirements: it must remain bounded so generation can continue indefinitely, while still recovering what was observed at a place when the camera returns, regardless of how long ago it was seen. Reconstruction-based memories provide spatial recall by caching and reprojecting explicit geometry, but require growing storage and depend on estimated 3D structure. Recurrent memories keep a constant-size state, but retention is governed by temporal recurrence, causing old observations to fade with time. We introduce SMART, a slot memory addressed by region tokens, which combines bounded storage with spatially addressed recall. SMART stores content in a constant-size slot recurrence whose writes are addressed by the observing camera and whose reads are queried by the rendering camera, without constructing an explicit 3D representation or maintaining a growing cache. Consequently, recall is conditioned on inter-view geometry rather than on recency. Each read additionally provides a confidence estimate that conditions a chunk-causal diffusion transformer, allowing it to selectively trust retrieved content while relying on its generative prior elsewhere. On long trajectories in Memory Maze and Minecraft, SMART maintains high-fidelity recall far beyond its training horizon while using constant memory and per-step cost.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 13 figures, 6 tables. Project page: https://eren-cetin.github.io/SMART/

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [Outcome-Routed Distillation and Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19271v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19271

**作者：** Wentao Lu、Tianyu Zhu、Jesse Clark

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19271v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19271v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向专家混合扩散语言模型（dLLM）提出无似然训练流程：按验证器结果对 rollout 分组，混合组用无比值的组中心 REINFORCE，若学生全失败而教师成功则在学生的去噪画布上施加稠密 KL，并用锚定 KL 限制偏离蒸馏检查点，在工具调用任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其按结果路由的蒸馏与 RL、去噪轨迹上的稠密监督及锚定约束，可能为 LVR 中轨迹级贡献分配与监督、以及 diffusion 式推理状态更新的能力保持提供可供核查的线索；但该工作限于纯文本工具调用，未涉及视觉证据路径与多模态状态表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Outcome-Routed Distillation and Reinforcement Learning for Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation provides dense supervision on the student's own samples, but its targets still come from the teacher. Verifier reinforcement learning (RL) instead uses an outcome signal, but it provides no direct constraint on capabilities outside the verifier. Combining the two is difficult for diffusion language models (dLLMs), because bidirectional denoising makes the policy likelihoods required by ratio-based RL unreliable. We develop a likelihood-free pipeline for a mixture-of-experts dLLM. Each rollout group is routed by verifier outcome. Mixed groups use ratio-free, group-centred REINFORCE. If every student rollout fails but a teacher succeeds, the model receives dense KL supervision on the student's denoising canvases. All other groups are skipped. A KL term anchored to the distilled checkpoint limits changes outside the verifier reward. The tool-calling teacher succeeds on only 18.6% of the training prompts. At 24 denoising steps, distillation exceeds the teacher by 6.77 percentage points, and routing improves the distilled checkpoint by a further 18.31 points. Routing lowers reasoning-trace accuracy by 0.71 points but raises direct-answer accuracy on the same items by 13.21 points. The anchored arm restores trace accuracy. Its tool-calling score differs by -0.16 points, with a confidence interval that includes zero, although this comparison also removes a small fallback distillation route. A domain-conditional recipe retains a 16.37-point tool-calling gain; a second seed gives 18.23 points and reproduces both preservation results, but the math result is seed-dependent. We pre-registered every arm and evaluated each comparison on paired items in a single sweep on one machine.

**comment/C1：** V1: camera ready version for the diffulm@neurips 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Whose turn is it? Context-guided model routing for stateful agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19292v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19292

**作者：** Anikesh Parashar、Avik Dutta、Vashu Chauhan、Sumit Gulwani、Priyanshu Gupta、Hosein Hasanbeig、Vu Le、Ashish Tiwari、Jack Williams

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19292v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19292v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究有状态 agent 在用户轮边界上的零样本、成本感知模型路由：系统变化路由规则、是否可见会话历史、候选模型（规模/推理强度/厂商）与 prompt cache 行为，在多轮 SheetCopilot 和 SpreadsheetBench 2 上评估；发现同等卸载率下历史感知路由提升通过率，且下切（贵→廉）的缓存成本两次调用内可收回，上切则不能回本。

**相关性简评：** 把路由刻画为依赖会话状态的序贯决策并量化模型切换的非对称缓存成本，可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配、推理模式切换与状态/KV cache 复用的收益—成本分析提供可核查的借鉴线索；该工作为纯文本表格任务，迁移性需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Whose turn is it? Context-guided model routing for stateful agents

**abstract/A1：** Model routing can reduce the cost of LLM applications by assigning simpler requests to cheaper models without sacrificing the quality of the response. However, most routers treat requests as independent. Stateful agents do not operate under this assumption: a user turn may require multiple model and tool calls, its difficulty depends on the state produced by earlier turns, and changing models can sacrifice reuse of the conversational prompt cache. We study zero-shot, cost-aware routing at the user-turn boundary, systematically varying the routing rule, access to session history, candidate models that differ in size, reasoning effort, or model provider, and prompt-cache behavior. We evaluate on multi-turn versions of SheetCopilot and SpreadsheetBench 2, where persistent workbooks and cross-turn dependencies make isolated routing decisions especially brittle. At a matched offloading rate, giving the router session history improves task pass rate over the same router without history, with gains for every model pair; history-aware routing remains within one point of always using the expensive model. We also found that cost depends on execution dynamics rather than token prices alone: the cheaper model's per-task cost advantage narrows from 6.8x to 3.2x on longer sessions. A closed form cache analysis further shows that downward switches (expensive to cheap) recover their cache cost within two LLM calls, whereas upward switches do not break even under the evaluated conditions. Together, these results show that agent routing is a sequential decision problem: effective policies must account for what happened earlier in the session and for the asymmetric cost of switching models.

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [Verifier-Grounded Agentic RL for Provably Correct Code](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19298v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19298

**作者：** George Ma、Yuhao Zhang、Bing He、Zeren Shui、Ferhat Erata、Murali Krishna Ramanathan、Somayeh Sojoudi、Hao Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19298v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19298v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为代码形式化验证构建了 agentic RL 环境与奖励：读取验证器的诊断信息而非仅用通过/失败判定，按已完成证明义务的加权比例打分并加入参考证明的隐藏探针，另设完整性审计拒绝篡改规范或预设待证结论的候选，在多规模 Qwen 模型上提升代码生成与仓库修复的验证通过率。

**相关性简评：** 其「用验证器诊断而非二元判定构造密集奖励、并以审计拦截策略钻空子」的思路，可能可供核查地迁移到 LVR 中判断 latent 内容是否真被使用、抑制视觉旁路与奖励作弊的监督设计，但原文未涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifier-Grounded Agentic RL for Provably Correct Code

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning for code usually rewards passing tests, which a model can do while remaining wrong on the inputs the tests do not cover. A program verifier instead decides whether a program satisfies a formal specification for all inputs. Language models are almost always paired with verifiers at inference time only, and turning a verifier into a reward raises two difficulties. It reports only acceptance or rejection, which gives no gradient when every attempt fails, and a policy can cheat it, most simply by telling it to assume what the proof was supposed to establish. We build an agentic environment and reward that address both. The reward reads the verifier's diagnostics rather than its verdict, scoring a candidate by the weighted fraction of proof obligations it discharges, extended with hidden probes from a reference proof's annotations. An integrity audit outranks the verifier, rejecting any candidate that alters the specification or assumes what it was meant to prove. The model works as an agent against a live verifier over many turns, with a separately trained proof specialist as a frozen collaborator. Across Qwen3 models from 1.7B to 14B and a 35B Qwen3.6 mixture-of-experts model, held-out verification on code generation rises from 66.1% to 93.5% on DafnyBench and from 56.3% to 100% on SWE-Proof. This reward leads every other training signal we compare, including a binary pass-or-fail reward trained under otherwise identical settings, with the widest margin on code generation. When the agent must also deliver the repository patch, graded by each project's withheld tests, the fraction of held-out issues it resolves rises from 59.6% to 72.5%. Evaluated unchanged on three verification suites withheld from training, the checkpoints improve on all of them, including one whose language and verifier are absent from training.

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Beyond ICD Codes: Semantic IDs for Generative Medical Coding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19301v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19301

**作者：** Yihang Cheng、Qian Zhang、Yonghui、Huang、Baoshuo Kan、Junyi Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19301v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19301v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SemICD，用 LLM 生成按层次聚类构建的语义 ID（SID）代替原始 ICD 编码来完成自动医学编码，并用编码文本档案与 SID 之间的双向生成辅助任务对齐语义，在 MIMIC-III/IV 上取得 F1 提升。

**相关性简评：** 该工作通过层次聚类嵌入构造共享前缀的离散语义 ID，并用双向生成任务为其提供语义监督，这一「结构化离散 token 构造 + 对齐监督」思路可供核查如何设计 LVR 中分层、可解释的 latent token 及其训练信号；属纯文本医疗应用，与视觉推理主线无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond ICD Codes: Semantic IDs for Generative Medical Coding

**abstract/A1：** Generative approaches to automatic International Classification of Diseases (ICD) coding use large language models (LLMs) to predict ICD codes from clinical documents. However, original ICD code strings are not ideal generation targets because the ICD hierarchy does not always reflect clinical relationships, and LLMs struggle to interpret and generate these codes. We introduce SemICD, a framework that trains LLMs to generate Semantic IDs (SIDs) instead. SemICD constructs SIDs by hierarchically clustering embeddings of textual code profiles, allowing semantically related codes to share prefixes while each complete SID uniquely identifies one ICD code. To align the SID tokens with clinical semantics, we employ two auxiliary tasks for bidirectional generation between textual code profiles and SIDs. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV with four LLMs show that SemICD outperforms direct code generation, improving Micro-F1 by 1.34-8.00 points. Our code is available at https://github.com/youwillbethebest/SemICD.

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>

### 67. [DTDD: Divergence-Triggered Dynamic Distillation for Reliable On-Policy Supervision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19302v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19302

**作者：** Qijia He、Zequn Li、Spencer Luo、Tianle Chen、Pradyumna Narayana、Zixian Ma、Ranjay Krishna、Lan Nie、Sugato Basu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19302v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19302v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DTDD，一种自适应 on-policy distillation 框架：用分段级的学生—教师分歧触发教师接管，学生前缀仍按标准 OPD 监督，教师生成的恢复后缀则以行为克隆训练；在长程推理、无 SFT 的基础初始化蒸馏和多轮 agent 三类设置上评估。

**相关性简评：** 其「分歧触发 + 教师接管」的分段判据或可供核查 LVR 中何时中断并重采样 latent 轨迹或注入视觉证据的干预/停止决策，但该工作属纯文本、训练期的监督分配机制，与推理期状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DTDD: Divergence-Triggered Dynamic Distillation for Reliable On-Policy Supervision

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision on student rollouts, but this supervision can become unreliable when the student and teacher diverge, either because of a large initial policy gap or compounding errors in long-horizon reasoning and tool use. We introduce Divergence-Triggered Dynamic Distillation (DTDD), an adaptive OPD framework that uses segment-level student-teacher divergence to trigger teacher takeover during training. When divergence triggers an intervention during reasoning, the student prefix continues to receive standard OPD supervision. The teacher then generates a recovery suffix, which the student learns through behavior cloning, using cross-entropy on the teacher-generated tokens. We evaluate DTDD in three regimes: long-horizon reasoning, distillation from a base initialization without offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and multi-turn agents. We also compare alternative trigger designs and teacher-intervention rates. DTDD reduces the post-warmup peak gradient norm by $8.6\\times$ and, from a base initialization, reaches $21.3\\%$ held-out math accuracy in $2.6\\times$ fewer optimizer updates than OPD; both methods finish near $22\\%$. It improves best-observed performance by $4.8$ and $4.1$ percentage points on ALFWorld with LoRA and agentic code, respectively. Ablations show that segment-averaged triggers outperform token-spike triggers. Behavior cloning is also more effective than the tested OPD surrogate for training teacher-generated recoveries. Across 23 experimental arms, takeover tends to help when the teacher substantially outperforms the OPD-trained student, provided that the teacher can recover from student-visited states, the student can learn those recoveries, and teacher intervention is neither excessive nor insufficient.

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Data-free Multistep Diffusion Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19305v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19305

**作者：** Ngoc Duy Tran、Kien Do、Duc Thanh Nguyen、Truyen Tran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19305v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19305v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PMDD，一种无数据多步扩散蒸馏方法：为每个学生分布分配专用的适配网络（同时避免多网络的内存开销），并引入分段式 unforget 损失保留早期步的生成结果，从而减少分布匹配偏差、加快收敛，并用单一模型支持多种推理预算。

**相关性简评：** 该工作本身属扩散图像生成，与 LVR 无直接关系；但其「按步分阶段训练 + 保留早期步生成」的监督设计和「单一模型覆盖多个推理预算」可能可供核查 LVR 中多步轨迹训练、早期状态遗忘与动态计算/长度泛化的训练方案。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-free Multistep Diffusion Distillation

**abstract/A1：** One-step diffusion distillation achieves strong performance on small datasets but remains insufficient for high-resolution or complex text-to-image settings. Extending to multistep distillation often requires training data, suffers from early-step degradation, inconsistent outputs across sampling budgets, and incurs high computational cost. We identify that these limitations stem from simultaneous training, where a single adapted network approximates multiple score functions of different student distributions at once, causing biased distribution matching and unstable optimization. Therefore, we propose Progressive Multistep Data-free Distillation (PMDD), which assigns a dedicated adapted network per student distribution while avoiding the memory overhead of maintaining multiple adapted networks. Combined with a stage-wise unforget loss to preserve earlier-step generations, PMDD reduces optimization bias in the student, provides faster convergence, and a single unified model that supports multiple inference budgets. Extensive experiments show that PMDD matches or outperforms data-free few-step baselines at every multistep budget, reaches FID 1.99 on ImageNet 64x64 in two steps, below its 512-NFE teacher (2.32), and achieves FID 7.76 on zero-shot COCO 512x512, while requiring 4-5x less training compute than data-free DMDv2 to support three inference budgets.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 14 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Did the LLM Answer the Question? A Semantic Reconstruction Game for Jailbreak Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19306v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19306

**作者：** Liam Davis、Romain Emil-Popescu、Suman Jana

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19306v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19306v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 LLM 越狱评测中攻击成功率依赖判定器、易把拒答或话题相关回复误判为成功的问题，提出无训练的「语义重构游戏」评估器：只用回复反推其所回应的请求，再与相似干扰请求比较以判断是否匹配原请求，并允许弃权，从而降低误报。

**相关性简评：** 其由回复反推请求、与干扰项比对并允弃权的判别思路可能可供核查，用于探测 VLM 答案是否真正使用了给定视觉证据而非语言先验，但属推测性迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Did the LLM Answer the Question? A Semantic Reconstruction Game for Jailbreak Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Jailbreak attacks against large language models (LLMs) are evaluated using attack success rate (ASR), which depends on correctly identifying responses that fulfill prohibited requests. Existing evaluators can mistake refusals or topically related responses for successful attacks. We show that changing only the judge LLM can alter reported ASR by up to $82$ percentage points. We introduce the semantic reconstruction game, a training-free evaluator that decomposes compliance judgments into constrained semantic comparisons. Given only a response, an LLM reconstructs the request answered by its substantive content, excluding information merely repeated or refused. The game compares this reconstruction against similar decoy requests to determine whether it matches the original request, allowing abstention when neither candidate matches. Across three off-the-shelf LLM judges, the game achieves the lowest mean error against human labels among evaluated methods and reduces variation in reported ASR. It prioritizes avoiding false positives, even at the cost of missing some successful jailbreaks.

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [MMKG-R1: Constructing Multimodal Knowledge Graphs via Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19341v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19341

**作者：** Zongyu Wu、Yilong Wang、Xiaochen Wang、Minhua Lin、Yuan Zhong、Fenglong Ma、Xiang Zhang、Suhang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19341v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19341v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MMKG-R1，用多组质量感知奖励引导强化学习，把 MLLM 训练成多模态知识构建器，以生成支撑多模态 Graph RAG 的高质量多模态知识图谱，并提升下游问答准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其「直接评价中间抽取与组装质量而非只看最终答案」的奖励设计，可能为 LVR 中轨迹监督与贡献分配提供线索；但研究目标是知识图谱质量而非推理期 latent 状态的使用，具体可迁移性需核查原文。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>
