**title/T1：** MMKG-R1: Constructing Multimodal Knowledge Graphs via Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) organize information across different modalities to support multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which aims to improve the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). The effectiveness of these multimodal Graph RAG methods largely depends on the quality of the constructed MMKGs. However, most existing methods construct MMKGs without explicit guidance on graph quality, while reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods that rely on final-answer correctness may provide only indirect feedback on MMKG quality. To address these limitations, we propose MMKG-R1, a novel MMKG-quality-driven RL framework for training an MLLM as a multimodal knowledge constructor. To be specific, MMKG-R1 introduces multiple quality-aware rewards to directly evaluate both the quality of information extracted from individual knowledge sources and the quality of the assembled MMKG. These rewards are then used to guide the RL training. Experiments across multiple datasets show that MMKG-R1 constructs MMKGs that improve downstream question-answering accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Where to Start, How to Move: Analyzing Curricula under GRPO for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19349v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19349

**作者：** Minchan Kim、Sungho Bae、Youngjin Kim、Hyunwoo Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19349v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19349v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究长时程 LLM agent 的 GRPO 训练课程：通过回放成功参考前缀（Guide Assisted GRPO），考察起始位置、交接单元与位置移动规则对无辅助任务完成率的影响，并在 BFCL 与 AppWorld 上跨三个模型规模比较。

**相关性简评：** 其「从哪里开始学习、起始位置如何移动」的课程与轨迹前缀回放设计，可能为 LVR 的 latent 轨迹训练、步骤级监督与贡献分配提供可供核查的方法线索；但论文为纯文本 agent RL，未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where to Start, How to Move: Analyzing Curricula under GRPO for Long-Horizon LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) provides no reward-relative learning signal when all rollouts receive identical rewards. In long-horizon tasks that require multiple interactions, rewards are given only upon successful task completion, making all-fail groups common early in training and limiting opportunities to practice later steps. Guide Assisted GRPO replays a successful reference prefix before the learner continues. We study how initial positions, handoff units, and movement rules affect unassisted task completion. Across three model sizes on BFCL, starting near completion is more effective under a fixed turn-level threshold rule. Finer handoffs alone do not consistently help, but outcome-informed tool-call selection outperforms GRPO on trained and untrained task categories. Guided starts also improve AppWorld success on both evaluation splits. These findings highlight where learning starts and how starting positions move as key curriculum choices.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. Minchan Kim and Sungho Bae contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [Steering Vectors for Competing Ethical Frameworks Are Interchangeable: A Competing-Target Test for Activation Steering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19359v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19359

**作者：** Weilun Xu、Zipeng Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19359v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19359v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「竞争目标检验」，在开源权重模型中对康德义务论与行为功利主义两种伦理框架的激活引导向量做等价性比较，发现两个向量方向高度重合、共享的只是「套用某种框架」这一成分，因而按目标命名的引导并不特异。

**相关性简评：** 其「竞争目标对照+等价性判据」可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态究竟携带任务必要视觉内容、还是坍缩为可被邻近行为共用的泛化方向（如信息坍缩、贡献分配）提供方法线索，但需人工核查该因果归因套路能否迁移到多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Steering Vectors for Competing Ethical Frameworks Are Interchangeable: A Competing-Target Test for Activation Steering

**abstract/A1：** Activation steering adds a vector to a model's hidden states to control a named behaviour. A gain on that behaviour, however, does not show that the vector controls it directly: it may act through a component shared with neighbouring behaviours, which unsteered and random baselines cannot separate. We introduce the competing-target test: a vector is compared with the vector built the same way for a competing target, and an equivalence test states when their difference is below a useful margin. We apply it in open-weight models to vectors for Kantian deontology, which judges an act by the rule it follows, and act utilitarianism, which judges it by the welfare it produces. On held-out situations, prompting a framework raises its score and lowers the other's, whereas vectors for either framework move both scores alike, and none reaches the margin. The two vectors share most of their direction, a request to apply some framework; the same construction separates an optimistic from a pessimistic tone, whose vectors share much less. What a vector shares with its competitors therefore sets what it controls: steering a perspective can deliver a mixture, and measuring a disposition can measure its neighbours, unless tested against the competing target.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 5 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [VLX-Go: Robust Embodied Tracking through Multi-Stage Learning and On-Policy Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19361v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19361

**作者：** Kyusong Lee、Heting Ying、Tiancheng Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19361v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19361v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 VLX-Go，一个用于具身视觉追踪的视觉-语言 waypoint 策略，聚焦在有干扰者时跟随指定人物；方法上组合冻结视觉编码器、多尺度时序上下文与 transformer 主干，训练依次经过视觉-语言对齐、多模态推理中期训练和追踪专化，并初步尝试用过滤后的 oracle 标签对策略访问状态做 on-policy refinement。

**相关性简评：** 其「先做视觉-语言对齐、再做任务专化」的多阶段配方及基于过滤 oracle 标签的 on-policy 细化，可能为 LVR 中视觉信息保留、轨迹监督与贡献分配提供可核查线索；干扰者场景的目标辨别也与视觉证据筛选相关，但论文并不研究推理期 latent 状态的生成与使用，需阅读正文确认迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VLX-Go: Robust Embodied Tracking through Multi-Stage Learning and On-Policy Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Embodied visual tracking requires a robot to identify a designated target from visual observations and control its own motion to follow that target through an environment. Following an isolated person is a comparatively simple demonstration of embodied visual tracking, but following the intended person among distractors remains challenging. Existing methods exhibit a substantial drop in success from single-target to distractor tracking, motivating a focus on the shared spaces that person-following robots must navigate. We present \\method{}, a vision--language waypoint policy built around the hypothesis that visual and language representations should be aligned before tracking-specific motion is learned. The policy combines complementary frozen visual encoders, multiscale temporal context, a transformer backbone, and a lightweight head for robot-centric waypoint prediction. Its training progresses through vision--language alignment, multimodal reasoning mid-training, and tracking specialization on 19,532 oracle episodes. Compared with 41.54\\% distractor-tracking success for our earlier OmTrackVLA model, the complete multi-stage recipe achieves 55.80\\%. We then explore on-policy refinement using filtered oracle labels for states visited by the policy. This preliminary study adds 2.42 percentage points of distractor-tracking success, reaching 58.22\\%, while reducing collision rate by 1.99 pts. The final policy records the highest distractor- and ambiguous-tracking success rates among the compared methods. These results support staged multimodal learning as a promising direction for tracking among distractors and motivate further study of on-policy refinement for person following in shared spaces.

**comment/C1：** tech report

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [Dynamic Graph World Modelling: Learning Future Dynamics from Temporal Context](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19364v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19364

**作者：** Bo Li、Yue Yang、Yujin Huang、Siao Liu、David Lemphers、Sara E Rodriguez Gomez、Luke Borgnolo、Can Wang、Xin Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19364v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19364v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DyG-WM 框架，从历史时序上下文出发联合预测未来的节点与关系表示，分别用历史节点-关系上下文编码器、路径签名编码器和双层级联合嵌入目标刻画「已演变什么、如何演变、接下来演变什么」，在 12 个链接预测与 2 个节点分类数据集上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「不重建原始图、直接在隐空间预测未来状态」的联合嵌入目标，以及路径签名对有序结构变化的编码，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态预测任务、信息坍缩规避和深度/位置顺序编码提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamic Graph World Modelling: Learning Future Dynamics from Temporal Context

**abstract/A1：** Dynamic graph learning models how entities and their relations evolve from historical observations to predict future graph states. Existing methods do not fully address dynamic graph world modelling, which requires learning transitions from temporal context to future latent graph states. This is difficult for three reasons: nodes and relations rely on different historical evidence, structural evolution is path-dependent, and future snapshots provide sparse and realization-specific targets. We propose Dynamic Graph World Modelling (DyG-WM), the first framework to jointly predict future node and relation representations from temporal context by answering three questions: what has evolved, how it evolved, and what will evolve next. To capture what has evolved, a historical node-relation context encoder summarizes node-neighborhood trajectories and pair-specific histories. To characterize how it evolved, a node-relation path-signature encoder captures ordered structural changes. To predict what will evolve next, a dual-level joint-embedding objective predicts target-snapshot node and relation representations without reconstructing the raw graph. Experiments on 12 link-prediction and two node-classification datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DyG-WM across both tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [DANGERLENS: Probing Dangerous Representations Hidden by Safety Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19365v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19365

**作者：** Tianzhuo Yang、Shaoheng Yan、Lin Lu、Jiawei Chen、Kaile Wang、Fanyu Meng、Minglu Liu、Yaodong Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19365v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19365v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用成对训练历史与中间表示审计，提出 DANGERLENS 探针把隐状态映射到词表空间并结合盲评，区分有害目标与安全控制信息，发现行为同样安全的模型在内部有害概念可及性上仍存在差异，且差异集中在中间与较后层。

**相关性简评：** 其「隐状态探针＋readout 消融」评估中间层可及性的做法，或可迁移用于核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真正保留任务必要的视觉证据；但研究属安全对齐领域，与多模态推理无直接关联，借鉴价值待人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DANGERLENS: Probing Dangerous Representations Hidden by Safety Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Safety alignment teaches language models to refuse harmful requests, yet the internal accessibility of hazardous concepts can retain traces of earlier training. Can checkpoints with equally safe behavior differ in what remains accessible behind their refusals? In this work, we investigate this question through paired training histories and a direct audit of intermediate representations. We introduce DANGERLENS, a probe that maps hidden states into vocabulary space and uses blinded semantic judgments to distinguish harmful goals and operational means from hazard recognition and safety control. Experiments on three Qwen1.5 scales show that checkpoints with prior harmful exposure generally retain greater hazardous concept accessibility after the same safety alignment, with the strongest separation in intermediate and later layers. The same harmful update also produces different behavioral trajectories from closely matched starting points. Human relevance validation and readout ablations reinforce the measured accessibility contrast. Our findings show that training history remains visible inside behaviorally matched models and motivate internal accessibility audits alongside output safety evaluations.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [DeLoop: Repeated Actions as Self-Supervision for Long-Horizon Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19376v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19376

**作者：** Tianle Chen、Pushkar Singh、Ketan Todi、Biswajit Paria、Qijia He、Wenqi Wang、Yuwen Tan、Jike Zhong、Sugato Basu、Deepti Ghadiyaram、Arjun Akula

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19376v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19376v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把长程 LLM agent 反复发出相同工具调用、得到相同观察的现象定义为「loop」，并提出 DeLoop：取自身 rollout 中循环动作为负例、其后第一个非循环动作为正例，用 DPO 自监督训练，不需要任务奖励、标准答案或 LLM 评判；在 CodeGym 压缩历史设定下显著降低含 loop 任务比例与步数，大模型上任务成功率也有提升。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本 agent 工作、未涉及视觉或多模态 latent 状态，但其对「重复且不带来新信息的步骤」的刻画与免奖励偏好对构造方式，可能可供核查 LVR 中冗余推理步的识别、停止决策与轨迹级监督；历史压缩触发 loop 这一点也与状态压缩下信息保留问题相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeLoop: Repeated Actions as Self-Supervision for Long-Horizon Agents

**abstract/A1：** Language-model agents that run long tasks often redo work they have already done: they re-issue a tool call they made earlier and get back exactly the same observation, a step that adds no new information. We identify this failure as a loop. Loops appear natively in Qwen, Llama, and Gemini agents and become far more common once the agent's history is compressed to fit its context window. To reduce these loops, we propose DeLoop, a self-supervised training method. DeLoop builds on a simple observation: the agent's own rollout can provide the supervision needed to learn from its loops. We use a looping action as a negative example and the first later non-looping action in the same rollout as a positive example. DeLoop learns from these pairs through direct preference optimization and does not require task rewards, gold answers, or LLM judges. On CodeGym under compression, DeLoop reduces the share of Qwen3-8B tasks containing loops from 57.3% to 6.1%. It also uses about one third as many steps per task, with similar task success. On Qwen3-32B, DeLoop reduces loops and steps while raising task success from 37.7% to 44.8%.

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [EvoWeave: Composing Reusable Agent Experience through Operation Level Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19381v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19381

**作者：** Guanhua Zhu、Bo Shen、Lei Zhang、Fadi Deek、Zhi Wei、Mengnan Du、Chenxi Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19381v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19381v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EvoWeave 经验记忆框架：将抽象状态表示为节点、可复用操作表示为带输入输出契约与来源信息的有向边，由规划器把当前任务分解为依赖图，再经操作检索与有界有向搜索组装经验片段，并在 AppWorld 等四个基准、三个语言模型骨干上评估记忆带来的任务完成率提升与检索开销代价。

**相关性简评：** 其「抽象状态节点 + 操作边 + 反射区分具体执行证据与可复用抽象」的图式记忆，可能为 LVR 的推理状态抽象、轨迹监督/贡献分配与证据筛选提供可核查的方法线索，尚需确认能否迁移到 latent 推理片段的组合与复用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoWeave: Composing Reusable Agent Experience through Operation Level Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Different tasks often require interactive agents to perform similar operations at intermediate steps. Retrieving an entire past workflow can introduce irrelevant steps, whereas retrieving isolated operations can discard the dependencies needed to reuse them. We introduce EvoWeave, a framework for experience memory that represents abstract states as nodes and reusable operations as directed edges with input and output contracts, provenance, and capability references. A planner decomposes the current task into a dependency graph; operation retrieval and bounded directed search then assemble relevant experience fragments. An execution agent can inspect these fragments and their referenced capabilities, while reflection separates concrete execution evidence from reusable abstractions. We study the framework across four benchmarks, five evaluation settings, and three language model backbones. Across backbones, AppWorld Normal and Challenge task goal completion average 51.91 and 27.99, respectively, exceeding the baseline without memory by 6.99 and 4.99 percentage points. An AppWorld ablation associates directed connections with an additional improvement of 2.08 points over independent operation cards. The results motivate composition of reusable operations while exposing tradeoffs in retrieval overhead and conditions for valid reuse.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [RA$^2$: Retain-Anchored Attraction Preserves Forget-Adjacent Utility in LLM Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19385v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19385

**作者：** Zinan Ling、Yang Xiao、Ruimeng Ye、Sijia Liu、Bo Hui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19385v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19385v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RA$^2$，一种 latent 空间遗忘方法：把 forget token 的隐状态重定向到邻近的 retain 支持表示，同时保持 retain 数据在高层的原有行为，以缓解「遗忘邻域效用损失」——即靠近遗忘领域的良性推理退化。

**相关性简评：** 其核心操作是在隐状态层面按「锚点」定向迁移表示，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新方向与约束方式提供借鉴线索，且「邻域效用损失」这一诊断思路可供核查 latent 内容是否保留任务必要信息；但任务为文本 LLM 遗忘，与视觉多模态推理距离较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RA$^2$: Retain-Anchored Attraction Preserves Forget-Adjacent Utility in LLM Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning is often evaluated primarily by target suppression and clean retain utility. These metrics can miss a local failure mode: a model may perform well on standard retain prompts, yet degrade on benign reasoning when prompts lie near the forgotten domain. This phenomenon is termed forget-adjacent utility loss. A fundamental question is: where should forget representations go after they leave the removed behavior? Successful unlearning depends not only on whether forget-conditioned representations depart from the harmful computation, but also on the direction in which they are redirected afterward. We propose RA$^2$ (Retain-Anchored Attraction), a latent-space unlearning method that redirects forget-token states toward nearby retain-supported representations while preserving higher-layer behavior on retain data. On WMDP and MUSE, RA$^2$ yields higher forget-adjacent utility than strong unlearning baselines at similar broad-utility levels. These results show that the migration direction of forget representations materially affects benign behavior near the forget boundary. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZacLing/retain\_anchored\_attraction.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [STRATA: Self-Maintaining Structured Experience Graphs for Self-Evolving Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19386v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19386

**作者：** Guanhua Zhu、Hanwen Li、Zhi Wei、Mengnan Du、Chenxi Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19386v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19386v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** STRATA 为自进化 LLM agent 提出三层类型化经验图记忆，用多维相似度检索、类型化反射器、社区检测、压力触发的垃圾回收与对比边五种机制自维护，并在四个交互基准、两个冻结骨干与七个基线对比中取得最佳平均分与最低延迟。

**相关性简评：** 其「按操作与目标」的多维相似度几何及标记方向相反经验的对比边，可能为 LVR 中潜在状态的相似检索、误导性轨迹复用与负迁移抑制提供可核查的方法线索，但原文面向纯文本 agent 记忆、无视觉或 latent 推理验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** STRATA: Self-Maintaining Structured Experience Graphs for Self-Evolving Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve at test time by accumulating experience: retrieving relevant memories before acting and writing new lessons back afterwards. Yet most are single-level: trajectories, distilled workflows and reasoning items all land in one undifferentiated pool indexed by embedding similarity, with no typed abstraction hierarchy. They never forget or deduplicate, and are prone to negative transfer, where a superficially similar but directionally opposite experience is recalled and its concrete steps are copied verbatim, actively harming the agent. We introduce STRATA, a structured, outcome-grounded, self-maintaining memory that organizes experience into a three-layer graph of atomic skills and code snippets, task workflows and generalized meta-frameworks, connected by typed edges. STRATA maintains itself through five mechanisms sharing one similarity geometry: a multi-dimensional similarity that scores retrieval by operation and target; a typed reflector crystallizing procedures from successes and pitfalls from failures; community detection that induces meta-frameworks; pressure-triggered garbage collection with articulation-point protection; and contrast edges that flag confusable, direction-opposite experiences. Across four interactive benchmarks (AppWorld, WebArena, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld), two frozen backbones and seven baselines, STRATA attains the best average score while simultaneously using the lowest latency and fewest environment steps of any memory-augmented method, and 44% fewer lines of injected memory than the most context-hungry baseline. We isolate two failure modes of prior memories: single-level memory degrades sharply as the task set grows, and single-level concrete memories suffer catastrophic negative transfer, which contrast edges repair, lifting ScienceWorld from 78.7 to 96.7 within the same system.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [SERO: Learning from Executable Oracle Continuations for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19397v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19397

**作者：** Dingyi Rong、Yue Shi、Chaofan Ma、Zongrui Wang、Jiezhang Cao、Yixin Cao、Liang He、Ning Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19397v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19397v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SERO，在长程 agentic RL 中从当前策略实际到达的状态出发，用仅训练期可用的 oracle 与环境继续交互，把后续观测与 oracle 动作作为监督，并按冻结行为策略对前置 oracle 动作的兼容性做可达性加权，在 ALFWorld、WebShop 上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「用 oracle 延续扩展策略未访问轨迹」与按行为策略兼容性加权监督的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 分支或轨迹监督的权重设计与离策略状态复用；但工作为纯文本智能体 RL，与视觉 latent 推理联系需进一步判断，暂列为方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SERO: Learning from Executable Oracle Continuations for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon agentic RL is constrained by the futures visited by the current policy. When rollouts fail to reach useful behaviors, improved credit assignment cannot provide supervision for states and actions that were never encountered. On-policy distillation (OPD) introduces teacher guidance on policy-induced states, and recent multi-turn variants improve how this guidance is scheduled, replayed, refined, or validated. However, they do not directly use the oracle-controlled future itself as the training trajectory. We introduce \\textbf{State-Rooted Executable Rollout Optimization (SERO)}, which augments on-policy agentic RL with executable oracle continuations. SERO restores a state actually reached by the current policy and lets a training-only oracle continue interacting with the real environment, making the downstream observations and oracle actions themselves available for learning. It then applies \\textbf{policy-reachability weighting}, which weights each oracle step by the frozen behavior policy's compatibility with the preceding oracle actions. This allows deeper supervision to become effective as the policy becomes more compatible with the oracle branch. The oracle loss is optimized separately from the base RL estimator and no oracle is used at inference time. We evaluate SERO on two challenging interactive-agent benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop, using Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. On ALFWorld, learning the full executable continuation is substantially more effective than supervising only the root oracle action, while policy-reachability weighting further improves out-of-distribution performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [Train to Stitch: Topology-Preserving Regularization for Interoperable Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19401v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19401

**作者：** Akshay Malhotra、Nicolas Zilberstein、Shahab Hamidi-Rad

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19401v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19401v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用拓扑正则化对齐两个独立训练的自编码器隐空间，使编码器与解码器无需训练期协调即可互相配合，并给出条件性理论分析与重建实验。

**相关性简评：** 其以拓扑/结构一致性约束对齐独立学习隐空间的思路，可能为 LVR 中跨模块或跨模型的 latent 推理状态对齐、表示几何兼容性与拼接复用提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Train to Stitch: Topology-Preserving Regularization for Interoperable Representations

**abstract/A1：** As large, powerful pre-trained models become more prevalent, there is growing interest in developing methods that allow different neural networks to be seamlessly combined with little or no direct communication between them. Take autoencoder-based data compression as an example: the ability to independently choose architectures and train encoder and decoder models, while still ensuring they work together, could fundamentally transform how such models are built, deployed, and updated. In this work, we introduce a novel approach that leverages topological regularization to align the latent spaces of two independently trained autoencoder models, entirely eliminating the need for coordination during training. We provide a conditional theoretical analysis showing how the structural agreement encouraged by our regularizer can translate into approximate geometric compatibility between independently learned representations. Reconstruction experiments validate this approach empirically, substantially narrowing the interoperability gap across multiple datasets.

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [W-JEPA: Wavelet Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Wearable Activity Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19411v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19411

**作者：** Yogesh Prabhu、Loki Natarajan、Jingjing Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19411v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19411v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** W-JEPA 是一个面向可穿戴活动识别的自监督框架：将惯性信号窗组织成按尺度、传感器轴和时间索引的小波 token 网格，并以 EMA 目标编码器提供的表示作为掩码 latent 预测目标，在六个基准上提升了跨数据集与跨被试泛化。

**相关性简评：** 其「掩码 token 表示预测」目标与多尺度 token 化可作为 LVR 中 latent 状态生成和尺度相关细节保留的方法线索，值得核查能否迁移；但该工作限于惯性时序信号、不涉及视觉语言推理，与主线的联系需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** W-JEPA: Wavelet Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Wearable Activity Recognition

**abstract/A1：** A major challenge in wearable device activity recognition is the heterogeneity across datasets in sensor placement, channel count, sampling rate and subjects. Moreover, the motion patterns that distinguish activities often appear as subtle, scale-specific variations within otherwise similar waveforms. As a result, raw signals are often ambiguous, and models trained on them tend to rely on cues specific to a particular sensor setup or participant, which limits both cross-dataset transfer and generalization to unseen participants. Addressing this requires representations that preserve such activity-relevant, scale-specific variation. We introduce W-JEPA, a self-supervised framework that exploits the multiscale structure of inertial motion and leverages a joint-embedding predictive architecture, by organizing windows into a fixed grid of wavelet tokens indexed by scale, sensor axis, and time. These wavelet tokens help expose activity-specific detail within broadly similar raw signals, and serve as the units of masked latent prediction. W-JEPA predicts masked token representations supplied by an exponential-moving-average (EMA) target encoder. Because recovering a masked token requires relating motion at one scale and axis to motion at other scales and axes, the model learns the cross-scale, cross-axis structure of human movement. Across six wearable activity benchmarks, W-JEPA outperforms state-of-the-art methods under random-window splits and remains the strongest when subjects are held out, improving average macro-F1 by 8.7 percentage points over the strongest published baseline. These results indicate that physically grounded multiscale tokenization, combined together with latent predictive learning, provides a promising foundation for transferable representations of heterogeneous wearable inertial data.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Evaluating Agentic Capabilities Before Post-Training: An End-to-End Approach](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19425v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19425

**作者：** Jiarui Qin、Junjie Huang、Yunjia Xi、Di Yin、Yong Yu、Weinan Zhang、Xing Sun、Weiwen Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19425v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19425v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ASAP 框架：不把预训练基座模型改造成对话式 agent，而是保留补全接口，通过结构化轨迹延续、短参考前缀与最小恢复机制，在软件工程和深度研究基准上端到端引出并评测各预训练检查点的 agent 行为。

**相关性简评：** 其「不改接口、用轨迹延续与短前缀引出行为」的做法，可能可供核查如何在不改变生成形式的前提下引出并观察模型的探索、恢复与终止行为，与 LVR 推理状态的使用和评价有间接方法联系；但论文面向纯文本 agent 评测，与视觉 latent 推理无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evaluating Agentic Capabilities Before Post-Training: An End-to-End Approach

**abstract/A1：** Agentic capabilities are increasingly important for modern LLMs, yet remain difficult to evaluate during pretraining. Existing approaches typically rely on static trajectory-derived tasks or likelihood-based proxies, which are lightweight but provide limited visibility into how base models behave in interactive environments. We introduce ASAP (Agent Scaffold Adaptation for Pretraining Evaluation), a lightweight framework for eliciting and evaluating agentic behavior from pretrained base models on end-to-end tasks. Rather than converting base models into chat-style agents, ASAP preserves the completion interface and adapts existing agent scaffolds through structured trajectory continuation, short reference prefixes, and minimal recovery mechanisms. Across software-engineering and deep-research benchmarks, ASAP yields discriminative end-to-end performance across base models and pretraining checkpoints. Beyond aggregate scores, ASAP exposes model-generated execution trajectories, enabling analysis of exploration, editing, testing, recovery, and task completion across training stages. These trajectories provide a behavioral view of how agent-relevant capabilities change during pretraining and how execution patterns differ after post-training. Our results suggest that end-to-end agent evaluation can complement conventional pretraining benchmarks by providing a practical diagnostic of interactive model behavior.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [Learning to Select Features with Manifold Deep Unfolding PCA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19436v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19436

**作者：** Jingjing Liu、Pei Tai、Xianchao Xiu、Wanquan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19436v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19436v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MDUFS-Net：在 PCA 框架中引入 ℓ2,0 范数，将基于 ADMM 的迭代算法展开为端到端可训练网络以自动学习参数，并加入流形优化提升数值稳定性；进一步以 ℓ0 范数扩展出 MDUFS-Net+，在无监督特征选择与聚类任务上取得优于现有方法的表现。

**相关性简评：** 把迭代优化展开成可训练网络、用稀疏范数做特征筛选，可能为 LVR 中循环式 latent 状态的逐步更新与视觉证据/特征筛选提供方法线索，具体对应关系可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Select Features with Manifold Deep Unfolding PCA

**abstract/A1：** Unsupervised feature selection (UFS) is of great significance in image processing and pattern recognition. Nevertheless, existing model-based methods often rely on manually tuned hyperparameters, while deep learning-based methods generally require massive training data. To address these issues, this paper proposes an efficient manifold deep unfolding feature selection network (MDUFS-Net). Technically, we first incorporate the $\\ell\_{2,0}$-norm into the classic principal component analysis (PCA) framework, then unfold the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-based iterative algorithm into an end-to-end trainable network, thereby enabling automatic parameter learning. Moreover, a manifold optimization strategy is developed to improve the numerical robustness. On this basis, we further extend MDUFS-Net into an enhanced variant called MDUFS-Net+ by introducing the $\\ell\_{0}$-norm for more discriminative feature representations. Extensive experiments validate that our proposed MDUFS-Net and MDUFS-Net+ outperform state-of-the-art methods in the UFS task, with promising performance in clustering. Our code is available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/MDUFS.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Progress-Aware Distillation for Mitigating Stagnation in Small Language Model Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19439v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19439

**作者：** Yuanpu Cao、Saket Sathe、Hanyu Wang、Ziyi Yin、Fenglong Ma、Jinghui Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19439v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19439v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统分析了小语言模型智能体在长交互轨迹蒸馏中出现的「进展停滞」现象（反复失败、陷入无效循环），并提出 ProD：检测学生轨迹中的停滞，将其与教师的差距编码为自适应 margin 进行惩罚，迭代训练以逼近教师行为，在 8 个 0.6B–8B 模型上验证成功率与效率提升。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 agent 蒸馏，无视觉与 latent 表示，但「停滞检测 + 自适应 margin 惩罚」这一轨迹级监督线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中循环式 latent 状态的重复/退化诊断、轨迹训练与停止决策的贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Progress-Aware Distillation for Mitigating Stagnation in Small Language Model Agents

**abstract/A1：** While LLM-based agent systems have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in reasoning and solving complex real-world tasks, they also come with substantial token consumption and inference latency, limiting their practical deployment. To enhance the efficiency of agent systems, recent efforts have focused on transferring agentic capabilities from large-scale models to small language models (SLMs) via supervised fine-tuning-based distillation. Nevertheless, as interaction trajectories grow longer, involving extended reasoning-action chains and extensive environmental feedback, distilled student agents tend to experience progress stagnation: becoming trapped in unproductive loops characterized by persistent failed strategies and difficulty making meaningful progress. Our systematic analysis shows that this phenomenon consistently occurs across diverse SLMs and task domains. To address this issue, we propose Progress-Aware Distillation (ProD), which explicitly detects and penalizes stagnation in student-generated trajectories. ProD encodes the stagnation gap between student and teacher into an adaptive margin for dynamically penalizing possible stagnation. By iteratively applying this procedure, ProD enables student agents to progressively approximate teacher behavior. Extensive experiments involving 8 SLMs, ranging from 0.6B to 8B parameters, on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, demonstrate that ProD substantially mitigates progress stagnation while enhancing both the success rates and task-completion efficiency of distilled SLM agents.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [MIPS: Mitigating Mode Collapse in Reinforcement Learning for Large Outcome Spaces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19457v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19457

**作者：** Armaan Sandhu、Abhijeet Sinha、Dianbo Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19457v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19457v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对大规模结果空间中 GRPO 类组内策略梯度的模式坍缩，提出 Marginal IPS：用采样轨迹与学习到的「通向某结果的轨迹模型」联合对结果概率做边缘估计，替代组内频率估计，在 Hypergrids、分子合成与系统发育推断上维持多样且高质量的采样。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及视觉或 latent 推理，但其「对通向同一结果的多条轨迹做边缘化以估计贡献」的思路，可能可供核查 VLM 推理 RL 训练中推理轨迹多样性退化与 latent 路径贡献分配的问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MIPS: Mitigating Mode Collapse in Reinforcement Learning for Large Outcome Spaces

**abstract/A1：** Expected-reward maximization in reinforcement learning concentrates probability mass on a few outcomes, even in tasks that require many distinct, high-quality solutions, a failure known as mode collapse. Inverse Probability Scaling (IPS) counters this in group-based policy gradients such as GRPO by dividing each reward by the outcome's probability, estimated from its frequency within the sampled group. When the outcome space is large relative to the group, outcomes rarely repeat and these estimates become uninformative. Since an outcome can be constructed along many trajectories, its probability is a sum over all of them, and computing it directly is intractable. We propose Marginal IPS (MIPS), which performs marginal evaluation by combining the sampled trajectory with a learned model of the trajectories that lead to each outcome. On Hypergrids, where the exact sampling distribution is computable, MIPS stays near the reward-distribution target as the outcome space grows relative to the rollout group, while frequency-based IPS degrades. In molecular synthesis and phylogenetic inference, MIPS sustains diverse, high-quality sampling where GRPO and frequency-based IPS collapse, showing that MIPS serves as a reliable solution to mode collapse in RL.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [DeBoneDiT: Depth-Driven Conditional Bridge Diffusion Transformers for Bone Suppression in Chest X-Ray Images](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19466v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19466

**作者：** Yifei Sun、Fenglei Fan、Junhao Jia、Wenming Deng、Hongxia Xu、Changmiao Wang、Ruiquan Ge

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19466v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19466v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 DeBoneDiT，用深度驱动的条件桥扩散 Transformer 做胸片骨抑制：以 Depth Anything V2 特征的多层级深度损失训练结构感知分词器（DAE）做潜空间压缩，并把任务建模为源条件布朗桥扩散，另发布 741 对配对数据集。

**相关性简评：** 论文把「重构图生偏置的潜压缩」与结构信息丢失列为瓶颈，并用深度损失约束分词器保留解剖结构，这可能为 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留在内部状态、潜表示坍缩等问题提供可核查的表示侧线索；但任务属图像翻译而非推理，联系需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DeBoneDiT: Depth-Driven Conditional Bridge Diffusion Transformers for Bone Suppression in Chest X-Ray Images

**abstract/A1：** Chest X-Ray (CXR) is a primary modality for diagnosing pulmonary diseases, yet the overlap of bony structures often obscures critical radiographic findings, increasing the risk of diagnostic uncertainty. While Dual-Energy Subtraction (DES) is the clinical benchmark for bone suppression, its widespread adoption is primarily hindered by specialized hardware requirements and increased radiation exposure. Recently, diffusion-based generative models have shown promise for this task, yet they are still constrained by reconstruction-biased latent compression, prior-induced domain gap, and a scarcity of high-quality paired datasets. To address these challenges, we propose DeBoneDiT, a depth-driven conditional bridge diffusion Transformer architecture for efficient high-resolution bone suppression. We first introduce Depth Auto-Encoder (DAE), a structure-aware vision tokenizer for latent compression. By incorporating a multi-level depth feature loss derived from the pretrained Depth Anything V2 encoder, DAE preserves both visual fidelity and structural consistency. Second, we formulate bone suppression as a source-conditioned Brownian bridge diffusion process, leveraging CXRs as both the diffusion prior and structural guidance to narrow the source-to-target domain gap and maintain anatomical plausibility. Furthermore, we have constructed and released SZCH-X-Rays, the largest publicly available high-quality paired dataset for bone suppression to date, comprising 741 pairs of CXR and DES soft tissue images. Extensive experiments and downstream evaluation demonstrate that DeBoneDiT achieves superior bone suppression performance with reduced computational overhead.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 13 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [MemCode: Discrete Semantic Representations for Long-Term Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19472v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19472

**作者：** Xin Li、Liang Hu、Duoqian Miao、Ming Peng、Qi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19472v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19472v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MemCode 面向 LLM agent 的跨会话长期记忆，将对话改写为自包含原子记忆单元，用可学习码本做离散语义量化，并以码本共现拓扑作为向量检索之外的补充检索路径，同时分离在线写入与离线巩固。

**相关性简评：** 该工作不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但可学习离散码本与共现拓扑检索可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态离散化、记忆式证据获取与状态巩固提供方法线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MemCode: Discrete Semantic Representations for Long-Term Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Long-term memory remains a fundamental bottleneck for large language model (LLM) agents across sessions. Existing systems typically adopt a write-then-retrieve paradigm, storing context-dependent dialogue fragments and retrieving them via nearest-neighbor search in a continuous embedding space. This design yields unstable access units and systematically misses logically related but lexically distant evidence. We propose MemCode, a long-term agent memory framework that couples retrieval-oriented writing with discrete associative retrieval. MemCode rewrites dialogue into self-contained atomic memory units (AMUs), maps AMUs into a learnable codebook with isomorphic semantic quantization (ISQ), and uses codebook co-occurrence topology as a retrieval path complementary to vector search. For long-term memory stores, MemCode separates online atomic writes from asynchronous offline consolidation. On LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo with GPT-4o-mini and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507 backbones, MemCode achieves the best overall accuracy while keeping per-dialogue memory construction within roughly 40~130 seconds, making it 3~21 times faster than LightMem and over an order of magnitude faster than A-MEM and MemoryOS.

**comment/C1：** 27 Pages, Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [Beyond Retrieval: Evidence Access and Event Aggregation in Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19474v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19474

**作者：** Zerui Yang、Quan Xue、Chaorui Zhang、Xueyan Niu、Xuanyu Zheng、Xiyuan Wang、Haolin Ruan、Guangcheng Song、Weixi Zhang、Wei Han、Bo Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19474v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19474v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对需聚合事件的智能体历史问答，论文把「保留原始轨迹、按问题构建来源可寻址阅读视图」的 Trajectory Memory 与「模型生成事件规格 + 确定性执行的事件账本」TallyMem 结合，在 MINTEval 与 AMA-Bench 上报告增益。

**相关性简评：** 其中「保留源证据并按需访问 + 可执行聚合」的线索或可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与跨事件信息聚合的对应问题，收益依任务与事件规格而变的诊断也可能类比 latent 推理的贡献分配，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Retrieval: Evidence Access and Event Aggregation in Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Questions about an agent's history often require aggregating events, which retrieval alone does not resolve. We combine source-preserving evidence access with executable aggregation. Trajectory Memory (TM) retains the original trajectory and constructs a source-addressable reading view at question time. TallyMem augments it with a reusable event ledger, model-generated event specifications and deterministic execution. With a commit-specific adaptation on 776 questions from 99 held-out MINTEval repositories, TallyMem raises Qwen3-32B exact match from 35.2% to 49.0%, a 13.8-point gain over TM; gains span 12.8-13.9 points across three readers. A controlled probe with event sets parsed independently from raw logs shows an 11.0-point gain, from 82.8% to 93.8%, without routing. On full AMA-Bench, the routed system yields a smaller 1.3-point gain, from 69.8% to 71.1%. These results support executable aggregation on tasks that require combining events, with benefits dependent on the task and event specification, the largest residual system-error category in our diagnostic analysis.

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [VLRLA: Deployment-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models through In-Context Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19517v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19517

**作者：** Diyuan Shi、Yiqi Tang、Hongyin Zhang、You Wu、Donglin Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19517v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19517v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VLRLA 框架，在部署阶段用历史观察、动作、奖励与终止信号作为上下文，配合强化学习模块让现有 VLA 策略在不更新参数的前提下逐步改进动作生成，并在仿真与真实部署中验证其适应性。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结参数、以历史交互序列构成可更新上下文来改进决策」的机制，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态的累积更新与利用、以及终止信号驱动的停止/决策设计存在可供核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VLRLA: Deployment-Time Adaptation of Vision-Language-Action Models through In-Context Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language-action (VLA) models connect visual perception and language understanding with robot control, providing a foundation for instruction-conditioned manipulation. However, conventional pretrained VLA policies lack a mechanism for gradual adaptation through accumulated interaction experience during deployment. We therefore propose VLRLA, a general framework that equips existing VLA models with \\textit{in-context reinforcement learning} capabilities for deployment-time adaptation. Our approach augments a VLA model with a reinforcement learning module that learns from historical observations, actions, rewards, and termination signals. During deployment, the policy gradually refines its action generation and progressively improves task success while keeping model parameters fixed. Our approach is also designed for general applicability and compatibility with diverse learning history construction strategies and model training pipelines. Finally, through extensive experiments, we systematically investigate the learning properties of VLRLA in simulation and demonstrate its ability to adapt to unseen scenarios during real-world deployment.

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Diagnosing and Correcting Reward Hacking in Verifiable Instruction Following](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19525v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19525

**作者：** Sangyeon Yoon、Wooseok Seo、Hamish Ivison、Hyesoo Hong、Donghoon Kim、Wonjun Kang、Albert No、Jaehyung Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19525v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19525v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出现有可验证指令遵循评测与训练奖励存在盲点：模型可仅满足输出约束、几乎不实质作答却拿到满分；据此提出 CALIF，用冻结参考策略下的校准似然奖励补充验证器奖励，在 IFBench 等基准上兼顾约束满足与回答质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「验证器奖励易被投机、需辅以质量校正」的诊断，可能对 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配与监督设计有借鉴，但迁移到多模态 latent 推理仍属推测、可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diagnosing and Correcting Reward Hacking in Verifiable Instruction Following

**abstract/A1：** Verifiable instruction-following benchmarks emphasize output-constraint satisfaction but can overlook whether responses meaningfully answer the underlying request, creating an evaluation blind spot. We show that frontier models can receive full credit for responses with little substantive answer content. Using the same verifier-based criterion as a training reward can induce reward hacking, as publicly released checkpoints exhibit higher constraint satisfaction but lower response quality after reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. To address this reward hacking, we introduce CALIF, a lightweight method that augments verifier rewards with a calibrated likelihood-based bonus to preserve response quality. CALIF evaluates response likelihood under the request alone using the existing frozen reference policy, requiring no additional judge or learned reward model. Across models ranging from 1.7B to 8B in reasoning and non-reasoning modes, CALIF improves constraint satisfaction on IFBench while maintaining response quality near or above pre-RL levels. It also outperforms corresponding base policies in 40 of 45 model-metric comparisons across three rubric-based instruction-following benchmarks, demonstrating gains beyond training verifiers.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [Reducing the Gap Between Spatial and Space-Filling Curve Neighbors in 3D Point Cloud Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19530v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19530

**作者：** Konan Sasaki、Junya Komori、Kazuhiro Hotta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19530v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19530v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对3D点云分割中「空间填充曲线（SFC）近邻」与欧氏空间近邻不一致的间隙，提出一种损失函数，在原始3D空间中相距远但沿SFC相邻的点对上优化特征相似度，并在三个室内外点云数据集上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该方法用显式目标校正「序列/遍历顺序邻近」与「真实空间邻近」的错配，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度受位置、token 顺序与缓存结构影响、以及视觉关系是否保留在内部表示的问题提供可供核查的正则化线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reducing the Gap Between Spatial and Space-Filling Curve Neighbors in 3D Point Cloud Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** As a lightweight and highly accurate method for nearest-neighbor search in 3D point clouds, approaches based on space-filling curves (SFC) have been proposed. However, due to the inherent properties of SFC, a gap arises between proximity relationships along the SFC and those in the original Euclidean space. In this study, we propose a loss function that reduces the influence of this gap. Specifically, we compute the distances between adjacent points along the SFC and train the model to optimize feature similarity between pairs of points that are distant from each other in the original 3D space. We incorporate the proposed method into a high-performing 3D point cloud segmentation model and evaluate its effectiveness on three datasets. The results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional methods on both indoor and outdoor point cloud datasets.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ECCV2026 Workshop "3rd Workshop on Scalable 3D Scene Generation and Geometric Scene Understanding"

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [TextVerifier: Seeing Beyond Recognition to Verify Chinese Text Rendering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19551v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19551

**作者：** Yiyang Chen、Guo-Hua Wang、Yaoli Liu、Qing-Guo Chen、Zhao Xu、Weihua Luo、Xiujun Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19551v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19551v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将中文文本渲染校验形式化为目标条件判定问题：给定目标字与图像裁片，判断渲染是否正确；方法用 IDS 结构描述序列替代单字符号输入文本塔，并构建 1250 万裁片-IDS 对与「同字不同质」难例训练校验器，另发布字级基准，并将其分数用作 Flow-GRPO 的后训练奖励。

**相关性简评：** 其核心观察「识别训练的 OCR/VLM 对结构偏差不变」与 LVR 中视觉细节坍缩、身份捷径的担忧相通；用 IDS 结构序列替代身份符号、以及从生成轨迹采样难例，或可为内部状态是否保留必要视觉细节的表示诊断提供可核查线索，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理状态，联系属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TextVerifier: Seeing Beyond Recognition to Verify Chinese Text Rendering

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-image models increasingly generate readable Chinese text that still contains local rendering defects, such as a character with an extra or missing stroke or a malformed component. Such errors are invisible to OCR and vision-language models, which are trained for recognition and are therefore invariant to the very structural deviations that define a rendering error. We formalize text rendering verification as a target-conditioned problem: given a target character and an image crop, decide whether the crop is a correct rendering of that character. Our verifier TEXTVERIFIER feeds the text tower a character's Ideographic Description Sequence (IDS) rather than a single-character symbol, and is trained on 12.7M crop-IDS pairs plus label-free "same-character, different-quality" hard pairs sampled from the training trajectory of a text-region inpainting model. To evaluate this ability we build TEXTRENDERBENCH-ZH, the first per-character benchmark for generated Chinese text, annotating position, identity, and correctness for 7,358 characters. TEXTVERIFIER reaches an F1 of 0.844, far above OCR confidence (0.526), a general VLM (0.329), and TextPecker (0.590). Used as a per-character reward for Flow-GRPO post-training, it further reduces rendering errors, showing that its score can both detect and correct errors.

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Divergence-Routed On-Policy Distillation for Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19572v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19572

**作者：** Tiantian Peng、Guannan Lv、Hongyang Li、Qiuhe Hong、Hang Cheng、Hongjian Dou、Xiangyu Wu、Yuyang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19572v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19572v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 on-policy 蒸馏中 RKL 对教师支持但学生概率极低的 token 矫正信号偏弱的问题，发现按散度路由比按熵路由更能定位师生性能差距所在位置，并据此提出 DrOPD，在高 JS 散度位置保留 RKL 的同时额外加入 FKL 监督。

**相关性简评：** 其「按分歧度定位高贡献位置并差异化施加监督」的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 步骤的贡献分配、监督信号选择与证据位置筛选提供借鉴；但该工作限于纯文本蒸馏、无视觉状态或 latent 推理，迁移线索可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Divergence-Routed On-Policy Distillation for Language Models

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers knowledge from a teacher model to a student along student-generated trajectories, typically by minimizing the reverse Kullback-Leibler (RKL) divergence. However, RKL can provide weak corrective signals for teacher-supported tokens to which the student assigns very low probability. This raises two questions: which positions are most consequential for the behavioral gap between the student and teacher, and what additional supervision is needed at these positions? Through cross-sampling, we find that divergence-based routing more effectively pinpoints positions contributing to the teacher-student performance gap than entropy-based routing. Further analysis reveals teacher-mode under-coverage at high Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence positions, where standard OPD increases the large teacher-student probability gaps. Motivated by these findings, we propose Divergence-Routed On-Policy Distillation (DrOPD). DrOPD retains RKL supervision at all positions and selectively adds forward Kullback-Leibler (FKL) divergence supervision at positions with high JS divergence. Experiments across six math benchmarks show that DrOPD improves over standard OPD and entropy-based routing. DrOPD also reduces severe under-coverage cases by 37.9 percent relative to OPD, supporting targeted FKL as an effective complement to OPD.

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Who's Behind the Text? Robust Model Authorship Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19583v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19583

**作者：** Minseok Ko、Hyunseok Lee、Woomin Song、Beomjun Kim、Jinwoo Shin、Kunwoong Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19583v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19583v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ATLAS，把模型来源归因改为「以来源为条件的文本建模」，通过比较给定文本在各来源条件下的似然来判定来源，并对 token 级证据做裁剪聚合以避免单 token 主导；同时构建 MAABench 评测域与版本偏移下的 OOD 归因。

**相关性简评：** 其似然比较与 token 级裁剪聚合策略，可能为 latent 推理中的证据筛选、轨迹贡献分配与 OOD 泛化评价提供可核查的方法线索，但论文本身并不研究视觉或多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who's Behind the Text? Robust Model Authorship Attribution

**abstract/A1：** As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate text, their misuse has become a growing societal problem, with implications for academic misconduct, model distillation, and cyberattacks. For these reasons, Model Authorship Attribution (MAA), which identifies the source model of a given text, is important for promoting the responsible use of LLMs and improving AI safety. In practice, attribution systems must remain reliable beyond the distributions observed during training. They may encounter texts from unseen domains and writing settings, as well as generations from future versions of model families that were not available when the system was trained. However, existing MAA methods often exhibit substantial performance degradation under such distribution shifts. To address this challenge, we propose Authorship Tracing via Likelihood-based Attribution of Sources (ATLAS), which formulates MAA through source-conditioned text modeling rather than direct source prediction. ATLAS learns how text from each source family is generated and attributes a given text by comparing its likelihood under each learned source condition. We aggregate this likelihood evidence at the token level with clipping so that no single token dominates the decision. We further introduce MAABench, a broad MAA benchmark for evaluating practical OOD settings such as domain and version. Our experiments show that ATLAS consistently outperforms existing baselines across MAABench and OpenTuringBench. In particular, when both the generation domain and the model version change on MAABench, ATLAS achieves 74.27% accuracy, 9.00pp higher than the strongest baseline. Beyond MAA, ATLAS can also help identify distillation source models by measuring which source model a target LLM most closely resembles. Our extended experiments show that ATLAS recovers the known teacher of every distilled model we evaluate.

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [JockeyBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Horse-Racing Decision-Making](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19589v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19589

**作者：** Zhongwei Xie、Xingsu Li、Jingqiao Yu、Zihao Wang、Hong ChiTat、Zhaowei Wang、Weizhi Du、Yuxuan Liu、Huihao Jing、Jiaxin Bai、Yangqiu Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19589v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19589v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 JockeyBench，以历史日本赛马为场景构建 LLM agent 重复事件预测基准，结合时间门控证据、付费工具与冻结程序配对对照，在 324 场比赛、28 种模型配置上分别评估预测质量、净效用与固定规则下的下注收益和风险。

**相关性简评：** 其「付费证据获取 + 配对冻结对照 + 区分预测质量与决策价值/成本」的评测协议，可供核查如何归因与计量 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 步骤的增益和代价；但对象为纯文本 agent 决策，未涉及视觉推理或内部推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** JockeyBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Horse-Racing Decision-Making

**abstract/A1：** Repeated event forecasting requires extracting predictive signals from noisy evidence and evaluating their value across successive decisions. We introduce JockeyBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that studies this problem through historical Japanese horse racing. The protocol combines time-gated evidence, priced tools, paired frozen-program controls, and two workflows: executing an LLM-written predictor or acquiring evidence for a fresh per-race LLM forecast. Evaluation separates forecast quality, benchmark-specific net utility, and retrospective betting returns and risk under fixed decision rules. Across 28 model--scaffold configurations on 324 races, paired tests identify seven revision gains and two declines after multiple-testing correction. Yet every configuration has negative mean profit under capped-Kelly settlement. In a four-model comparison, the reusable-program workflow has higher mean betting profit and lower research fees, although both workflows lose on average. Further diagnostics reveal that mean probability bias reverses on selected bets and that profitable individual runs can depend on concentrated returns. These findings motivate evaluating predictive competence, adaptation, resource use, and decision value separately when assessing agents for repeated event forecasting.

</details>

<a id="paper-97"></a>

### 97. [Beyond Static Positional Encoding: Bayesian Time-aware Attention for Non-Stationary Long-term Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19603v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19603

**作者：** Xinjie Lan、Yunbo Mei、Leheng Zhou、Hao Qi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19603v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19603v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 self-attention 解释为对注意力位置的贝叶斯后验（内容相似度作先验、位置编码作时间证据似然），据此推导 ELBO 说明静态位置编码在非平稳长时序列预测中的局限，并提出 BTA，用 MoE 路由按输入自适应生成 head-specific 位置偏置。

**相关性简评：** 把注意力权重分解为内容先验与位置似然、并按输入自适应调节位置偏置，可能为核查 LVR 中位置与注意力结构对 latent 相似度的影响、以及内容与位置贡献的分离提供方法线索，迁移可行性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Static Positional Encoding: Bayesian Time-aware Attention for Non-Stationary Long-term Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Transformers have demonstrated strong performance in long-term time series forecasting (LTSF), yet modeling non-stationary temporal dependencies remains challenging. In this paper, we interpret self-attention as a Bayesian posterior over attended positions, combining a content-based prior induced by scaled dot-product similarity with a temporal-evidence likelihood induced by positional encoding. This formulation provides a probabilistic interpretation of how positional encoding shapes the temporal dependencies captured by self-attention. Building on this perspective, we derive a population-level evidence lower bound (ELBO) that characterizes the role of positional encoding in forecasting weakly stationary time series. Our analysis further highlights a limitation of static positional encoding: when temporal dependence structures vary across input windows, a fixed temporal bias must accommodate heterogeneous dynamics and can therefore be suboptimal for non-stationary forecasting. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Bayesian Time-aware Attention (BTA), which employs a mixture-of-experts (MoE) router to adaptively combine temporal kernels and generate input-conditioned, head-specific positional biases. Extensive experiments on standard LTSF benchmarks demonstrate that BTA consistently improves forecasting performance across multiple Transformer backbones and achieves competitive overall performance with modest computational overhead and stable training.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 12 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-98"></a>

### 98. [Sparse Autoencoders Are a Shared Coordinate System for Symbolic Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19636v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19636

**作者：** Xiaocong Yang、Xuanyu Meng、Yuji Zhang、Jiawei Han、ChengXiang Zhai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.SC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19636v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19636v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAE-IDF：用稀疏自编码器把符号在其原生上下文中编码为稀疏码，按维度稀有度重新加权，从而在不同异构符号系统间匹配单元；在 6 类符号系统的 12 个任务上优于同 LLM 的稠密基线，且稀疏码各维具有可读含义。

**相关性简评：** 其「稀疏特征 + 稀有度加权」的表示对齐方式，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的内容对齐、特征级相似度度量与可解释性分析提供方法线索，但研究对象是符号系统匹配而非视觉推理，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sparse Autoencoders Are a Shared Coordinate System for Symbolic Matching

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) hold broad knowledge in one set of weights, but lack interpretability and trustworthy reasoning, especially in high-stakes use cases. A neuro-symbolic model, with its interpretable architecture and System 2 reasoning capacity, is a promising alternative if built at the scale of LLMs; however, its symbolic backbone is usually constructed with human effort, so existing neuro-symbolic models are bottlenecked by the scope of the symbol component and usually domain-specific. A data-driven method could instead build a larger symbolic system by aligning and merging existing ones. We target its first, foundational step: matching units across these heterogeneous systems. Our method, SAE-IDF, encodes a symbol in its native context into a sparse code with a sparse autoencoder (SAE), re-weights each dimension by its rarity and matches across systems. On 12 tasks across 6 kinds of symbolic system, SAE-IDF outperforms the same LLM's dense baseline on all tasks with Gemma-2-9B and all but one with Llama-3.1-8B, by up to 45 points, consistently across SAE layers and configurations. SAE-IDF is also interpretable: every dimension of the sparse code has a human-readable meaning, so each match decomposes into named concepts (features). Our work paves the way to a universal symbolic backbone.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Remember, Revisit, Revise: Scaffold Memory for Long-Horizon 3D Geometry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19674v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19674

**作者：** Soumick Sarker、Mengmi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19674v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19674v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SMORE，为冻结的几何基础模型配备可修订的外部记忆，将观察者位置与观测内容关联，使长序列下的位姿与三维重建可被存储、检索、用新证据验证并在发现不一致时修订，在多项几何任务上提升长程一致性。

**相关性简评：** 虽属几何重建领域，但其「存储—检索—验证—修订」的外部记忆循环与 LVR 的 latent 状态更新、证据核验及误差纠正可能相通，可供核查记忆读写与验证触发时机如何设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Remember, Revisit, Revise: Scaffold Memory for Long-Horizon 3D Geometry

**abstract/A1：** Geometry foundation models estimate camera poses and 3D scene structure from video sequences, but maintaining consistent reconstructions over long horizons remains challenging. Feed-forward models process long sequences as independent, fixed-length frame windows and align their predictions, causing pose and reconstruction errors to accumulate across windows. Streaming models retain information from past frames to inform future predictions, but cannot revise earlier estimates when later observations reveal errors. To address this, we introduce Scaffold MemOry for Revision (SMORE), a framework equipping frozen geometry foundation models with a revisable external memory for long-horizon inference. Inspired by hippocampal associative memory in biological brains, which links spatial context with sensory experience, SMORE associates where an observer is with what it observes, enabling past observations to be stored, retrieved, verified against new evidence, and revised when inconsistencies are detected. We evaluate SMORE with eight competitive geometry models on five datasets spanning camera pose estimation, multi-view depth estimation, 3D point-cloud reconstruction, simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM), and novel view synthesis. Across all evaluated models, SMORE consistently improves long-sequence pose estimation, reduces multi-view depth error by up to 58%, reduces Chamfer distance for 3D point-cloud reconstruction by up to 43%, and achieves average trajectory error as low as 2.9 cm in long-horizon SLAM. By retaining corrected poses and observations, SMORE further improves the fidelity and spatial consistency of large-scale 3D scene reconstruction. These results establish revisable external memory as a flexible interface for extending pretrained geometry models beyond fixed-context prediction toward iterative, error-correcting inference over long visual sequences.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, 3 main figures, 3 supplementary figures

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [MCAIR: Monitoring and Correcting Agent Unfaithfulness via Internal Representations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19679v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19679

**作者：** Yan Xu、Dandan Song、Luan Zhang、Jun Yang、Ke Duan、Yuhang Tian、Yading Li、Xudong Li、Xing Pei、Guangyuan Feng、Chenhao Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19679v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19679v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MCAIR，用 LLM agent 多步执行中的内部表示训练风险探针，在线检测「完成不忠实」（最终报告未反映真实轨迹），并以风险引导的纠正循环促使 agent 复核执行历史；同时构建带步级内部表示与一致性标注的数据集 ACUD。

**相关性简评：** 其以多步内部表示训练探针做运行时监测与纠正的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用视觉 latent 内容、以及依据内部状态触发复核或停止的做法；但研究对象是纯文本工具型 agent，无视觉推理，迁移需核实。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MCAIR: Monitoring and Correcting Agent Unfaithfulness via Internal Representations

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in completing complex tasks through multi-step reasoning and tool interactions. However, recent studies have revealed that LLM-based agents can exhibit unfaithful behaviors, where their final reports fail to faithfully reflect their actual execution trajectories. Specifically, when agents fail to accomplish tasks through the intended sources or procedures, they may resort to alternative sources or strategies while failing to disclose such deviations, leading users to incorrectly believe that the tasks have been completed according to the original requirements. We refer to this specific form of agent unfaithfulness as Agent Completion Unfaithfulness. Detecting and mitigating agent completion unfaithfulness is critical yet remains largely unexplored. In this work, we propose MCAIR, a runtime monitoring and correction framework that leverages internal representations of LLM-based agents. MCAIR uses multi-step internal representations as inputs to train a risk-aware probe, enabling online detection of potential completion unfaithfulness during execution. Furthermore, MCAIR introduces a risk-guided correction loop that prompts agents to review their execution history and mitigate potential inconsistencies between their execution trajectories and final reports. Additionally, we construct ACUD, a dataset for Agent Completion Unfaithfulness detection that provides step-level execution traces, step-level internal representations, and consistency annotations for training and evaluating runtime detection probes. Experiments demonstrate that MCAIR outperforms LLM-as-a-judge baselines, generalizes across prompts and tasks, and effectively reduces agent completion unfaithfulness.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Enhancing Latent World Models with Associative Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19683v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19683

**作者：** Yuwei Sun、Siyu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19683v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19683v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 JEPA 式潜动态世界模型上引入内容可寻址记忆库，并设计槽位多样性与时间利用均衡两项损失来防止表示坍缩，在多个具身规划基准上验证了长程规划表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「防表示坍缩损失 + 记忆读写 + 多时间尺度共享潜空间」可能为 LVR 中的信息坍缩、证据筛选与 latent 状态更新提供方法线索，可供核查；但研究场景是具身规划而非 VLM 推理，需人工判断可否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Enhancing Latent World Models with Associative Memory

**abstract/A1：** World models trained via latent dynamics offer a scalable path for embodied planning, yet existing methods suffer from representational collapse, computational overhead, and limited long-horizon capability. We introduce a memory-enhanced latent world model that augments JEPA-style prediction with a content-addressable memory bank. Our approach learns high-level representations directly from observations, operating at multiple temporal scales within a shared latent space, and conditions predictions via adaptive layer normalization. To prevent collapse and ensure effective memory usage, we design two complementary losses that encourage slot diversity and balanced temporal utilization. Experiments on PushT, Tworoom, Reacher, and OGBCube demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with a lightweight architecture, outperforming conventional latent world models on long-horizon planning tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [Beyond Distribution Matching: Selective Margin Preservation for Supervised Fine-Tuning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19685v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19685

**作者：** Yuxuan Wang、Guangxiang Zhao、Xinyu Tan、Yahui Han、Xinxiang Yin、Zhonghai Wu、Xiangzheng Zhang、Lin Sun、Tong Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19685v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19685v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Selective Rank-Preserving SFT（SRP-SFT）：在 teacher-forced 前缀上，用 hinge 对参考模型 top-K 非目标 token 的 margin 下界施加软约束，并分析违反 margin 的 KL 代价与可行域；在 Qwen3-4B-Base 与 UltraFeedback 上报告 MATH-500 和代码生成分数优于 CE 与 LP-SFT。

**相关性简评：** 该文属纯文本训练期正则化，不直接研究多模态推理；其「选择性保留非目标 token 相对 margin 关系」的监督设计，可能可供核查 LVR 微调时如何保留内部状态中的关系结构、缓解信息坍缩。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Distribution Matching: Selective Margin Preservation for Supervised Fine-Tuning

**abstract/A1：** Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) changes logit margins among non-target tokens at teacher-forced prefixes. Matching a local reference distribution keeps those probabilities and also penalizes margin expansions. We ask whether a soft auxiliary can preserve selected local non-target margin relations without freezing that distribution. We introduce \\textbf{Selective Rank-Preserving SFT (SRP-SFT)}, a soft margin target: a hinge on reference-scaled lower bounds for pairs from the reference's top-$K$ non-target tokens, applied at fixed teacher-forced prefixes. The hinge stops once the requested bound is met. We derive the KL cost of violating a margin, characterize the feasible region and its conditional utility, and give a finite-step condition for reducing aggregate violations. In one-epoch experiments on Qwen3-4B-Base and UltraFeedback, this target raises MATH-500 and code-generation scores over CE and LP-SFT at all three training seeds. At seed 1234, MATH-500 pass@1 rises from 49.56\\% to 54.56\\% and the four-metric code mean rises from 65.91\\% to 69.50\\% relative to LP-SFT. Ranking controls, held-out cache diagnostics, sensitivity sweeps, and six additional backbones show where these score changes appear.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [Function-Space Temporal Self-Distillation for Policy Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19687v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19687

**作者：** Aryan Das、Arnab Bhattacharya、Vinay Kumar Verma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19687v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19687v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FSPO 单策略 RLVR 框架：把稀疏的历史 next-token 分布存为固定目标做函数空间时序自蒸馏，并加入特权分歧纠正、可靠性自适应监督与质量/覆盖率感知记忆，在推理与工具使用基准上提升平均准确率并降低显存。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RLVR 训练方法，不涉及视觉或多模态状态；其用历史分布作固定目标以稳定自蒸馏、并按自生成证据可靠性调节监督强度的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的监督与贡献分配提供训练侧线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Function-Space Temporal Self-Distillation for Policy Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in language models, but scalar outcomes provide coarse supervision over response trajectories. Self-distillation offers denser token-level guidance by comparing an unassisted policy with a feedback-informed self-teacher, yet the teacher evolves with the student, creating unstable targets and motivating additional regularization. We introduce Function-Space Policy Optimization (FSPO), a single-policy framework that combines current privileged adaptation with function-space temporal consolidation. FSPO stores sparse historical next-token distributions as fixed temporal targets, providing temporal separation without maintaining a persistent teacher model. It further introduces privileged disagreement correction to explicitly suppress locally overvalued actions, reliability-adaptive grounding that strengthens reference supervision when self-generated evidence repeatedly fails, and quality and coverage-aware temporal memory that retains verified behaviors and improves privileged demonstrations. Across reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, FSPO achieves the highest average accuracy under a 5-hour training budget, improving over the strongest baseline by approximately 4% points on average while reducing peak memory from about 70 GB to 56 GB on Olmo3-7B-Instruct.

**comment/C1：** Aryan Das, Vinay Kumar Verma: Equal Contributions. Under Review

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [C3: Rethinking Process Supervision via Confidence-Guided Credit Correction in Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19695v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19695

**作者：** Ai Jian、Haoran Pan、Eryu Guo、Haojin Yang、Jinghan He、Xiaoyun Zhang、Jiangbo Pei、Peiyu Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19695v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19695v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对多轮工具交互智能体中仅用结果奖励会误奖不良局部行为的问题，提出插件式方法 C3：定位对应不良行为的片段，并按行为策略对相应行为的置信度自适应修正优势估计中的正 credit，同时不改动任务奖励与原始 credit。

**相关性简评：** 其「按片段做过程监督与 credit 修正」的思路，可能为 LVR 轨迹训练中的贡献分配、抑制无效推理片段提供可核查的方法线索；但论文面向纯文本多轮 RL，未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理，迁移价值需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** C3: Rethinking Process Supervision via Confidence-Guided Credit Correction in Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are increasingly trained as multi-turn agents that interact with external tools. However, outcome-only reinforcement learning can produce a form of misguided reinforcement: even on successful trajectories, undesirable local behaviors may receive positive token-level advantages and be favored by the policy update. To address this issue, we propose Confidence-Guided Credit Correction (C$^3$), a plug-in method for local credit correction. C$^3$ identifies spans corresponding to undesirable behaviors and selectively corrects positive credit after advantage estimation, with the correction strength adaptively controlled by the behavior policy's confidence in the corresponding behavior, while leaving the task reward and original credit estimation unchanged. Across multiple multi-turn benchmarks, C$^3$ improves performance under both {critic-based and critic-free reinforcement learning methods}. Injecting the same behavioral signals as turn-level or outcome rewards does not consistently improve performance. Our results show that local credit correction provides an effective form of process supervision for outcome-driven RL.

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [ReGRA: Residual-Gradient Adaptation for Complementary Low-Rank Fine-Tuning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19700v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19700

**作者：** Fanyao Li、Yuxi Liu、Xiaohao Liu、He Chang、Zhaoxin Wang、Zhulin Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19700v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19700v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究低秩微调（LoRA）在固定秩预算下的有效利用问题，提出 ReGRA 两阶段方法：先在标定集上估计任务梯度，减去其在一阶段更新所张成子空间上的投影，再用残差梯度方向初始化第二阶段适配器，并以正交正则鼓励两次更新捕捉互补方向，且合并后无额外推理开销。

**相关性简评：** 其「去除已占据子空间投影、再补足正交互补方向」的训练思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的方向冗余与视觉信息坍缩诊断、以及互补监督的设计提供可供核查的方法线索，但原文并不涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReGRA: Residual-Gradient Adaptation for Complementary Low-Rank Fine-Tuning

**abstract/A1：** Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning by learning low-rank weight updates. Increasing rank expands adaptation capacity, but does not guarantee that all learned directions improve downstream performance. In a diagnostic experiment with rank-16 LoRA, we show that retaining only the top singular components achieves the best performance, whereas adding further components degrades it. Motivated by this, we propose Residual-Gradient Adaptation (ReGRA), a two-stage method for making more effective use of a fixed rank budget. After the first stage, ReGRA estimates task gradients on a calibration set and removes their projections onto the two-sided subspace induced by the first-stage update. It then aggregates the residual-gradient directions to initialize a second-stage adapter without changing the first-stage update. An orthogonality regularizer encourages the two updates to capture complementary directions. Both updates can be merged into the pretrained weights, incurring no additional inference overhead. Across diverse benchmark sets, ReGRA achieves stronger performance than alternatives with the same total rank and even matches LoRA adapters with twice the rank. Ablation studies and further analysis support the contribution of its core design choices. Code is available at https://github.com/Fanyao-Li/ReGRA.

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [SceneAE: Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Queryable 3D-Anchored Latent Space](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19708v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19708
