**作者：** Shuofeng Sun、Wenzhao Zheng、Yunpeng Zhang、Yi Wu、Linqing Zhao、Haibin Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19708v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19708v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SceneAE，把场景表示为一组稀疏的 3D 锚定 latent 点，每个 latent 由显式 3D 位置与隐式内容特征组成；该表示既可用于按新相机轨迹重投影解码出 RGB-D 观测，也可先由几何再生成内容以解耦相机与外观。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式几何锚点＋隐式内容特征」的可查询 latent 设计与从任一视角解码为观测的能力，可能为 LVR 内部视觉状态的几何/内容组织及证据读取方式提供方法线索，可供核查迁移可行性；但该工作面向场景生成与重建而非推理，关联属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SceneAE: Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Queryable 3D-Anchored Latent Space

**abstract/A1：** Recent video world models have increasingly adopted 3D memory mechanisms to integrate historical observations into a lightweight and persistent scene representation. This naturally raises a complementary question: if 3D representations can serve as an effective mechanism for memorizing observed scenes, can they also be directly used as the latent space for reconstructing and generating scenes? Motivated by this idea, we represent a scene as a compact and unstructured 3D-anchored latent space (\\textbf{SceneAE}). Each latent combines an explicit 3D position with an implicit content feature and is spatially grounded to describe its local scene region, forming a sparse latent point cloud. Unlike conventional video-VAE latents that describe how a scene appears from particular viewpoints, our representation directly describes the scene itself, making it naturally applicable to both reconstruction and generation. For reconstruction, once the scene has been generated, our persistent 3D representation can be projected and decoded into RGB-D observations by different target cameras, without the need to regenerate a video for each new camera trajectory. For generation, we leverage the geometry-content separation to first generate camera-controlled 3D geometry and then synthesize content conditioned on it, reducing camera-appearance entanglement and improving camera controllability. Experiments show that this 3D latent achieves superior generation quality, computational cost, and camera controllability.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [New Model, Old Habits: Behavior Transfer under Model Swapping in Multi-Session LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19712v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19712

**作者：** Youngbin Choi、Sehyun Hwang、Suhyeon Jun、Seoyeon Lee、Sunghyun Choi、MoonJeong Park、Dongwoo Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19712v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19712v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 通过多会话任务的受控实验发现，模型切换后接收方会继承历史生成模型的推理与动作模式，并据此提出方向与作者感知的历史摘要方法 DASH，以保留强模型行为、抑制弱到强的行为迁移。

**相关性简评：** 「原史直接迁移」与「摘要」造成的行为迁移差异，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态或轨迹应保留何种推理信息、如何分配监督贡献提供可核查线索；但对象为纯文本多会话智能体，与视觉推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** New Model, Old Habits: Behavior Transfer under Model Swapping in Multi-Session LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Multi-session agents can switch between LLMs to balance performance and inference cost. Such switches require transferring a history for continuity, yet their effects on the recipient remain underexplored. Through controlled experiments with multi-session tasks, we uncover behavior transfer: recipients tend to adopt reasoning and action patterns from the models that generated their histories. This effect is most evident with raw-history transfer but is substantially reduced by summarization. Based on these findings, we propose Direction- and Authorship-aware Summarization of History (DASH) that preserves stronger-model behavior while minimizing weak-to-strong transfer via summarization. Experiments with repeated swaps show that DASH outperforms raw-history transfer and naive summarization baselines. These results suggest that effective multi-model orchestration requires considering not only which model to use, but also which behavioral patterns to carry forward.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Learning Individual Scientific Taste with Privileged Hindsight Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19719v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19719

**作者：** Hanwei Fan、Yanqiao Wang、Yiqi Zhu、Xuanyu Lei、Peng Li、Yang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19719v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19719v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「科学品味」建模为研究者个体从共享的、有证据支撑的准则级评估到相对偏好的映射，提出 Privileged Hindsight Distillation：训练时借助事后才出现的科学证据改进准则评估与群体映射，推理时只暴露提案可见信息，并以量子领域的配对比较验证个体偏好捕捉效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「训练期可用特权/事后信息、推理期仅用可见输入」的监督设计，可能为 LVR 中训练与推理信息不对等、贡献分配、视觉旁路与信息坍缩等问题提供可核查的方法线索；但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，联系属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Individual Scientific Taste with Privileged Hindsight Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Scientific taste shapes which research directions researchers pursue, and it varies across individuals. Existing work evaluates research directions along multiple evaluation criteria or models scientific taste using community feedback signals such as citations and reviews, but neither directly captures how individuals translate assessments under the same criteria into different preferences. We model scientific taste as a researcher-specific mapping from criterion-level assessments in a shared, evidence-grounded evaluation space with proposal-visible evidence, to relative preferences. A population-level mapping learned from retrospective supervision provides a cold start when individual feedback is scarce. We introduce Privileged Hindsight Distillation (PHD), which uses later-emerging scientific evidence during training to improve proposal-time criterion assessment and the population mapping, while exposing only proposal-visible information at inference. We evaluate our approach primarily in quantum research, where structured public reviews are largely unavailable, and use pairwise proposal comparison as a direct interface for observing individual taste. With only 15 comparisons per researcher, our method captures sharply different tastes, including opposing preferences over the same research-direction pairs when they trade off across evaluation criteria. In an interactive study, personalized rankings also surface proposals associated with greater subsequent user engagement, connecting learned taste to downstream research exploration.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [What Happens to the Goal? Distinct Failures of Policy Alignment in Goal Misgeneralization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19731v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19731

**作者：** Julia Barth、Armin Sommer、Kunvar Thaman、Jannik Schilling

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19731v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19731v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究目标误泛化问题，在三个 Procgen 环境和五个机器人任务中区分出「功能目标替换」「策略捷径」「线索竞争」三类策略-目标错位，并通过追踪目标信息在内部保存的深度、影响策略的强度及其在动作选择中的作用，配合 steering 与下游重接线来检验何种恢复手段有效。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息在内部仍可达但未进入决策、或已完全失效」的分层刻画与干预式恢复，可为 LVR 诊断 latent 视觉信息是否真被答案使用、视觉旁路与信息坍缩提供可核查的方法模板；但对象是 RL 策略而非 VLM 推理，迁移需另行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Happens to the Goal? Distinct Failures of Policy Alignment in Goal Misgeneralization

**abstract/A1：** Goal misgeneralization is a common failure mode in which an agent behaves competently out of distribution but does not pursue the intended goal. However, we still have a weak understanding of what happens to the intended goal in misgeneralizing agents. Across three Procgen environments and five robot tasks, we find three distinct forms of policy-goal misalignment: (1) functional target substitution, where behavior follows an alternative goal, (2) a policy shortcut, where behavior follows a learned action strategy, and (3) cue competition between intended and proxy goal cues. We distinguish these regimes by tracing where goal information remains available internally, how strongly it influences the policy, and what role it plays in action selection, and find that the goal is lost at different depths. Where the goal reaches the decision, as in cue competition, steering redirects the policy to the goal's location, raising intended-goal reach from 16.0% to 95.3%. Where it reaches the policy but not the decision, only new downstream wiring recovers goal reach, from 31.6% to 84.3% in the environment with the action shortcut. Where it is inert, neither works. Our results show that goal misgeneralization is not a single failure of goal representation, but distinct failures of policy alignment at different depths, tightly connected to which recovery works.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages main text, 120 pages total. Code: https://github.com/Julbarth/what-happens-to-the-goal

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [Physics-Shaped, Not Physical: Auditing Physics-Guided Underwater Image Restoration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19763v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19763

**作者：** Mochamad Rizal Fauzan、Irgi Surya

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19763v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19763v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 本文以多随机种子消融、容量匹配对照和与实测距离的对照检验，审计物理引导水下图像复原网络 PhysGuide-Net，发现其收益来自「物理形状」的归纳偏置而非真实物理量。

**相关性简评：** 其审计协议——容量匹配对照、多次运行消融（可反转符号）、以及检验内部预测图是否真与外部物理量对应——可能为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否携带视觉证据、是否具因果贡献提供可借鉴的验证范式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics-Shaped, Not Physical: Auditing Physics-Guided Underwater Image Restoration

**abstract/A1：** Physics-guided networks for underwater image restoration predict transmission and backscatter and invert the image-formation model, yet rarely test whether this physics does any work. We audit one representative design, PhysGuide-Net, with four controlled experiments: five-seed ablations on UIEB with zero-shot evaluation on the complete LSUI (4279 pairs) and AUIED3K (3000 pairs) sets; a data-efficiency study with a capacity-matched control that removes the formation model; and a validation of the predicted maps against measured range on Sea-thru. With all 800 training images the physics module gives no zero-shot gain (mean PSNR differences of at most 0.16 dB; all 95% intervals include zero) at 3.4x the FLOPs. With 80 images it helps more (+0.65 dB on UIEB, +1.48 dB on AUIED3K; seed-to-seed s.d. on AUIED3K from 1.3 to 0.5 dB), although not individually significant after correction, and the control does not reproduce this (0.8-1.0 dB lower). Yet the predicted transmission does not behave like a physical quantity: its correlation with range is no stronger than for the range map of a different image (pooled difference +0.02, versus -0.06 for the dark-channel prior), and a Beer-Lambert fit explains 0.4% of its variance. The effect of the module also changes sign with degradation severity between datasets. The benefit thus comes from a physics-shaped inductive bias, not from physics. We further show that single-run ablations can reverse sign and document implementation defects that invalidate loss ablations.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-111"></a>

### 111. [Reinforcement Learning via Advantage Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19783v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19783

**作者：** Zhiyuan Zhou、Sergey Levine

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19783v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19783v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Advantage Guidance：以 advantage 为条件进行监督式策略训练，并在采样时用对比式 diffusion guidance 把生成结果导向 KL 正则化的最优策略，在 26 个长时程稀疏奖励任务上优于既有 RL 方法。

**相关性简评：** 其「用 advantage 条件信号训练 + 采样期引导修正生成分布」的机制，可能可供核查于 latent 推理轨迹的价值条件监督与推理期引导（涉及贡献分配与停止决策时的目标导向）。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reinforcement Learning via Advantage Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning (RL) holds the promise of learning a policy that can outperform what is shown in the data. However, in practice, such systems suffer from instability and hyperparameter sensitivity. In contrast, supervised learning provides a stable and scalable training objective, but is fundamentally limited by the quality of the demonstrated behavior. In this paper, we ask whether we can perform policy optimization with something as simple and stable as supervised learning but as performant as RL. Unfortunately, naively training the policy with supervised learning and conditioning on future rewards or advantages typically underperform dedicated RL methods. We introduce a new policy extraction method, Advantage Guidance, that retains the simple supervised learning structure with advantage conditioning while outperforming prior RL methods through effective conditional training and contrastive diffusion guidance during sampling. We show that Advantage Guidance can significantly improve a model's ability to respond to conditional advantage signals, and the contrastive guidance during sampling can use the learned advantage-conditioned policy to target the KL-regularized optimal policy. Empirically, we find that Advantage Guidance significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL methods on 26 long-horizon, sparse reward tasks across datasets with varying degrees of optimality.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [StateCraft: Augmenting Video World Models with Abstract-State Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19795v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19795

**作者：** Lorenzo Venturoli、Nedko Savov、Xi Wang、Marc Pollefeys、Luc Van Gool

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19795v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19795v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 StateCraft，将可持久更新的抽象状态与视觉状态解耦，用语言模型跟踪实体随动作发生的变化并条件化视频扩散模型生成，配套自动数据采集管线与衡量生成质量、记忆和可控性的基准，在长轨迹上提升自一致性与可编辑性。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式抽象状态 + 条件生成」的设计可供核查 LVR 中长期状态更新、视觉证据筛选与状态编辑对生成影响的借鉴，但该工作面向视频世界模型，与 VLM 推理及 latent 使用的直接联系仍待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StateCraft: Augmenting Video World Models with Abstract-State Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Video world models aim to simulate complex environments with realistic appearance and dynamics. Maintaining consistency over long interactions requires tracking past actions and understanding how those actions affect the environment. Video world models can only generate partial observations of the entire world at a time. Remembering past observations alone may not be sufficient in every scenario, as they may no longer reflect the world after a sequence of actions. We introduce a paradigm that decouples a persistent, updatable abstract state from the visual state. The abstract state keeps track of the entities' changes as the world evolves, and is used to condition the visual state, which represents the current view. We propose StateCraft, a novel architecture that pairs a language model with a video diffusion model to evolve the dynamic world. We evaluate our approach on generative interfaces, which pose a substantial challenge because a single action may change parts of the state that are not currently visible. To support training and evaluation, we develop a data collection pipeline that produces informative, densely annotated trajectories at scale without human or agent supervision, together with a benchmark measuring generation quality, memory, and controllability. On this benchmark, our approach improves surpasses the state-of-the-art baselines. We demonstrate that StateCraft maintains high self-consistency and strong generation quality over long trajectories. Because the abstract state is explicit, it can be edited directly, and generation follows edits to states never observed in the data. Finally, a proof-of-concept 3D experiment, in which actions change the illumination of an unobserved room, shows that the paradigm can also be applied to spatial environments. Project page: https://insait-institute.github.io/StateCraft/.

</details>

<a id="paper-113"></a>

### 113. [Learning Perception to Action Rules through Reinforcement Learning for Theory-of-Mind Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19796v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19796

**作者：** Soyoung Oh、Nursulu Sagimbayeva、Nicholas Jennings、Paulina Hussein、Mehwish Alam、Vera Demberg

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19796v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19796v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究用显式 if-then 规则连接感知与行动、并以 GRPO 微调 Qwen2.5 与 Llama3，在四个 ToM 基准上考察能否弥合「正确推断心理状态却未用于行动判断」的缺口，并检验大模型生成规则的跨模型迁移性。

**相关性简评：** 其「推断正确但未用于输出」的失败诊断与 latents 未被答案使用的信息坍缩问题可能同源，规则生成加 GRPO 的监督形式或可作为 LVR 轨迹训练与贡献分配的借鉴线索，需核查是否涉及推理期状态使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Perception to Action Rules through Reinforcement Learning for Theory-of-Mind Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** In Theory-of-Mind (ToM) reasoning, large language models may correctly infer the agent's mental state but fail to use that inference when predicting or judging the agent's subsequent actions. In this work, we investigate whether explicitly connecting perception, belief, and action with rules improves ToM reasoning. We generate concise \\textit{if--then} rules that express relations between perception to action and use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to train Qwen2.5 and Llama3 models across four ToM benchmarks. GRPO with rule generation outperforms prompting, supervised fine-tuning, and think reasoning. Moreover, rules generated by larger models ($\\geq$3B) provide transferable knowledge that improves downstream performance of GPT-4o-mini. These findings suggest that explicit rules help models bridge the reasoning gap in ToM and support knowledge transfer across models.

</details>

<a id="paper-114"></a>

### 114. [When Does Depth Help? Understanding Layerwise Inference in Tabular Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19814v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19814

**作者：** Wenwen Gong、Haotian Gu、Zhen Yang、Jiaying Ma、Hanqing Zhang、Zhongshen Wang、Jingjun Wang、Shouyi Xu、Wei Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19814v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19814v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 结合表示几何、预测轨迹、逐层截断与结构干预，分析 TabPFN-3 全部 24 层在 272 个表格分类数据集上的逐层推理，考察后层是否冗余、哪些输入真正受益于更深的计算。

**相关性简评：** 其按输入区分「继续加深计算是否获益」的截断、层序扰动等诊断，可能可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的收益—成本与深度冗余评估；但属表格预测域，向多模态推理状态分析的迁移需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Depth Help? Understanding Layerwise Inference in Tabular Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve state-of-the-art performance on small to medium-sized tabular prediction tasks while processing diverse inputs through a shared stack of layers. Recent layerwise analyses identify strong predictive performance in early TFM representations and substantial overlap in computation across layers. However, early predictive accessibility alone does not establish whether later layers are dispensable for every input, and aggregate accuracy can obscure which inputs benefit from additional computation. We study all 24 in-context-learning layers of TabPFN-3 across 272 classification datasets, combining representation geometry, prediction trajectories, truncation, and structural interventions, while also examining related representation dynamics in four additional TFMs. We uncover a pronounced expansion-compression trajectory, with stronger late dimensional contraction associated with larger increases in class-prototype separation. A targeted analysis of early-stabilizing samples shows that substantial representational changes persist after predicted classes stabilize, while locally ambiguous queries obtain larger accuracy gains from shallow to late layers than well-separated queries. Repeating a single pretrained block or perturbing layer order reduces predictive performance on average, constraining simple post-hoc substitutions in the frozen model. These findings distinguish predictive accessibility, class stability, and the benefits of continuing computation, supporting an input-dependent assessment of depthwise redundancy in TFMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-115"></a>

### 115. [ActionOPD: Distilling Decisions, Not Tokens](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19820v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19820

**作者：** Huining Yuan、Xiangmin Yi、Zelai Xu、Zixu Wang、Xinlei Chen、Xiao-Ping Zhang、Yu Wang、Chao Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19820v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19820v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ActionOPD，一种面向语言模型智能体的动作级 on-policy 蒸馏方法：在学生实际遇到的状态上分别从学生与黑盒教师采样动作，按语义一致性给予奖励并做策略优化，从而无需教师 token 概率或同族分词器即可对齐学生决策，在多个工具使用基准上取得 3.3–6.9 个百分点的平均提升。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉/多模态 latent 推理无直接关系，但其「以语义动作而非 token 为监督单元、在学生遇到的状态上做 on-policy 对齐」的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理步的监督粒度、轨迹训练与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ActionOPD: Distilling Decisions, Not Tokens

**abstract/A1：** Distilling capable language-model agents into smaller models requires aligning the student's behavior with the teacher's. This raises a fundamental question: at which states and at what granularity should this alignment be performed? Offline imitation provides dense but token-specific supervision and can suffer from state-distribution mismatch. Token-level on-policy distillation (OPD) addresses this mismatch but typically requires teacher token probabilities and compatible tokenization. We argue that semantic actions provide a natural unit of supervision for agent distillation. We introduce ActionOPD, an action-level on-policy distillation method motivated by maximizing expected teacher-action likelihood at student-encountered states. At each such state, ActionOPD samples actions from both the student and a black-box teacher, rewarding student actions according to their semantic agreement with the sampled teacher actions. Through policy optimization, ActionOPD aligns the student's action-level behavior with the teacher's decisions without requiring reproduction of teacher-written reasoning or access to teacher token probabilities. Training Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B with two teachers on diverse, curated tool-use tasks yields average gains of 3.3-6.9 percentage points across four out-of-distribution benchmarks. ActionOPD scales favorably with student size and teacher capability, without requiring the teacher and student to share a model family or tokenizer. On $τ^2$-bench, ActionOPD achieves competitive performance against offline distillation and token-level OPD. We further explore Self-ActionOPD with a same-model teacher and privileged context. These results establish ActionOPD as a promising approach to agent distillation.

</details>

<a id="paper-116"></a>

### 116. [MAPLE: A Multi-Axis Parametric Knowledge Extraction Lens for LLM Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19827v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19827

**作者：** Minsuh Joo、Youna Kim、Choonghyun Park、Soo Kyung Kim、Hyunsoo Cho

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19827v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19827v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MAPLE 诊断框架，在表层、概率与采样外三个层级用恢复/破坏指标，比较解码调整、激活引导与提示等六种知识提取增强技术在 OLMo2、Qwen2.5、Llama3 上的效果与权衡。

**相关性简评：** 激活引导等推理期内部状态操控、以及区分「恢复失败样本」与「破坏成功样本」并考察超出固定采样预算的答案恢复，可能为核验 latent 推理干预的真实贡献与代价提供评价层面的借鉴线索，需人工进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAPLE: A Multi-Axis Parametric Knowledge Extraction Lens for LLM Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** LLMs absorb vast knowledge from training data, yet their inherently probabilistic nature offers no guarantee that this knowledge will surface in their outputs. To bridge this gap, Knowledge Extraction Enhancement (KEE) techniques such as activation steering, decoding adjustments, and prompting modify inference, potentially reinforcing existing predictions or surfacing less accessible answers. Yet their evaluations centered on aggregate accuracy, which obscures diverse effects by collapsing recovered failures and disrupted successes into a single score. We introduce MAPLE, a unified framework for characterizing how KEE techniques affect parametric knowledge extraction. Its diagnostic metrics disentangle these effects at the surface, probability, and beyond-sampling levels by measuring answer recovery and disruption, gold-token rank shifts on unrecovered failures, and recovery of answers missed by fixed-budget sampling. We evaluate six KEE techniques spanning decoding, activation steering, and prompting across three model families: OLMo2, Qwen2.5, and Llama3. Our analysis reveals distinct effects and trade-offs across techniques, highlighting the value of MAPLE for understanding and comparing KEE beyond aggregate accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [CTIA-ODE: Continuous-Time Inter-Variable Attention for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19831v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19831

**作者：** Chengyi Pan、Huangqimei Zheng、Hao Xue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19831v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19831v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CTIA-ODE 连续时间多变量时间序列预测框架：将连续时间变量间注意力嵌入 ODE 求解器，让持续演化的内源轨迹在积分路径上动态查询静态外源上下文，并辅以线性残差增强与抗离群混合损失稳定连续潜动态，在八个数据集上报告了较优精度与效率。

**相关性简评：** 其由 ODE 驱动的连续潜状态更新、以及在积分路径上查询静态上下文而不必显式对齐的机制，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态生成与更新方式、以及缓解潜状态漂移/信息坍缩的稳定化设计提供可核查线索，但跨模态与跨任务的可迁移性尚待验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CTIA-ODE: Continuous-Time Inter-Variable Attention for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Although multivariate time series forecasting has broad application prospects, effectively incorporating exogenous variables remains challenging. Prevailing discrete-time architectures often impose rigid temporal alignment across heterogeneous variables, which may limit their ability to capture continuously evolving external effects. To address this limitation, we introduce CTIA-ODE, a continuous-time forecasting framework centered on Continuous-Time Inter-Variable Attention (CTIA). Specifically, CTIA is embedded within an ordinary differential equation (ODE) solver, enabling the continuously evolving endogenous trajectory to dynamically query a static exogenous context along the integration path without requiring explicit temporal alignment. To improve the stability of the continuous latent dynamics, we further introduce a dual-stabilization strategy: architecturally, a Linear Residual Augmentation (LRA) module provides a macroscopic trend anchor, while an Outlier-Robust Hybrid Loss (ORHL) mitigates the influence of heavy-tailed anomalies during optimization. Extensive experiments across eight real-world datasets demonstrate that CTIA-ODE achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance across diverse datasets and prediction horizons, while maintaining a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off with a compact memory footprint and low inference latency, particularly under long-horizon forecasting settings. Anonymous code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CTIA-ODE-15A6/.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [Rent-Seeking Language Models: Reward Models Set the Prices, Frequent Audits Curb the Rent](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19832v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19832

**作者：** Zixiao Wang、Xingbo Du、Duzhen Zhang、Le Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19832v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19832v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 RLHF 中的 reward model overoptimization 重新表述为「寻租」：策略作为企业从 proxy reward model 这一监管者处获取真实质量无法支撑的收益，并在受控测试平台上用 rent、capture index、deadweight loss 等指标量化，考察审计频率与强度、policy 与 regulator 的亲缘关系等条件。

**相关性简评：** 其「寻租」度量与审计/阈值设计可能可供核查能否用于诊断 LVR 训练中答案是否真的依赖视觉证据（如视觉旁路、指标劫持），但论文本身不涉及多模态或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rent-Seeking Language Models: Reward Models Set the Prices, Frequent Audits Curb the Rent

**abstract/A1：** Reward model overoptimization in RLHF is usually described as reward hacking. We recast it as rent-seeking: the policy, a firm, earns reward from a regulator, the proxy reward model, that true quality does not back. The lens turns overoptimization into measurable quantities, namely rent, a capture index and deadweight loss, and into testable predictions from the economics of regulation. In a fixed, version-controlled testbed with three model families, accuracy-matched proxy reward models and two gold reward models, RL turns 41% to 66% of its proxy gain into rent while gold reward plateaus. Rent follows each regulator's prices. Neither proxy verifies facts, but one pays for confident specifics and the other for displays of diligence, so RL policies learn to fabricate details about nonexistent entities (97% to 100% of probes) or to deflect factual questions (67% to 98%). Kinship between policy and regulator adds a fixed premium of about 0.1 standard deviations but gives the policy no extra leverage under optimization. At a fixed expected fine, frequent mild audits beat rare severe ones, and random audits are as efficient per audit as targeted ones. Bringing the bunching estimator of tax economics to RL training, we also find a first sign that policies learn to stay just below a targeted audit's threshold.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [LAGO: Language Agents with Reusable Growing Options](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19843v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19843

**作者：** Yuran Wu、Mengkang Hu、Qionglin Qiu、Fanqing Meng、Qiguang Chen、Yuhang Zhou、Qiudan Zhang、Xu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19843v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19843v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LAGO，把专用语言 agent 当作可复用的时序扩展选项，由 meta-agent 从持久选项库检索或新建选项、分派子任务并验证结果，在 SWE-bench Verified 上以冻结模型验证其解决率提升。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身面向软件工程长程任务、不涉及视觉状态，但其「可复用选项库＋meta-agent 调度与验证准入」可供核查 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 调度与可复用推理子策略的组织方式，属推测性迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LAGO: Language Agents with Reusable Growing Options

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon tasks expose a weakness of monolithic tool-use loops: one agent must decompose, execute, and verify a task within a single trajectory. We introduce LAGO, which treats specialized language agents as reusable temporally extended options. A meta-agent retrieves options from a persistent library or spawns new ones, dispatches sub-tasks, verifies results, and admits successful novel options. The library grows across tasks without updating model weights. On 500 SWE-bench Verified tasks, frozen Qwen3.5-35B-A3B in both roles resolves 58.6% under single-attempt evaluation with the official Docker evaluator. On a fixed 150-task subset, we re-run mini-SWE-agent, Terminus 2, and LAGO with the same policy, temperature, task IDs, and evaluator; their resolve rates are 49.3%, 57.3%, and 60.3%, respectively. Cross-model experiments provide evidence consistent with a role for decomposition quality. A 50-task component ablation finds four fewer resolved tasks without decomposition, two fewer with shuffled reused content, and six fewer without the library. These results support reusable agent-options as a practical abstraction for task streams, while leaving component-level causality task- and model-dependent.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 7 figures. Equal contribution: Yuran Wu, Mengkang Hu. Corresponding author: Xu Wang

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [Energy Consumption Analysis of Discrete Diffusion and Autoregressive Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19855v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19855

**作者：** Boris Ruf、Marcin Detyniecki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19855v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19855v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文对离散扩散语言模型（LLaDA-8B、Dream-7B）与自回归模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B）做推理能耗基准，比较每 token 焦耳数以及 KV-cache 在不同序列长度下的节能量。

**相关性简评：** 其比较并行扩散与 AR 生成、并量化 KV-cache 成本的方法，可能为 LVR 在「实际成本」维度评估不同推理期状态生成范式提供可借用的测量与指标线索，但论文本身不涉及多模态或 latent 状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Energy Consumption Analysis of Discrete Diffusion and Autoregressive Language Models

**abstract/A1：** As Large Language Models (LLMs) continue to scale, their energy consumption during inference has become a critical concern. While Autoregressive (AR) models are the industry standard, Diffusion Language Models (DLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative capable of parallel token generation. In this paper, we perform a comparative energy benchmark of diffusion models (LLaDA-8B, Dream-7B) against AR counterparts (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B). We analyze the trade-offs between model architecture, KV-cache optimization, and Joules-per-token efficiency. Our results show that at longer sequences (200--600 tokens), diffusion models achieve 2.5--2.7 J/token, approximately 2$\\times$ more efficient than AR models with KV-cache (5.3--5.7 J/token). KV-cache provides substantial benefits for AR models at longer sequences (50--70\\% energy reduction).

</details>

### 121. [OmniPoser: Flexible Human Motion Recovery in the Wild with Masked Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19857v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19857

**作者：** Minghao Liu、Tutian Tang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19857v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19857v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OmniPoser，用跨模态掩码机制与掩码条件编码器，在单一模型中从 3D 关键点、稀疏 IMU、头显位姿等任意异构传感器子集恢复全身 SMPL 运动，并以 Conditional Flow Matching 解耦双流骨干实现单步推理。

**相关性简评：** 方法线索可供核查：掩码条件编码器对任意输入子集的兼容设计可能对应 LVR 中不完整视觉证据的筛选与融合，而单步 Conditional Flow Matching 生成完整运动序列，或可启发低成本 latent 假设的一次性生成，但其有效性在视觉推理上未经检验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OmniPoser: Flexible Human Motion Recovery in the Wild with Masked Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Recovering 3D human motion from sparse wearable sensors is challenging due to the inherent noise, occlusion, and instability of real-world tracking signals. Existing approaches are restricted to fixed sensor configurations and, when built on diffusion models, are computationally prohibitive for real-time deployment. We propose OmniPoser, a universal framework that recovers full-body SMPL motion from a flexible combination of heterogeneous inputs, such as 3D keypoints, sparse IMU rotations, head-mounted 6-DoF poses, or any subset thereof, within a single model. OmniPoser introduces a cross-modal masking mechanism paired with a Masked Conditioning Encoder, enabling one architecture to incorporate diverse sensor configurations through a masked design. A decoupled dual-stream generative backbone generates complete motion via Conditional Flow Matching, requiring only a single ODE integration step at inference. Across three settings and seven benchmarks, including in-the-wild Nymeria and Ego-Exo4D datasets, OmniPoser performs on par with state-of-the-art methods while running at over 700 FPS. The project page is available at https://jamesbridgewater51.github.io/omniposer-eccv/.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a poster at the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Euler: Towards Human-Centered Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19864v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19864

**作者：** Qiuyu Chen、Qi Wang、Liang Xu、Xin Jin、Baao Xie、Wenjun Zeng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19864v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19864v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Euler 是一个面向人的多智能体数学研究系统，把命题、假设、证明依赖、备选路线、失败尝试与验证证据组织成相互连接的研究图，为跨会话的规划、协作与核验提供持久且任务相关的知识，并以三个数学案例展示其能力，附带系统实现与软件验证。

**相关性简评：** 其结构化研究图与持久状态可供核查是否能迁移为 LVR 的推理状态表示、证据核验与轨迹回溯机制，但纯文本多智能体数学发现设定与视觉主线无直接关联，线索较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Euler: Towards Human-Centered Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Mathematical research drives scientific discovery, and recent advances in AI for mathematics have shown that language-model agents can solve challenging problems and develop substantial mathematical proofs. Yet much of today's AI-for-mathematics work centers on obtaining a final answer. This emphasis leaves the exploration behind a result difficult for researchers to understand, while long-running investigations face fragmented context and the risk of unsupported claims propagating across reasoning steps. We present Euler, a human-centered mathematical research system built around a shared, structured representation of the research process. Euler organizes mathematical statements, assumptions, proof dependencies, alternative approaches, failed attempts, and verification evidence into connected research graphs. This representation allows researchers to trace how results are developed and supplies agents with persistent, task-relevant knowledge for planning, collaboration, and verification across sessions. Researchers can also select a graph node linked to a saved team state, revisit the agents' earlier reasoning, and explore alternative directions through continued interaction. We illustrate Euler's capabilities through three mathematical research case studies and describe the implemented system and its software validation.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 11 figures, 5 tables, 1 algorithm. Technical report. Code: https://github.com/canghaimeng/Euler

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [SPARS: A Stability-Plasticity-Aware Region Selection Framework for Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19876v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19876

**作者：** Teif Aldaajani、Huda Hakami、Maha A. Thafar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19876v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19876v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向持续学习提出无需回放的混合框架 SPARS：以 Fisher 信息矩阵或新的无标签「置信度分数」（基于 logit 敏感度）估计参数重要性，将参数划为获胜、邻近与非重要三类区域，分别施以选择性冻结、软正则与自由学习，并用二次判别分析缓解类别增量下的分类器偏置。

**相关性简评：** 其「按重要性分区、差异化更新参数」的机制可为 latent 推理训练中的贡献分配与容量分配提供借鉴线索，尤其是无标签的 logit 敏感度估计能否充当 latent 轨迹的监督权重，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SPARS: A Stability-Plasticity-Aware Region Selection Framework for Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** Continual learning aims to acquire new knowledge while retaining previously learned knowledge. However, updating shared parameters when learning new tasks often overwrites existing knowledge, causing catastrophic forgetting. Memory replay methods mitigate catastrophic forgetting but rely on previous samples, raising privacy, memory, and scalability concerns. On the other hand, parameter isolation-based methods preserve learned knowledge but may limit knowledge transfer, whereas regularization promotes adaptability but remains susceptible to interference between old and new tasks. To maintain the stability-plasticity trade-off, this paper proposes Stability-Plasticity-Aware Region Selection (SPARS), a replay-free hybrid framework combining global parameter isolation with localized parameter regularization. SPARS estimates parameter importance using either the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) or a novel Confidence Score (CS) estimator that leverages logit sensitivity and achieves comparable performance to FIM without requiring labelled data. Based on parameter importance, SPARS partitions parameters into three importance-aware regions namely, winning, neighboring, and non-important parameter regions. Each region receives a different adaptation strategy: selective freezing, soft regularization, or unrestricted learning based on its role in the stability-plasticity trade-off. To mitigate classifier bias in class-incremental learning, we employ Quadratic Discriminant Analysis to improve performance on previously learned classes .Experiments on CIFAR-10 and TinyImageNet demonstrate consistent improvement in average incremental accuracy over existing isolation- and regularization-based methods across task- and class-incremental learning. On TinyImageNet, SPARS achieves the best performance with 6% improvement for class-incremental learning while retaining over 60% of parameters for future tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [CoDy: Codec Dynamics as Complementary Priors for Unsupervised Temporal Action Localization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19880

**作者：** Tianyu Yan、Qingyi Yang、Qin Yang、Xufei Guo、Yuan Tang、Rui Wang、Yixue Hao、Long Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19880v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CoDy 面向无监督时序动作定位，把视频编码器的量化模式、运动矢量与残差变化当作互补先验：通过置信度感知的共识聚类融合 RGB／光流生成软伪标签，并将编码器动态转成类别无关的边界先验来监督边界学习与候选精修，编码器分支仅训练期使用。

**相关性简评：** 其「训练期额外证据通道＋置信度共识伪标签＋类别无关状态切换先验」的结构，可能为 LVR 的监督信号构造与 STOP／状态切换判据提供可核查线索，但任务本身是视频动作定位，与 VLM 内部状态推理距离较远，迁移需自行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoDy: Codec Dynamics as Complementary Priors for Unsupervised Temporal Action Localization

**abstract/A1：** Unsupervised temporal action localization (UTAL) aims to identify action categories and localize their temporal boundaries without annotation supervision. Existing methods typically derive supervision for both tasks solely from RGB and optical-flow representations, often resulting in noisy category assignments and inaccurate temporal boundaries. In this work, we reveal an overlooked property of video codecs: group-of-pictures quantization patterns preserve category-discriminative dynamics, while motion-vector and residual variations exhibit pronounced responses around action transitions. Based on this insight, we propose CoDy, a codec dynamics-guided framework that exploits the codec as complementary information to the visual view for UTAL. CoDy integrates a codec-enhanced view with RGB and flow through confidence-aware consensus clustering, producing reliable soft pseudo-labels. It further converts codec dynamics into a class-agnostic boundary prior, which supervises visual boundary learning and guides proposal initialization and iterative refinement. The codec branches are only used during the training stage and introduce no extra codec-side computation at inference. Extensive experiments on THUMOS14 and ActivityNet v1.3 demonstrate that CoDy consistently outperforms existing UTAL methods and achieves state-of-the-art localization performance. These results establish codec signals as effective annotation-free priors for jointly inferring which actions occur and when they occur.

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Occupancy Modeling via Drifting: One-Step Generation Enables Direct Policy Extraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19883v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19883

**作者：** Robin Uhrich、Marco Bagatella、Núria Armengol Urpí、Pavel Kolev、Georg Martius

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19883v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19883v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把「在无奖励轨迹上预训练生成式 occupancy（折扣状态访问分布），再用少量奖励数据抽取策略」这一流程拆成三条设计轴——多模态结构建模、生成模型类别与策略抽取方式，并据此提出用 drifting 一步生成采样建模 occupancy、通过直接求导抽取策略、免去 critic 蒸馏的简化方法。

**相关性简评：** 其一步式 drifting 采样与「对生成模型直接求导」的抽取方式，可能为 diffusion/一步式 latent 推理状态生成、以及免蒸馏的监督与贡献分配提供可供核查的方法线索；但该工作属强化学习，与视觉多模态推理无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Occupancy Modeling via Drifting: One-Step Generation Enables Direct Policy Extraction

**abstract/A1：** The growing interest in behavior foundation models, combined with recent advancements in generative modeling, has led to an appealing recipe for reinforcement learning from unlabeled data: pre-train a generative model to model occupancies, i.e., discounted state visitations, on reward-free trajectories, such that a policy can be easily extracted once a small reward-labeled dataset becomes available. We decompose this recipe along three axes: (i) the degree to which the multi-modal structure of occupancies must be captured, (ii) the class of generative model, and (iii) the method used for policy extraction. Along each axis, we consider existing choices, propose simpler alternatives, and validate each decision experimentally. This analysis culminates in a novel, streamlined method that leverages drifting, a modern one-step generative sampler, to model the occupancy. We show that this enables policy extraction via direct differentiation through the occupancy model, and bypasses the need for critic distillation. Overall, we observe that this nimble setup leads to improvements in performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [How GUI Agents Fail: GraceBench for Execution Stability Diagnosis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19900v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19900

**作者：** Ridong Zhang、Shuxian Liang、Chaoqun Wan、Zhen Huang、Xian-Sheng Hua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19900v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19900v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GraceBench：以 UI Transition Graph 刻画移动应用可达交互空间，用视频对齐的轨迹评估在步骤层面诊断 GUI agent 的执行稳定性，报告偏离率、循环负担率与恢复能力等指标，覆盖 169 个任务、3718 条轨迹，并指出成功率会掩盖过程不稳定。

**相关性简评：** 其图对齐的状态匹配与循环、停滞、恢复的步骤级度量，可能为 latent 循环推理的循环/停滞判据及 STOP 决策评价提供可核查的方法线索；但对象是 GUI 动作轨迹而非内部视觉推理状态，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How GUI Agents Fail: GraceBench for Execution Stability Diagnosis

**abstract/A1：** As GUI agents are increasingly deployed for mobile task automation, their reliability depends not only on final task outcomes, but also on whether they reach these outcomes through stable interaction trajectories. In automation workflows, unnecessary actions, detours, and execution errors can add noise to collected data, introduce irreversible side effects, or undermine trust, even when the agent eventually completes the task. It is therefore important to systematically diagnose the execution stability, including whether an agent deviates from a feasible path, enters a loop, becomes stagnant, or recovers from unstable actions. Existing benchmarks, however, primarily assess final task success or a small set of intermediate milestones, leaving step-level execution behavior unclear. To bridge this gap, we introduce GraceBench, an online evaluation framework that represents the reachable interaction space of mobile applications as UI Transition Graphs (UTGs), providing a structural reference for step-level execution diagnosis. To support reliable alignment between dynamic UI observations and graph states, GraceBench uses video-grounded trajectory evaluation over this graph-aligned representation, assessing process stability beyond final success with metrics such as deviation rate, loop burden rate, and recovery ability. GraceBench contains 169 tasks spanning 10 applications and constructs 10 UTGs with 2,909 canonical GUI states and 3,116 observed transitions. Across 3,718 trajectories, GraceBench shows that outcome scores hide execution instability: GPT-5.6 Sol achieves the highest success rate (SR) but the lowest recovery ability, 51.24% of successful runs still contain a deviation or loop, and trajectories with the same success rate (SR) and completion rate (CR) can differ substantially in recovery behavior. Code and data will be released on https://iaie-physicalai.github.io/GraceBench.

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [Sparse Transformer: a Controlled Testbed for Mechanistic Interpretability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19907v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19907

**作者：** Anton Korznikov、Alexey Dontsov、Elena Tutubalina

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19907v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19907v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Sparse Transformer 测试台：在 TinyStories 上训练一个 5 层语言模型，用宽而 TopK 稀疏的 MLP 迫使神经元专门化，以 AutoInterp 和少量神经元电路验证其可解释性，并比较 Transcoder 与 Sparse Autoencoder 恢复内部特征向量的能力，再通过把稀疏 MLP 蒸馏为稠密模型来研究多义性与特征叠加。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本可解释性工作，但其「用 Transcoder／SAE 恢复内部特征并检验保真度」「蒸馏压缩导致特征叠加」的评测思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留视觉内容及信息坍缩问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sparse Transformer: a Controlled Testbed for Mechanistic Interpretability

**abstract/A1：** Mechanistic interpretability aims to decompose neural network computations into human-understandable features, yet progress is limited by the absence of ground-truth units to evaluate against. We address this with a Sparse Transformer (ST): a 5-layer language model trained on TinyStories whose wide, TopK-sparse MLP forces neuron specialization. We show that ST neurons are highly interpretable: they outperform a loss-matched dense transformer by 43% on AutoInterp, and circuits of a median of 12 neurons suffice to reproduce individual model predictions. This testbed allows us to directly compare feature learning methods: Transcoder, which approximates the MLP input-output mapping, recovers ST's neuron vectors with 52.6% higher cosine similarity than Sparse Autoencoder, which only reconstructs outputs. Furthermore, the ST enables a controlled study of polysemanticity: distilling its sparse MLP into a dense counterpart reveals how compression leads to feature superposition. These results show how a known reference basis sharpens evaluation and understanding, a role we hope the ST can serve for the field.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [When Non-Adversarial Noise Hurts Learning: Benchmarking Skill Self-Evolution in Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19910v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19910

**作者：** Tao Yu、Zhongtian Luo、Yuchen Long、Yiheng Lu、Yuxuan Zhou、Yanpei Gong、Siyi Cao、Yuqing Wu、Haopeng Jin、Zhenghao Zhang、Yujia Yang、Jingmin Zhu、Yunyun Hou、Xinming Wang、Hongzhu Yi、Jiachen Liu、Sirui Wang、Nan Duan、Yan Huang、Liang Wang、Tianjin Huang、Yanyan Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19910v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19910v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 「提出 RSE-Bench，在普通非对抗噪声下评测语言模型 agent 的技能自演化，并给出统一噪声注入与过程诊断框架 RSEClaw，发现噪声学习仅保留干净自演化增益的 25.5%–42.1%。」

**相关性简评：** 「其分离学习期噪声与评测期输入噪声、并诊断轨迹暴露与技能更新的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 轨迹监督中错误经验如何固化、以及贡献分配问题，但对象为纯文本 agent，线索需人工确认。」

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Non-Adversarial Noise Hurts Learning: Benchmarking Skill Self-Evolution in Agents

**abstract/A1：** Large language model agents increasingly improve by distilling experience into reusable skills, but errors in that experience can persist in learned rules and affect later tasks. Existing evaluations largely examine clean learning or perturbations encountered after skills are frozen, leaving robustness during self-evolution insufficiently understood. We introduce RSE-Bench, to our knowledge the first benchmark for evaluating skill self-evolution under ordinary, non-adversarial noise. It spans four domains and six benchmarks, with controlled perturbations to instructions, environmental evidence, execution records, and outcome--record pairings that preserve the underlying tasks and native scoring rules. The protocol separates noise during learning from input-side noise at evaluation, testing frozen skill stores on held-out tasks. We also introduce \\textsc{RSEClaw}, a method-agnostic framework supporting consistent noise injection and process diagnostics of actual exposure and skill updates. Across three methods and two backbones, noisy learning retains only $25.5\\%$--$42.1\\%$ of the aggregate gain achieved by clean self-evolution, depending on the noise type. Perturbations confined to execution records and outcome--record pairings cause average losses comparable to input-side noise, despite leaving the original executions and evaluations intact. Diagnostics further identify differences in candidate skill text that coexist with small changes in mean held-out performance.

**comment/C1：** 35 pages, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Quantifying Annotation Agreement in Object Detection with Optimal Transport](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19912v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19912

**作者：** Kirill Prokhorov、Alexandr A. Kalinin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19912v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19912v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究用最优传输（OT）度量目标检测标注之间的一致性，不再对框重叠做二值化，而是对匹配与未匹配的框给出分级代价；通过理论分析和真实及合成扰动标注实验，说明 OT 能捕捉一致性的渐变，并区分被阈值指标判为相同的标注对。

**相关性简评：** 其 OT 集合匹配与分级代价思路可能为 latent 状态与视觉实体、视觉证据之间的软对齐和筛选提供可核查的方法线索，但原文目标在数据集验证而非 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantifying Annotation Agreement in Object Detection with Optimal Transport

**abstract/A1：** Reliable validation of machine learning models is a crucial step in designing robust and trustworthy systems. Yet validation relies on annotated test sets treated as ground truth, even though annotations can be noisy or ambiguous. In object detection, sources may disagree on object presence, box location, or class label. Standard agreement metrics usually binarize box overlap before computing a summary score, obscuring the degree and structure of disagreement between annotation sets. Here, we study an Optimal Transport (OT)-based metric for measuring agreement between object detection annotations. Instead of binarizing bounding box overlap, this metric assigns graded costs to matched and unmatched labeled boxes. Through theoretical analysis and experiments on real and synthetically perturbed annotations, we show that OT captures graded changes in agreement and distinguishes annotation pairs that thresholded metrics score identically. Our results suggest that OT provides a richer measure of annotation agreement in object detection, especially for domains with many small or ambiguous objects. Such agreement estimates can support more reliable validation when labels are noisy or conflicting.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [SAM3RL: Memory Control via Reinforcement Learning for Visual Object Tracking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19931v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19931

**作者：** Tomáš Čížek、Matej Straka、Illia Volkov、Matěj Kripner、Ervin Macić、Jiri Matas、Klara Janouskova、Martin Schmid

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19931v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19931v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 SAM 3 跟踪器的记忆库更新改造成序列决策问题，用一个轻量 RL 控制器决定哪些帧进入记忆库，在 12 个跟踪与视频分割基准中的 10 个取得最好结果，且无额外运行时开销。

**相关性简评：** 用强化学习替代手工规则来学习「保留哪些视觉帧作为记忆」的选择策略，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、记忆/缓存更新与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索；但它面向视频目标跟踪，未涉及 latent 推理状态或答案生成，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAM3RL: Memory Control via Reinforcement Learning for Visual Object Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM 3) is a foundation model for image and video segmentation that sets the state of the art in visual object tracking. Its tracker, inherited from SAM 2, stores information from past frames in a memory bank, managed by a simple First-In-First-Out rule, allowing temporal consistency in complex videos. Several recent methods improve SAM 2 with hand-crafted memory update rules, each targeting a specific failure mode, such as distractors, occlusions, or object motion. However, their gains transfer only partially to the stronger SAM 3 backbone. We instead present a more general, data-driven approach that frames memory control as a sequential decision-making task. SAM3RL is an extension of the SAM 3 tracker with a lightweight memory controller, trained by reinforcement learning, that decides which frames enter the memory bank in order to maximize tracking performance. SAM3RL sets a new state of the art on 10 of 12 tracking and video object segmentation benchmarks, outperforming all hand-crafted rules without any runtime overhead. The same approach applied to SAM 2 gives SAM2RL, which outperforms hand-crafted rules on 9 of the 12 benchmarks, showing the generality of our method across model generations.

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [Empirical Boundaries of Reservoir Computing for Few-Shot Sequence Pattern Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19997v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19997

**作者：** Mario Mallea、Angela Nebot、Franscisco Mugica

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19997v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19997v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在少样本 seq2seq 合成模式任务（延迟、静默期、触发标记）上实证分析 Echo State Networks 的分布、结构与优化变体，刻画其相对 SOTA 的性能边界；提出数据依赖的非线性输入注入机制 G-ESN，并在 Deep ESN 上评估跨层共享 readout。

**相关性简评：** 其固定随机循环状态、线性状态区间对记忆任务的关键作用、输入注入的非线性调制与跨层共享 readout，可供核查是否对应 LVR 中循环 latent 状态的稳定性、输入/状态注入方式与多步读出监督；但材料无视觉或多模态内容，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Empirical Boundaries of Reservoir Computing for Few-Shot Sequence Pattern Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Efficient sequence modeling remains a fundamental challenge in machine learning. Despite the success of Transformers, recurrent neural networks (RNNs) have recently re-emerged as efficient alternatives. Particularly, Echo State Networks (ESNs) offer a compelling framework of randomized RNNs that have been extensively studied for time-series forecasting and dynamical systems. This paper presents an empirical analysis of distributional, architectural, and optimization variants of ESNs evaluated on few-shot sequence-to-sequence (seq2seq) pattern tasks. These synthetic tasks utilize discrete symbol sequences, characterized by delays, inactive phases, and triggered markers to isolate and evaluate memory and pattern recognition capabilities. The minimality of these tasks is crucial to understand the strengths and limitations of machine learning models. Furthermore, when the input signal utilizes a one-hot encoding, ESN dynamics are governed strictly by their weight distributions, offering an ideal lens through which to study reservoir computing. Our findings map the performance boundaries of ESNs relative to state-of-the-art (SOTA) models. We show that preserving a linear state regime is vital for memory tasks, such as pattern completion or simple copy. We propose a data-dependent modulation mechanism for nonlinear input injection into the reservoir (G-ESN). G-ESN functions as a statistical filter capable of solving tasks that conventional ESNs cannot (the adding problem and bracket matching). Finally, for Deep ESNs, we evaluate a shared readout mechanism across layers that outperforms conventional concatenated readouts on structural tasks, including selective copy, sorting problem, and bracket matching.

**comment/C1：** 4th International Workshop on Reservoir Computing (RC 2026) 35th International Conference on Artificial Neural Networks (ICANN 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Provable Transformer Context Compression for In-Context Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20004v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20004

**作者：** Yajie Zhao、Yingjun Mo、Zhiyong Wang、Bo Tang、Chao Xu、Fang Kong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20004v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20004v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究固定策略 TD 评估中 transformer 上下文压缩的内在必要性：证明精确复现前 L 步 TD 迭代所需的最小上下文维度等于采样转移诱导的 Krylov 维度，刻画维度不足时最优近似误差的衰减阶，并给出保留内存规模与共享执行器经预训练可学习的理论结果。

**相关性简评：** 属纯 RL 理论、不涉及视觉，但「最小上下文维度—误差」下界与数值秩远小于精确秩的实验观察，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的容量需求、以及上下文压缩导致的信息坍缩提供可供核查的分析线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Provable Transformer Context Compression for In-Context Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Transformers can perform in-context reinforcement learning without parameter updates. However, existing theoretical constructions often require context sizes that grow with the number of sampled transitions, leading to increasing memory costs in large-scale problems. This raises a fundamental scalability question: how much context is intrinsically necessary to carry out such learning? We answer this question for fixed-policy TD evaluation. We show that, with a shared linear decoder, the minimum context dimension required to exactly reproduce the first $L$ TD iterates is the Krylov dimension induced by the sampled transitions. When the available context dimension $m$ falls below this threshold, exact reproduction is no longer possible. In this regime, we characterize the optimal approximation error and show that it decays as $Θ(γ^m\\max\\{1,1/(m(1-γ))\\})$ where $γ$ is the discounting factor. Moving from representation complexity to actual memory cost, we characterize the optimal retained memory for exact continuous representations as $Θ(nk\_L)$ and show experimentally that on a sampled task whose exact rank is full but whose numerical rank is small, a context accurate to $10^{-2}$ is $88.7\\%$ smaller than the sparse sufficient statistics of TD. Finally, we establish the learnability of the shared executor through pretraining by proving global convergence of an auxiliary objective over effective attention parameters under suitable task diversity. Experiments across diverse TD evaluation problems validate parameter recovery and the predicted context--accuracy tradeoff.

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [Trainable at Depth, Solvable at Small Size: What the Loss Surface of a Polynomial Activation Dictates for Designing, Initializing and Training Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20069v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20069

**作者：** Xiuyi Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20069v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20069v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多项式激活 f(z)=z+λz³（截断并投影到单位球）加恒等路径的网络：深层堆叠在 MNIST 上无需归一化即可训练至百层，小规模模型的平方损失可精确求解为多项式系统，用以解释深度堆叠的设计、初始化与训练。

**相关性简评：** 其关于深度可训练性、初始化与损失面几何的分析，可能为深层 latent 迭代推理（loop 式状态更新的稳定性、长度泛化与训练监督设计）提供可供核查的方法线索，但需确认是否可用于多模态场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Trainable at Depth, Solvable at Small Size: What the Loss Surface of a Polynomial Activation Dictates for Designing, Initializing and Training Networks

**abstract/A1：** What makes a deep network train, and what shape is its loss surface? We answer both with one construction at two scales: a polynomial activation f (z) = z + λz 3 , clamped and projected onto the unit sphere, plus an unnormalized identity path. As a deep stack it trains to one hundred layers on MNIST with no normalization machinery; as a small model its square loss is a polynomial system we solve exactly. The small scale explains the large one: it prescribes how to design, initialize and train the stack.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [Rethinking Prediction Horizons: Decoupling Model Queries from State Updates in Few-Step MeanFlow](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20071v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20071

**作者：** Tinglan Wang、Haixu Liu、Shaobo Chen、Nuo Qian、Qi Gao、Kai Wen、Yu Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20071v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20071v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在少步 MeanFlow 采样中把「模型查询的预测区间」与「状态更新时长」在推理期解耦：固定模型、更新网格与查询次数，只改变预测视野，发现其收益取决于查询位置和进入该查询的状态分布，并据此提出免训练的四查询规则以改善 FID，同时指出该视野并非普遍更优。

**相关性简评：** 预测视野与状态更新的解耦、以及规则对进入状态分布的敏感性，可能为 LVR 中循环 latent 状态更新的步长与查询/计算预算分配提供方法线索，需核查其是否可迁移到多模态推理轨迹。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rethinking Prediction Horizons: Decoupling Model Queries from State Updates in Few-Step MeanFlow

**abstract/A1：** MeanFlow predicts interval-average velocities, but conventional few-step sampling ties the interval queried from the model to the duration of the state update. We separate these two quantities at inference, fixing the model, update grid, and number of queries while varying the prediction horizon. Our central finding is that the horizon response depends on both query position and the states entering that query. Across seven independently trained CIFAR-10 checkpoints at classifier-free guidance (CFG) scale 2, a longer horizon worsens FID at early steps but improves it at the midpoint. To probe whether query time alone determines this response, we fix the midpoint query and current/subsequent guidance while changing only earlier CFG. In held-out factorial outcomes on five previously studied checkpoints, the mean midpoint horizon benefit increases from 0.109 to 1.308 FID, indicating sensitivity to the incoming-state distribution. A frozen four-query rule reduces mean CIFAR-10 FID by 2.023 without retraining or extra queries, including gains on five checkpoints trained after rule selection. A milder rule also improves mean FID by 1.041 on a stronger frozen grid, with fixed-rule FID gains on the evaluated public ImageNet-256 and AFHQ checkpoints. However, matched-budget calibration favors query-start shifting in historical CIFAR tests, and the tested Horizon allocation adds no FID benefit beyond Grid+Start on two public ImageNet models. Thus, prediction horizon is a distinct, position- and state-sensitive inference control, but not a universally preferred one.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [Designing Textual Interfaces To Enhance Llm Reasoning On Audio-Visual Question Answering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20085

**作者：** Yi Wang、Tianzi Wang、Tong Lei、Dahan Wang、Peter Bell

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、eess.AS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20085v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在音频-视觉问答任务上比较三种文本接口设计（单一 OmniLLM 直接推理、captioner-reasoner 两段式、主智能体编排子 OmniLLM 的 agentic 架构）对自动提示优化效果的影响，并对受优化影响的案例做错误分析。

**相关性简评：** 论文把多模态推理外化为可被文本优化器操作的接口，其「直接推理 vs 先描述再推理」的对比可能与潜状态中视觉证据是否被保留、caption 旁路导致的信息坍缩有可供核查的联系，但未涉及 latent 状态本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Designing Textual Interfaces To Enhance Llm Reasoning On Audio-Visual Question Answering

**abstract/A1：** Automatic prompt optimization (APO) has shown strong effectiveness for text-based LLMs and LLM systems, but its application to audio-visual understanding systems requires suitable textual interfaces through which multimodal reasoning can be exposed to text-based optimizers. We investigate how the design of textual interfaces affects prompt optimization for multimodal reasoning, on the audio-visual question answering task. We compare three interface designs, including a direct inference system consisting of a single omni-modal large language model (OmniLLM), a captioner-reasoner system, and an agentic architecture in which a primary agent orchestrates subagent OmniLLMs. Experiments with Qwen3-Omni and Gemini show that direct prompt optimization provides model-dependent gains; the captioner-reasoner system and the agentic system expose more interpretable optimization interfaces but are harder to optimize, where error analysis of cases affected by optimization is conducted. Our study highlights textual interface design as an important factor in making prompt optimization effective for omni-modal reasoning systems. Our implementation details are specified in GitHub repo.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ICASSP 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [When Pairwise Preferences Cycle: A Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk for Video GRPO](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20089v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20089

**作者：** Xinrui Lin、Fan Li、Shumin Wang、Dehong Kong、Yanyong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20089v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20089v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文审计视频生成模型 GRPO 中成对偏好比较形成的偏好图，发现循环偏好普遍且持续存在，并提出 Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk：用可靠性引导的偏好游走将成对胜率与循环图中的关系证据结合，做图感知的优势聚合。

**相关性简评：** 研究对象是视频生成而非 VLM 推理，但「循环/不一致的成对偏好会破坏优势估计」及可靠性加权的图感知聚合，可能为 LVR 中多假设或多轨迹间的贡献分配与偏好监督提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Pairwise Preferences Cycle: A Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk for Video GRPO

**abstract/A1：** Pairwise preference feedback has become a widely adopted supervision signal for reinforcement learning in video generation models, as pairwise comparisons provide a more reliable alternative than pointwise scalar ratings for evaluating the quality of generated videos. Specifically, we define a preference graph for each GRPO group under a pairwise evaluator, where nodes represent sampled rollouts and edges encode pairwise preferences. Auditing 6,976 on-policy comparison graphs, we find that cyclic preferences are prevalent and persist throughout training. Naive pairwise reward aggregation based on average win rates discards the underlying comparison structures and treats each rollout independently, which may introduce unreliable advantage estimates for certain rollouts and consequently affect reinforcement learning optimization. Therefore, we propose the \\emph{Reliability-Anchored Preference Walk} (\\ours{}), a graph-aware advantage aggregation method that strengthens the global preference signal from win rates with reliable relational evidence from cyclic preference graphs through a reliability-guided preference walk. Under matched evaluator, comparison protocol, group size, prompt schedule, and query budget, \\ours{} requires no additional evaluator queries beyond the observed comparisons within each rollout group and improves over Pairwise-GRPO across controlled comparisons, transfer settings, and blind human evaluations.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [RTAformer: Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer for Video Scene Graph Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20091v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20091

**作者：** Jaeyeon Heo、Geuntaek Lim、Yukyung Choi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20091v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20091v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RTAformer 单阶段框架用于视频场景图生成，将实体持续性与关系变化分开建模：用相邻帧关系 query 之间的校准差分引导谓词更新，并在训练时通过跨帧实例对齐减轻表示漂移，以缓解「关系语义惯性」。

**相关性简评：** 相邻帧 query 差分驱动状态更新、训练期对齐抑制跨帧表示漂移，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新方式与轨迹监督、以及 latent 相似度受位置/深度影响的问题提供可核查线索；但其任务为视频场景图，需核查差分信号能否迁移到视觉推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RTAformer: Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer for Video Scene Graph Generation

**abstract/A1：** Video scene graph generation (VidSGG) aims to capture objects and their evolving relationships in videos. This requires accounting for both the persistence of object identities and abrupt changes in relationships. Existing methods aggregate information across frames to exploit temporal context, but an emphasis on consistency can obscure these changes. As a result, a model may retain its previous prediction even after the underlying relation has changed. We refer to this failure mode as relational semantic inertia. To address this issue, we propose Relation-Transition-Aware Transformer (RTAformer), a one-stage framework that separately models entity persistence and relation changes. It injects calibrated differences between adjacent-frame relation queries to guide predicate updates, while cross-frame instance alignment during training helps reduce cross-frame representational drift. Experiments on Action Genome show improvements in recall and mean recall over existing methods, with ablation studies supporting the contributions of the proposed components and comparisons using matched backbones showing larger gains on relation-transition frames than on non-transition frames.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [RepForcing: Representation-Space Initialization for Autoregressive Video Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20111v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20111

**作者：** Siming Fu、Zheming Fu、Lianrui Mu、Haoyang Huang、Chaojie Gu、Ruizhe He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20111v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20111v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究把预训练视频扩散模型蒸馏为少步自回归生成器，提出 Representation Forcing：用冻结视频扩散 transformer 的归一化特征做表示空间监督来学习少步因果初始化，以替代中间教师的轨迹回归，提示直接 latent 回归会偏向 posterior mean、细节模糊。

**相关性简评：** 虽属视频生成领域，但其「在冻结模型的表示空间监督、而非回归 latent 轨迹」的做法，可能为 LVR 中避免 latent 状态信息坍缩、设计 latent 轨迹监督与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RepForcing: Representation-Space Initialization for Autoregressive Video Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Distilling pretrained video diffusion models into few-step autoregressive (AR) generators typically combines student initialization with distribution matching distillation (DMD). An intermediate AR teacher can provide causally compatible targets for ODE initialization. However, causally compatible targets alone do not ensure high-quality generation under a short sampling schedule. We analyze the posterior-mean predictions favored by direct latent regression and empirically find that causal ODE initialization alleviates blur but still leaves fine detail unresolved. To address this limitation, we propose Representation Forcing, which learns few-step causal initialization through representation-space supervision rather than intermediate-teacher trajectory regression. The student is trained with teacher forcing by comparing clean predictions and training targets through normalized features of a frozen video diffusion transformer. This phase reuses the synthetic videos generated by a bidirectional model for AR teacher training and replaces both teacher training and causal ODE distillation, without generating additional causal ODE trajectories. The subsequent asymmetric DMD procedure remains unchanged. Empirical results show that, at four sampling steps, RepForcing better preserves generation quality than intermediate-teacher-based initialization before DMD. After DMD, our four-step chunk-wise model improves VBench Total from 84.04 to 84.66 (+0.62 points) and VisionReward from 6.326 to 10.074 (+59.25%) over the reported causal-ODE-based baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [DreamHMR: From Video Generation to Human Motion Modeling via Structural Focusing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20124v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20124

**作者：** Zhengjie Xu、Ruihao Gong、Xianglong Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20124v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20124v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DreamHMR，把视频生成预训练先验迁移到人体网格恢复：先以「预测完整人体渲染」迫使视频骨干学习表面对应与姿态几何，再用可靠局部证据细化身体与手部细节，在多个数据集与遮挡场景下评估。

**相关性简评：** 其方法线索是把生成式视频表征对齐到显式几何，并在遮挡下筛选「可靠局部证据」以抑制背景干扰，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与内部状态是否保留任务必要几何细节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DreamHMR: From Video Generation to Human Motion Modeling via Structural Focusing

**abstract/A1：** Human mesh recovery (HMR) provides a geometric foundation for understanding human motion, but accurately reconstructing both body motion and fine hand articulation from videos remains challenging, especially in real-world scenes where occlusion and partial visibility are common. Video generation pretraining offers rich spatial and temporal priors for this task, but its focus on visual synthesis leaves a gap between these representations and explicit human geometry. We present DreamHMR, a general framework that adapts video generation priors for complete body-and-hand reconstruction. It follows a global-to-local strategy, first grounding video representations in human geometry and then refining body and hand detail with reliable local evidence. Global Body Representation Transfer trains the video backbone to predict complete human renderings, turning its native prediction task into a means of learning surface correspondence and posed geometry. Local Joint Feature Enhancement combines contextual estimates with reliable local evidence to recover fine body and hand detail, while limiting interference from occluders and background. Experiments on five datasets with four video backbones demonstrate strong reconstruction performance across backbone families. Under a unified evaluation protocol, our best model outperforms the best existing methods on all reported metrics of 3DPW, EMDB-1, and RICH, reducing body MPJPE by 4.2%, 4.7%, and 6.1%, respectively. In more challenging occlusion settings on held-out BEDLAM and EgoBody sequences, DreamHMR reduces overall body and hand MPJPE by 7.0% and 10.0%, respectively. Our code will be made publicly available.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 7 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [StreamErase: Towards Ultra-Fast Streaming Video Erasing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20137v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20137

**作者：** Zhuobai Dong、Xiaobing Tu、Xiantao Zhang、Jinkui Ren、Yinggui Wang、Yue Ma、Linfeng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20137v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20137v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** StreamErase 面向流式视频物体擦除，把双向视频生成模型的擦除能力蒸馏为单向流式擦除器，用滑动窗口因果注意力、attention sink 与 KV cache rolling 训练实现固定窗口下的任意长视频生成，并建立 LongErase 基准。

**相关性简评：** 固定上下文窗口下的 KV cache 滚动、attention sink 与自 rollout 训练，可能为 LVR 的长程 latent 长度泛化、误差累积及缓存/注意力结构影响提供可核查的方法线索，但视频擦除领域向 LVR 的迁移需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** StreamErase: Towards Ultra-Fast Streaming Video Erasing

**abstract/A1：** Current video object removal methods suffer from prohibitive latency (minutes per video) and non-causal processing that prevents real-time interaction. We present StreamErase, enabling sub-second latency with up to 26 FPS streaming generation on a single GPU. Our approach first augments a text-to-video model with mask input to generate high-quality videos that erase target objects and synthesize coherent backgrounds, but does not perform inference on the fly. As such, we progressively transfer the erasing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming eraser, enabling long-horizon erasing without sacrificing visual fidelity. Several key challenges arise when generating videos over long, potentially infinite horizons: (1) bridging the domain gap from training on finite length and extrapolating to long horizons, (2) sustaining high quality by preventing error accumulation, and (3) maintaining fast inference, without increasing computational cost as the context grows. To address these challenges, we carefully designed sliding-window causal attention with attention sinks. By incorporating self-rollout with attention sinks and KV cache rolling during training, we simulate inference-time extrapolations with a fixed context window, enabling constant-speed generation of arbitrarily long videos. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark, LongErase, for streaming long-video object removal. Our model achieves strong performance compared to existing video object erasing models and generalizes well to real-world video scenarios, uniquely enabling infinite-length streaming. With StreamErase, users can input a mask and see the erasing results unfold in real-time, delivering a truly interactive experience.

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [OneLA: Learning Many Worlds from Latent Actions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20160v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20160

**作者：** Ziyu Zhu、Zhaoxu Sun、Fan Jiang、Chiyu Wang、Junfeng Ni、Mu Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20160v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20160v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OneLA：无需动作标注，通过共享正交字典与统一预测目标从多域视频中学习连续 latent action，作为世界模型的共享条件接口，并以当前视觉状态为条件预测可控 rollout 的转移。

**相关性简评：** 其以预测目标在 latent 空间分离可迁移的转移结构与域内视觉线索的做法，可能与 LVR 关注的内部状态表示、视觉信息坍缩及监督信号设计相关，可供核查；但属视频世界模型方向，与 VLM 推理的关联需进一步确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>
