**title/T1：** OneLA: Learning Many Worlds from Latent Actions

**abstract/A1：** Learning world models across diverse domains calls for a unified control space, yet ac- tion interfaces vary across domains and action annotations are often scarce. Latent ac- tions learned directly from videos offer a promising way to establish such a space without explicit action supervision. However, learning shared latent actions across domains re- mains challenging, as variations in appearance, embodiment, and dynamics can entangle domain-specific visual cues with transferable transition structure. To this end, we intro- duce OneLA, a simple framework that learns continuous latent actions through a shared orthogonal dictionary and a common predictive objective, without task-specific designs or cross-domain alignment losses during latent action pretraining. These latent actions pro- vide a shared conditioning interface for world models, with lightweight adapters mapping native control commands into the same space. Conditioned on latent actions and the cur- rent visual state, the world model predicts context-dependent transitions for controllable rollout. OneLA improves action readout across domains and reduces action cycle-transfer rotation error by 24.8% over the strongest evaluated baseline. A joint-training comparison further shows a 20.5% improvement in Human Preference Score v3 (HPSv3) for real- scene generation. These results suggest a path toward general-purpose world models that learn from diverse videos and support flexible control through latent action space.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [RM-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Generative Reward Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20174v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20174

**作者：** Qinglin Su、Licheng Pan、Zhen Fang、Jiakang Wang、Qingnan Ren、Yunsheng Lu、Yan Gao、Zhixuan Chu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20174v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20174v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RM-OPD 两阶段框架：先用单域 GRPO 微调出领域专家教师，再通过路由式 on-policy distillation、域贡献重加权和可选的准则上下文蒸馏，将多个专家整合为统一的学生生成式奖励模型，在多个 RM 基准上优于 Mix-RL 等基线。

**相关性简评：** 其路由式 on-policy 蒸馏在学生自生成输出上给出稠密、带符号的 token 级监督并按域重加权，可能为 LVR 的轨迹训练、监督信号设计与能力失衡/贡献分配问题提供方法线索，可供核查能否迁移到推理状态的稠密监督。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RM-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Generative Reward Models

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement Learning on Mixed-domain preference data (Mix-RL) has become the mainstream approach for training general-purpose Generative Reward Models (GRMs). However, Mix-RL suffers from two critical bottlenecks: the reward sparsity induced by outcome rewards, and the cross-domain interference arising from jointly optimizing heterogeneous capabilities, which together give rise to imbalanced capability acquisition and pervasive reward hacking. Inspired by the success of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) in multi-domain capability integration, we introduce RM-OPD (Reward Modeling via On-Policy Distillation), a two-stage framework that separates specialist acquisition from capability integration. First, we cultivate domain-specialized teacher models via single-domain GRPO fine-tuning, allowing each expert to reach its performance ceiling in isolation; then we consolidate these teachers into one student model through (i) routed on-policy distillation, which provides dense signed token-level supervision on student-generated critiques; (ii) domain contribution reweighting, which reweights token guidance toward target domain shares, preventing specific domains from dominating optimization; and (iii) optional criteria context distillation, which conditions teachers on domain-specific evaluation criteria to sharpen token-level supervision. Across multiple RM benchmarks and two base models, RM-OPD consistently outperforms vanilla Mix-RL and other strong baselines, achieving overall superior performance and exhibiting an emergent "teacher-surpassing" effect. These results establish RM-OPD as a promising paradigm for building generalist GRMs.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 4 figures; includes references and appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [HEM-Med: A Hierarchical Agentic Memory Framework for Safe Multi-Center Medication Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20191v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20191

**作者：** Min Xiao、Jin Li、Shoujin Wang、Yeguang Zhen、Yishuo Li、Wenpeng Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20191v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20191v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向多中心药物推荐，提出分层 agentic 记忆框架 HEM-Med：用解耦的层级记忆把跨医院可迁移经验与医院特有处方模式分开，并配合安全感知的集合解码，在真实多中心 eICU 数据上兼顾推荐准确性与用药安全。

**相关性简评：** 其记忆将共享经验与个体残差分开存储、检索并参与解码，可能为 LVR 中区分任务通用与实例特有的 latent 内容、以及带约束的 latent 状态生成提供线索，可供核查记忆读写是否真正影响输出。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HEM-Med: A Hierarchical Agentic Memory Framework for Safe Multi-Center Medication Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Medication recommendation (MR) is critical for clinical decision support. While existing MR methods are mostly designed for single-center learning, where only one independent hospital is involved, multi-center MR with hospitals of different scales has become increasingly demanding in practice. In multi center settings, abundant data from large medical centers can improve recommendation performance for low-resource centers. Despite a few preliminary attempts on this task, two critical gaps remain. Gap 1: Existing multi-center methods fail to disentangle transferable cross-hospital clinical experience from hospital-specific prescribing patterns, limiting clinical feasibility and physician acceptance. Gap 2: They commonly overlook multi label safety constraints of MR, including drug-drug interaction risks and local formulary feasibility, which are essential for safe real-world deployment. To address these gaps, we propose HEM-Med, a novel hierarchical agentic memory framework for multi-center MR. To address Gap 1, we design a disentangled hierarchical memory module that builds patient-level medication context attribution records and disentangles them into cross hospital experience memory and hospital-specific residual memory. To address Gap 2, we introduce a safety-aware medication set generation module, which integrates dual-level experience retrieval with safety-aware set decoding to produce clinically relevant, safer, and locally adaptive medication recommendations. Experiments on a real-world multi-center eICU dataset show that HEM-Med outperforms representative transfer learning, LLM-based, and MR baselines in terms of both recommendation accuracy and safety.

**comment/C1：** BIBM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Continual pretraining Prostate MRI ViT Encoder for task alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20217v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20217

**作者：** Bilal A. Sidiqi、Yipei Wang、Shaheer U. Saeed、Shiqi Huang、Daniel C. Alexander、Yipeng Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20217v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20217v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在公开预训练 ViT 编码器上用 LoRA 在前列腺分割任务上做 continual pretraining 以提升与下游任务的对齐，并用 CKA 逐层比较表示相似度与任务表现的关系，报告 6 项下游任务中 3 项提升、其余持平。

**相关性简评：** 其逐层 CKA 表示偏移分析可能可供核查，用于刻画 LVR 中 latent 状态相似度随网络深度的变化及其与推理收益的关联；但本文面向医学分割编码器适配，不涉及多模态推理状态生成或证据使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continual pretraining Prostate MRI ViT Encoder for task alignment

**abstract/A1：** Building foundation models for MRI has emerged as a promising direction for advancing medical image analysis and improving accessibility of task specific models. Existing approaches primarily focus on effective pretraining strategies and evaluating their performance across diverse downstream tasks. While these methods show performance gains, limited attention has been given to understanding task alignment between pretraining and downstream objectives. Unlike language models, where the two stages are naturally aligned, such alignment is less evident in vision based applications. Motivated by this gap, we explore continual pretraining. Specifically, continually pretraining a pretrained ViT encoder using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on a prostate segmentation task to enhance task alignment and downstream performance. We further perform layer-wise representational analysis using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) to assess representational shifts and their correlation with task outcomes. Our experiments show improved performance in 3 of 6 downstream tasks and comparable results in the others. Our Feature analysis reveals that, for segmentation tasks, continually pretrained encoder features exhibit greater similarity to those of the finetuned encoder than the original pretrained encoder, correlating with observed performance gains.

**comment/C1：** ISBI Conference 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [MAEM: Mitigating Recency Bias via a Replay-Free, Capacity-Controlled Dynamic Mixture of Experts for Class-Incremental Graph Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20238v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20238

**作者：** Junlin Chen、Wenhao Wang、Junchao Xu、Qingsong Yao、Fei Xue、Li Sun、Xiao Wang、Ruijie Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20238v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20238v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MAEM：面向类别增量图学习，用 SGC 表示、每会话训练并冻结一个专家；测试时无需任务 ID，靠掩码自编码器的重建误差把节点分配给专家以缓解近因偏差，专家池满时用 Git Re-Basin 对齐合并少用专家并借伪数据蒸馏为融合专家，全程不回放历史图数据。

**相关性简评：** 其「以生成式重建误差在推理时无监督地选择专家」与容量受限下合并、蒸馏冗余专家的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 假设／计算分支的选择与状态复用提供方法线索，但领域为图增量学习，可迁移性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAEM: Mitigating Recency Bias via a Replay-Free, Capacity-Controlled Dynamic Mixture of Experts for Class-Incremental Graph Learning

**abstract/A1：** Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) excel on graph-structured data but suffer catastrophic forgetting when classes arrive sequentially. In class-incremental graph learning, predictions are often dominated by recently learned classes, causing severe recency bias. Dynamic expansion can isolate knowledge across sessions, but many methods assume task-incremental settings and require task identities at test time to select branches or heads---information unavailable in class-incremental learning. Learnable routers offer an alternative, yet continual fine-tuning the router on new data can bias routing toward recent components. We propose MAEM, a replay-free, capacity-controlled dynamic mixture-of-experts framework for class-incremental graph learning. MAEM builds representations with Simplified Graph Convolution (SGC) and trains a dedicated expert for each new session, then freezes it to protect prior knowledge. For routing without task IDs or a shared trainable router, each expert uses a masked autoencoder (MAE); test nodes are assigned to the expert with the lowest reconstruction error, reducing routing-level recency bias. When the expert pool reaches a fixed capacity, MAEM merges the least-used experts via Git Re-Basin alignment and further distills them into a fused expert using topology-aware pseudo data synthesized from MAE distributions (DeepInversion-style), without storing or revisiting historical graph data. On four class-incremental graph benchmarks, MAEM mitigates recency bias, outperforms strong continual graph learning baselines, and keeps model capacity bounded under strict replay-free learning.

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [PAWA: Panoramic Action-conditioned World Modeling for Multiple Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20240v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20240

**作者：** Siqi Yang、Yimu Wang、Ziqi Cai、Chengxuan Zhu、Shuchen Weng、Erwin Wu、Zhaofei Yu、Kaipeng Zhang、Boxin Shi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20240v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20240v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PAWA：面向多玩家 Minecraft 的动作条件全景世界建模数据集与框架，把每个玩家的 POV 视频与世界稳定化的全景视频及动作对齐，并训练双塔模型经跨注意力联合预测两路视频。

**相关性简评：** 其「以全景视频作为世界状态中间表示」以及用跨注意力对齐共享世界坐标系下同方向 token 的设计，可能为 LVR 中跨视角视觉状态保持与视觉证据筛选提供可核查线索，但面向视频帧生成而非 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PAWA: Panoramic Action-conditioned World Modeling for Multiple Agents

**abstract/A1：** A multiplayer world model must predict how the independent actions of several players change a shared environment, including changes outside a player's current field of view. A point-of-view (POV) video captures such changes only after they enter its field of view, whereas panoramic video records the surroundings in every viewing direction and has served as an intermediate representation of the world state in recent world models. Learning this task therefore requires synchronized POV and panoramic videos of interacting players together with their actions, yet no existing dataset provides this combination in Minecraft, an established testbed in which players act independently in a shared, modifiable 3D world. We present PAWA, a dataset and framework for action-conditioned panoramic multiplayer world modeling. The dataset pairs each player's POV video with world-stabilized panoramic video and aligned actions. Its distributed pipeline decouples CPU gameplay capture from GPU rendering, scales beyond 64 simultaneous players, and re-renders each recorded session under new observation configurations without replaying the gameplay. On this dataset, we develop a dual-tower model that jointly predicts POV and panoramic video and couples the two streams through cross-attention that aligns tokens observing the same direction in a shared world frame. We further introduce a quadratic-phase player encoding that provides many more player codes than existing rotary codes at the same embedding dimension. Experiments show that PAWA outperforms state-of-the-art multiplayer world models in POV prediction quality while also producing high-fidelity panoramic predictions. The dataset and collection pipeline are open source to support further research on multiplayer world models.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages. Project page: https://pawa.siki.moe

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [Contrastive uncertainty learning for robust multi-structure segmentation in lower-pelvic MR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20251v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20251

**作者：** Bilal Sidiqi、Yipei Wang、Wen Yan、Shaheer U. Saeed、Daniel C. Alexander、Yipeng Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20251v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20251v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Contrastive Uncertainty Learning：用 Monte Carlo Dropout 的体素级预测不确定性直接指导表示学习，把低不确定且预测正确的特征聚成稳定体素、把高不确定或错分特征推开，在 589 例低盆腔 MRI 八结构分割上主要改善表面距离而非 Dice。

**相关性简评：** 以不确定性标签塑造嵌入几何、防止困难边界处特征坍缩的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的可信度监督、视觉证据筛选提供可核查的方法线索；但原文任务为医学分割而非多模态推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Contrastive uncertainty learning for robust multi-structure segmentation in lower-pelvic MR

**abstract/A1：** Boundary delineation of anatomical structures in prostate MRI is often compromised by regional ambiguity. Automated segmentation methods relying on global overlap loss functions tend to be insensitive to surface deviations, leading to significant errors in ambiguous regions. Most existing uncertainty-aware methods treat uncertainty as a post-hoc quality indicator or prediction filter. We propose Contrastive Uncertainty Learning (CUL), a supervised framework that uses predictive uncertainty to directly guide representation learning. Using Monte Carlo Dropout, CUL identifies voxel-wise uncertainty and structures the embedding geometry accordingly: low-uncertainty, correctly predicted features form stable voxels while high-uncertainty or misclassified features are actively repelled, preventing feature collapse at difficult boundaries. We evaluate CUL on a 589-scan 3D prostate MRI multi-structure segmentation task (8 anatomical structures) using a ViT with UperNet decoder architecture. Average Dice ($83.38\\%$) remains comparable to the baseline ($83.35\\%$), while surface distance decreases significantly ($p&lt;0.001$) across 6 of 8 anatomical structures. A hard-case analysis reveals a $22{\\times}$ asymmetry between significantly improved and degraded cases ($18.8\\%$ vs $0.9\\%$). Ablation studies show that removing uncertainty-guided sample selection eliminates improvements for the transition zone, while replacing the contrastive objective with a boundary loss additionally removes bladder improvements, demonstrating that both uncertainty-guided sampling and the contrastive objective are essential for the observed gains. These findings demonstrate that the proposed uncertainty-guided representation learning is a useful mechanism for reducing the risk of extreme errors in safety-critical medical segmentation

**comment/C1：** UNSURE MICCAI 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [Long-Horizon RL Needs Muon](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20256v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20256

**作者：** Kai Ruan、Jinghao Lin、Tao Chen、Ziqi Zhou、Qianshan Wei、Zihe Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20256v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20256v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究长时程智能体的强化学习训练配方，将 Muon 优化器与 token 平均损失、截断重要性采样、长度惩罚和工具错误惩罚组合，采用单 rollout 异步采样在仓库级 issue 解决任务上考察优化器与各组件的作用。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本 agent RL，与 LVR 主线无直接交集；但其 token 平均损失与长度惩罚涉及变长轨迹的信用分配与长度泛化，可能为推理长度收益/成本权衡与轨迹监督提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Long-Horizon RL Needs Muon

**abstract/A1：** Long-horizon agents must coordinate reasoning, tool use, and environment feedback over extended interactions. Learning from sparse outcome rewards requires weighting trajectories of different lengths and updating the policy while rollouts are still being collected. We examine a single-rollout asynchronous recipe combining Muon, token-averaged loss, truncated importance sampling, and auxiliary length and tool-error penalties. Single-rollout sampling allows asynchronous learning without waiting for multiple trajectories for the same issue. We study optimizer choices and recipe components for repository-level issue resolution across two model families. The evaluated training runs show higher training success with the full recipe than with the baselines.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 5 figures. Work in progress

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [Harness Policy Distillation: Aligning Agent Policies with Runtime Semantics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20261v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20261

**作者：** Junze Zhu、Weihao Chen、Zhiyang Zhou、Zhen Wu、Xinyu Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20261v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20261v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Harness Policy Distillation（HPD）：从参考轨迹中还原运行时 harness 的控制语义为「控制文档」特权信息，由教师生成经 harness 校验的可执行动作，并与原生历史联合训练，使部署时无需额外输入即可遵循运行时契约。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但其「特权状态在每个决策边界作为密集监督、再内化进策略、部署时不携带」的训练范式，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态监督与贡献分配提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Harness Policy Distillation: Aligning Agent Policies with Runtime Semantics

**abstract/A1：** Deploying a general-purpose model as an agent means running it inside a product-specific runtime harness that decides which actions are executable, how state advances, and when the loop may terminate. Ordinary trajectory supervision shows which action was taken but leaves the runtime semantics behind that action implicit. We introduce Harness Policy Distillation (HPD), which makes these runtime control semantics an explicit training target alongside actions to align the harness policy. HPD replays reference trajectories through the harness to recover the runtime semantics that are implicit in ordinary trajectory data, and represents them as control documents at each model-call boundary. These documents provide privileged information for a teacher to generate actions, while harness verification ensures that the resulting targets are executable under the runtime contract. We jointly train a shared model on the runtime semantics recovered from control-state privileged information and native history, thereby internalizing these semantics into the model policy. At deployment, the trained model uses the original harness interface without requiring the control document or any additional supplement. On TRAJECT-Bench with the Pi harness, Qwen3-8B trained with HPD reaches 22.98% autonomous task success and 49.23% runtime compliance, against 3.09% and 19.90% for the unadapted model. Harness-verified teacher actions substantially improve the native deployment policy, while joint control-state supervision provides further gains in task completion and runtime compliance. HPD turns executable runtime semantics into dense supervision for learning harness-compatible actions at each decision boundary.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 9 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-150"></a>

### 150. [Beyond Static Cues: Unified Dynamic Detection of MLLM Backdoors via Dual-Flow Trajectory Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20319v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20319

**作者：** Yinzhen Huang、Jiahe Xie、Yitong Sun、Ruochen Zhang、Xingxing Wei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20319v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20319v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 EVO-Detect，把 MLLM 后门检测从静态快照转向解码轨迹的动态演化，通过 Attention Flow（图文注意力分配随生成步的变化）与 Logits Flow（用模态干预消融衡量输出分布敏感度）两条通道刻画早期解码行为，并归一化后区分自然扰动与后门触发。

**相关性简评：** 其按解码步追踪图文注意力分配、并用模态消融测 logits 敏感度的做法，可能为诊断 VLM 推理是否真正使用视觉证据（视觉旁路、模态贡献坍缩）提供可借鉴的探测手段，尚需核查其指标能否迁移到 latent 推理场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Static Cues: Unified Dynamic Detection of MLLM Backdoors via Dual-Flow Trajectory Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical domains, yet their susceptibility to backdoor attacks remains a formidable challenge. While existing defenses have evolved from visual-centric patch cues to unsupervised internal attention statistics, they are fundamentally constrained by a reliance on static snapshots and attack-specific heuristics. Given the intricate interplay between dual modalities, the vast design space for multimodal triggers allows diverse threats, such as textual injections, sub-perceptual noises, and coordinated cross-modal triggers, to easily circumvent such rigid priors, particularly when attack types are unknown in real-world applications. In this paper, we rethink backdoor detection by shifting the diagnostic paradigm from static observations to dynamic output evolution. We observe that a successful attack inevitably hijacks the model's decoding trajectory, skewing internal modal contributions during generation while maintaining an abnormal output invariance despite input perturbations. To capture this behavior, we propose EVO-Detect, a unified detection framework that monitors the dynamic evolution of internal signals during early decoding steps through two complementary channels: Attention Flow and Logits Flow. The attention branch measures how image-text attention allocation evolves during early generation, whereas the logits branch quantifies the sensitivity of the output distribution to modal perturbations through interventional ablations. We then convert these trajectories into compact sample-level metrics and normalize them against negative-control signatures so that natural perturbations are less likely to be mistaken for backdoor evidence. Extensive evaluations show that our approach substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines such as BYE and TCAP across diverse attack families.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ACM MM 2026 (Oral). Code: https://github.com/kaoru0822-kitauji/EVO\_Detect

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [Argument Graph Uncertainty: Quantifying Uncertainty from the Logical Structure of Reasoning Chains](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20328v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20328

**作者：** Simon Baur、Sumire Honda、Wojciech Samek、Jackie Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20328v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20328v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Argument Graph Uncertainty：用轻量模型或规则把单条思维链切分为命题单元，再用 NLI 推断关系边构建两类论证图，并将边分数聚合为答案选项上的 Dirichlet 后验，从推理链逻辑结构直接估计置信度，在 GPQA、MMLU-Pro、TruthfulQA 上优于 token 级与一致性基线。

**相关性简评：** 该文为纯文本、无视觉输入与 latent 状态，不属主线；但其免额外调用、带逐节点不确定性归因的低成本置信度估计，可能为 LVR 中的停止决策（STOP）与答案可靠性判定提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Argument Graph Uncertainty: Quantifying Uncertainty from the Logical Structure of Reasoning Chains

**abstract/A1：** Reliable uncertainty estimation is essential for deploying large language models in high-stakes reasoning tasks, yet most existing approaches rely on expensive sampling strategies or shallow token-level signals that ignore the internal logical structure of model outputs. We introduce Argument Graph Uncertainty, a post-hoc framework for multiple choice question tasks, that estimates model confidence directly from the logical structure of a single chain-of-thought reasoning trace. Our method uses either a lightweight instruction-tuned model or a simple deterministic rule to segment the reasoning chain into propositional units, then applies a NLI model to infer relational edges and construct two complementary argument graphs over these segments. The resulting edge scores are aggregated into a Dirichlet posterior over answer options, whose concentration yields calibrated uncertainty estimates reflecting both local step-to-step coherence and global logical consistency across the reasoning trace. Evaluated on GPQA, MMLU-Pro and TruthfulQA, our method consistently surpasses token-based and consistency-based uncertainty baselines across multiple reasoning models. We further demonstrate through extensive combination analyses that argument graph uncertainty captures an orthogonal signal to existing approaches, making it a complementary component in uncertainty ensembles. Crucially, our framework requires no additional calls to the evaluated model and relies entirely on small, efficient auxiliary models, producing fully interpretable, per-node uncertainty attributions at low computational cost.

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [Read, Parse, Describe: Unified Document Parsing with Visual Element Description Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20335v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20335

**作者：** Yuheng Chen、Yufan Chen、Zhuojun Cai、Ruiping Liu、Junwei Zheng、Jiale Wei、Di Wen、Weijia Fan、Kunyu Peng、Jiaming Zhang、Rainer Stiefelhagen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20335v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20335v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 Read-Parse-Describe 统一文档解析基准（2,402 篇文档、18,163 页），要求模型对页面图像输出包含边界框、语义标签、阅读顺序、文本内容与视觉元素替代描述的有序元素列表，并提出复用多层视觉特征、向视觉-语言 token 注入网格空间引导的即插即用空间感知增强器。

**相关性简评：** 其空间感知增强器复用多层视觉特征并向 VLM token 注入网格空间引导，可能为 LVR 中如何在内部状态保留几何与任务必要视觉细节提供方法线索，可供核查；但论文主线是文档解析基准与任务性能，未涉及推理期 latent 状态或动态计算。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Read, Parse, Describe: Unified Document Parsing with Visual Element Description Generation

**abstract/A1：** Document understanding systems are typically evaluated as separate components via layout analysis, optical text recognition, reading-order prediction, and image captioning. This separation leaves scientific figures, captions, and visual semantics weakly connected, and fails to measure whether a model can produce a single page-level parse that is both structurally faithful and semantically accessible. We introduce Read-Parse-Describe, a unified dataset for document parsing with visual element description generation. Given a page image, models are required to output an ordered list of layout elements containing bounding boxes, semantic labels, reading-order indices, recognized textual content, and alternative text descriptions for visual elements. The benchmark is built from 2,402 documents, comprising 18,163 continuous pages, 125,218 layout elements, and 11,338 visual instances. Besides, we further propose a plug-and-play space-awareness enhancer that reuses multi-layer visual features and injects grid-aware spatial guidance into vision-language tokens for layout-aware generation. Experiments across end-to-end VLMs and pipeline parsers show that existing systems still struggle to jointly recover reading order and visual semantics, while our enhanced model achieves strong joint performance. These results highlight the need to move beyond isolated document parsing modules toward unified models that jointly read, parse, and describe complex documents. The new dataset and code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/yufanchen96/RPD

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Training Dynamics of Transformers on Unbounded Inputs: Attention Learning Can Lag Output Layer Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20337v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20337

**作者：** Yaomengxi Han、Debarghya Ghoshdastidar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20337v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20337v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究浅层 transformer 在无界长度输入上的训练动力学：证明识别某 star-free 语言无法靠单步注意力更新加微调完成，而需「先更新输出层、再更新注意力、最后微调输出层」的三阶段梯度下降，并在语言识别、回归和 in-context learning 任务上给出经验支持。

**相关性简评：** 其「注意力学习滞后于输出层」的分阶段训练结论，可能为 LVR 中注意力/隐状态与答案读出层的训练顺序、轨迹监督与贡献分配提供可核查线索；但该工作是纯文本理论，未涉及视觉证据筛选，迁移需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training Dynamics of Transformers on Unbounded Inputs: Attention Learning Can Lag Output Layer Learning

**abstract/A1：** The success of transformers is attributed to several factors, including their capacity to express formal languages over variable-length inputs, and ability of the attention mechanism to focus on relevant input features. However, there is limited understanding of the training dynamics of shallow transformers, specifically how to decouple the learning of the attention and the output (feedforward) layer. A natural extension of the theory of one-step gradient feature learning in feedforward networks suggests that one could first learn the attention weights with a single gradient step, and then train the output layer. We show that this fails for a shallow transformer trained to recognize a star-free language from unbounded-length input. We prove that the transformer can be trained to recognize the language with a three-phase gradient descent: the output layer is updated first, followed by an attention update, and the output layer is fine-tuned in the third phase. In contrast, the language cannot be learned by training only the attention or output layer, or by a single attention-layer update followed by finetuning the output layer. We empirically show that \`\`attention learning lagging output layer learning" is beneficial in other language recognition, regression, and in-context learning tasks as well.

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [Non-asymptotic Convergence of Average-reward Q-learning with Options](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20347v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20347

**作者：** Kintan Saha、Ahana Deb、Anders Jonsson、Debabrota Basu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20347v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20347v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 average-reward 设定下带 options 的 Q-learning 的有限样本收敛性，证明 inter-/intra-option Bellman 算子在 unichain 假设下满足多步 span 收缩，并提出两种同步估计的 option Q-learning 变体及收敛速率。

**相关性简评：** options 的时序扩展动作与终止函数、多步 Bellman 算子的 span 收缩及随机逼近收敛分析，可能为 latent 循环计算中步数分配与 STOP/终止决策的收敛性刻画提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Non-asymptotic Convergence of Average-reward Q-learning with Options

**abstract/A1：** We study the finite-sample convergence of Q-learning algorithms for the average-reward formulation of Reinforcement Learning (RL) with options. While options allow temporarily extended and hierarchical actions, the average-reward formulation incorporates continual tasks beyond discounted and episodic RL. We establish the first $\\tilde{O}(1/\\text{iterations})$ convergence guarantees, in the mean-square sense, for average-reward option Q-learning algorithms in both the inter- and intra-option settings. First, we prove that both inter- and intra-option Bellman operators satisfy multi-step span contractions under unichain assumptions. Then, we propose synchronous estimators of these multi-step option Bellman operators leading to two variants of option Q-learning. Finally, we extend the stochastic approximation framework to prove convergence rates of these two algorithms under the minimal assumptions used in the literature.

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [NeuroDETR: Multiscale Discrete Spatiotemporal Tokenization for Asynchronous EEG Event Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20349v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20349

**作者：** Yaozhi Wen、Kun Zhao、Wei Wei、Haoyang Chen、Kangning Wang、Yi Sun、Shaowei Gu、Dingkun Liu、Shuang Qiu、Huiguang He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20349v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20349v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NeuroDETR，用多尺度时间码本与 Spatial-Pyramid VQ 对连续 EEG 做离散时空 token 化，并以事件感知稀疏注意力 FOCAL 精炼特征，联合分类事件并回归其起止点。

**相关性简评：** 其离散码本式状态压缩、事件存在性引导的稀疏注意力与起止点回归，可能为 LVR 中 latent 视觉 token 生成、证据筛选与停止/边界决策提供可核查的方法线索，但属远领域迁移，需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NeuroDETR: Multiscale Discrete Spatiotemporal Tokenization for Asynchronous EEG Event Detection

**abstract/A1：** Asynchronous EEG event detection supports practical brain-computer interfaces by identifying event classes and temporal boundaries directly from continuous recordings without predefined event boundaries at inference time. The primary challenge lies in extracting event-related patterns embedded in ongoing background activity. To address this, we propose \\textbf{NeuroDETR}, a discrete spatiotemporal multiscale framework that combines structured tokenization with event-aware feature refinement. Multiscale temporal codebooks capture brief responses and sustained dynamics, while Spatial-Pyramid VQ hierarchically encodes spatial structure across EEG channels. These complementary representations form a compact event memory for jointly classifying events and estimating their onsets and offsets. Before tokenization, we apply FOCAL, an event-aware sparse attention module that uses predicted event presence to guide temporal feature refinement and spatial aggregation, emphasizing likely event periods while preserving context across the recording. Experiments on eight EEG datasets that span diverse paradigms demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. These findings provide a basis for the development of BCI systems that recognize meaningful neural activity during continuous interaction and monitoring.

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [ReAl: Refine neural operators via aligning dynamical flow](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20369

**作者：** Jing Xu、Zenglin Xu、Li Yi、Ruoxi Jiang、Yuan Qi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20369v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReAl：在预训练 PDE 代理模型上以条件 flow matching 学习确定性基预测周围的残差分布以恢复高频结构，并用同输入多条轨迹的轨迹级奖励对齐，缓解自回归展开中的误差累积，提升保真度与长时稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 「残差生成式细化 + 轨迹级奖励对齐」或可为 LVR 的 latent 状态细化与长程轨迹监督、误差累积抑制提供方法线索，可供核查；但论文属科学计算 PDE 代理，无视觉或多模态推理设定，联系较远、迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReAl: Refine neural operators via aligning dynamical flow

**abstract/A1：** Neural partial differential equation (PDE) surrogates trained with mean squared error (MSE) can capture dominant dynamics but often lose high-frequency information and fine-scale structures. Generative models (e.g., Flow Matching) can capture the distribution of fine-scale details, yet stochastic errors may accumulate during autoregressive rollouts and compromise long-term stability. To address these limitations, we introduce Refine and Align(ReAl), a model-agnostic plug-in that augments pretrained PDE surrogates to improve the reconstruction of high-frequency structures associated with complex physical phenomena. In the refinement stage, a conditional flow-matching model learns the residual distribution around the deterministic base prediction, recovering missing fine-scale structures while retaining the dominant dynamics. In the alignment stage, to mitigate the generative discrepancies amplified in the autoregressive rollout, particularly in chaotic systems, we generate multiple trajectories from the same input and reinforce the refiner with trajectory-level rewards, thereby achieving long-horizon dynamical alignment. Experiments across diverse surrogate architectures and PDE tasks demonstrate that ReAl consistently improves high-frequency fidelity, forecasting accuracy, and long-horizon stability.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [From Relevance to Validity: Zero-Shot Tool Selection via Functional and Contextual Validation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20388

**作者：** Xiaoxuan Dong、Jin Zhang、Zichen Yuan、Yue Zhou、Zihao Rao、Dexu Yu、Tingyong WU、Wenhao Deng、Junchen Fu、Pengwei Sun、Yang Ran、Bo Lan、Yongxin Ni、Youhua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20388v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向 LLM agent 的零样本工具选择，指出仅靠语义相关性排序存在「迁移有效性缺口」，提出渐进式验证框架，用功能性兼容与上下文证据补充相关性，并在跨域、跨数据集迁移中改进能力排序。

**相关性简评：** 其「相关性≠有效性」的诊断与渐进验证/证据筛选思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中语义相关但功能无用的视觉证据筛选与推理期验证机制；但该工作针对纯文本工具选择，与视觉推理的距离需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Relevance to Validity: Zero-Shot Tool Selection via Functional and Contextual Validation

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face tasks from unseen domains and execution contexts, making zero-shot tool selection essential for identifying tools that can satisfy task requirements. Existing dominant methods mainly rely on relevance ranking, but semantic matching alone cannot ensure that a tool is functionally compatible or remains applicable in a new context. We preliminarily identify a \\emph{transfer-validity gap}, where semantically relevant tools may still fail to satisfy functional or contextual requirements after transfer. To address this gap, we propose \\methodname{}, a progressive validation framework that complements semantic relevance with endpoint-grounded functional compatibility and task-conditioned contextual evidence. Across cross-domain and cross-dataset transfer, \\methodname{} consistently improves capability ranking, with larger gains under functional or contextual ambiguity. Ablation results further confirm the complementary contributions of functional and contextual validation.

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Where Should the Gaussians Go? Reusable Label-Aware Allocation for Budget-Constrained Gaussian Splatting of Cinematic CT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20397v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20397

**作者：** Bowen Xiang、Dingjie Su、Jon S. Heiselman、Michael I. Miga

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20397v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20397v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究预算受限下 3D 高斯溅射中「高斯该放在哪里」：提出可复用的标签感知分配前端，把 CT、标签与传递函数映射为体素密度并抽取固定 B 个位置，含群体训练的神经先验与七参数可见性感知闭式（VACF），其余属性仍逐患者拟合。

**相关性简评：** 其「可复用、标签感知的固定预算位置分配先验」可能与 LVR 中在有限 latent token/计算预算下决定视觉证据落在何处的问题同构，可作为按相关性分配内部状态容量、避免逐例搜索的参考线索，尚需核查可迁移性。该工作本身面向医学 CT 重建，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Should the Gaussians Go? Reusable Label-Aware Allocation for Budget-Constrained Gaussian Splatting of Cinematic CT

**abstract/A1：** Anatomical labels help 3D Gaussian splatting preserve vessels in cinematic renderings of CT, but the representation is still found by optimising each patient's scene, where densification decides where Gaussians go, while real-time displays cap their number. We address one part of this process: deciding where a fixed budget of B Gaussians should be placed. We propose a reusable, labelaware allocation front end that maps a patient's CT, labels and transfer function to a density over 2mm cells and draws exactly B positions from it without per-patient optimisation; the other Gaussian attributes are still fitted per patient. The front end is learned once from per-patient optimised scenes and implemented as a population-trained neural prior and an interpretable seven-parameter visibility-aware closed form (VACF). With identical attribute fitting on 20 held-out patients at 10K-100K Gaussians, both raise vessel-masked PSNR over uniform allocation by 4.5-7.1dB and over pruning a dense voxel-aligned model by 1.6-3.3dB. The neural prior does not differ significantly from per-patient optimised allocation at any budget, and VACF is within 0.6dB of it on vessels; on 19 external patients the neural prior again matches it on vessels and VACF is within 0.7dB. As an initialisation for position optimisation, VACF allocation beats per-patient densification on vessels at 10K Gaussians and, at 25K, reaches a higher vessel PSNR after 118s of optimisation than densification after 248s. Under altered rendering presets, VACF transfers to new colours and lighting but not to a label-free transfer function, whereas the neural prior shows the opposite pattern.

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [Hyperspherical Contrastive Representations Unlock Planning and Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20409v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20409

**作者：** Jonghae Park、Sungil Son、Jonghun Shin、Junseo Lee、H. Jin Kim、Benjamin Eysenbach

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20409v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20409v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 C-Slerp：在超球面参数化下学习自监督对比表示，把表示当作可复用构建块，通过球面线性插值闭式推断中间 waypoint，从零学习长时程导航与堆叠立方体等操作任务，并表示几何可用于按目标难度调整 waypoint 数量。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示几何决定规划步数」与「在潜空间闭式插值生成中间状态、无需额外高层策略」可能为 LVR 的动态计算、停止决策与 latent 状态生成提供可核查线索，但该工作属自监督 RL，未涉及 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hyperspherical Contrastive Representations Unlock Planning and Exploration

**abstract/A1：** We develop a self-supervised reinforcement learning algorithm that learns to build towers of up to eight cubes from scratch, without prior data, rewards, demonstrations, or pretrained models. Our method, Contrastive Planning via Spherical Linear Interpolation (C-Slerp), uses contrastive representations as reusable building blocks and repeatedly composes them to infer a sequence of intermediate waypoints. Under our hyperspherical parameterization, the learned representations allow intermediate waypoints to be inferred in closed form through spherical linear interpolation, without training a separate high-level policy or performing graph search. Importantly, the representation geometry also tells us how to adjust the number of waypoints based on the difficulty of each goal-reaching problem. Experiments show that our method succeeds on long-horizon navigation and robotic manipulation tasks, including settings where strong baselines fail to achieve a single success. Ablations further show that using a fixed number of waypoints substantially degrades performance, highlighting the importance of adapting the plan to task difficulty.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://jonghaepark.github.io/c-slerp

</details>
