### 160. [When Does Momentum Help in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20413v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20413

**作者：** Kaifei Wang、Zikang Shan、Liwei Wang、Yinyu Ye、Han Zhong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20413v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20413v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用带偏置校正 EMA 动量的 bandit 形式分析 RLVR 中一阶动量何时加速优化，给出 β 与学习率耦合的理论条件，并在 Qwen3-4B-Base、Qwen2.5-32B 的 RLVR 实验中验证增大 β₁ 的收益。

**相关性简评：** 不涉及视觉或多模态状态，但其关于 RLVR 训练优化器中动量系数与学习率关系的结论，可能为 LVR 模型 RL 微调阶段的超参设置与训练稳定性提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Momentum Help in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards?

**abstract/A1：** Momentum is a classical tool for accelerating convex optimization and a standard component of modern large language model training. Yet recent empirical work has questioned the usefulness of first-moment momentum in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We study this question through a bandit formulation of RLVR with bias-corrected exponential moving average (EMA) momentum, where the EMA coefficient $β$ controls how quickly the weights on past gradients decay. When policy gradients are evaluated exactly, we prove that increasing $β$ within a range that prevents overshoot strictly accelerates optimization. When gradients are estimated from sampled responses, a positive expected gain can be obscured by sampling fluctuations. We show that as the learning rate $η$ decreases, the expected gain with fixed $β$ vanishes faster than these fluctuations, whereas increasing $β$ to keep $η/(1-β)$ within the safe regime preserves a nonvanishing gain that eventually dominates them. Bandit simulations quantitatively validate these predictions. RLVR experiments on Qwen3-4B-Base using SGD with EMA and AdamW support our principle of choosing larger coefficients in practical LLM post-training. On Qwen2.5-32B with AdamW, simply increasing $β\_1$ improves mean validation accuracy by nearly $2$ percentage points over the conventional choice, demonstrating the principle's effectiveness at a larger model scale.

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Quantifying privacy risks in foundation model representations of brain MRI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20494v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20494

**作者：** Imen Bajar、Héber Hwang Arcolezi、Antoine Boutet、Carole Frindel、Mohamed Maouche

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20494v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20494v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 OASIS-2 与 MIRIAD 两个纵向 T1 数据集上评估五个冻结的预训练脑 MRI 编码器，用带公开编码器与辅助扫描的攻击者模型做患者链接和敏感属性推断，发现嵌入仍保留大量个体信息（最高 rank-1 链接近 99%）。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结编码器嵌入 + 攻击者探针」的信息保有量测量范式，或可供核查 LVR 内部状态究竟保留多少视觉细节、是否存在信息坍缩；但论文本身研究隐私泄露而非推理，迁移价值需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Quantifying privacy risks in foundation model representations of brain MRI

**abstract/A1：** Pretrained representations are increasingly used to share and reuse information from medical images without exposing the original scans, but it is unclear how much patient-specific information these representations retain. We investigate this question for brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) by evaluating five frozen pretrained encoders spanning different architectures, pretraining objectives, and training domains on two longitudinal T1-weighted datasets, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD. Under an attacker model with access to released embeddings, the corresponding public encoder, and auxiliary MRI scans, we evaluate patient linkage and sensitive-attribute inference. Across the evaluated representations, we observe substantial subject-specific information: rank-1 patient linkage reaches up to 99\\% on MIRIAD and exceeds 90\\% on OASIS-2 for the highest-risk representation, despite the absence of an identification objective during encoder training. We further show that the released embeddings retain information about a demographic attribute, with recorded-sex prediction reaching balanced accuracy of 90.9\\% on OASIS-2 and 95.5\\% on MIRIAD. Overall, our results demonstrate that replacing raw brain MRI with learned embeddings does not, by itself, eliminate patient-linkage or attribute-inference risk, and is therefore insufficient on its own to establish anonymity.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [VascuSR: Iterative Anatomy-Aware Diffusion for CT Super-Resolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20509v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20509

**作者：** Hairong Ni、Jiahe Huang、Minghao Guo、Wojciech Matusik、Rose Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20509v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20509v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VascuSR：用显式 3D 血管表示加轻量 3D 生成细化器获得结构引导，由 anatomy controller 注入扩散解码器做逐层 CT 超分，并以迭代反馈从新生成的 SR 体积更新引导，在 MSD Task08 与 HVM 上改善分割与 3D 血管结构指标。

**相关性简评：** 其「生成→结构引导→再注入」的迭代反馈回路与结构化先验注入解码器的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的循环注入与推理状态更新提供方法线索；「视觉合理但解剖上无支持的细节」这一诊断设置也可供核查视觉证据筛选与幻觉式细节的评价设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VascuSR: Iterative Anatomy-Aware Diffusion for CT Super-Resolution

**abstract/A1：** Generative super-resolution (SR) can recover sharp detail in computed tomography (CT) images from severely downsampled observations, but the missing high-frequency content is not uniquely determined by the low-resolution input. Consequently, visually plausible reconstructions may contain anatomically unsupported vascular detail, such as missing, spurious, or incorrectly connected vessels. To overcome this challenge, we introduce VascuSR, an iterative anatomy-aware diffusion framework that combines image reconstruction with explicit 3D vascular guidance. First, to provide richer anatomical guidance, we use an explicit 3D vascular representation that captures structural information beyond individual slices. This representation also simplifies vascular refinement by allowing a lightweight 3D generative refiner to focus on vascular structure rather than complex CT appearance. Second, to guide slice-wise SR, a lightweight anatomy controller injects the refined structure into the diffusion decoder, while iterative feedback updates this guidance from each new SR volume. On re-annotated MSD Task08 and HVM datasets, VascuSR outperforms interpolation and learning-based SR baselines in segmentation accuracy (Dice and clDice) and 3D vascular structure (connectivity and mean centerline distance), while maintaining image quality comparable to its SR backbone. Compared with learning-based baselines, VascuSR reduces class-averaged connected-component counts by over 20% on both datasets and mean centerline distance by over 10% on MSD.

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [Generative Representation Learning for Scalable Latent Tokenization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20533v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20533

**作者：** Alan Baade、Kyle Sargent、Vincent Sitzmann、Eric Ryan Chan、Justin Johnson、Li Fei-Fei、Gordon Wetzstein

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20533v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20533v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一种面向生成建模的自监督表示学习方法，用作 latent tokenizer：通过分解数据点互信息、以生成模型条件预测之差估计目标来监督表示损失，声称可避免维度坍塌与特征抑制，并分析 tokenizer 与生成模型联合 FLOP 效率及缩放律。

**相关性简评：** 其「避免维度坍塌/特征抑制」的表示损失设计与计算-最优缩放分析，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的信息坍缩诊断和视觉细节保留提供可供核查的训练侧线索，但论文本身面向图像生成 tokenizer，与多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative Representation Learning for Scalable Latent Tokenization

**abstract/A1：** We introduce an approach for self-supervised representation learning designed to be used as a tokenizer for generative modeling. Our objective supervises a representation loss by factorizing the pointwise mutual information of the data, which we estimate by taking information differences between conditional predictions from a learned generative model. We justify our algorithm theoretically and empirically, demonstrating that our generative approach to representation learning avoids dimensional collapse and feature suppression in cases where commonly used methods like DINO and LeJEPA fail. Moreover, we show our representation learning technique is scalable; expending more compute leads to predictably better latent tokenization. For the first time, we perform a careful analysis of the combined flop efficiency of tokenizer and generative model and demonstrate that our approach is a compute-optimal improvement over direct diffusion for pixel-space generation, obtaining an FID-50K of 2.18 with JiT-L. Applied to ImageNet and the text-to-image dataset GPIC, we show tokenizer encoder scaling laws for diffusability, where extrapolating our approach achieves representations in 7x fewer FLOPs than DINOv2 on ImageNet and 8x fewer FLOPs than WebSSL on GPIC.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Revisiting Simple Baselines for Relational Deep Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20534v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20534

**作者：** Viet The Nguyen、Vijay Prakash Dwivedi、Gunther Gust

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20534v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20534v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 RelBench 上重新审视关系深度学习的简单基线：把固定低通特征传播用于关系实体图并训练 MLP 做预测，再用 Correct-and-Smooth 图标签传播结合历史标签精化结果，并指出目标实体间需单独构建元图。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定传播 + 简单预测头」基线及基于元图的标签传播迭代精化，可能为 LVR 中潜在状态的传播式更新、简单基线与复杂架构的收益对照提供可供核查的方法线索，但与多模态推理的可迁移性尚不清楚。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Revisiting Simple Baselines for Relational Deep Learning

**abstract/A1：** Relational deep learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for learning end-to-end predictive models over relational databases by representing them as temporal heterogeneous graphs. Recent advances based on graph neural networks and Transformers have achieved strong performance across diverse domains. However, the effectiveness of simpler relational baselines remains underexplored, making it difficult to assess how much architectural complexity is needed to achieve strong predictive performance. Motivated by the observation that predictive signals arise from target attributes, relational attributes, and historical labels, we revisit how effectively these signals can be exploited using simple propagation methods. We adapt fixed low-pass feature propagation to relational entity graphs and train an MLP for prediction. The resulting predictions are further refined using Correct-and-Smooth, a graph-based label propagation algorithm that incorporates historical labels over task-specific meta-graphs connecting target entities. Across classification and regression tasks on RelBench, an MLP trained on neighborhood-filtered features outperforms one using only target-entity features on most tasks and recovers a substantial portion of the performance of heterogeneous GNNs. Correct-and-Smooth provides further gains from historical labels, although applying it requires a separately constructed meta-graph because connections among target entities are not directly specified by the relational entity graph. Together, our studies revisit the effectiveness of established propagation methods as simple RDL baselines while identifying canonical meta-graph construction as a key challenge for effective label propagation.

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [Reliability-Aware Multimodal Sentiment Prediction Under Local Modality Missingness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20543v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20543

**作者：** Minghao Kong、Deng Yao、Xi Zhang、Ying Gao、Jiurun Chen、Zirong Liu、Rongjie Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20543v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20543v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究对齐 CMU-MOSEI 特征上连续片段缺失时的多模态情感预测，提出 RM-Net：保留原始时序位置、用可学习 token 表示未观测位置、输出受观测质量门控的有界模态强度贡献，并以双视角片段掩码训练，另用 Shapley 归因与位置级遮挡做解释。

**相关性简评：** 其「缺失位置用可学习 token + 按观测质量门控模态贡献」的设计，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据缺失、证据筛选与 latent 状态贡献分配提供方法线索；位置级遮挡与 Shapley 归因可供核查证据因果作用与评价方式，虽属情感分类任务，仍需较多迁移核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliability-Aware Multimodal Sentiment Prediction Under Local Modality Missingness

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal sentiment models are usually evaluated on complete utterances, yet streams often lose a contiguous span of text, audio, or video. We study local missingness on aligned CMU-MOSEI features. Our reliability-aware network (RM-Net) keeps the original temporal positions, represents unobserved positions with a learned token, outputs bounded per-modality intensity contributions gated by observation quality, and is trained with dual-view masking of contiguous segments. We fuse five RM-Net seeds with a LoRA-adapted BERT text expert, using a fusion weight that depends only on the visible fraction of content tokens. Architecture, checkpoint, and fusion choices were made on the validation split. On the held-out split, the fused model reaches a macro-F1 of 0.655 on clean inputs and 0.634 under 40\\% mixed missingness; its two-view average exceeds the auxiliary-head baseline by 0.024 (95\\% video-cluster bootstrap interval \[0.003, 0.045\]). Because this split was inspected during development, these numbers are not a blind test. For explanations, exact Shapley attributions over the eight modality subsets and position-level occlusion show that top-ranked positions reduce the predicted-class probability faster than random positions (AUC gap 0.113 on validation). We also describe an auditable word-level alignment pipeline for raw clips; its outputs do not feed the predictive models.

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [Speculative Sampling in Interactive Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20556v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20556

**作者：** Yubo Huang、Yuxuan Zhang、Jiaming Liu、Sirui Zhao、Enhong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.HC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20556v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20556v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究交互式视频生成中的延迟问题，提出 Speculative Sampling：用噪声 latent 作填充扩大双向建模窗口，缓存预测并在条件不变时复用，从而增量交付预测、保留未提交未来的可替换性。

**相关性简评：** 「缓存 latent 预测＋增量提交＋未提交未来可替换」的机制可能与 LVR 中候选 latent 状态的推测式生成、提交/回滚及条件不变时的缓存一致性有可供核查的线索，但原文属视频生成领域，能否迁移到多模态推理状态仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Speculative Sampling in Interactive Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Interactive video generation commonly relies on small bidirectional chunks to reduce response latency. Yet our preliminary experiments show that larger chunks improve video quality and better utilize GPU parallelism, allowing more latents to be sampled with only a modest increase in sampling time. Motivated by these observations, we propose Speculative Sampling, a strategy that combines the modeling power of large bidirectional windows with the low-latency interaction of small ones. It expands the modeling window by appending noise latents as padding and caches the resulting predictions for reuse when the condition remains unchanged. Predictions are delivered incrementally, while the uncommitted future remains replaceable. Our latency analysis shows that this removes the waiting-time lower bound imposed by chunk boundaries, shifting the optimization focus from reducing chunk size to accelerating per-chunk sampling. Bidirectional window size thus becomes a decision variable for jointly optimizing visual quality and sampling time, rather than a necessary constraint on low-latency interaction. This opens a path to serving large bidirectional models in interactive streaming systems without complex streaming post-training.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://yubo-shankui.github.io/speculative\_sampling

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Recovering Reliable Supervision via Critique-Guided On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20572v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20572

**作者：** Kaixiang Zhao、Tianrun Yu、Shangzhe Li、Yuxiao Yang、Amanda Hughes、Weitong Zhang、Taylor W. Killian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20572v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20572v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 on-policy distillation 中教师无法从学生的错误前缀恢复出正确答案（即 recoverability 失效），提出 CriticOPD：由教师批评学生错误 rollout 并引导其改写，改写成稿连同批评作为特权信息被评分，学生则仅用不含批评的修订轨迹训练。

**相关性简评：** 属纯文本推理的蒸馏监督方法，未涉及视觉或 latent 状态；其「错误识别 + 特权信息改写 + 学生仅见修订轨迹」的监督构造，或可为 LVR 在错误中间状态下如何构造轨迹监督、分配各步贡献提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recovering Reliable Supervision via Critique-Guided On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its own rollouts with token-level supervision from a teacher, assuming that the teacher can reach a correct answer from any prefix the student writes. We call this property the recoverability of the teacher and show it fails on incorrect rollouts: the teacher follows student errors, although it recognizes them. We propose CriticOPD, using error recognition to restore recoverability. The teacher critiques an incorrect rollout, allowing the student to rewrite its solution following the error. The resulting output, including the critique as privileged information, is scored by the teacher while the student uses the revision without the critique for training. On two Qwen3 students and six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CriticOPD improves OPD, with the largest gains on competition problems.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages. Code: https://github.com/kzhao5/CriticOPD

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [One Task, Many Interfaces: Simple Cross-Interface Consistency Training Improves LLM Agent Robustness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20576v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20576

**作者：** Artin Tajdini、Manpreet Kaur、Peng Qi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20576v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20576v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Cross-Interface Policy Regularization（CIPR）：把动作名替换为无信息标识符，并将同一训练样本以两种语义等价的接口呈现，用两组动作分布之间的 KL 散度作为自监督正则，使 LLM agent 的策略在接口变化下保持一致；在监督与强化学习后训练中均提升了接口扰动下的鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义等价输入应诱导一致内部决策」的正则化思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节扰动下 latent 推理状态是否稳定的训练与贡献分配设计；但该文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，需人工判断迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Task, Many Interfaces: Simple Cross-Interface Consistency Training Improves LLM Agent Robustness

**abstract/A1：** Post-training binds the competence of an LLM agent to the interface it was trained on. Semantics-preserving perturbations of that interface, such as renaming an action or changing the notation, leave the underlying task unchanged yet make the agent worse at it. Scoring each sample independently means interface randomization only broadens this dependence from a single interface to the training interface distribution, so out-of-distribution perturbations still degrade the agent. We propose Cross-Interface Policy Regularization (CIPR), a self-supervised objective that replaces action names with uninformative identifiers and presents each training sample under two interfaces in which equivalent actions are identified by construction. CIPR penalizes the KL divergence between the action distributions induced under the two interfaces, so the policy behaves consistently across them. We evaluate CIPR in both supervised and reinforcement-learning post-training, where it improves robustness. On agentic function calling, held-out interface perturbations reduce the accuracy of a post-trained agent by nearly 25%, and CIPR recovers 10.5% while leaving accuracy on the unchanged interface intact.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [Learnable Hodge-Laplacian Driven Mixture of Experts for Steady-PDE Surrogates on Coarse Unstructured Meshes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20585v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20585

**作者：** Stavros Nousias、Akis Nousias、Konstantinos Gkrispanis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20585v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20585v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为粗粒度非结构网格上的稳态 PDE 代理模型提出一种架构：用离散 Hodge Laplacian 作为拟合层度量解的高频含量，将解按谱尺度分配给多个 vanilla transformer 专家，并以网格为输入的 gating 逐顶点生成权重来重组专家输出；在缩小并重网格化的 AirFRANS RANS 翼型基准上对比 Transolver，报告相对 L2 误差降低 31%、内部 MSE 降低 47%。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR/VLM 推理主线无直接关系，但其按谱尺度分解内部表示、再由逐顶点 gating 加权重组的机制，可能可供核查 latent 视觉细节保留与视觉证据筛选/加权这类表示设计问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learnable Hodge-Laplacian Driven Mixture of Experts for Steady-PDE Surrogates on Coarse Unstructured Meshes

**abstract/A1：** Current approaches for steady partial differential equations surrogate models on unstructured meshes apply a single mixing mechanism to the whole solution. This paper proposes an architecture that partitions the solution among vanilla transformer experts, each focused on a different spectral scale. The partition is built from a discrete Hodge Laplacian $L\_0=\\star\_0^{-1} d\_0^{\\top}\\star\_1 d\_0$, that serves as a fitting layer measuring high-frequency content. A gating mechanism takes the mesh as input and produces the per-vertex weights that recombine the expert outputs. AirFRANS is a steady Reynolds-averaged Navier--Stokes (RANS) airfoil benchmark. We evaluate our approach in a reduced remeshed version of the benchmark with each unstructured mesh counting 9.6K faces and 4.9K vertices. We compare our approach with the Transolver on the same dataset and dataset slit. Our approach reduces relative $L\_2$ error by 31\\% and interior mean squared error by 47\\%.

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [Continuum Attention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20586v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20586

**作者：** Afshin Khadangi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20586v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20586v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Continuum Attention：以对连续分段线性记忆曲线的积分替代离散 key/value 查表，每个 query 产生指数相关性密度，借助闭式指数矩（含半 token 边界贡献）得到精确归一化注意力，梯度可由至多二阶矩解析给出，在语言建模验证损失上小幅优于 softmax 与 focal attention。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本注意力算子，未涉及视觉或推理期 latent 状态；但其把记忆视为连续曲线并用相关性密度积分做加权的思路，可能为 LVR 中跨位置视觉证据聚合与潜在状态的连续性/软边界表示提供注意力结构层面的核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continuum Attention

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Continuum Attention (CA), a causal Transformer attention operator that replaces discrete key and value lookup with integration over a continuous, piecewise linear memory curve. Each query induces an exponential relevance density along the curve. Integrating the density and interpolated values yields exact normalized attention from closed form exponential moments, including half token boundary contributions. The gradients follow analytically from moments of order at most two, enabling parallel GPU implementations while retaining quadratic sequence complexity and the standard Transformer decoder. Compared with ordinary softmax and focal attention, CA reduces mean validation negative log likelihood by 0.0200 and 0.0480 nats per byte, respectively, winning all paired comparisons. An additional disjoint validation slice yields a 0.0457 nats per byte advantage over the strongest selected softmax baseline, with a paired 95% confidence interval. These findings motivate continuous integration as a promising attention alternative while leaving larger scale generalization open.

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [Co-Evolving Acting and Forecasting for Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20597v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20597

**作者：** Haofei Yu、Yixing Chen、Yining Zhao、Xuyan Ye、Yuxiang Liu、Zhigang Hua、Yan Xie、Shuang Yang、Jiaxuan You

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20597v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20597v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoPE：在成功轨迹上除终局奖励外，还让共享策略骨干学习「由历史预测后续多步动作序列」，把动作预测作为辅助监督形成时序集成，使语言智能体的行动与预测协同演化，推理时仍单步行动、无额外开销。

**相关性简评：** 这是纯文本语言智能体的训练期辅助监督工作，与 LVR 关注的轨迹训练与贡献分配可能有联系：其「由历史预测后续动作、由共享骨干吸收」的监督方式可供核查是否能用于 latent 推理状态的中间步监督，但无视觉或多模态成分，迁移需另行验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Co-Evolving Acting and Forecasting for Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** Training language agents is bottlenecked by the high cost of long-horizon rollouts, yet standard agentic reinforcement learning (RL) learns only from a terminal scalar reward, leaving the supervision in intermediate steps largely unused. Prior work exploits this supervision through value targets that estimate per-step success probabilities or observation targets that fit environment response. Both are hard to construct well: (1) value targets come from the same single scalar signal for each step in the trajectory, and (2) observation targets, such as web pages and terminal logs, are long and noisy and reach only one step ahead. We propose \\name, which adds a third kind of multi-step action targets. On successful on-policy trajectories, \\name trains the policy backbone to generate the action sequences that follows each history. Each action is also supervised when forecast from the preceding histories, forming a temporal ensemble that the shared backbone absorbs during training, and this enables acting and forecasting to co-evolve. The policy still acts one step at a time at inference, with no overhead. Across five benchmarks (ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, $τ^2$-bench, and AppWorld), \\name (1) achieves stronger aggregate performance than GRPO and RL baselines with predictive auxiliary targets across simulated environments, while improving overall performance on both realistic applications; (2) delivers up to $1.9\\times$ and $2.0\\times$ higher sample efficiency than ECHO and GRPO, respectively, on ScienceWorld; and (3) achieves these gains with a single policy backbone, without additional models, separate pretraining, or inference overhead. The codebase is available at https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/CoPE.

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Representation Conditioning for Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20608v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20608

**作者：** Saeed Razavi、Xingjian Leng、Liang Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20608v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20608v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RECON：训练时把预训练视觉编码器 token 注入 diffusion transformer 的后层，推理时用保留下来的 MLP 投影头作为编码器代理继续注入，不增加参数并显著加快收敛。

**相关性简评：** 该文「后层难以访问语义」的观察与用投影头代理视觉证据、条件化注入深层状态的做法，可能为 VLM 推理中视觉细节在深层是否保留、以及视觉旁路与表示条件化设计提供可供核查的方法线索；但研究对象是图像生成而非推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Representation Conditioning for Generation

**abstract/A1：** Representation-alignment methods effectively accelerate the diffusion transformer training. REPA, for example, uses a pretrained vision encoder to supervise an intermediate layer through an MLP projection head which is discarded after training. It aims to import semantics from the vision encoder to the diffusion backbone. While this objective is achieved at early layers by back-propagating the alignment loss, there is no guarantee that later layers, supervised by the velocity prediction loss alone, can well access such semantics. We propose a simple solution, representation conditioning (RECON), to improve semantics in later layers. During training, we feed the pretrained vision encoder tokens into later layers; during inference, because we no longer have the clean image, we reuse the MLP head (as a proxy to the vision encoder) and project its output tokens to the later layers. Our method adds no parameters and leads to much faster convergence: RECON is $63\\times$ faster than SiT and $25\\times$ faster than REPA. Code is available at https://github.com/saeidrazavi/RECON

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://recondiffusion.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Can Learning to Answer Spatial Questions Improve Novel-View Generation?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20610

**作者：** Jahyeok Koo、Hyeonseo Yu、Seungho Jang、Jaewoo Jung、Jiwon Kang、Seokju Cho、Seungryong Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20610v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察 3D 语言理解能否为 novel-view generation 提供互补监督，提出基于 MoT 统一多模态模型的 Ask4NV：把相机几何注入 VAE 与 ViT 表示，并通过联合自注意力与模态感知注意力掩码在生成中利用理解特征，报告了图像质量、几何一致性及 3D 语言理解表现。

**相关性简评：** 其模态感知注意力掩码与联合自注意力给出了「理解特征如何进入生成内部状态」的可核查方法线索；对 VAE 表示跨视角对应与 3D 感知特征空间相似度的分析，也可能与 LVR 中视觉细节是否保留、latent 相似度受内容与结构影响的问题相关，但属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Learning to Answer Spatial Questions Improve Novel-View Generation?

**abstract/A1：** Generating novel views from limited observations requires preserving both the appearance and spatial structure of a scene. Although recent approaches exploit fine-grained geometric cues, they do not explicitly model high-level semantic and spatial properties such as object identity, attributes, and inter-object relationships. In this work, we investigate whether 3D language understanding can provide complementary supervision for novel-view generation. We introduce Ask4NV, built on a pretrained unified multimodal model with a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. Our model incorporates camera geometry into both VAE and ViT representations and leverages understanding features during generation through joint self-attention with a modality-aware attention masking strategy. Experiments demonstrate strong image quality and geometric consistency in novel-view generation, alongside competitive 3D language understanding. Further analysis shows that joint training improves cross-view correspondence in VAE representations and similarity in a 3D-aware feature space.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/Ask4NV/

</details>

<a id="paper-174"></a>

### 174. [Do Vision CIL Methods Transfer to Protein? A Diagnostic Benchmark of Frozen Protein Encoders](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18879v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18879

**作者：** Ziyuan Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18879v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18879v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在五种冻结蛋白编码器与两个酶功能数据集上对九种类增量学习（CIL）方法做受控基准，发现没有方法能跨编码器稳定迁移，并将其归因于梯度稳定性信号弱、嵌入几何高度异质以及协方差假设所需容量随数据规模与编码器变化。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示几何应被视为持续学习设计的核心变量、方法需按表示调整统计假设与容量」的观点，可能为 LVR 中 latent 内容/几何对相似度、深度与位置效应以及训练监督配置提供类比线索；但领域为蛋白 CIL，迁移性需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Vision CIL Methods Transfer to Protein? A Diagnostic Benchmark of Frozen Protein Encoders

**abstract/A1：** Protein function annotation operates over an expanding label space, making class-incremental learning (CIL) a natural framework for incorporating newly discovered functions without full retraining. Yet existing CIL methods have been developed largely in vision and have not been systematically evaluated over frozen protein representations. We conduct a controlled benchmark of nine representative CIL methods across five sequence-, structure-, and sequence--structure-based protein encoders and two enzyme function datasets at different scales, while keeping the encoders frozen and evaluation settings consistent across representations. We find that no method transfers reliably across encoders, and that these failures persist across alternative phase constructions. Diagnostic analyses reveal that this instability arises from weak gradient-based stability signals, substantial heterogeneity in embedding geometry, and covariance assumptions whose appropriate capacity changes with both dataset scale and encoder. These findings suggest that the representation itself should be treated as a central component of continual-learning design rather than a passive feature extractor. More generally, reliable protein CIL will require encoder-aware methods that adapt their statistical assumptions and capacity to the geometry of the representation on which they operate. Code is available at https://github.com/zygao930/ProteinCIL.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Continual Learning for Foundation Models and Agents (CL4FM Agents)

</details>

<a id="paper-175"></a>

### 175. [Do Perturbation Predictors Learn Gene Interactions? Diagnosing Extrapolation in Agentic Screening](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18880v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18880

**作者：** Ziyuan Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.GN

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18880v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18880v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究基因组合扰动效应的外推预测：提出「加性分歧」（AD），即模型预测与两个已观测单基因效应之和的差值，用以衡量模型对未见组合额外引入的互作效应，并在八个预测方法与三个 Perturb-seq 数据集上比较其与传统输出／嵌入置信信号的表现，以及在 agentic 筛选流程中的决策作用。

**相关性简评：** 来自基因组扰动筛选这一较远领域，但「偏离简单加性基线的程度越大则误差越大」这一结构化可靠性信号，可能为 latent 推理中的组合泛化诊断、置信估计与「继续取证据／停止」的决策提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do Perturbation Predictors Learn Gene Interactions? Diagnosing Extrapolation in Agentic Screening

**abstract/A1：** High-throughput perturbation screens increasingly use machine-learning models to predict the effects of gene combinations that were never measured. A difficult case arises when both genes in a test pair were seen individually during training, but their combination was not. In this setting, standard OOD methods often fail because neither gene appears unfamiliar on its own. We therefore compare each model prediction with a simple additive prediction obtained by summing the two observed single-gene effects. The difference between them, which we call additive disagreement (AD), measures how much extra interaction effect the model introduces for an unseen combination. Across eight prediction methods and three Perturb-seq datasets, specialised neural predictors do not consistently outperform this simple additive baseline. More importantly, predictions with larger deviations from the additive baseline tend to have larger errors. Conventional output-based and embedding-based confidence signals are inconsistent across datasets and models, whereas AD remains informative across all evaluated compositional settings. We further study how this signal can guide decisions in an agentic perturbation-screening workflow. The code is available at https://github.com/zygao930/OOD.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop ML4Molecules: Agentic Systems for Molecular Sciences

</details>

<a id="paper-176"></a>

### 176. [Why Static Skill Inconsistencies Do Not Equate to Runtime Risks: A Behavioral Analysis of LLM Agent Execution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18927v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18927

**作者：** Fengrui Fan、Huahao Wang、Jingyi Fang、Yutao Hu、Yueming Wu、Deqing Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18927v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18927v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对 575 个社区与基准 skill 做大规模审计，人工确认 1,182 处契约不一致，再用 49 个可执行 agent 任务、三种 LLM 与 NoSkill/OriginalSkill/FixedSkill 三种设置，对 441 条配对执行轨迹做细粒度行为分析，考察静态缺陷是否真的带来运行时性能退化。

**相关性简评：** 其「静态缺陷量不能可靠反映运行时行为代价」的实证结论，与 LVR 中潜在状态指标（如 latent 相似度、内部表示诊断）未必对应推理行为变化的问题可能同构，可供核查评价设计与轨迹分析思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why Static Skill Inconsistencies Do Not Equate to Runtime Risks: A Behavioral Analysis of LLM Agent Execution

**abstract/A1：** LLM agent frameworks heavily rely on external skill assets to standardize tool usage, encode operational workflows, and supply executable runtime resources. Despite their widespread adoption, multi-component skill packages frequently suffer from cross-artifact inconsistencies, where documentation, scripts, and configuration files specify conflicting behavioral constraints. Although static auditing techniques can uncover such contractual discrepancies, static defect volume cannot reliably reflect real-world agent performance degradation. This gap leaves practitioners without rigorous evidence to assess the practical risk of flawed agent skills. This paper presents a systematic empirical study to demystify the runtime implications of skill inconsistencies. We conduct large-scale auditing over 575 community and benchmark skills, yielding 1,182 manually validated contract inconsistencies. We further build a controlled evaluation pipeline with 49 executable agent tasks and three representative LLMs across NoSkill, OriginalSkill, and FixedSkill experimental settings. Through fine-grained behavioral analysis of 441 paired execution trajectories, we quantify the actual performance impacts of resolved defects.

</details>

<a id="paper-177"></a>

### 177. [The Agentic Scientist: Orchestrating Coding Agents for Automated Scientific (Co-)Discovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18933v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18933

**作者：** Robert Tjarko Lange、Luca Grillotti、Yutaro Yamada、Rujikorn Charakorn、David Ha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18933v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18933v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「The Agentic Scientist」，把计算研究拆成选题、构思、规划、沙盒实验、结果汇总、基线与消融、事后分析和报告撰写等阶段，由元控制器编排并行 coding agent 并在每阶段做 rubric 式自验证，用于人机协作或全自主的科学发现。

**相关性简评：** 其多智能体并行探索加元控制器分阶段验证的编排方式，可能与 LVR 中多假设并行维护、验证与停止决策等问题表述有概念联系，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态内部推理状态，属较远领域的启发线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Agentic Scientist: Orchestrating Coding Agents for Automated Scientific (Co-)Discovery

**abstract/A1：** Modern coding agents are fundamentally reshaping the role of human input for computational research: they write code, launch in silico experiments, analyze their results, and autonomously operate with ever-increasing time horizons. Despite these developments, fully autonomous research agents suffer from a limited ideation radius and myopic hill-climbing search dynamics. To bridge this gap, we introduce The Agentic Scientist, an end-to-end distributed pipeline for human researchers to conduct computational research alongside modern coding agents. Building on its predecessor, our system decomposes research into task drafting, idea generation, planning, sandboxed experimentation, result aggregation, baseline and ablation construction, post-hoc analysis, and report writing. Importantly, each step in the pipeline is powered by the next generation of coding agents and leverages agentic self-verification. The overall research process is driven by a meta-controller that manages parallel research agents and enforces rubric verification at each stage. This design enables the same orchestration layer to be reused across heterogeneous domains with different execution environments, agentic coding harnesses, and evaluation metrics. Our system can be used across a spectrum of autonomy: either as a co-scientist tool or to perform end-to-end scientific investigation. Within the co-scientist setting, we leveraged a development version of our system to submit a single research paper, which got accepted for publication at ALIFE 2026. We further apply The Agentic Scientist fully autonomously to open problem-solving tasks in machine learning, and demonstrate the system's capability of scientific discovery across different domains of computational research. Our ablations suggest that reliable scientific autonomy depends not only on base model capabilities, but also on the cross-agent orchestration layer.

**comment/C1：** 88 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [Beyond Equation Search: Conjecture-Guided Discovery with SCOPE and ClosureBench](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18938v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18938

**作者：** Yuxiao Hu、Qian Li、Ding Wang、Siheng Chen、Linfeng Zhang、Jinyue Yan、Yuntian Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18938v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18938v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SCOPE 框架，让多个科学家智能体各自沿一条猜想轨迹进行方程建模、问题提出与主动实验，再交换证据以修订猜想，并给出 ClosureBench 基准（60 个跨五个领域的闭合发现任务，含证据忠实度、机制洞见与实验有效性三项指标）。

**相关性简评：** 其「并行维持多条猜想轨迹、按证据保留或拒绝假设、主动实验取数」的组织方式，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的维护与竞争、视觉证据筛选及停止决策提供问题表述上的类比与线索，可供核查；但属符号回归领域，联系为推测性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Equation Search: Conjecture-Guided Discovery with SCOPE and ClosureBench

**abstract/A1：** Most symbolic regression methods optimize candidate equations, but improving an equation's fit does not establish whether its underlying scientific conjecture should be retained, revised, or rejected. A conjecture may admit multiple mathematical realizations, and similarly accurate equations may imply different explanations. This motivates a shift from refining individual expressions to developing and testing the conjectures that guide them. We introduce SCOPE, a framework that sustains multiple conjecture-driven research trajectories. Multiple scientist agents independently pursue conjectures through equation modeling, question formulation, and active experimentation, then exchange evidence to revise their conjectures and research directions. Their findings are consolidated into a scientific report linking models, conclusions, and evidence. Existing equation-discovery benchmarks primarily assess recovered expressions, leaving the evidence and scientific conclusions established through the investigation underexamined. We therefore introduce ClosureBench, an interactive benchmark of 60 closure-discovery tasks across five scientific domains. ClosureBench focuses on closure-discovery tasks to give equation discovery a concrete scientific purpose. Each task embeds an unknown algebraic relation in a known PDE system, where agents control experimental conditions in OpenFOAM to acquire new data for equation discovery. Beyond equation accuracy, ClosureBench proposes three metrics to evaluate the discovery process: evidence faithfulness, mechanism insight, and experimental effectiveness. Compared with diverse baselines, SCOPE achieves higher equation accuracy on both ClosureBench and a real-world scientific discovery case, while producing more accurate, reliable, and effective scientific discoveries.

</details>

<a id="paper-179"></a>

### 179. [Discovering Undisclosed Concepts in LoRA Adapters for Diffusion-Based AIGC Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18990v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.18990

**作者：** Yupeng Qiu、Han Fang、Dhananjai Sharma、Ee-Chien Chang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18990v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.18990v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何从一个 text-to-image 扩散模型的 LoRA 适配器中反查其被训练生成的隐含概念，提出两阶段框架：先用免渲染的子空间对齐（SGES）筛选候选提示，再对少量候选渲染并与基座模型对比（PVI）以确认概念。

**相关性简评：** 其「用低成本子空间信号先筛选候选、再有选择地付出高成本视觉核查」的结构，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、以及 THINK/LOOK 之间成本—收益权衡的问题表述有类比启发，但属生成与模型审计领域，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Discovering Undisclosed Concepts in LoRA Adapters for Diffusion-Based AIGC Models

**abstract/A1：** Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has made diffusion model personalization affordable and widely shareable, but it also creates moderation risks when harmful or problematic content is not faithfully disclosed. Hence, it is desired to develop a mechanism that, given a LoRA adapter, discovers the concept it was trained to generate, so as to support downstream investigation. We characterize the target concept through the conditioning input that most clearly exposes what the LoRA has learned. Such an input should induce coherent LoRA outputs that are distinctive from those of the base model and strongly align with the learned LoRA subspace. We therefore formulate concept discovery as searching for the conditioning input that jointly maximizes output coherence, LoRA-induced distinctiveness, and subspace alignment. Even with a clear objective, however, optimizing it over an open-ended input space is prohibitively expensive, since reliable evaluation of coherence and distinctiveness requires repeated diffusion rendering. Crucially, we observe that an input need not be rendered to determine whether it is promising. Its alignment with the learned LoRA subspace provides a lightweight signal for localizing promising candidates before visual verification. Based on this insight, we develop a two-stage framework for text-to-image LoRAs. Subspace-Guided Evolutionary Search (SGES) uses render-free subspace alignment to explore candidate prompts and retain a compact shortlist, while Paired Visual Identification (PVI) renders only these candidates and compares the LoRA and base-model outputs to identify those exhibiting coherent and distinctive generation behavior. This design enables efficient and reliable discovery of previously unknown concepts encoded in LoRA adapters.

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [Lag: A Mean-Field Optimal Policy for Online Assignment with Reusable Resources](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19038v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19038

**作者：** Keerthana Gurushankar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.PF、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19038v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19038v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究可重用资源下的在线任务分配，将其建模为 contextual restless bandit，由占用度量 LP 的上界及对偶推出闭式 Lagrangian index 策略，在服务器与到达量同步放大的规模下证明流体极限中的渐近最优，并在同行评审与呼叫中心数据上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「对偶索引 + 平均场流体极限」的思路可能为 LVR 中有限计算步或视觉证据查询预算的在线分配与停止决策提供借鉴线索，但与原问题相距较远，需核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Lag: A Mean-Field Optimal Policy for Online Assignment with Reusable Resources

**abstract/A1：** We study online task assignment with reusable resources. This problem arises in peer reviewer assignment, skill-based call routing, rideshare matching, emergency dispatch, and crowdsourcing: Tasks arrive according to a geometric process and must be immediately assigned to servers that become temporarily busy, yielding rewards that depend on the task-server pair. The goal is to maximize long-run average reward. We cast the problem as a contextual restless multi-armed bandit (CRMAB). We derive an occupancy-measure LP (OCC-LP) that upper-bounds the reward of any work-conserving policy. From its dual we obtain Lag, a closed-form index policy. We introduce a scaling regime in which the number of copies of each server and the number of arrivals per step grow together. This regime yields the first deterministic fluid limit for a contextual bandit. In it, Lag is asymptotically optimal. Finally, we evaluate our algorithms on peer review datasets and call center routing. The Lagrangian index achieves 83-99% of the LP upper bound across all load levels. Moreover, the Lagrangian index policy significantly outperforms the natural greedy assignment scheme, improving reward by up to 12.3% on journal data and 36% on structured synthetic instances.

</details>

<a id="paper-181"></a>

### 181. [Graph Coarsening Enables Escaping Local Optima in Compute-Constrained Nonconvex MAX-CUT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19043v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19043

**作者：** M. Harper Langston、Pierre-David Letourneau、Dalton Jones、Harris Teague、Peter Scott、Mingu Lee、Richard Lethin、Chris Lott、Roland Memisevic、Fatih Porikli

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、math.NA、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19043v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19043v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究计算受限下的非凸 MAX-CUT：用谱图粗化方法 CMG 为基于矩松弛的 PPR 连续重述提供结构感知的热启动解，并配合稀疏 CSR 表示、谱稀疏化与局部搜索，在若干基准上逼近最优解并大幅降低内存。

**相关性简评：** 图粗化本质是多尺度状态抽象、热启动对应状态初始化，其对非凸景观中逃离局部最优的讨论可能与 latent 推理状态的初始化、多尺度组织及低内存状态表示形成类比线索，但属较远领域，需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph Coarsening Enables Escaping Local Optima in Compute-Constrained Nonconvex MAX-CUT

**abstract/A1：** Because its binary optimization landscape contains exponentially many local optima, MAX-CUT is highly sensitive to initialization. We study Particle Polynomial Relaxation (PPR), a moment-based nonconvex continuous reformulation possessing an advantageous optimization landscape. We warm-start PPR with Combinatorial Multigrid (CMG), a spectral graph-coarsening method that produces structure-aware initial solutions in near-linear time without training data. On \\texttt{t2g20} ($n=400$), benchmark instance with known exact optimum, the combined CMG+PPR pipeline reaches $97.5\\%$ of optimum on average and $98.4\\%$ on the best run. An extended variant with discrete local-search refinement reaches the published optimum on all sixty Biq Mac instances evaluated by a recent neural branch-and-bound method. Using a sparse CSR representation, the same approach scales to G-set instances with up to $20{,}000$ vertices reducing memory requirements by up to $3333\\times$ while maintaining a consistent improvement over the CMG warm start. Gains are largest on sparse weighted graphs; on dense graphs we complement the pipeline with spectral sparsification.

</details>

<a id="paper-182"></a>

### 182. [What Information Buys in Active Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19046

**作者：** Melih Kandemir、Mustafa Mert Çelikok

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19046v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在同一 tabular MDP 状态抽象下把 Active Inference 与 Bayes-UCBVI 放在同等条件下比较，发现 AIF 的信息增益衰减过快、无法充当置信宽度，经平方根校准后可得 √T 遗憾界，并分析奖励由对手从数据中选择时两种探索宽度的优劣。

**相关性简评：** 其对信息增益数值性质、校准方式与「信息何时值得获取」的理论刻画，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK 收益-成本权衡与证据获取停止决策提供可供核查的视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Information Buys in Active Inference

**abstract/A1：** Information-theoretic exploration is of primary interest in methodological studies of reinforcement learning (RL) and computational cognitive neuroscience, while the established RL theory builds regret guarantees on confidence sets. This situation leaves open the conditions under which the information-driven exploration found in nature is more advantageous than the exploration of the established RL theory. We put the state-of-the-art methods of these two paradigms, Active Inference (AIF) and Bayes-UCBVI, on an equal footing: both act through the same state abstraction of a tabular MDP with $S$ states, and their greedy forms differ only in the exploration term. We find that AIF's information gain decays too fast to serve as a confidence width, locking greedy agents that explore by it out of the optimal action. Calibrating the information gain by a square root yields calibrated AIF, which attains regret of order $\\sqrt T$ for an exact abstraction, but no better guarantee than Bayes-UCBVI for a fixed reward. When an adversary chooses rewards from the agent's data, Bayes-UCBVI's value-specific widths lock out, whereas calibrated AIF pays a factor $\\sqrt S$, which no goal-memoryless agent avoids on some MDP whose start states the adversary also chooses. Calibrated information gain thus acts as a reward-agnostic $\\ell\_1$ confidence set, preferable to value-specific widths when rewards are chosen from the agent's data, not when they are fixed.

</details>

<a id="paper-183"></a>

### 183. [Deep Generative Models for Phylogenetic Inference with Complex Evolutionary Processes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19053v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19053

**作者：** Ethan Baron、Alan N. Amin、Andrew Gordon Wilson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、q-bio.PE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19053v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19053v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向复杂进化过程下的系统发育树后验推断，提出一族条件生成模型，用 top-down、middle-out、bottom-up 三种范式迭代构建树并以离散扩散框架训练，在真实与合成数据上比 Phyloformer 2 更接近 MCMC 后验。

**相关性简评：** 其把离散扩散与多种迭代构建顺序用于结构化离散对象的后验建模，可能为 LVR 中结构化 latent 状态的生成、逐步更新与不确定性表示提供可核查线索，但领域较远，迁移性需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Generative Models for Phylogenetic Inference with Complex Evolutionary Processes

**abstract/A1：** Inference of phylogenetic trees from sequences plays a central role in understanding evolutionary relationships, with applications ranging from tracking pathogen spread to reconstructing the history of life. Conventionally, practitioners obtain posteriors over trees using likelihood-based methods like MCMC. However, likelihoods are only available for simple evolutionary models with restrictive assumptions. For realistic evolutionary models, inference using conventional methods is prohibitively expensive, inaccurate, or even impossible. In practice, practitioners settle for using as flexible a conventional method as possible, even though they expect it is misspecified. In principle, we could use simulation-based inference: simulate tree and sequence data from an evolutionary model and train a neural network to predict the posterior over trees given sequences. However it is a mathematical challenge to represent and train complex posterior distributions over trees. Here we present a flexible family of conditional generative models over trees. Our models iteratively build trees using three natural paradigms -- top-down, middle-out, or bottom-up -- and are trained using a discrete diffusion framework. For real and synthetic datasets of up to $65$ taxa and $&gt;10^5$ tokens, our models recover MCMC posteriors much more accurately than the previous state-of-the-art simulation-based method Phyloformer 2. Finally, we compare to IQ-Tree, a popular flexible-but-misspecified conventional method. Our models match its tree estimates on simple data, but on more realistic data, our method is reliably more accurate.

</details>

<a id="paper-184"></a>

### 184. [WorldMemBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Memory of Video World Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19063v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19063

**作者：** Nan Zhou、Jianxiong Gao、Qiuyu Wang、Wen Wang、Jiahao Wang、Yufeng Yuan、Shuaiting Li、Hao Ouyang、Yujun Shen、Weiming Lu、Jun Xiao、Yueting Zhuang、Yongliang Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19063v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19063v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WorldMemBench，包含 260 个测试用例，通过受控的相机离开与多次重访轨迹，从静态内容保持和动态事件状态延续两方面评测视频世界模型的「世界记忆」，并结合外观与几何指标及视频质量、相机跟随诊断，评测了九个代表性模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「静态保持好不等于动态状态可靠」「多次重访后记忆退化」的观测，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的内容保留、信息坍缩与重访时表征退化提供可核查的评测思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WorldMemBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Memory of Video World Models

**abstract/A1：** As video models enable longer and more realistic interactive experiences, maintaining a world consistent with its history becomes essential. This requires both preserving previously observed content and ensuring coherent world evolution over time. World memory is a critical capability to evaluate alongside visual fidelity and video duration. We introduce WorldMemBench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating world memory in video world models. Across 260 test cases, it assesses static memory through the retention of human identity and appearance, object appearance and geometry, and environmental content and geometry, and dynamic memory through event progression and state persistence. Using controlled camera departures and multi-revisit trajectories, the benchmark evaluates static content against fixed initial references and dynamic events against expected state transitions. It combines appearance and geometry metrics with event-specific assessments, and reports video quality and camera-following accuracy as complementary diagnostics. Evaluation of nine representative models shows that strong static retention does not imply reliable dynamic state evolution, while repeated revisits reveal memory degradation beyond the first return. Human studies support the alignment between our automatic evaluations and human judgments. WorldMemBench provides a systematic testbed for diagnosing memory failures and guiding the development of persistent, temporally coherent video worlds.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages, 10 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-185"></a>

### 185. [MonoMIP: Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping for Monocular 3D Object Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19071v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19071

**作者：** Emir Ulurak、Furkan Kıraç

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19071v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19071v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向单目 3D 目标检测，提出双解码器框架 MonoMIP：在地面到车顶的 K 个高度平面上做逆透视变换，得到按高度排序的度量 BEV 特征栈，再用多尺度可变形注意力按 query 聚合多高度证据，与深度引导的透视图解码器互补，在 KITTI 与 Waymo 上优于近期单目方法。

**相关性简评：** 其「多高度平面特征栈 + 按 query 聚合多高度证据」接近视觉侧的多假设表示与证据筛选，可能为 LVR 中多种 latent 假设及视觉证据筛选提供线索；但任务属驾驶场景 3D 检测，迁移到 VLM 推理尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MonoMIP: Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping for Monocular 3D Object Detection

**abstract/A1：** Monocular 3D object detection methods increasingly rely on depth-guided transformers, but they operate entirely in the perspective image plane where spatial relationships are distorted by foreshortening. We propose MonoMIP, a dual-decoder framework that adds complementary 3D geometric reasoning through Bird's-Eye View (BEV) features constructed via Multi-Height Inverse Perspective Mapping. We perform IPM at K elevation planes spanning ground to rooftop, producing an ordered stack of height-conditioned metric BEV features that reduces above-ground misprojection and exposes depth-dependent appearance variation across planes. A BEV decoder with multi-scale deformable attention aggregates this multi-height evidence per query, while a depth-guided 3D decoder provides complementary perspective-space predictions. Without extra training data, MonoMIP attains 25.84% Moderate AP3D\|R40 on KITTI val and 21.66% on the official test set for Car, outperforming recent published monocular methods on both the KITTI and Waymo benchmarks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to ACCV 2026. 18 pages main paper and 14 pages supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-186"></a>

### 186. [Theoretical and Practical Advances in Hierarchical Bayesian Deep Learning Methodology](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19074v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19074

**作者：** Neil K. Chada、Simon Cotter、Kody J. H. Law、Xinzhu Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.CO、stat.ME

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19074v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19074v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MLPMMH 方法，用伪边缘推断处理层级超参数、用耦合序列蒙特卡洛在按网络宽度截断的层级上做条件参数推断，给出 MSE 与计算成本的理论阶，并设计 PCA 近似的可扩展版本在 MNIST 上验证不确定性与预测表现。

**相关性简评：** 其粒子滤波/序列蒙特卡洛式的序贯状态推断与层级截断，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的逐层累积、停止决策及计算—精度成本评价提供概念性核查线索，但属跨领域推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Theoretical and Practical Advances in Hierarchical Bayesian Deep Learning Methodology

**abstract/A1：** Reliable uncertainty quantification remains an important challenge in AI. Hierarchical Bayesian methods provide a principled framework for this by representing unknown quantities through posterior distributions. Joint inference of neural-network parameters and prior hyperparameters is challenging for conventional Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods due to strong dependencies. In this work, we introduce the multilevel particle marginal Metropolis-Hastings (MLPMMH) method, which combines pseudo-marginal inference for the hyperparameters with coupled sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) across hierarchy of network-width truncations for conditional parameter inference. The hierarchy is induced by a trace-class neural network (TNN) prior with width-dependent decay, motivated empirically by the compressible structure observed in trained neural-network parameters. Under standard multilevel coupling assumptions, we establish that MLPMMH achieves mean-square error (MSE) $\\mathcal{O}(\\varepsilon^2)$ at expected computational cost $\\mathcal{O}(\\varepsilon^{-2})$, and verify the predicted rate on a Bayesian regression problem. To address larger-scale applications, we further introduce S-MLPMMH, a practical approximation that replaces the exact sampling components with a PCA-based hierarchy and scalable Bayesian Monte Carlo. Experiments on MNIST demonstrate competitive predictive performance and uncertainty quantification while exploiting the parallel structure of the approximation. Software has been made publicly available at https://github.com/submission-code-anon/mlpmmh-anon.

**comment/C1：** 43 pages

</details>

### 187. [Beyond Aggregate Accuracy: Item-Level Transitions in Prompt Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19084v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19084

**作者：** Chinmoy Mitra、Tarek Mahmud

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19084v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19084v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RFDPO，在自动 prompt 优化中用「恢复／退化／总变动」等逐题结果转移指标替代聚合准确率，并在法律、知识、指令遵循、数学与隐私五个基准上比较验证集与留出测试的增益。

**相关性简评：** 该文属于 prompt 优化的评价方法学，与 LVR 无直接关系；但其「逐样本转移＋churn」视角可能为 LVR 评价提供线索，用于核查 latent 训练或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 策略是否在提高整体表现的同时掩盖大量逐题得失，以及收益是否泛化到未见问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Aggregate Accuracy: Item-Level Transitions in Prompt Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Automatic prompt optimization (APO) uses feedback from model responses to improve prompts automatically. However, its effectiveness is usually measured through overall scores, which do not reveal which questions become correct, which previously correct answers become wrong, or whether improvements extend to unseen questions. We introduce Reflective Feedback-Driven Prompt Optimization (RFDPO), a tool that tracks recoveries (incorrect to correct), regressions (correct to incorrect), and churn (all changed outcomes). RFDPO uses this feedback to guide subsequent revisions and records prompt changes throughout optimization. We evaluate RFDPO across five benchmarks covering law, knowledge, instruction following, mathematics, and privacy, using GPT-5 as the optimizer and GPT-4o-mini and GPT-4.1 Mini as solvers. Our results show that overall accuracy can hide substantial changes: in five of nine configurations, churn was at least five times the absolute net change, and a single-item net change concealed up to 17 changed outcomes. Average validation gains were 7.9 percentage points, compared with 5.2 points on held-out tests. In a controlled ablation on LegalBench-Hearsay, item-level feedback reduced churn by 40% ($p=0.024$) while maintaining similar net gains. These findings motivate reporting recovery, regression, churn, and performance on unseen data alongside overall accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-188"></a>

### 188. [PairIT: Autoregressive Transformers for Low-Data Molecule Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19123v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19123

**作者：** Hannah Lawrence、Bodhi P. Vani、Ji Won Park、Natasa Tagasovska、Samuel Don Stanton、Andrew Martin Watkins、Michael Maser、Ewa M. Nowara

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、physics.chem-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19123v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19123v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PairIT：在 SMILES 序列上用自回归 Transformer 学习结构相似分子对，实现以种子分子为条件的局部化学编辑生成，并用 prompt token 指定目标性质、改动方向与近似幅度，支持多性质拼接优化与 RL 后训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「以种子为条件、由 prompt 控制改动方向与幅度、并在贴近种子与优化目标间权衡」的可控局部编辑思路，可能与 LVR 中控制 latent 状态更新量与推理长度、以及在保留视觉证据与推进推理之间取舍的问题存在概念联系，属远领域启发，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PairIT: Autoregressive Transformers for Low-Data Molecule Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Generating novel molecules with desired properties is a central challenge in drug discovery. Although foundational work focused on de novo generation, practical use cases require a more complex setting: conditioning on an initial seed molecule, optimizing multiple properties, and operating in data-sparse regimes. We introduce PairIT, a flexible training framework for autoregressive transformers over SMILES sequences that enables seeded generation by learning from pairs of structurally similar molecules. By leveraging matched pairs, the model learns to generate local, chemically meaningful edits that improve a property of the seed molecule. Prompt tokens extend this framework by specifying the target property, desired direction, and approximate magnitude of change. Multiple prompt tokens can be concatenated to enable multi-property optimization at inference time. For properties where pair supervision alone is insufficient, reinforcement learning post-training can further improve optimization, providing a tunable trade-off between proximity to the seed and the optimization objective. PairIT naturally supports pretraining on a large dataset and fine-tuning on a smaller dataset, which is critical for performance in data-sparse regimes. Surprisingly, we observe diminishing returns to the scale of the pretraining dataset. We demonstrate empirically on MoleculeNet and Therapeutics Data Commons datasets that, with appropriate paired-data curation, a standard transformer model of modest size (68M parameters) can achieve robust seeded molecular optimization in low-data regimes.

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [Imagining Goals with Geometric Horizon Models for Policy Generalization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19129v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19129

**作者：** Daniel Lawson、Dhruv Sreenivas、Adriana Hugessen、Khimya Khetarpal、Glen Berseth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19129v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19129v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TD-Aug，从几何 horizon 模型采样，直接「想象」出由已有知识组合而成的新未来结果，并将其作为目标训练 goal-conditioned BC 策略，在 OGBench 的 stitching 任务上提升零样本组合式目标泛化。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联（机器人离线策略学习），但用几何 horizon 模型表示并采样可组合的未来状态来生成监督目标，可能为 latent 轨迹/状态生成、组合泛化的构造与评价提供可核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Imagining Goals with Geometric Horizon Models for Policy Generalization

**abstract/A1：** Training from offline data has allowed for substantial progress in domains such as robotics, leading to general-purpose policies that can be easily applied zero-shot or efficiently finetuned for downstream tasks. However, these policies can still have poor generalization, both due to the choice of modeling objective and from learning from a static dataset. In this work, we focus on the challenging task of zero-shot goal generalization, where a policy is evaluated on unseen tasks that require combining its existing knowledge (compositional generalization). An avenue for improving a policy's generalization is by generating new experience with world models; however, such generation has proven difficult for longer horizons. Thus, to alleviate this issue, we propose TD-Aug, which samples from a geometric horizon model and allows for directly imagining novel outcomes that can be achieved by composing existing knowledge. We demonstrate that training on these future outcomes as goals for goal-conditioned BC policies significantly improves generalization in stitching-based OGBench tasks.

</details>
