### 190. [MA-Bench: Measuring Operational Planning in Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19138

**作者：** Rodney Lafuente-Mercado、Robert Sincero、Phil Sage、Yuan Xue

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19138v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MA-Bench：用 10 个专家构建的军事「任务分析」场景（每场景 10–12 份合成文档加 1,257 条人工通过的 rubric），考察语言模型在信息不全且部分矛盾时能否先界定问题再成文，并以推理模型作为第五位评分者；结果显示最强模型仅答对 62%，需推断的判断最弱（33%），六成失败是遗漏而非错误。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本规划评测，与视觉 latent 状态无直接机制联系；但其 rubric 条目级评分、评分者一致性验证（Fleiss κ、跨模型 Spearman 复现）以及「遗漏多于错误」的失败分类，可能为 LVR 的推理过程评价、证据筛选诊断与停止/覆盖率度量提供参照。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MA-Bench: Measuring Operational Planning in Language Models

**abstract/A1：** How well can a language model turn a large, incomplete and partly contradictory body of documents into a plan when no single correct answer exists? We introduce Mission Analysis (MA)-Bench, built on mission analysis, the first step of military operational planning, in which a staff reads the full situation and states the problem before anyone proposes a solution. Its 10 expert-built scenarios pair synthetic packets of ten to twelve documents with 1,257 expert-written pass/fail requirements, each containing information on their source, question type, and importance level. A reasoning-model judge grades each brief against the requirements without seeing the documents; it grades like a fifth credentialed planner, leaving a four-planner panel's agreement unchanged (Fleiss $κ$ 0.42 to 0.41), and a judge from a second provider reproduces the model ranking (Spearman 0.94). Across twenty frontier and open-weights models the best gets 62% of the rubric items right. Models are strongest on facts stated in the documents (58%) and weakest on judgments that must be inferred (33%), and six failures in ten are omissions rather than errors. MA-Bench grades the determinations, not the prose. We release the packets, rubrics, prompts, scorer and every graded brief with item-level verdicts.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/ScaleAI/MA-Bench

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Into the Unknown: Benchmarking AI agents on open scientific research problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19159v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19159

**作者：** Seungone Kim、Jiayi Geng、Young-Jun Lee、Bo Liu、Juyoung Suk、Nicholas Lubbers、Mitchell Wood、Marcus Noack、Yong-Xin Yao、Yuan Shi、Roxana Bujack、Cristina Garcia Cardona、Austin Rodriguez、Ilon Joseph、Woojin Cho、Zora Zhiruo Wang、Chuanyang Jin、Ian Wu、Apurva Gandhi、Tianjian Li、Weiwei Sun、Carolin Lawrence、Kiril Gashteovski、Dongyeop Kang、Jay Shin、Akari Asai、Graham Neubig、Sean Welleck

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19159v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19159v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「Into the Unknown」基准：让 9 个前沿 agent 各就 DOE 国家实验室科学家提供的未发表后续研究方向写出 15 篇后续论文，以科学家给出的条件构成 rubric 进行评估，结果 135 篇无一满足全部条件，114 篇未通过完整性检查。

**相关性简评：** 其「完整性检查」关注产出是否声称未做的工作、数字是否与记录证据矛盾，可能为核查推理输出是否真实使用内部或视觉证据、以及在无参考答案情形下设计评估标准提供借鉴线索，属较远领域，需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Into the Unknown: Benchmarking AI agents on open scientific research problems

**abstract/A1：** Can AI agents carry out research on open scientific problems? We introduce Into the Unknown, a benchmark that provides an agent with a science paper along with a research direction to pursue and tasks it to write a follow-up paper. We work with scientists at US Department of Energy (DOE) national laboratories, who each contribute an unpublished follow-up direction from their own work together with the necessary conditions a submission must meet. While assessing the quality of a follow-up paper is challenging in general, using these conditions to form a rubric makes evaluation more tractable for research problems that have no reference answer. We have nine frontier agents write 15 follow-ups each, spanning quantum computing, plasma physics, computational chemistry, materials science, and scientific machine learning domains. None of the 135 submissions satisfies all the conditions, and 114 fail our integrity check, most often because the paper claims work that was never done or reports numbers that contradict the recorded evidence. Overall, agents readily produce papers that look like follow-ups, but none meets the standard of the domain scientist, leaving substantial headroom. Our code and data are publicly available.

**comment/C1：** preprint under review

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [ListQA: A Benchmark for Evaluating List-Formatted Factual Knowledge Retrieval in Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19165v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19165

**作者：** Mayank Kulkarni、Rakesh Vaideeswaran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19165v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19165v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ListQA 基准：9,045 道人工校验题目，要求模型回忆多个事实并组织成 3–10 元素的结构化列表；以二分图最优匹配加 LLM 语义打分联合评估事实准确、幻觉与格式，测试 34 个模型，最佳者 LLM-Judge-F1 不足 37%，且补充维基原文后得分大幅提升。

**相关性简评：** 其「能给证据就组织得好、仅靠参数则检索失败」的拆分与来源上下文对照，可能为 LVR 中「必要信息是否留在内部状态、是否被外部旁路替代」提供可核查的探针范式；但论文为纯文本事实检索、不涉及视觉或 latent 生成，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ListQA: A Benchmark for Evaluating List-Formatted Factual Knowledge Retrieval in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Many real-world questions demand not a single fact but an organized collection of them, yet existing factual knowledge benchmarks almost exclusively target single answer retrieval. We introduce ListQA, a benchmark of 9,045 human-curated, cross-validated questions (audited error rate of 1.7%) that require LLMs to recall multiple facts and compose them into structured lists of 3-10 elements. Questions range from flat lists ("Which NCAA teams went undefeated between 2000 and 2024?") to hierarchical lists with sub-attributes ("...and what was their record and result?"), spanning eight categories across 60+ countries. An evergreen design (explicit temporal constraints or historically immutable facts) keeps ground truth valid without periodic updates. For evaluation, we frame element matching as a linear sum assignment problem: optimal bipartite matching paired with LLM-based semantic grading jointly captures factual accuracy, hallucination, and format compliance. We benchmark 34 models (8 families, 42 configurations across standard and thinking enabled inference) and find: the best standard-inference model scores below 37% LLM-Judge-F1, multi-faceted questions are harder across the board, and providing the source Wikipedia page lifts scores dramatically, confirming that models can organize facts but struggle to retrieve them from parameters alone. Extended analyses cover base vs. instruction-tuned checkpoints and token efficiency. We release the dataset and evaluation code.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026, Evaluations and Datasets Track. Project Page: https://listqa.github.io

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [Identity, Incentives, and Cooperative Behavior for AI: Reverse-Engineering Multiagent Cooperation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19188v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19188

**作者：** Andrew Perrault、Bryan Wilder

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19188v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19188v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一套「反向工程」框架，用于推断多智能体协作行为背后的机制是利他偏好还是工具性自利，并给出可区分竞争性解释的实验设计，在模拟环境中应用于若干前沿模型，发现不同模型的行为源自实质不同的机制。

**相关性简评：** 其「多种机制可产生相似可观测行为、需用实验设计加以区分」的思路，可能为 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容、区分推理与视觉旁路／捷径提供诊断线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Identity, Incentives, and Cooperative Behavior for AI: Reverse-Engineering Multiagent Cooperation

**abstract/A1：** The behavior of AI systems increasingly depends on the extent to which agents cooperate or coordinate with one another, in ways that may be desirable or not depending on the situation. Conventional explanations for cooperative behavior distinguish between purely other-regarding preferences ("altruism") and instrumental reasons for self-interested agents to cooperate. The way that agents should enact these mechanisms for cooperation (or not) is complicated by the fact that there is not a clear or unambiguous locus of identity or selfhood for AI agents. For example, it is unclear whether agents involved in instances of misaligned collective behavior are motivated by goals at the level of the collective or by various forms of instrumental reasoning. We develop a framework for reverse-engineering why a given set of agents engage in a particular set of cooperation-related behaviors. We argue that many different mechanisms can often produce similar observed behaviors and introduce a set of experimental designs along with conditions under which they can successfully disentangle competing explanations for multiagent behavior. We apply this framework to interactions from a set of frontier models in a simulated environment and demonstrate that the behavior of different models arises from substantively different mechanisms.

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [Adaptive Computation with Conformal Certification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19189v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19189

**作者：** Shehtab Zaman、Aneesh Rangnekar、Nancy Lee、Harini Veeraraghavan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19189v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19189v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Conformal Adaptation，用分层模型先在低分辨率预测、以 conformal risk control 认证信号可信度、再对通过认证的部分做细化，在 3D 医学分割、星系通量预测和钙信号尖峰率预测上实现 2.5–72 倍加速并附带不确定性。

**相关性简评：** 科学影像与 VLM 推理距离较远，但其「先粗算、经风险认证再决定是否继续细化」的动态计算与证据筛选思路，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策和计算成本—收益权衡提供可核查的类比。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adaptive Computation with Conformal Certification

**abstract/A1：** Scientific imaging data involving medical (e.g., 3D radiographic, microscopic images) and astronomical sensors pose different challenges from natural images. One key challenge is that these datasets are often orders of magnitude larger than natural images. Efficient training and inference on such data is challenging due to the increased hardware requirements, often needing bespoke solutions. Moreover, the relevant objects such as tumors and astronomical phenomena are sparsely distributed with respect to the background information. Standard efficiency techniques like quantization and pruning apply uniform scaling of all regions, potentially compromising the precision/fidelity of predictions. Thus, efficient methods that reduce computational requirements without losing resolution for relevant parts of the data and with uncertainty estimates on predictions are essential. We present \\textit{Conformal Adaptation} with risk guarantees for uncertainty-aware sparse computations. Our formalism is general, spanning dense pixel-level classification and regression tasks where hierarchical models predict at low resolution, certify the signal using conformal risk control, and refine the certified predictions. We benchmark our method on multiple tasks such as 3D medical image segmentation, 2D galaxy flux prediction, and 1D calcium spike rate prediction, achieving $2.5$--$72\\times$ wall-clock speedup across the various tasks, including on commodity CPUs.

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [Narrowing the Collaboration Gap, Probably](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19215v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19215

**作者：** Mirah Shi、Marcel Hussing、Ira Globus-Harris、Natalie Collina、Aaron Roth、Surbhi Goel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19215v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19215v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究多智能体在部分信息下如何简洁通信：比较只传动作与传对动作的校准信念（概率），在合成设定与协同迷宫任务（训练过的小 transformer 和预训练 LLM）中检验，并提出事后对话校准干预来纠正偏差。

**相关性简评：** 「传递校准信念而非动作」这一通信接口与 LVR 中 latent 状态是否承载必要不确定性、信息在接口处坍缩、以及如何评价和修正 latent 内容的问题可能相通，可供核查其信念状态视角能否迁移到 latent 推理状态的监督与评价。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Narrowing the Collaboration Gap, Probably

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are increasingly deployed as teams of agents that hold different private information about a shared task. How should such agents concisely communicate in a way that promotes aggregation of decision-relevant information? Communicating only proposed actions can discard decision-relevant uncertainty: two agents may prefer the same action for different reasons. We study an alternative interface in which agents communicate calibrated beliefs over actions. We first analyze a simple synthetic setting that captures the structure of multi-round reasoning under partial information. In this setting, we show theoretically and empirically that communicating unbiased probabilities can be strictly more powerful than communicating actions. We then test the same principle in a collaborative maze-solving task with trained transformers and pretrained LLM agents. Our results demonstrate that probability communication outperforms action communication when the exchanged predictions preserve calibrated uncertainty. When communicated probabilities are biased, we show that the benefits of probability communication can disappear. We give a post-hoc conversational calibration intervention that consistently improves decision-making by correcting these biases both in a toy transformer setting and when pretrained LLMs use the corrections in context. These results suggest that probabilistic communication is useful for multi-agent collaboration not merely because it transmits more information, but because calibrated uncertainty gives other agents a reliable object to update on.

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [Spectral-MoE: Heterogeneous Per-Layer Expert Utilization in Dense-to-MoE Conversion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19232v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19232

**作者：** Zachary Broveak、Christian Kubicka、Thierry Tambe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19232v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19232v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Spectral-MoE，在稠密 FFN 到 MoE 的转换中学习按层非均匀分配专家激活预算（静态的逐层标量与动态的逐 token 调度），在解析式与学习式两类 SOTA 转换方法上均优于每层统一利用率。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，但其「按层/按 token 分配计算预算并学习该分配」的视角，可能为 LVR 中动态计算的预算分配与逐步骤调度提供概念类比，线索较远，仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spectral-MoE: Heterogeneous Per-Layer Expert Utilization in Dense-to-MoE Conversion

**abstract/A1：** Large language models impose substantial memory demands and arithmetic costs during inference. Dense-to-mixture-of-experts (MoE) conversion is a model optimization technique that aims to reduce these arithmetic costs. Dense-to-MoE conversion partitions pretrained feed-forward network (FFN) neurons into experts and selects a subset for each token. Leading methods fall into two categories: analytic methods produce a converted model quickly using heuristics, while learning methods produce a model much more slowly by optimizing via gradients. Both approaches, however, fix the same active expert count, or "utilization," at every layer. We introduce Spectral-MoE, which learns to allocate a utilization budget heterogeneously across layers. Its name draws a metaphor between separating a dense FFN into experts and separating a light beam into its spectrum. Spectral-MoE consists of two distinct approaches: a static approach that uses a single per-layer scalar which is shared across tokens, and a dynamic approach which emits a per-token schedule. We demonstrate that both allocators improve performance over uniform on both the analytic-based and learning-based SOTA dense-to-MoE methods. At 25% FFN utilization, dynamic allocation reduces WikiText-2 perplexity relative to uniform by 1.04, 2.96, and 2.71 points on the learned base for Llama-2-7B, Llama-3-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B, respectively. The corresponding reductions on the analytic base are 2.45, 6.26, and 2.17 points. On the learned base, dynamic allocation improves six-task mean accuracy over uniform by an average of 1.5, 4.2, and 3.6 percentage points across the three models at FFN utilizations of 12.5%, 25%, and 50%, respectively. These results establish per-layer expert utilization as a novel optimization axis in dense-to-MoE conversion.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 5 figures. Project repository: https://github.com/ZacharyBroveak/Spectral-MoE

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [Beyond Time Induction: Structured Conditional Neural Solvers for Parametric Stochastic-Volatility Pricing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19254v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19254

**作者：** Jose Miguel Lara Rangel、Mixtli Vazquez Mendoza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、q-fin.CP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19254v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19254v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 BeTINN，一种结构化条件神经求解器，通过对时间与参数的多方向条件调制及联合残差通路，在无标签条件下学习 Heston 随机波动率 PDE 的解族，并评估期权价值与一、二阶敏感性的联合精度，辅以组件消融。

**相关性简评：** 其「多方向条件调制＋残差通路」可能为 latent 状态的输入条件化更新提供架构借鉴；而「验证物理损失不能完全决定导数保真度、整体值准确可伴随局部导数误差」这一发现，可类比 latent 推理评价中总体指标掩盖局部失败的问题，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Time Induction: Structured Conditional Neural Solvers for Parametric Stochastic-Volatility Pricing

**abstract/A1：** Physics-informed neural networks provide a flexible framework for learning parametric PDE solution families without labeled data, yet preserving accurate derivatives across heterogeneous regimes remains challenging. Structured neural solvers offer compact representations, but restrictive conditional parameterizations can limit adaptation across the solution family. We introduce BeTINN, a structured conditional architecture extending time-induced modulation through multi-directional time-parameter conditioning and a joint residual pathway. We evaluate BeTINN on the Heston stochastic-volatility PDE, a demanding parametric problem with mixed derivatives and economically relevant first- and second-order sensitivities. BeTINN improves joint accuracy across normalized option value and key sensitivity metrics relative to our baselines. Controlled ablations characterize the roles of its architectural components. We further find that validation physics loss is informative but does not fully determine differential fidelity, and that accurate solution values can coexist with localized derivative errors. These results support structured conditional adaptation and motivate derivative-aware evaluation when sensitivities are downstream quantities of interest.

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Dimension-Free Scaling Laws for Size Generalization: An Exponential Separation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19279v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19279

**作者：** Behrooz Tahmasebi、Melanie Weber

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19279v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19279v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文为集合与图等可变规模数据建立统计学习理论，证明 size generalization 的样本复杂度存在指数级分离：多项式衰减的源条件下跨规模泛化误差仅为 (log n)^{-Θ(1)}，并给出匹配的 minimax 下界与与输入维度无关的 scaling law。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨输入规模迁移的统计代价」表述可能与 LVR 的长度泛化及动态计算成本问题相关，可供核查其无维度界与下界技术是否可迁移到多模态推理状态的规模外推分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dimension-Free Scaling Laws for Size Generalization: An Exponential Separation

**abstract/A1：** Modern machine learning models increasingly operate on variable-size structured data, such as sets and graphs. A central requirement is \\emph{size generalization}: the ability of a model trained on inputs of one size to generalize to inputs of a different, potentially much larger, size. Classical statistical learning theory, however, primarily considers fixed input domains, and its guarantees often deteriorate with input dimension. Although extensive work has established when models on sets and graphs can be transferred across input sizes, such results do not characterize the statistical cost of doing so: the sample complexity of size generalization, and its relationship to classical in-domain learning, remain largely unknown. We develop a statistical learning theory of size generalization and establish a sharp exponential separation in sample complexity. Letting $n$ denote the number of training samples, we show that polynomial source decay is insufficient for efficient size generalization: while it yields error $n^{-Θ(1)}$ and hence polynomial sample complexity for in-domain learning, size-generalization error decays only as $(\\log n)^{-Θ(1)}$, yielding an exponential separation. Thus, polynomial in-domain sample complexity can become exponential across sizes. We then identify an exponential source condition under which size generalization achieves error $n^{-Θ(1)}$, recovering polynomial sample complexity. These guarantees are independent of input dimension and depend only on the number of training samples, yielding dimension-free scaling laws. We also provide matching minimax lower bounds, establishing optimality. To our knowledge, this work provides the first dimension-free minimax theory of size generalization and a rigorous characterization of when, and at what statistical cost, learning can transfer across input sizes.

**comment/C1：** 32 pages, 1 figure. An extended abstract of this paper appeared at the ICML 2026 HiLD Workshop (July 10, 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [Exact Scaling Laws for a Random-Features Bigram Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19281v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19281

**作者：** Julian Enoch、Elvis Dohmatob

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19281v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19281v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在具有 Zipf 词频统计的瓶颈式 bigram 玩具模型上，把固定随机嵌入当作表示容量的代理，推导出预测遗憾关于模型宽度、词表规模和 Zipf 指数的精确 scaling law，并用 WikiText2、OpenWebText 上的实验验证曲线吻合。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把 scaling 归因于纯表示容量约束而非优化动态，可能为 LVR 中 latent 宽度瓶颈、容量受限与信息坍缩的度量与评价提供概念线索，但对象是纯文本 bigram，迁移性待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exact Scaling Laws for a Random-Features Bigram Model

**abstract/A1：** Understanding neural scaling laws is increasingly important for theory and practice, yet first-principles explanations remain limited even in simple settings. In this work, we study capacity-limited scaling in a bottlenecked bigram model with power-law (Zipf) token statistics, viewed as an analytically tractable toy model of a large language model. Fixed random embeddings serve as a proxy for representational capacity and induce an explicit bottleneck of width, allowing us to derive scaling laws for prediction regret as a function of model width, vocabulary size, and the Zipf exponent. Our results complement recent theoretical accounts based on optimization dynamics by isolating scaling due purely to representational constraints. In the appropriate regime, the theory recovers the functional form of classical neural scaling laws and suggests that heavy-tailed language statistics may underlie both optimization- and capacity-driven scaling. Experiments natural language datasets (WikiText2, OpenWebText) closely match the predicted curves.

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [Historical-Feature Residual Adaptation for Sequential Screening](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19315v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19315

**作者：** Yixiang Hong、Yuchen Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19315v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19315v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究固定实验预算下的序贯筛选策略：用固定历史特征构建小型排序校正，锚定于反馈揭晓前记录的基分，在 AssayFormer 上做残差式适配（20 个官方测试筛选上命中恢复率由 22.58% 升至 28.61%），并在独立非线性基模型上做了无该模型资产的小规模试点。

**相关性简评：** 其「不重训预训练策略、仅在固定预算序贯过程中以历史反馈做残差校正」的设定，可能与 LVR 中证据获取、停止决策与轻量策略适配问题相通，可供核查是否可类比到 VLM 推理状态更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Historical-Feature Residual Adaptation for Sequential Screening

**abstract/A1：** Sequential screening policies must use acquired feedback effectively under a fixed experiment budget. We study acquisition-time residual adaptation: a small ranking correction built from fixed historical features and anchored to base scores recorded before feedback is revealed. The interface accepts a history-conditioned scorer and a candidate feature table; its primary instantiation augments AssayFormer's ten-dimensional representation with 64 historical directions while retaining the pretrained model. In retrospective evaluation on 20 official-test screens from 15 recorded sources, a comparison fixed before target-outcome access increased source-macro fraction of hits recovered from 22.58% to 28.61%, exceeding all six prespecified comparators. A same-feature ordinary ranking head achieved 25.73%. Supplementary strong logistic, rank-fusion, and unprojected controls achieved 26.42%, 26.02%, and 26.78%, respectively. A separate model-independent pilot on 13 reused development screens reconstructs the features without AssayFormer assets. Across three seeds, residual adaptation improves random-forest hit recovery from 28.87% to 31.27%, while logistic recovery changes from 30.22% to 29.78%. The results show effective pretrained-policy adaptation and useful transfer to an independent nonlinear base, with gains that depend on the base model.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Agentic AI for Biological Discovery Workshop

</details>

<a id="paper-201"></a>

### 201. [Why don't Graph Transformers attend to the same token?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19324v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19324

**作者：** Alexandre Bismuth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19324v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19324v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究图 Transformer（GRIT）为何在分子图上很少形成注意力汇：证明图的对称性与局部性会限制节点可获得的注意力，并指出注意力打分函数是关键——把 gated logit 换成点积后汇率从 0.09 升到 0.68，故汇是 dot-product attention 的性质而非 Transformer 本身。

**相关性简评：** 关于注意力汇形成条件（打分函数、可区分节点、过度混合压力）的机制分析，可能为 LVR 中 latent 视觉状态的注意力结构集中、视觉证据是否被真正使用以及信息坍缩诊断提供可核查的类比视角。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why don't Graph Transformers attend to the same token?

**abstract/A1：** In large language models (LLMs), attention concentrates heavily on the first token. This 'attention sink' is believed to protect deep models against over-mixing. Even though graph transformers face a related pressure, we find that a SoTA graph transformer, GRIT, rarely forms sinks on molecules, and that it sinks less when trained on larger graphs, the opposite of the trend in LLMs. In principle, a graph can prevent sinks: we prove and show empirically that its symmetry and locality limit the attention any node can receive. On molecules, however, with distinguishable atoms and global attention, neither limit prevents sinks; what stops them lies in the model. We find that the attention scoring function is the main cause. Replacing GRIT's gated logit with a dot product raises its sink rate from 0.09 to 0.68 on identical graphs. In Graph Transformers using dot-product attention, removing the sink gives about the same error as removing a random node, while GRIT's most-attended nodes matter to its predictions. Attention sinks are therefore a property of dot-product attention rather than of Transformers. In our experiments, they form when dot-product scoring has a distinguishable node to concentrate on.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the Geometric Distributional Deep Learning (GDDL) workshop at NeurIPS 2026. 8 pages, 1 figure. Code: https://github.com/alexandre-bismuth/graph-transformer-sinks

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [Counterfactual Gate-Support Decomposition: Separating Selection from Redistribution in Training-Data Filtering](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19343v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19343

**作者：** Hyojung Han、Jongmin Kim、Seung-Hun Jeon

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19343v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19343v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CGSD 四臂、预算匹配的对照设计，将训练数据质量门的效果分解为支持内选择、支持间再分配与交互项，刻画有限数据池下的可构造条件，并在韩英/韩日检索与数学门上检验其显著性与不可构造情形。

**相关性简评：** 其「选择 vs 再分配」的因果分解思路可能可供核查，用于拆解数据或轨迹筛选对 VLM 推理训练的作用究竟来自样本级质量还是质量分布迁移；属远域统计方法，与 latent 推理状态本身无直接联系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Counterfactual Gate-Support Decomposition: Separating Selection from Redistribution in Training-Data Filtering

**abstract/A1：** A training-data quality gate changes both which examples survive within regions of the input space and how training mass is distributed across those regions, so a gate-versus-random comparison does not isolate these mechanisms. We introduce Counterfactual Gate-Support Decomposition (CGSD), a four-arm, budget-matched design that crosses gate eligibility with support distribution and decomposes the observed gate effect into within-support selection, between-support redistribution, and their interaction. The practical constraint is finite-pool realizability: we characterize mass and token-assembly conditions under which the required arms can be constructed, together with a support-extinction case in which the ordinary design is undefined. On Korean-English and Korean-Japanese retrieval, the mean within-support selection component is the only one that exceeds its calibrated null band (+29.7 and +5.6 holdout items; 30/30 positive replicates), decisively in Korean-English and marginally in Korean-Japanese; redistribution and interaction remain unresolved at the available power. The same pattern holds for a second, unrelated gate family -- a model-confidence threshold rather than a deterministic verifier -- judged against its own null (+27.3 items, 9/9 positive), so the result is not a property of one gate's mechanism. On a mathematics gate, zero-eligibility supports leave the ordinary design undefined at every budget, while the extended design is constructible only inside narrow budget bands that an exhaustive scan locates and that the natural operating point misses -- the assembly condition separating a property of the pool from a limitation of the packer. CGSD therefore returns a decomposition, a constructible-but-underpowered design, or an explicit non-constructibility result, and says which; it prevents support redistribution from being silently interpreted as sample-level gate quality.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 2 tables, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [When Is Smaller Greener? Right-Sizing Language Models for Agentic AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19345v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19345

**作者：** Sajid Siraj、Maria Barbati

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19345v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19345v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多步 agent 任务中应如何选择 LLM 规模，主张以「每个成功完成任务」的能耗、成本与时间作为评价单位，提出含交叉任务长度与三区间划分的决策模型，并用 15 个开源模型在两个多步任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「按成功完成任务计量成本」与交叉任务长度的思路，可能为 LVR 中动态计算/思考步数的收益—成本权衡及停止决策的评价提供借鉴线索；但论文不涉及视觉或 latent 状态机制，属概念层面的可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Is Smaller Greener? Right-Sizing Language Models for Agentic AI

**abstract/A1：** AI agents use large language models (LLMs) to plan and act over multiple steps, yet models are usually compared per call rather than per completed task. This paper asks: which model should an agent use to complete tasks reliably while minimising energy, cost and time? We show that per-call comparisons can be misleading for agentic AI. Because a task succeeds only if all required steps succeed, small differences in per-step reliability can produce large differences in overall task success. Failed attempts also consume resources. We therefore evaluate models using energy, cost and time per successfully completed task, treating the completed task as the functional unit. On this basis, we develop a decision model for choosing LLMs in agentic workflows. The model applies to tasks with binary or graded outcomes and to settings with or without retries. We show that each resource has a crossover task length at which a smaller, less reliable model ceases to be the greener, cheaper or faster option. These crossovers divide tasks into three regimes: for short tasks smaller models are preferred, for long tasks larger models are preferred, and only between these extremes do preferences among objectives matter. We embed these results in a two-stage framework that separates efficiency from preference. A Pareto screen first removes models that are never competitive; a weighted decision is required only when a genuine trade-off remains. A pilot study of 15 open-weight models from five developers, ranging from 0.6B to 27B parameters, on two multi-step agent tasks with measured GPU energy supports the predicted structure. The model with the lowest energy per completed task changes with task length and task type, and observed crossover points generally match those predicted from per-step measurements. These findings provide a practical framework for right-sizing language models in agentic AI.

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [Integrated Novelty Detection Systems: From Sensor Networks to Multi-Novelty Computer Vision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19347v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19347

**作者：** Irené Tematelewo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19347v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19347v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究「新颖性检测」：在气象传感器网络中评估四种雨量计堵塞质控方法并指出其局限，进而提出三种融合概率故障检测与一致性诊断的新方法；在计算机视觉中构建多类新颖性的 NOVEL-SS 数据集，并给出检测—刻画—适配的 DCA 基线系统。

**相关性简评：** 其多类新颖性的检测与刻画流程可能可供核查，用于 LVR 中判断视觉证据是否超出训练分布并据此调整证据筛选与停止策略，但这一联系属于推测，论文本身不涉及 latent 推理或 VLM 生成过程。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Integrated Novelty Detection Systems: From Sensor Networks to Multi-Novelty Computer Vision

**abstract/A1：** Novelty detection is crucial in various technological and scientific domains. Its importance spans from ensuring the reliability of sensor networks to enhancing the adaptability of computer vision systems. As AI systems become increasingly sophisticated and complex, effective anomaly detection is essential for maintaining reliability and accuracy. This work addresses novelty detection in weather sensor networks and computer vision. In weather sensor networks, particularly for monitoring rainfall, detecting blocked rain gauges can significantly impact data reliability, food security, and disaster management. This work evaluates four existing quality control methods for detecting blocked rain gauges and demonstrates their limitations, particularly in detecting clusters of faulty sensors. By comparing methods such as the Conditional Mixture Model (CMM), PSWQ (Fz-filter), Kernel Regression, and Gaussian Method, we identify their shortcomings and propose three novel quality control techniques that integrate probabilistic fault detection and consistency-based diagnosis framework to improve timely detection of blocked sensors, while reducing false alarms. In the realm of computer vision, current systems often fail to handle multiple types of novelty due to their focus on single-novelty scenarios. We highlight the need for integrated systems capable of handling multiple types of novelty. To support this, we introduce the Novel Snapshot Serengeti (NOVEL-SS) dataset, derived from the Snapshot Serengeti camera trap data, which includes various forms of novelty. We also present the DCA (Detect, Characterize, and Accommodate) baseline system, designed to handle multiple novelty by detecting, characterizing, and accommodating them. This work aims to advance the robustness and adaptability of novelty detection systems across both weather sensor networks and computer vision applications.

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [BFHD: Bidirectional Feature Harmonization Decomposition for Heterogeneous Clinical Assessments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19352v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19352

**作者：** Yuanhao Zhuo、Zixi Qin、Ling Qin、Wanqing Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19352v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19352v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把跨量表临床评估的协调问题重构为测量级的双向可恢复性问题，提出 BFHD 框架：通过双向耦合的翻译与特征级输出门控，在原测量空间显式分离可可靠互译与不可互译的成分，并在合成数据和真实评估对上比较覆盖度与子集稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「双向可恢复性 + 容差内判定 + 特征级门控分解」的评估视角，可能可供核查地迁移为判断 LVR 内部状态中哪些视觉细节维度能被双向重建、哪些属于不可迁移成分。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BFHD: Bidirectional Feature Harmonization Decomposition for Heterogeneous Clinical Assessments

**abstract/A1：** Clinical assessments are often collected using heterogeneous assessment systems across centers and time, leading to records that mix different but related sets of measurements. This motivates harmonization beyond total-score linking. We formulate clinical harmonization at the measurement level as a bidirectional recoverability problem: given paired observations from two assessment systems, the goal is to identify which measurements can be reliably translated in both directions within an application-defined tolerance, while separating non-translatable components. We propose Bidirectional Feature Harmonization Decomposition (BFHD), a feasibility-driven framework that enforces bidirectionally coupled translation and uses feature-wise output gating to produce an explicit decomposition in the original measurement space. Experiments on synthetic data and real clinical assessment pairs show that BFHD achieves broader feasible harmonization coverage and improved subset stability compared to baselines.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 2 figures, 9 tables. Extended version of the paper published in IJCAI-ECAI 2026, including additional supplementary material in the appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [Asymptotic Behavior of Transformers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19358v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19358

**作者：** Katy Blumer、Da-Cheng Juan、Jon Kleinberg、Ravi Kumar、Cyrus Rashtchian、Andrew Tomkins

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.ST

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19358v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19358v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从理论上研究 transformer 长期生成文本的渐近行为：连三 token、单层纯注意力模型都可能不收敛；而对含任意深度、LayerNorm、MLP、RoPE 及局部/全局层混合的一大类 transformer，存在 softmax 温度阈值使经验缓存分布收敛到唯一极限，且这类阈值的上确界无穷，故不存在保证全部收敛的全局最小温度。

**相关性简评：** 「缓存分布收敛」与温度阈值刻画的是状态演化是否趋于稳定，可能与 latent 推理中循环计算的状态收敛性、缓存结构和停止决策评价相关，但原文面向文本生成的渐近性质，能否迁移到多模态 latent 状态分析可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Asymptotic Behavior of Transformers

**abstract/A1：** In this paper we study the behavior of transformer networks generating text over long periods of time. We show that even a three-token, single-layer attention-only transformer can fail to converge. We then study a rich class of transformers with arbitrary finite depth, layer normalization, MLPs, RoPE encodings, and an arbitrary mix of local and global layers. Every transformer in this class has a softmax temperature threshold above which its empirical cache distribution converges to a unique limit, independent of initial conditions. We show further that, while each transformer has a temperature threshold, the supremum of this set is infinite, so there is no global minimal temperature that forces all transformers to converge.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages, 3 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Fully sequential Gaussian process regression for preference learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19391v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19391

**作者：** Ilya O. Ryzhov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19391v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19391v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对只能观测噪声成对比较、无法直接评估目标函数的连续全局优化问题，提出基于矩匹配的全序列近似高斯过程回归（均值和协方差有闭式快速更新），并给出结合信息增益与随机采样的采样策略 DOVE，在正则条件下证明统计误差与简单遗憾的收敛率。

**相关性简评：** 其顺序近似贝叶斯更新、信息增益驱动的采样准则与误差累积控制，可能为 LVR 中信念/推理状态的增量维护、证据获取与停止决策提供概念类比，但该工作无视觉或多模态成分，迁移路径仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fully sequential Gaussian process regression for preference learning

**abstract/A1：** We study continuous global optimization problems in which the objective function cannot be directly evaluated; rather, we observe only which of two selected design points appears to perform better, subject to noise. Such comparisons complicate the use of Gaussian process regression because they yield non-Gaussian posteriors. We consider a fully sequential approximation, based on moment-matching, that yields a fast closed-form update of the metamodel's mean and covariance. We then propose DOVE, a fully sequential sampling policy that combines an information gain criterion with randomized sampling designed to control error accumulation. Under fairly standard regularity conditions, we prove convergence rates for both statistical error and expected simple regret that depend on kernel smoothness. To our knowledge, these are the first such rates for fully sequential approximate Bayesian inference in continuous global optimization.

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Selective Next-Location Prediction with MetaModelSelect: A Lightweight Post-Hoc Confidence Metamodel](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19394v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19394

**作者：** Calvin Jary、Nafiseh Kahani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19394v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19394v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把轻量后验置信度元模型 MetaModelSelect 用于选择性蜂窝下一位置预测：在不重训骨干 RNN 的前提下，用预测位置的嵌入、前四大的 softmax 概率和 Softmax Concentration 判断 top-1 预测是否正确，并在风险—覆盖类指标上与多种标量置信度、查表和 logit MLP 基线比较。

**相关性简评：** 其「对冻结骨干的输出做后验置信度学习以决定是否采纳预测」的思路，可能为 LVR 中何时停止推理、是否信任 latent 答案或中间状态的可信度估计提供线索；但任务域（蜂窝移动性预测）相距较远，迁移可行性与替代性可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Selective Next-Location Prediction with MetaModelSelect: A Lightweight Post-Hoc Confidence Metamodel

**abstract/A1：** Next-location prediction can support proactive mobile-network decisions, but acting on unreliable predictions can waste resources. We apply MetaModelSelect, a lightweight post-hoc confidence metamodel, to selective cellular next-location prediction. The mobility-specific model combines a learned embedding of the predicted location with the four largest softmax probabilities and Softmax Concentration. It learns whether a frozen recurrent neural network's top-1 prediction is correct, using only its outputs and without retraining the backbone. On path-balanced Changchun cellular mobility trajectories, we evaluate backbones trained with cross-entropy (CE) and self-adaptive training plus entropy minimization (SAT+EM), comparing against eight scalar confidence scores, two location-aware lookup methods, and a raw-logit multilayer perceptron. Relative to maximum softmax probability, MetaModelSelect achieves mean per-run reductions in area under the risk--coverage curve (AURC), excess AURC (E-AURC), and area under the generalized risk--coverage curve (AUGRC) of 2.6%, 4.2%, and 3.0% for CE, and 2.0%, 3.3%, and 1.9% for SAT+EM. It also achieves the lowest reported mean risk at every evaluated partial coverage. The results show that MetaModelSelect improves confidence ranking in this mobility setting while leaving the underlying location predictor unchanged.

**comment/C1：** accepted at IEEE International Conference on Tools with Artificial Intelligence (ICTAI) 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [EpiGuard: An Autonomous Infectious-Disease Early Warning Agent Platform](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19396v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19396

**作者：** Tonglong Wei、Zheng Yuan、Lingshuai Lin、Yuhui Yao、Xing Xie、Na Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19396v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19396v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EpiGuard，一个自动监测官方传染病监测数据、自主完成风险评估的智能体平台，按证据可得性三种情形动态切换分析策略，并把有效流程沉淀为可复用标准流程，覆盖 50 余种疾病与多个地区。

**相关性简评：** 其按证据可得性（无历史、稀疏、充足）自适应选择分析策略与流程复用机制，可能为 LVR 中「何时获取证据、计算多少、如何复用推理轨迹」的决策与泛化设计提供类比线索，需核查其策略切换是否依赖显式不确定性信号。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EpiGuard: An Autonomous Infectious-Disease Early Warning Agent Platform

**abstract/A1：** Infectious disease outbreaks often spread rapidly before signs of widespread transmission become apparent, leaving a big challenge for rapid response and effective intervention. However, current infectious-disease early-warning systems face three major challenges: fragmented epidemiological evidence across diverse surveillance sources, highly uneven data availability across diseases and geographic regions, and delayed warning procedure caused by manual data collection, analysis, and reporting. To address these challenges, we present EpiGuard, an autonomous, continually self-improving agentic platform for timely and reliable early warning of more than 50 infectious diseases across different regions. EpiGuard proactively monitors and captures newly released official surveillance records, using the latest available evidence for risk assessments. It dynamically adjusts its analytical strategy across three evidence availability regimes, enabling reliable risk assessment under diverse data conditions, ranging from the absence of historical observations to sparse and abundant evidence. Moreover, EpiGuard learns from the successful execution process by preserving effective data-acquisition and analytical procedures as reusable standard operating procedures, enabling it to progressively generalize to previously unseen diseases and regions. We use officially released infectious-disease surveillance data from the China CDC and the United States CDC to evaluate EpiGuard's effectiveness. Experimental results demonstrate that EpiGuard outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting systems, improving forecasting performance by 9.68% and 14.04% on the China CDC and U.S. CDC datasets, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [Recognition Is Not Grounding: Benchmarking Multilingual Financial Evidence Extraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19432v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19432

**作者：** Lingfei Qian、Yan Wang、Xueqing Peng、Polydoros Giannouris、Nadhem Zmandar、Yi Han、Yanguang Liu、Víctor Gutiérrez-Basulto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19432v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19432v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 MULTIFINGROUND，一个基于英、法、希腊语财务披露构建的多语言基准，用于考察「概念识别」与「事实性证据穷尽还原」之间的落差，并在 15 个模型上发现高识别准确率与不完整证据回收并存。

**相关性简评：** 其「识别不等于 grounding」的评价框架、无候选标签下的概念判别与穷尽性证据选择任务，或可供核查是否能迁移为衡量 VLM 是否真正使用内部 latent 中任务必要视觉证据、而非仅看答案正确性的评测思路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recognition Is Not Grounding: Benchmarking Multilingual Financial Evidence Extraction

**abstract/A1：** Comparing financial disclosures across languages requires identifying shared accounting concepts and recovering all numerical facts that express them. Existing benchmarks evaluate aspects of these capabilities, but provide limited insight into whether both succeed on the same multilingual evidence. In particular, it remains unclear whether language models can recognize these concepts across languages and exhaustively recover their corresponding numerical evidence from the same contexts. We introduce MULTIFINGROUND, a benchmark designed to investigate this recognition-grounding gap in multilingual financial reports. Constructed from FY2025 disclosures of 90 issuers in English, French, and Greek, MULTIFINGROUND aligns independently authored reports through a shared accounting taxonomy and provides traceable fact-level annotations. The benchmark evaluates financial fact identification, concept recognition, exhaustive fact selection, and concept discrimination without candidate labels. Across 15 evaluated models, eight API and open-weight reference models achieve 97.3-99.4\\% concept recognition accuracy, yet miss at least one target fact in 26.6-41.9\\% of correctly recognized groups. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in concept discrimination and limited gains from the evaluated finance-specialized models. Our findings demonstrate that strong concept recognition can coexist with incomplete evidence recovery, highlighting the importance of evaluating semantic recognition and evidence completeness together.

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [EvoProbe: Evaluation-Driven Benchmark Evolution for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19433

**作者：** Qianhong Guo、Wei Xie、Xiaofang Cai、Shuoyoucheng Ma、Chengxin Hu、Baosheng Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19433v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EvoProbe：一个由评估驱动的 benchmark 演化框架，把任务生成、可验证子任务评分与多评审轨迹诊断、反馈再生成连成闭环，并在网络安全任务上对八个模型做两轮实验，揭示聚合分数之外的阶段级瓶颈与失败模式。

**相关性简评：** 其按阶段诊断轨迹瓶颈、归纳成功与失败模式的做法，可能为 LVR 推理轨迹的分阶段评价（如定位 THINK/LOOK/STOP 各阶段收益与失败）提供评价思路或线索；但对象是纯文本 LLM agent、无视觉与 latent 状态，可迁移性需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvoProbe: Evaluation-Driven Benchmark Evolution for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Static benchmarks for large language model (LLM) agents face saturation and data contamination risks, motivating continual updates to evaluation tasks. However, aggregate scores and success rates offer limited insight into specific problem-solving bottlenecks and provide limited guidance for subsequent task design. We propose EvoProbe, an evaluation-driven benchmark evolution framework that integrates task generation, diagnostic evaluation, and feedback-driven regeneration into a closed loop. It combines verifiable subtask outcome scoring with evidence-supported multi-judge trajectory evaluation and distills phase-level bottlenecks, success patterns, and failure patterns into diagnostic feedback for generating new executable tasks. Across two rounds of cybersecurity experiments, evaluations of eight models on 66 tasks reveal phase-level performance differences and common failure patterns beyond aggregate scores. In a cross-round comparison with the Examiner model set and solver configuration held fixed, all eight models achieve lower Outcome scores on the updated tasks, with relative decreases of 11.8\\% in mean Outcome and 40.0\\% in mean Pass\\textasciicircum{}3. These results support using fine-grained diagnostics to guide iterative task updates and probe agents' current capabilities and limitations.

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [xLightGCN: Explainable Graph Neural Network Based Recommendation Systems with Most Influential Peers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19446v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19446

**作者：** Calder Johnson、Robin Cohen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19446v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19446v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 xLightGCN，一种建立在 LightGCN 类推荐模型之上的精确图解释方法，通过识别「最具影响力的邻居」为用户生成推荐决策的解释，并给出解释保真度的理论保证，同时讨论其局限与后续方向。

**相关性简评：** 其「影响力邻居/子图」归因方式与解释保真度度量，可能为 LVR 中「答案是否真正使用 latent 内容」的贡献分配与忠实性评价提供类比线索，可供人工核查；但论文属推荐系统领域，未涉及多模态或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** xLightGCN: Explainable Graph Neural Network Based Recommendation Systems with Most Influential Peers

**abstract/A1：** Recommendation systems, systems that predict a user's preference for items in order to curate that user's feed, are a ubiquitous and central feature in online social networks. While they are a central piece of our modern digital infrastructure, many modern approaches are opaque black boxes, making them difficult to audit, understand, and assess the trustworthiness of. In this paper we present an exact, graph-based approach for generating explanations for decisions made by recommendation systems. We argue that in the risk-sensitive sociotechnical context of user-facing AI such as recommendation systems, explainability is a key pillar for establishing the trustworthiness of models and validating their effectiveness. We propose a technique built on LightGCN like architectures, an influential and popular class of recommendation models. We describe our approach and argue that it provides an appropriate assurance mechanism for these AI systems leading to specific advantages, including interpretable explanations and a theoretically guaranteed measure of explanation fidelity. We conclude by discussing limitations and directions for future research.

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [SKG: A Law-Induced Engine from Inference to Induction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19505v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19505

**作者：** Jian An、Chaoshi Wu、Jinhui Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19505v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19505v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SKG（Symplectic-Kernel Gradient）推理引擎，把辛几何、GENERIC、核方法与 RKHS、群等变网络和约束力学整合为统一框架，处理由「定律」约束的推断与归纳，并给出度量重构、核带宽由 Fisher 信息唯一确定、泛化与 OOD 增益、学习解耦等理论结果。

**相关性简评：** 其状态流形上由不变量经 Jacobian 构造度量、核带宽由 Fisher 信息决定的理论，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的表示几何与相似度度量提供概念参照；「归纳的唯一对象是任务能量参数」可供核查监督目标与贡献分配问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SKG: A Law-Induced Engine from Inference to Induction

**abstract/A1：** This paper proposes the SKG (Symplectic-Kernel Gradient) reasoning engine for law-induced inference and induction. Laws are a priori; data provide instances. Inference is geometric evolution under law constraints. We organize symplectic geometry, GENERIC, kernel methods, RKHS, group-equivariant networks, and constrained mechanics into a unified, analyzable, implementable, verifiable framework. We propose the SKG octuple S = (M, g, omega, H, S, G, Delta, Pi), whose four components correspond to four levels of laws: conservation laws, constitutive relations, symmetry groups, and axiom systems. Main results: (1) Invariants enter metric construction via the Jacobian: g = J^T Sigma^-1 J + epsilon\_G I. For constant cases, we establish inverse reconstruction theory: solvability rank condition (Thm 3.9), consistency criterion (Thm 3.11), and uniqueness theorem (Thm 3.14). (2) The kernel bandwidth matrix is uniquely determined by Fisher information and satisfies task-independence (Thm 3.19). (3) The metric reconstruction error decomposes into four terms (Thm 3.21), with squared condition number kappa(Sigma^\*)^2 as the dominant amplification factor (Thm 3.22). (4) For state-dependent cases, we establish function-space reconstruction theory on matrix-valued RKHS (Thms 4.8, 4.9). (5) Three-layer projections handle axiom, symplectic-step, and conservation constraints. (6) The residual equation yields the observable formula, four-dimensional rank condition, and noise propagation, while g\_qq is structurally unidentifiable. (7) Kernel parameterization yields the RKHS extrapolation bound (Thm 4.15) and strict OOD gain theorem in group directions (Thm 8.15). (8) The learning-decoupling theorem (Thm 9.1) proves the sole object of induction is the parameters of the task energy. Validation: 1D damped oscillator and 2D rotation-invariant system; 6 unit tests, 10 experiments (paired t-test, p &lt; 0.01).

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [Structure Before Intelligence: Gating What Agentic Scientific Workflows Release](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19507v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19507

**作者：** Ashish Acharya、Aman Al Amin、Hong Qin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19507v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19507v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为 agentic 科学工作流提出一个围绕 DAG 的发布控制 harness：强制检查、把故障限制在受影响分支、按版本重算、加入验证器，并由风险模型判断输出是否可以发布；在注入故障的 60 个配对实例上开关各组件做消融，发现隔离与恢复提高正确数、验证提供安全性、风险估计以更少验证器达到同等安全，语言模型本身无可测增益。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但「验证器 + 风险阈值决定是否发布」可能为推理中的 STOP 决策与证据获取的成本—收益权衡提供可核查的概念类比，其语言模型无增益、收益来自外部结构的结论也提示值得对照结构与模型能力的分工。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structure Before Intelligence: Gating What Agentic Scientific Workflows Release

**abstract/A1：** An agentic scientific workflow can run to completion and still release wrong results: a silent numerical error, a stale input, or a defect in a shared upstream step can contaminate several outputs while a sibling branch stays correct. We treat trust in such results as a release decision. A harness around the workflow's directed acyclic graph (DAG) decides which outputs may be used: it enforces mandatory checks, contains failures to the affected branch, recomputes from tracked versions, adds validators, and releases a result only if a risk model estimates its probability of being invalid as low. Language-model agents work inside the harness, choosing corrective actions and reviewing releases, but cannot override it. On 60 held-out paired instances of two workflows with injected faults, we switched components on and off. Each supplied a different property. Containment and recovery raised the number of correct results. Validation supplied safety: fixed rules without validation released 37 invalid results, while validating every candidate released none. Risk estimation supplied efficiency, matching that safety with 31% fewer validators at somewhat lower yield, although better targeting than random validation was not shown. Lineage evidence helped on one workflow. The language model added no measurable benefit: whether invalid results escaped depended on the harness, not the model. This proof of concept points to a practical path: placing agents inside explicit release-control structure can keep released scientific results trustworthy, and it gives more capable agents a measurable baseline to improve on.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages: 10-page main paper and 11-page supplement; 2 figures

</details>
