### 215. [Revisiting Triplet Contrastive Learning for 3D Shape Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19519v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19519

**作者：** Chaoyang Zhu、Zhe Liao、Jiayi Zhang、Jie Li、Jiayi Ji、Liujuan Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19519v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19519v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出渲染图像监督存在域差与自遮挡等不可靠因素，转而仅用语言学习可迁移的3D表示：把3D表示嵌入双曲空间，并用「语言蕴含3D形状」的部分序损失与测地对比目标联合训练，另提出跨模态对齐基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示几何」思路（双曲空间建模树状层级、非对称蕴含约束）可能可供核查：LVR 中视觉-语言层级与实体关系是否被保留在 latent 状态、以及跨模态 latent 空间该用何种几何结构。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Revisiting Triplet Contrastive Learning for 3D Shape Understanding

**abstract/A1：** In multi-modal 3D pre-training, aligning 3D shapes, their 2D image counterparts, and language descriptions has made tremendous progress. However, we find that supervision from rendered images is unreliable, the semantics encoded in synthetic views are distorted due to domain gap, self-occlusion, and rendering artifacts. Instead, we learn transferable 3D representations from language only. We observe that, in real world, 3D shapes are organized in a tree-like structure, ranging from general concepts to fine-grained instances with specific attributes, while language typically denotes generic concepts and subsumes these various 3D instantiations. Hence, we propose to embed 3D representations in hyperbolic space, whose geometry is well-suited for modeling tree-like data without distortion. To capture the asymmetric hierarchy between language and 3D shapes, we further impose a partial-order relation that language entails 3D shapes. Our method, Hyperbolic Contrastive Learning (HyperCL), jointly optimizes a hyperbolic geodesic-based contrastive objective and an entailment loss to learn discriminative cross-modal representations while explicitly preserving this hierarchy. We also introduce a rigorously verified benchmark for comprehensively evaluating cross-modal 3D alignment. Across two widely used benchmarks and ours, we improve the performance up to 4.6% absolute percentage on 3D shape classification and 5.5% on cross-modal text-to-3D retrieval under the same parameter budget. The code and evaluation benchmark will be made publicly available.

</details>

<a id="paper-216"></a>

### 216. [HyrCap: Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals for Temporal Event Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19526v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19526

**作者：** Rui Chen、Xingyu Chen、Pengxin Xu、Kai Chen、Junzhi Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19526v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19526v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 HyrCap 这一以提案为中心的查询式时序动作检测框架，用混合视频特征与排序校准（跨表示共识校准、定位质量估计、冗余抑制）提升提案可靠性，并把校准后的提案输入 LLM 做事件语义理解与分阶段评测。

**相关性简评：** 其把密集查询预测视为「过完备事件假设空间」并依赖可靠性与候选竞争来校准排序、抑制冗余，可能为 LVR 的证据筛选与停止决策提供可核查的排序/校验线索，但本身不涉及 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HyrCap: Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals for Temporal Event Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Temporal action detection in long untrimmed videos suffers from inaccurate localization and low-quality proposals, especially with sparse annotations and ambiguous boundaries. For open-ended video understanding and LLM-driven event analysis, reliable proposals connect temporal localization, event annotation, and semantic understanding. However, conventional temporal action detection (TAD) pipelines lack explicit modeling of proposal localization quality, cross-representation support, and inter-proposal relations. We propose Hybrid Rank-Calibration of Action Proposals (HyrCap), a proposal-centric query-based TAD framework for temporal event understanding. Hybrid combines complementary video features with different localization cues, while Rank-Calibration adjusts proposal ranking confidence to reflect localization reliability and candidate competition. HyrCap treats dense query-based predictions as an over-complete temporal event hypothesis space. Cross-Representation Consensus Calibration, Localization Quality Estimation, and Proposal Redundancy Suppression improve proposal ranking reliability, localization quality, and candidate discrimination. Inspired by LLMs' capabilities in fine-grained semantic recognition, event description, and reasoning, we introduce HyrCap for Semantic Action Understanding, using calibrated proposals for standard TAD and as inputs to LLM-based semantic understanding. We define a staged evaluation protocol to separately measure event localization, segment-level semantic understanding, and combined event understanding performance. Experiments on public benchmarks show that HyrCap achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics and provides a finer-grained paradigm and evaluation protocol for query-based event segmentation and annotation.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://zgca-hmi-lab.github.io/HyrCap

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Two-stage Residual Learning for Modelling Strength-Informed Optimisation of Thermal Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19539v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19539

**作者：** Talha Ansar、Waqar Muhammad Ashraf、Abdulelah S. Alshehri、Omar Bayomie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19539v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19539v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文面向热力系统优化，提出两阶段残差学习框架（TSRL）：基础 ANN 预测过程输出，残差 ANN 学习其带符号残差，并用验证集残差分数阈值把解标记为「建模良好」或「建模薄弱」区域，用于炼油与燃气轮机两个案例的域约束优化。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「残差网络修正基础预测」与「用锁定残差分数阈值判定解是否处于可信区域」的做法，可能为 latent 状态迭代修正及何时信任当前状态、是否停止（THINK/STOP）提供评价层面的类比线索，需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Two-stage Residual Learning for Modelling Strength-Informed Optimisation of Thermal Systems

**abstract/A1：** Aggregate performance metrics do not identify local modelling inaccuracies in machine learning (ML) surrogates used for thermal-system optimisation. This study assesses local modelling strength through validation-based reliability screening and post-optimisation residual assessment. A two-stage residual learning (TSRL) framework, built on artificial neural networks (ANNs), is developed for modelling-strength-informed and domain-constrained optimisation of thermal systems. Within the TSRL framework, a base ANN predicts the process output, and a residual ANN learns the signed residuals left by the base ANN. The TSRL framework is implemented on two case studies of thermal systems, including 817 simulation observations of a crude distillation unit and operating records from a 395 MW industrial gas turbine. The TSRL framework reduces the test root mean square error (RMSE) from 10.3 g/s to 4.7 g/s for Fuel Oil in the refinery dataset and from 151 kJ/kWh to 105 kJ/kWh for turbine heat rate (THR). The base ANNs trained within TSRL are embedded in domain-constrained optimisation problems, and the modelling strength at each computed solution is assessed using the locked residual-score threshold. Each computed solution receives a good modelled region (GMR) or weak modelled region (WMR) label to inform ML model-based decision-making for safety-critical operation of thermal systems.

</details>

<a id="paper-218"></a>

### 218. [TEX-MEX: Similarity-Guided Temporal Contrastive Learning for Time Series](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19557v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19557

**作者：** Aditya Radhakrishnan、Maxwell A. Xu、Wanting Mao、Santosh Kumar、Harish Haresamudram、James M. Rehg

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19557v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19557v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TEX-MEX，把时间序列窗口的「结构相似」与「时间邻近相似」统一为一种关系：由编码器判断批次内窗口的结构相似性，再将窗口拉近相似窗口的时间平移版本、推离不相似者，在 PPG、EEG、ECG 等数据上取得较好表现，并训练了一个小型 PPG 基础模型。

**相关性简评：** 其区分并统一「内容/形态相似」与「时间位置邻近相似」的思路，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的相似度定义（内容相似 vs 轨迹位置或深度邻近）提供可核查的类比，联系仍属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TEX-MEX: Similarity-Guided Temporal Contrastive Learning for Time Series

**abstract/A1：** Contrastive learning for time series depends on deciding which pairs of inputs should be treated as similar and dissimilar. Because few augmentations reliably preserve meaning in time series, prior methods have relied on two disjoint notions of similarity between windows: structural similarity, which relates windows with similar morphology, and temporal similarity, which relates windows that occur close together in time. We introduce TEX-MEX, to our knowledge the first approach to unify the two into a single relationship. Given a random batch of windows drawn across the dataset, TEX-MEX uses the encoder to identify which windows are structurally similar or dissimilar to each other. It then pulls each window toward the temporally shifted versions of similar windows and pushes it away from those of dissimilar ones, learning what typically accompanies similar windows in time. Across PPG, EEG, ECG, accelerometry, and continuous glucose monitoring, TEX-MEX broadly achieves state-of-the-art performance against contrastive, reconstructive, JEPA-based, and modality-specific approaches, establishing it as a strong general SSL objective. We further train a 560K-parameter PPG foundation model that achieves state-of-the-art performance across a broad range of downstream benchmarks while being roughly 50x smaller than the next-best unimodally pretrained PPG foundation model in our evaluation. We will release this foundation model open-source and open-weight to the community to support future work and practical applications.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [$τ$-Emotion-Bench: Exposing the Interaction Fragility of LLM Agents through Real-World User Emotions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19560v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19560

**作者：** Rishitosh Kumar Singh、Amir Saeidi、Shamanthak Hegde、Shrinidhi Kumbhar、Chitta Baral

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19560v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19560v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 τ-Emotion-Bench 十领域基准与 Tracer 数据生成流水线，在情绪化用户模拟与交互环境中评估并训练工具调用智能体，发现前沿模型任务成功率与稳定完成率之间差距明显。

**相关性简评：** 让不满用户终止低效交互所形成的「交互代价」信号，可能为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与轨迹筛选式监督提供类比线索，可供核查；但该工作为纯文本智能体交互评测，不涉及视觉证据或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** $τ$-Emotion-Bench: Exposing the Interaction Fragility of LLM Agents through Real-World User Emotions

**abstract/A1：** In long-horizon tool-calling tasks, LLM agents can fail not only because of tool-use errors, but also because of ineffective interaction with users. Yet existing benchmarks largely assume cooperative and behaviorally stable users, leaving the effect of emotional variation on agent reliability underexplored. We introduce $τ$-Emotion-Bench, a ten-domain benchmark for evaluating tool-calling agents under controlled emotion-conditioned user interactions while preserving the underlying task objective. To scale both benchmark construction and agent training, we introduce Tracer, a scalable agentic data-generation pipeline that constructs grounded, policy-valid tasks and collects trajectories through an interactive agent--user--environment harness. By allowing dissatisfied users to terminate inefficient interactions, the harness provides an interaction-aware signal that filters trajectories with excessive user dependence and favors effective tool use. Experiments with two user simulators show that frontier agents remain unreliable, with substantial gaps between average task success and consistent task completion. Tracer-generated supervision consistently improves Qwen3 models across scales, generalizes to unseen domains, and transfers to $τ$-Bench and $τ$-Trait. Behavioral analyses further show that higher task success is associated with a better balance between tool execution and necessary user follow-up. Overall, $τ$-Emotion-Bench and Tracer provide a scalable framework for evaluating agent reliability under diverse user behaviors and improving tool-calling agents through interaction-aware supervision.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the UserSim Workshop at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [Learning Across Temporal Graph Representation Silos](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19576v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19576

**作者：** Minwoo Yu、Young-guk Ha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19576v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19576v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究考察从关系历史中学到的预测结构能否在连续时间动态图（CTDG）与时序知识图谱（TKG）两种表示之间双向复用，并发现贡献复用的历史组织方式随源—目标组合而变；还尝试将独立预训练的异表示学习器在分数层面无对齐融合。

**相关性简评：** 虽属时序图领域，但「预测结构跨表示边界复用」「无实体关系语义对齐的分数级融合」可能为 LVR 中跨模态/跨表示的 latent 状态复用与多源 latent 融合提供概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Across Temporal Graph Representation Silos

**abstract/A1：** Temporal graph data are recorded in different representations, but whether these differences also determine the scope of learned knowledge reuse remains unclear. This study investigates whether predictive structure learned from relational histories can be reused between continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) and temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs). Bidirectional reuse emerges while retaining distinct input and prediction interfaces and independent vocabularies, and sources with the same representation are not always more useful. Controlled reorganizations of source histories show that the organization contributing to reuse varies across source-target conditions. We show that learned aspects of Relational Dynamics, the predictive structure present in relational histories, can be reused across conventional representation boundaries. Independently pretrained TKG and CTDG learners can also be fused at the score level to improve prediction on a third dataset without entity or relation-semantic alignment. Two proofs of concept in recommendation and security detection illustrate that external temporal sources without direct alignment can also become useful pretraining resources.

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [L3M: A Verifiable Harness and Runtime for Self-Extending and Self-Improving Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19591v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19591

**作者：** Carson Eisenach、Robert Joseph George、Vincent Quenneville-Belair、Kate Gwimm、Dean Foster

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19591v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19591v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** L3M 是用 Lean 4 实现的智能体运行时，把人类、模型与代码的计算状态统一存入 Git，使状态轨迹可恢复、回退、分叉与合并，并以可逆性决定工具是否需要人工审查，靠核验定理保证扩展后的安全性。

**相关性简评：** 其「以富状态轨迹而非对话作为一等对象」及回退、分叉、可逆性判定，可能为 LVR 中推理状态的保存、重写与停止/审查决策提供概念借鉴，但属远领域线索，具体迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** L3M: A Verifiable Harness and Runtime for Self-Extending and Self-Improving Agents

**abstract/A1：** The lasting gains in AI have come from methods that absorb more computation, and language models already scale computation within a call. Useful work, though, happens across calls: over hours or days, through tools and external systems, among many agents and the humans they work for, and increasingly through code the agents write themselves. Whether that work compounds is enabled by the harness around it, and the harness faces a constraint the model does not: of the three participants in such a computation, the human, the model and code, only the human's attention does not scale, so the computation can grow only if the human is not in every loop. L3M is a runtime built on that premise, written in Lean 4 so that one language implements the harness, states its invariants, and is the language agents extend it in. One computation spans humans, agents, models and code, and all of its state lives in Git: a rich state trajectory -- rather than a chat conversation -- is an object the runtime can resume, rewind, fork, compare and merge. Waiting, communication, delegation and undo are primitives, not patterns the model reasons through each time. When an agent works out a procedure, it can write it once as a sealed Lean program whose type is its permission request, and the harness offers it back as a tool -- machinery for the model to use. To take the human out of the loop, L3M uses reversibility to decide where that is safe: a tool's review tier follows from how far its effects can be undone, so agents act freely where the runtime can recover. Because the machinery can change, the properties that make this safe are stated as kernel-checked theorems about the code that runs. On Terminal-Bench, an untuned L3M reaches about two thirds of its (unsafe) shell-equipped pass rate using only safe (not needing human review) tools.

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [Are AI Decision Models Biased? A Case Study of Jev](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19619v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19619

**作者：** Ilaria Imperatrice、Taisia Kameneva、Adam Eric Greenberg、Alessandro Romano

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19619v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19619v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以 4 万余条回答评估自称「System One」的决策模型 Jev：在认知反射题和五类经典认知偏差范式中，其选项层面表现稳健，但对无关数值与选项顺序敏感，概率输出在偏差操纵下显著变化，并在合取类问题上随表述与答案顺序大幅波动。

**相关性简评：** 其「答案不变而概率输出改变」「同一概率规则在不同表述下被稳定违反」的发现，可能为 LVR 中区分最终答案与内部推理状态的评价方式提供线索，可供核查是否需要类似扰动式诊断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Are AI Decision Models Biased? A Case Study of Jev

**abstract/A1：** Do AI models designed for fast, structured decisions exhibit the cognitive and social biases observed in human judgment? We evaluate Jev, a model described as a System One decision-maker, using 41,500 responses across cognitive reflection tasks, five classical cognitive-bias paradigms, and a pricing experiment testing ethnic and gender bias. At the level of selected answers, the results are largely encouraging. We confirm previous findings that Jev performs extremely well on cognitive reflection problems. We also observe that it generally avoids the characteristic choice patterns associated with several well-documented human biases, and exhibits only small differences among demographic groups in the tested pricing scenario. However, this apparent robustness conceals important vulnerabilities. Its judgments are sensitive to irrelevant numerical information and the ordering of response options. Moreover, several cognitive-bias manipulations substantially affect returned probabilities even when selected answers remain unchanged. Most strikingly, Jev answers a well-known probability problem correctly in every response but violates the same underlying probability rule in every response to an adapted version. We investigate this discrepancy using 15,000 additional responses across 30 conjunction tasks. Jev violates the rule in 54\\% of responses, with accuracy varying dramatically across formulations and answer orders. These findings suggest that evaluating decision models solely on familiar benchmarks and selected answers may substantially overstate their reliability in practical applications. They also identify vulnerabilities for developers to address and practical considerations for users integrating such models into automated decision-making systems.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [RuleTrek: Benchmarking Agentic Rule Reasoning in Complex Scenarios](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19698v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19698

**作者：** Chenhao Yuan、Bohan Yu、Yinhao Xu、Chao Yin、Chenxi Zhou、Zhaoxuan Wang、Shuwen Xu、Shaoping Huang、Haolin Ren、Jiaxiang Liu、Yao Xu、Jun Zhao、Kang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19698v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19698v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RuleTrek 是一个多领域基准，评测 LLM 智能体在场景信息分散于多个文件时的规则识别、规则推演与端到端规则推理能力，并用规则图构建流程自动生成一致、可解的子场景；11 个前沿模型的得分均不高。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本规则推理基准，但「从多文件中筛选并应用相关规则」与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、任务必要信息是否保留有类比性，其规则图构建与增量组合流程可能可供核查用于构造多步推理的监督数据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RuleTrek: Benchmarking Agentic Rule Reasoning in Complex Scenarios

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) increasingly tackle complex real-world tasks in specialized domains. However, identifying and correctly applying domain-specific rules remains challenging when scenario information is distributed across multiple files. Existing rule-reasoning benchmarks typically represent scenarios using only a few factual statements, simplifying the identification and application of relevant rules. To address this gap, this paper introduces \\textbf{RuleTrek}, a multi-domain benchmark for agentic rule reasoning in complex scenarios. Its rule-grounded construction pipeline extracts structured rule graphs, generates and incrementally composes rule-specific sub-scenarios while ensuring consistency and solvability. To systematically evaluate agentic rule reasoning in complex scenarios, RuleTrek assesses three capabilities in interactive workspaces: Rule Identification (RI), Rule Deduction (RD), and End-to-End Rule Reasoning (E2E). Experiments on 11 frontier LLMs show that the highest RI and RD scores are only 62.47\\% and 60.43\\%, respectively, while E2E scores range from 26.97\\% to 55.29\\%. These results demonstrate that current LLM agents face substantial challenges in rule reasoning over complex scenarios. We make our code and dataset publicly available at \\href{https://github.com/yuan247/RuleTrek}{RuleTrek}.

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Runtime Monitoring of Supermartingale Certificates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19701v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19701

**作者：** Mirco Giacobbe、Thomas A. Henzinger、Konstantin Kueffner、Emily Yu、Đorđe Žikelić

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19701v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19701v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究超鞅证书（supermartingale certificate）的运行时监控问题：监控器只观察单条系统轨迹，用基于投注（betting）的序贯检验在给定误报概率上界下，当有充分统计证据表明证书在执行中被违反时报警，并利用空间规律性提升检测力，在文献基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「基于投注的序贯检验 + 误报概率有界」的随时有效监控框架，可能为 latent 推理轨迹的在线证伪、证据累积与何时停止/报警提供统计保证层面的参考，与 LVR 的 STOP 决策与评价问题存在较远但具体的概念联系，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Runtime Monitoring of Supermartingale Certificates

**abstract/A1：** Supermartingale certificates formally guarantee temporal properties of discrete and continuous-time stochastic dynamical systems. However, these guarantees rely on a mathematical model of the world, leaving a sim-to-real gap that can invalidate them for the deployed system. We formulate the runtime monitoring problem for supermartingale certificates, in which a monitor observes a single system trajectory and raises an alarm when there is sufficient statistical evidence that the supermartingale certificate has been violated during execution. We present an algorithm to automatically construct a monitor using betting-based sequential tests that bound the probability of ever raising a false alarm at a user-specified level. Within this betting framework, we formulate strategies that allow the monitor to exploit spatial regularity to improve detection power while preserving this statistical guarantee. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach on supermartingale certificate benchmarks from the literature.

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [TIMERSI: Towards Self-Improved Agentic Deployment for Time-Series Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19725v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19725

**作者：** Haoxin Liu、Deqing Fu、Lucheng Fu、Yiqiao Jin、Rushi Qiang、Harshavardhan Kamarthi、B. Aditya Prakash

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19725v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19725v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** TIMERSI 面向冻结的时序基础模型，构建自改进的 agent 化部署系统：由编码 agent 把任务描述转成可执行的 pipeline 改动，在历史预测窗口上测试并用观测误差指导后续修订，部署时再由自适应选择器在相近条件下挑选管线，并在 TIME、FEV-bench 的 198 个任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该工作虽属时序领域，但其「用已完成轨迹的误差反馈迭代修正」与「相似条件下从多条候选管线中选择」的机制，可能为 LVR 的轨迹级监督、贡献分配以及动态计算与停止决策的评价提供问题表述上的启发，迁移性需人工核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TIMERSI: Towards Self-Improved Agentic Deployment for Time-Series Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Time-series foundation models provide strong zero-shot forecast, but deployment for real- world applications still requires task-specific designs for model inputs and outputs. Direct inference can leave domain knowledge and re- curring forecast errors unaddressed, while tai- lored pipelines require substantial human ex- pertise and effort. We introduce TIMERSI, a self-improving agentic deployment system around frozen forecasting models. A cod- ing agent translates task descriptions into ex- ecutable pipeline changes. It tests each re- vision on historical forecasting windows and uses observed errors to guide subsequent re- visions. During deployment, an adaptive se- lector chooses among the resulting pipelines using completed forecasts under similar con- ditions. Across 198 tasks on TIME and FEV- bench, TIMERSI outperforms all submissions on six point and probabilistic forecasting met- rics. Controlled comparisons support the value of feedback-guided revision over non-iterative pipeline search. Further experiments verify the generalization with different coding agents and forecasting models.

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [Generative World Simulation with DAO and JING](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19726v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19726

**作者：** XGEN Team、Guohao Cai、Haoxuan Che、Frank Fan、Chenran Huang、Junjia Huang、Jiayi Jiang、Yanheng Li、Leon Liu、Shaoteng Liu、Xi Liu、Feng Nie、Zhefan Rao、An Wang、Hanwen Wang、Pengxiang Yan、Jieming Yu、Qian Zha、Wentao Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19726v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19726v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出生成式世界模拟框架，将可计算世界引擎 DAO（维护角色、物体、空间的状态与关系，并按交互规则和世界时间推进、保留过往动作后果）与自我中心交互体验模型 JING（据观察者特定条件结合引用、观察历史与当前动作生成视听体验）耦合，形成持续交互循环。

**相关性简评：** DAO 以显式持久状态承载历史并派生观察者特定条件，其「状态维护—条件派生—生成」结构可能与 LVR 中循环/并行推理的状态更新、证据筛选与条件化生成形成类比，可供核查；但该工作属世界模型与视听生成系统，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative World Simulation with DAO and JING

**abstract/A1：** We present a Generative World Simulation framework that couples persistent shared world state with continuous first-person audiovisual generation. The framework integrates DAO, a computable world engine, and JING, an Egocentric Interactive Experience Model, to support sustained interaction for humans and agents. DAO maintains the states of characters, objects, and spaces and their relationships, advancing the world according to interaction rules and world time while preserving the consequences of past actions. From this evolving state, DAO derives observer-specific conditions for JING, which combines these conditions with character, object, and scene references, observation history, and current actions to generate each observer's audiovisual experience. Users can explore environments, manipulate objects, and communicate with characters through viewpoint control and text instructions, with their actions shaping both the shared state and subsequent experiences. By connecting world-state evolution with experience generation in a continuous interaction loop, the framework offers an approach to building interactive worlds that retain their history, evolve through action, and persist beyond any individual observer.

**comment/C1：** XGEN DAO and JING Technical report. Contributors listed alphabetically by surname. Project page: https://xgenlabs.ai/research/generative-world-simulation; Model: https://huggingface.co/XGENlabs/XGEN-JING

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [HyperLoop: Hyperspectral-Assisted Loop Closure Detection in 3D-SLAM for Planetary Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19742v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19742

**作者：** Annie Bhalla、Dennis Rotondi、Riccardo Giubilato、Kai O. Arras、Wolfgang Stuerzl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19742v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19742v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** HyperLoop 将高光谱成像引入 3DGS SLAM 的回环检测模块，提出 BoSW 与 PCAK-VLAD 两类光谱描述子，并在 DLR 行星类比场地数据上测试 RGB 与高光谱描述子的四种融合策略，发现 600–860 nm 波段对岩石地形区分力有限。

**相关性简评：** 回环检测可视作在「外观相似但位置不同」条件下形成位置信念，可用作 LVR 中视觉歧义与多源证据筛选（何种模态真正贡献增益）的类比参照；但属机器人 SLAM 领域，与 VLM 推理状态的联系仅为可能，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HyperLoop: Hyperspectral-Assisted Loop Closure Detection in 3D-SLAM for Planetary Exploration

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous navigation of planetary rovers is challenged by visually repetitive, featureless terrain. SLAM systems based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offer dense mapping but rely on RGB appearance descriptors that degrade under perceptual aliasing - a common failure mode in extraterrestrial environments where physically distinct locations appear visually identical. This paper presents HyperLoop, the first framework integrating hyperspectral imaging (HSI) into the loop closure module of a 3DGS-based SLAM system. Hyperspectral descriptors encode material-level spectral signatures complementing RGB features, providing robustness to illumination changes and visual repetitiveness. Two HSI descriptor families are introduced: Bag-of-Spectral-Words (BoSW), encoding spectral material distributions via TF-IDF weighted histograms, and PCA-K VLAD (PCAK-VLAD), capturing first-order residual statistics of PCA-compressed spectral signatures. Both are evaluated with SIFT-VLAD and NetVLAD RGB descriptors under Strict-AND and Weighted fusion strategies on a custom dataset from the Moon-Mars Outdoor Test Site (MMOTS) at DLR, comprising synchronized RGB-D and hyperspectral sequences across four planetary analog traverses (65.5 m total). A key finding is that the sensor's Red-NIR range (600-860 nm) provides limited discriminability for rock terrain: the best HSI fusion (SIFT-VLAD + BoSW, Strict-AND) achieves 5.04 cm ATE RMSE, within 1.8% of SIFT-VLAD alone (4.95 cm). The gain over NetVLAD (8.81 cm) is attributable to SIFT-VLAD, not HSI fusion. This motivates future adoption of SWIR sensors capturing diagnostic mineral absorption features absent from the 600-860 nm range for planetary SLAM missions.

**comment/C1：** Paper accepted at International Astronautical Congress 2026, IAC-26,A3,IP,163,x107111

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [Learning Who Benefits Most: Multi-Treatment Causal Ranking for Population Health Stratification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19782v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19782

**作者：** Rocco Zaccagnino、Martina Girolamo、Alberto Lombardi、Delfina Malandrino、Donatello Telesca、Alessia Ture

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19782v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19782v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PROMETHEUS 框架，把人群健康分层形式化为多治疗因果排序问题：将每种临床可行的护理强化与维持现状相比较，联合学习在患者与护理画像间全局可比的增量收益排序，结合双稳健因果信号、Siamese 排序模型与对比式表示预训练，并用合成与半合成行政数据评估排序质量、分配价值与失效模式。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR/VLM 推理无直接关联，属较远领域；其「按期望增量收益排序而非追求校准的个体效应」的目标表述，以及跨多种治疗共享的对比式表示，或可启发 latent 状态与视觉证据贡献分配、候选排序的可比性设计，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Who Benefits Most: Multi-Treatment Causal Ranking for Population Health Stratification

**abstract/A1：** Population health stratification frameworks, such as the Italian DM77, organize patients by clinical need and care pathways but offer limited guidance on which care changes to prioritize under constrained resources. We present PROMETHEUS (Proactive Resource Optimization through Multi-treatment Effect-based Targeting in Healthcare Environments Using Stratification), a framework that formulates population health stratification as a multi-treatment causal ranking problem. Its objective is to order eligible patient-treatment opportunities by expected incremental benefit rather than obtain perfectly calibrated individual treatment-effect estimates. Each clinically admissible care intensification is compared with maintaining the patient's current pathway. Treatments are learned jointly to produce a globally comparable ranking across patients and care profiles, within domains sharing a reference condition, outcome, follow-up horizon, and benefit scale. This separates clinical need, treatment benefit, and resource-constrained allocation. PROMETHEUS combines doubly robust causal signals with a Siamese neural ranking model and contrastive representation pretraining. The shared representation captures similarities across care transitions, supporting prioritization under capacity constraints. Synthetic experiments and semi-synthetic experiments using real administrative covariates from ASL Benevento evaluate ranking quality, allocation value, robustness, and failure modes. The results support the framework's ability to recover generated benefit orderings and prioritize care opportunities in the evaluated settings. PROMETHEUS provides a preclinical approach to actionable stratification; validation with observed treatment-outcome data is required before clinical use.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 1 figure, 19 tables

</details>

### 229. [Improving Category-Aware Active Domain Adaptation via Uncertainty-Guided Region Coverage](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19803v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19803

**作者：** My Tra Nguyen Huynh、Viet Cuong Ta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19803v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19803v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究类别感知的主动域自适应，指出逐点选取易敏感、冗余并导致采集函数过拟合，转而提出基于区域覆盖的选取策略，并引入不确定性分数以改善目标域决策边界，在多个域自适应基准上超过原有基线。

**相关性简评：** 其「不确定性引导的区域覆盖」选取思路或可启发 LVR 中视觉证据/区域的筛选与主动获取方式，可供核查；但该工作面向域自适应的标注采样，与推理期 latent 状态生成无直接关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Improving Category-Aware Active Domain Adaptation via Uncertainty-Guided Region Coverage

**abstract/A1：** Active domain adaptation researches focus on alleviating the annotation cost, which comprises source data and target data labelling, as well as enhancing the performance of unsupervised domain adaptation. However, optimizing the adaptation model based on the average accuracy can lead to the drop in performance of some difficult categories. To overcome this issue, a point-wise selection strategy built upon the influence function can be used to improve domain adaptation performance. In our work, we first address the sensitive and redundant aspects of point-wise selection, which can lead to overfitting of the acquisition function. We thus introduce a region-based approach that ensures the improvement of the adaptation model by maximizing a positive manifold coverage. An uncertainty score is further introduced to the selection mechanism to learn a better decision boundary for the target domain. In comprehensive experiments, our proposed method surpasses the first category-aware active domain adaptation baseline as well as existing active domain adaptation methods across multiple domain adaptation benchmarks. Our implementation is available at: https://github.com/nhtm2702/WHO IS NEXT.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Machine Learning Journal through ACML Journal Track 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [Efficient contextual partial monitoring with general function approximation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19804v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19804

**作者：** Kapilan Balagopalan、Hao Qin、Kwang-Sung Jun、Chicheng Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19804v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19804v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究带辅助信息的上下文部分监测问题，基于 E2D 元算法提出计算高效的 E2D.PMSide，用在线回归预言机估计结果分布，并以 ε-greedy 与 inverse gap weighting 两种探索规则达到最优 regret，在合成任务和 LLM 路由任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 部分监测刻画了「付出代价只能获得部分反馈」时的决策，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 与视觉证据获取、信息价值的权衡提供形式化参考；其 LLM 路由实验按不确定性与内部状态特征决定是否升级模型，与动态计算分配或有联系，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient contextual partial monitoring with general function approximation

**abstract/A1：** We study finite stochastic partial monitoring (PM) games with side information, a paradigm of sequential learning with partial feedback. On each round $t \\in \[T\]$, both player and environment observe a context and player chooses an action and environment simultaneously chooses an outcome. The player neither observes the outcome nor the loss; instead, they only observe a feedback symbol which is partially informative about the outcome. We utilize the Estimation-to-Decisions (E2D) meta-algorithm of Foster et al.(2021) to design E2D.PMSide, a computationally efficient framework for contextual partial monitoring, which uses an online regression oracle to efficiently estimate the outcome distributions based on historical (context, action, feedback symbol) tuples. Since the exploration rule given by the original E2D algorithm is intractable to compute, we propose two computationally efficient exploration rules that achieve optimal regret orders in $T$: in the globally observable setting, our exploration rule is based on $ε$-greedy, while in the locally observable setting, our exploration rule uses inverse gap weighting (Foster &amp; Rakhlin, 2020), which was previously only known to be effective in the bandit setting. We show that a variant of E2D.PMSide named OE2D.PMSide can be combined with offline regression oracles to give an efficient algorithm with optimal regret when the contexts are drawn independently from a fixed distribution. We evaluate our algorithm on a synthetic label-efficient prediction game and an LLM routing task, in which a learner decides from prompt, uncertainty, and internal state features whether to escalate a query from a weak model to a strong one. We show that our method has superior performance to prior approaches.

**comment/C1：** 42 pages, 5 figures, ICLR 2027 submission

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [VaryST: Generalizable Spatio-Temporal Forecasting Beyond the Closed-World Setting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19813v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19813

**作者：** Shengnan Guo、Jinjun Cheng、Xiaowei Mao、Jianhao Zhang、Haochen Lv、Yang Jiang、YiHeng Huang、Yan Lin、Youfang Lin、Huaiyu Wan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19813v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19813v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出从零训练的时空预测模型 VaryST：用长期历史导出节点表示以应对时间漂移，动态选择超出预设邻域的功能相关支撑节点以应对空间变化，并在训练中掩码模型最依赖的历史片段，以在观测缺失时仍能利用替代依赖完成预测。

**相关性简评：** 属远领域（时空预测）工作，与 LVR 无直接关联；但其「掩码最依赖输入以迫使模型改用其他证据」及动态选取相关支撑节点的做法，可能与视觉证据筛选、信息坍缩与缺失观测下的鲁棒推理问题形成类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VaryST: Generalizable Spatio-Temporal Forecasting Beyond the Closed-World Setting

**abstract/A1：** Spatio-temporal forecasting is fundamental to many real-world systems, yet deployment environments are inherently varying: temporal distributions may evolve, spatial domains may change, and observation availability may shift from training conditions. Existing models are often tightly coupled to their training environments through node-specific representations, predefined spatial support sets, and reliance on consistently available observations, limiting generalization to changing environments. To this end, we propose VaryST, a compact spatio-temporal forecasting model trained from scratch that achieves strong generalization by dynamically constructing predictive context from available long-term observations. To accommodate temporal changes, VaryST derives node representations from long-term histories, capturing current behavior in the context of longer-term patterns. To adapt to spatial changes, it dynamically selects functionally relevant support nodes beyond predefined neighborhoods and integrates their information with recent and long-term target states and global context. To handle varying observation availability, VaryST selectively masks historical patches on which the model relies most during training, encouraging it to exploit alternative temporal and spatial dependencies for accurate forecasting when key observations become unavailable. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets against seventeen baselines show that VaryST achieves strong in-domain forecasting accuracy, substantially improves zero- and few-shot generalization under spatial and temporal changes, and remains robust to varying observation availability.

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [One Monitor Does Not Fit All: Formalism-Agnostic Runtime Verification for Agentic AI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19818v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19818

**作者：** Angelo Ferrando

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19818v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19818v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AgenticRV 运行时验证架构，把监控形式与执行边界解耦：适配器发出规范事件，各 monitor 接收自己的投影与抽象，决策策略依据规范化裁决选择由相应适配器支持的反应；并形式化观察充分性、monitor 替换保持决策、阻断阻止不安全效果等条件，附 LangGraph/MCP/A2A 实现与实验。

**相关性简评：** 其「按控制边界投影观察」与「区分被拒尝试和已完成效果」的形式化，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的运行时监测、裁决归一化及 STOP/提交决策提供可核查的线索，属较远领域的具体启发。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Monitor Does Not Fit All: Formalism-Agnostic Runtime Verification for Agentic AI

**abstract/A1：** Agentic AI systems combine model decisions, mutable state, tool calls, and delegation to external agents. Monitoring them requires observations from different execution boundaries, while prevention requires control before an operation produces its effect. We present AgenticRV, a runtime-verification architecture that separates these boundaries from the monitoring formalism. Adapters emit canonical events; each monitor receives its own projection and abstraction; and a decision policy uses normalised verdicts to select a reaction supported by the responsible adapter. We formalise when observations suffice, monitor replacement preserves decisions, and blocking prevents unsafe effects. We also distinguish rejected attempts from completed effects, avoiding the assumption that a blocked request must leave every monitor in a permanent violation state. The Python implementation supports LangGraph, MCP, and A2A. A controlled LangGraph experiment combines an external Reelay temporal monitor with a programmatic report monitor; ablations show why both are needed for the selected requirements. Differential checks and cross-adapter tests provide further integration evidence. An LLM-driven incident-response example illustrates the same enforcement path, while existing microbenchmarks isolate framework costs. The results support reuse of monitoring methods at explicit control boundaries, subject to the stated observation and deployment assumptions.

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [PhyMetric: Diagnosing Physical Plausibility in Text-to-Video Generation via Scene-Level QA](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19823v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19823

**作者：** Siwei Meng、Xuerou Cai、Rui Hu、Ping Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19823v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19823v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PhyMetric 提出场景级 T2V 物理合理性诊断基准：为 1115 个场景各配 8 个针对性是/否问题与结构化物理先验（支配规律、期望时序、可观测特征），由 VLM judge 在问题感知采样的帧上作答并分层聚合为场景、维度、类别与模型分数；实验表明把针对性问题换成单一笼统合理性提问会使评测准确率跌至随机水平。

**相关性简评：** 其「问题条件化帧采样＋显式物理先验」可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK 决策提供核查线索，但研究对象是视频生成评测而非 latent 推理状态，需人工判断迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhyMetric: Diagnosing Physical Plausibility in Text-to-Video Generation via Scene-Level QA

**abstract/A1：** Text-to-video (T2V) generation models can produce visually realistic videos that nevertheless violate the physical consequences implied by the prompt and scene. Existing benchmarks largely rely on holistic plausibility scores or broad quality dimensions, leaving it unclear which physical behavior is being tested and why a model fails. We introduce PhyMetric, a scene-level diagnostic benchmark that recasts physical plausibility evaluation as verifiable, scene-grounded question answering. PhyMetric contains 1{,}115 scenes spanning 50 physical categories across six domains, paired with 8{,}920 answer-balanced binary questions over 12 evaluation dimensions. Each scene is associated with eight targeted yes/no questions and a structured physical prior specifying the governing law, expected temporal sequence, observable signatures, and common failure modes. At evaluation time, a vision-language model (VLM) judge answers each question from question-aware sampled frames conditioned on the prior, and correctness is hierarchically aggregated into scene, dimension, physical category, and model scores. Experiments on four open-source T2V models and three VLM judges show that scene-specific binary QA is a critical component of the protocol: when each targeted question is replaced by a single generic plausibility query, evaluator accuracy drops to the chance level. Structured physical priors offer a complementary signal that improves human alignment of the resulting scores, with effects that vary across VLM judges of different capacity. These findings establish PhyMetric as a benchmark that turns physical plausibility evaluation from opaque holistic scoring into interpretable, scene-level inspection. Our project page is available at: https://mengsiwei.github.io/PhyMetric/.

**comment/C1：** This work is accepted by NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations and Datasets Track

</details>

<a id="paper-234"></a>

### 234. [Memory-Efficient Subspace Optimization with Residual Correction: From Momentum SGD to Muon](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19844v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19844

**作者：** Sifan Yang、Hongyi Tao、Dingzhi Yu、Haomin Bai、Wei Jiang、Silin Gao、Pi Bu、Lijun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19844v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19844v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出残差校正的子空间优化框架：把优化器状态限制在随机梯度的主子空间中，并把正交残差梯度加入参数更新，先给出 momentum SGD 版本 SMRC，再扩展到 Muon 得到 PMRC，并设计周期性刷新基的多阶段变体，理论上保持 O(T^{-1/4}) 收敛率。

**相关性简评：** 该工作属 LLM 训练的显存优化，不涉及多模态推理；但其「主子空间状态 + 正交残差修正」的低维状态压缩与基刷新机制，可能为思考 latent 状态如何在低维子空间中保留任务必要信息、以及被舍弃方向是否需要补偿提供类比线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Memory-Efficient Subspace Optimization with Residual Correction: From Momentum SGD to Muon

**abstract/A1：** Memory-efficient subspace optimization has emerged as a practical approach to reducing the memory cost of LLM training while preserving optimization performance. Previous algorithms typically rely on the leading singular subspace of stochastic gradients, yet the gradient noise may dominate the subspace in later training stages and can prevent convergence. To overcome this limitation, we adopt a residual correction framework, which maintains the optimizer states in the subspace and adds the orthogonal residual gradient to the parameter update. We first integrate this scheme into momentum SGD to develop SMRC, which attains an $O(T^{-1/4})$ convergence rate for a fixed orthonormal basis, matching that of full-space momentum SGD. Our analysis introduces a subspace virtual iterate to resolve the coupling between the momentum and the residual correction. Furthermore, we extend SMRC to a multi-stage variant which periodically recomputes bases to track the evolving dominant subspace and retains the same rate. Since each basis refresh breaks the consistency of the virtual iterates, we devise a boundary correction approach that aligns them across stages. Beyond momentum SGD, we incorporate our framework into Muon and propose PMRC, which applies the matrix sign to the projected momentum. Through a weighted momentum tracking analysis that accounts for the residual gradient, we establish an $O(T^{-1/4})$ convergence rate for PMRC, which matches the rate of full-space Muon. We also design a multi-stage PMRC with periodically refreshed bases, which maintains a convergence rate of the same order. Extensive experimental results confirm the effectiveness and efficiency of our algorithms.

**comment/C1：** 34 pages, 5 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-235"></a>

### 235. [Neural Wasserstein Splines: Representation-Invariant Second-Order Priors for Trajectory Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19878v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19878

**作者：** Amartya Banerjee、Harlin Lee、Caroline Moosmüller

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19878v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19878v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Neural Wasserstein Splines（NWSP）：用神经网络把 latent 变量映射为三次样条控制点，从而无需数值积分即可在任意时刻采样，并以有限势函数字典上的速度投影逼近 Wasserstein 加速度作为二阶平滑先验，证明固定字典下该惩罚只依赖边缘曲线，在多组合成与单细胞基准上与传输/流方法相当。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示不变性」惩罚及对矩估计误差的敏感性界，可能为 LVR 中 latent 相似度与内部状态度量如何摆脱采样、位置或缓存表示依赖提供可核查的度量设计线索；latent 到连续时间样条轨迹的生成方式也可能类比 latent 推理状态的连续更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Neural Wasserstein Splines: Representation-Invariant Second-Order Priors for Trajectory Inference

**abstract/A1：** Population dynamics are often observed only as unpaired snapshots, which leave the evolution between measurements underdetermined. Second-order regularization can constrain it, but a penalty on the accelerations of sampled particles is a prior on joint particle paths, and different particle representations of the same marginal curve can have different acceleration costs. We therefore introduce Neural Wasserstein Splines (NWSP), a sample-based method that learns a continuous population curve under a Wasserstein smoothness prior. A neural network maps latent variables to the control points of a cubic spline in time, so samples at any time come from the spline basis without numerical integration. We project the generator's velocity onto gradients of a finite dictionary of scalar potentials and differentiate this measure-dependent projection in time, obtaining a finite-dimensional surrogate for the intrinsic Wasserstein acceleration, trained jointly with distributional fit losses. For a fixed dictionary and ridge parameter, we prove that the population penalty depends only on the marginal curve and bound its sensitivity to moment-estimation errors. Learned potentials (NWSP-N) sharpen acceleration estimates. On various synthetic and single-cell benchmarks, NWSP is highly competitive with transport and flow-based methods, ranking in the top two in 11 of 16 settings. The penalty improves on an unregularized spline in 13 of 16 settings and, while competitive with a particle-acceleration penalty, adds provable representation invariance.

**comment/C1：** Code: https://github.com/amartya21/Neural-Wasserstein-Splines.git

</details>

<a id="paper-236"></a>

### 236. [BluePixel: Toward Native 4K Agentic Image Editing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19882v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19882

**作者：** Shanyan Guan、Shiyu Li、Xin Li、Zhizhou Chen、Yunzhe Xu、Yuehao Liu、Ruixue Shen、Shiju Zhang、Min Jin、Qiang Liu、Junbo Liu、Penggang Shi、Sheng Sun、Xiao Wang、Yong Zhang、Zhongang Qi、Wei Li、Chao Ma、Ying Tai、Yanhao Ge

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19882v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19882v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BluePixel，把原生 4K 指令图像编辑当作系统问题：一侧是 4K 编辑模型（渐进 VAE 适配、持续学习、强化学习调优、SATP 蒸馏把 50 步去噪压到 4 步），另一侧是 agent harness（把指令分解为专项与通用操作、用同一奖励模型验证结果并在有界次数内重试）。

**相关性简评：** 该 agent harness 的「分解—奖励模型验证—有界重试」决策闭环，可能为 LVR 中的验证、贡献分配与停止决策提供可核查的外部参照，但任务是图像编辑，迁移价值需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BluePixel: Toward Native 4K Agentic Image Editing

**abstract/A1：** Instruction-driven image editing has reached native 4K, yet how such editors are trained remains undisclosed, and pretraining one from scratch is beyond most teams. The task itself compounds the difficulty: a single end-to-end editor cannot make precise parametric adjustments, match specialized experts, or interpret the vague instructions users actually write. We argue that 4K editing is a system problem rather than a single-model problem, and present BluePixel, which couples a native-4K editing model with an agent harness; the complete training recipe is disclosed in this paper. The model makes 4K training practical: it turns a 1K-pretrained editor into a native-4K one through progressive VAE adaptation that keeps 4K training as affordable as 1K, absorbs a long tail of vertical editing skills through continual learning, and refines the aesthetic-fidelity trade-off with reinforcement learning. Distillation via source-anchored target projection (SATP) then compresses 50 denoising steps to 4, completing a 4K edit in 8.7 s on a single Kunlunxin P800 XPU while preserving task success rate and background consistency. The harness, BluePixel Agent, makes editing controllable: it decomposes user instructions into specialist and general operations, verifies every result with the same reward model that guided training, and retries within bounded attempts. On VINS-4KEval++ (3,305 native-4K cases), BluePixel ranks second among seven models, behind only GPT Image 2 in non-edited-region consistency.

**comment/C1：** 24 pages. Project page: https://tech.vivo.com/BluePixel

</details>

<a id="paper-237"></a>

### 237. [MolHop: Scaffold-Hopping Molecular Design with Agentic Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19920v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19920

**作者：** Yanyi Su、Chenyan Wang、Yulei Gong、Yeyun Chen、Xiaohui Yang、Jun Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19920v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19920v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MolHop 把约 160 万分子骨架组织成由粗到细的层级动作空间，让 LLM 智能体通过骨架导航与分子编辑进行跨骨架分子发现，并用历史轨迹记忆支持长程搜索，同时以 agentic RL 训练出 MolHop-9B。

**相关性简评：** 分子设计与 LVR 主线相距较远，但其「由粗到细层级动作空间＋历史轨迹记忆＋长程搜索」的组织方式，可能为 latent 工作区的分层表示、动态计算分配与停止决策提供类比线索，可供核查；是否有实质迁移价值需读原文判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MolHop: Scaffold-Hopping Molecular Design with Agentic Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Molecular design is often formulated as an optimization problem over existing molecular spaces. However, molecular optimization and the discovery of structurally distinct replacement molecules represent two fundamentally different search problems. LLM-based molecular design agents typically perform the former, conducting local refinement within the neighborhood of existing structures to improve target properties through structural modifications. In contrast, the latter requires exploration of a broader chemical space to discover molecules with entirely different scaffolds while preserving the functional characteristics of reference molecules. We propose MolHop, an agentic framework for cross-scaffold molecular discovery that enables models to navigate and edit within a pre-organized chemical space. MolHop organizes approximately 1.6 million molecular scaffolds from commercial molecules into a coarse-to-fine hierarchical action space. The agent selects new exploration regions through scaffold navigation actions and adjusts candidate properties through molecular editing. The framework maintains historical trajectory memory to support long-horizon search processes. On drug molecule and commercial compound tasks, connecting Grok-4.6 and DeepSeek-Flash-V4.1 to the MolHop framework substantially improves their cross-scaffold joint success rates. We develop MolHop-9B through agentic reinforcement learning, and MolHop-9B outperforms generative baselines including GPT-5.6-sol, Claude-5-Opus, and Gemini-3.1-Pro, while generating candidate molecules with larger scaffold differences from the reference molecules. These results demonstrate that effective molecular discovery beyond the traditional optimization paradigm requires structured chemical and action spaces, enabling agents to explore new structural regions rather than merely refine existing molecules.

**comment/C1：** Under review at ICLR 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-238"></a>

### 238. [MorphingNet: Symbolic Regression Where the Network Is the Formula](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19942v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19942

**作者：** Jincheng Gao、Jiahe Yi、Shuo Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19942v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19942v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MorphingNet 提出一种符号回归网络：单元函数形式随参数连续变化（无极点有理函数或复指数—对数函数），使公式搜索变成连续优化，训练中网络本身即可读作公式，训练后再由结构恢复步骤重写成短表达式。

**相关性简评：** 其把离散结构搜索松弛为连续参数化、且中间状态始终「可读」的思路，可能与 latent 推理状态的生成与更新、以及内部状态可解释性的研究路线有概念上的类比，但联系较间接，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MorphingNet: Symbolic Regression Where the Network Is the Formula

**abstract/A1：** We introduce MorphingNet, a network for symbolic regression (SR) whose units change their function continuously. Instead of searching over expressions built from a fixed operator library, MorphingNet trains the form of each function as a parameter, so SR becomes one continuous optimization problem. Its units are either pole-free rational functions or complex exponential--logarithm functions, which move between exponentials, sinusoids, damped oscillations, power laws and logarithms as their parameters change. The network is a readable formula at every step of training, and no operator library is needed to train it; a structure-recovery step afterwards rewrites the trained formula into short elementary expressions. Training is dense linear algebra, so it is GPU-friendly and scales to large data. MorphingNet achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on SRBench, recovers most generating formulas on synthetic benchmarks, and on large data is up to two orders of magnitude faster than the strongest search method. By turning the search for a formula into the training of a network, MorphingNet offers a new paradigm for SR and for scientific discovery from data.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 1 figure, 9 tables

</details>

### 239. [Why 3D Reconstruction Breaks Down on Under-Ice AUV Video, and How to Predict It](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19993v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19993

**作者：** Ana-Ştefania Kogălniceanu、Klára Orbán、Ashwin Nedungadi、Stefan Lüdtke、Kuderna-Iulian Benţa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19993v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19993v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在同一段南极冰下 AUV 视频上对比四种三维重建方法（特征匹配 SfM、可微分光度 splatting、体深度融合、无位姿前馈基础模型），发现它们全部失败且失败方式各异，并归因于相机基线相对场景深度过小的采集条件，进而提出可在数分钟内标记该类失败的检测系统。

**相关性简评：** 远域（冰下 AUV 视觉）的失败诊断揭示「光度指标健康但几何结构已损坏」的代理指标失配，可能启发 LVR 中对 latent 相似度指标与视觉证据是否真正保留的区分，以及证据不足时及早停止后续计算；联系仍属推测，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why 3D Reconstruction Breaks Down on Under-Ice AUV Video, and How to Predict It

**abstract/A1：** Reconstruction of 3D structures using under-ice AUV video footage has been inaccurate, although it is not certain whether this is due to shortcomings in the methods or an inherent deficiency of the capture system. We test four different models for reconstruction on the same Antarctic under-ice AUV video (Icefin, Kamb Ice Stream): classical feature matching SfM, differentiable photometric splatting, volumetric depth fusion, and a pose-free feed-forward foundation model. All four fail, each in a distinct way: near-total registration yielding almost no structure, geometrically broken output despite healthy photometric metrics, a persistent coverage artifact, and unstable cross-chunk scale. We show that all four are consistent with the same capture regime, a small camera-baseline-to-scene-depth ratio. Following our analysis, we propose a detection system that flags this type of failure in minutes, rather than hours of wasted compute.

**comment/C1：** Short paper presented at the 2nd Workshop on Marine Vision, ECCV 2026, Malmö, Sweden, September 8, 2026. Published on OpenReview: https://openreview.net/forum?id=6wgkZngDjW

</details>

### 240. [What Coding Agents Leave Unsaid: Measuring Task-State Gaps Between Execution and Reports](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20003v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20003

**作者：** Guoyang Xu、Rui Zhang、Xiuyu Wu、Hao Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20003v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20003v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** CTXGAP 测量编码代理在 37 个任务、4799 次探针中，其面向用户的报告与完整执行上下文之间在任务状态可恢复性上的差距（全上下文 92.7% 对仅报告 52.0%），并检验终端回滚、报告改写等缓解手段。

**相关性简评：** 其「内部状态经有限输出通道后有多大可恢复性」的探针式测量，可能为 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用 latent 内容、以及轨迹监督信息是否丢失提供评价视角，但论文属编码代理领域、不含视觉或多模态推理，联系尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Coding Agents Leave Unsaid: Measuring Task-State Gaps Between Execution and Reports

**abstract/A1：** Human supervisors rely on coding agents' reports to assess progress, verify repairs, and decide when to intervene. But a supervisor cannot investigate a failure they were never told occurred. Failed attempts, workarounds, and unresolved risks may disappear from routine reports, leaving supervisors unaware of issues that warrant scrutiny. We conduct CTXGAP, a measurement study quantifying the gap in task-state recoverability between agents' accumulated user-facing reports and their full recorded execution contexts. Across 37 coding tasks and 4,799 valid probes, agents answer 93.6% of task-state questions on demand; full-context readers recover 92.7%, while report-only readers recover 52.0%, a 40.7-point gap. At the final checkpoints of 30 verified fixes, the gap remains 34.8 points. Human annotation identifies states mentioned without enough supporting detail as the largest category of unrecovered information, and model-assessed supervision-relevant states retain substantial gaps. Full terminal scrollback improves recovery to 73.6% but still trails full context. Under matched report lengths, context-informed rewrites improve recovery by about eight points, without a detectable targeted-over-natural advantage. Positive gaps persist across harnesses and independent DeepSeek execution and measurement. CTXGAP establishes report-based task-state recoverability as a complementary evaluation dimension.

</details>

<a id="paper-241"></a>

### 241. [Outperforming 1000 Euclidean Layers With Hyperbolic Goal-Conditioned RL](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20009v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20009

**作者：** Timo Klein、Thomas Lang、Andrii Shkabrii、Lukas Miklautz、Yllka Velaj、Sebastian Tschiatschek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20009v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20009v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究用双曲几何替代欧氏几何做目标条件对比强化学习：在 CRL 目标中以双曲测地距离替换欧氏距离，在困难导航任务上以 64 层双曲智能体超越 1024 层欧氏网络，训练更快、参数更少。核心结论是表示几何匹配任务可替代网络深度。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关联，但「表示几何匹配任务、以几何偏置换取深度」的思路可能启发 latent 状态的几何选择与深度/计算权衡，可供核查表示几何如何影响推理状态的度量与更新。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Outperforming 1000 Euclidean Layers With Hyperbolic Goal-Conditioned RL

**abstract/A1：** Scaling self-supervised contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) to 1000-layer Euclidean neural networks has enabled state-of-the-art goal-reaching performance, yet wall-clock time scales linearly with depth. We find that changing the geometry achieves stronger performance with far shallower networks. This matters in complex navigation environments, where walls can make route distance deviate substantially from Euclidean distance. In a U-shaped maze, we show that the longer the route, the more it deviates from Euclidean distance. Hyperbolic CRL addresses this mismatch by replacing Euclidean distances in CRL's contrastive objective with geodesic distances in hyperbolic space. On hard navigation tasks, inductive bias beats scale: depth 64 hyperbolic agents outperform 1024-layer-deep Euclidean networks while training more than $12 \\times$ faster and using about $13 \\times$ fewer parameters. We observe that hyperbolic agents behave qualitatively better in hard humanoid mazes, where they follow corridors to reach goals, whereas Euclidean agents try straight-line shortcuts over walls. Our results show that matching the representation geometry to the task can substitute for substantial network depth. Code is at https://github.com/Probabilistic-and-Interactive-ML/HypGCRL .

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [On the Convergence of Semi-Gradient TD: A Mean Field Perspective](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20014v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20014

**作者：** Haruki Settai、Tadashi Kozuno、Shinji Ito

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20014v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20014v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 mean-field 框架下分析两层神经网络中 semi-gradient TD 的稳定性，指出熵诱导的松弛可压过 bootstrap 目标敏感性，从而证明指数收敛到唯一不动点并量化其相对 Bellman 解的偏差，并推广到 soft Q-learning。

**相关性简评：** 其关于迭代更新动力学的不动点存在性、收敛条件与正则化偏差的分析思路，可能可供核查 loop／迭代式 latent 推理状态的稳定性与停止判据，但与本主线无直接关联，属远域借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Convergence of Semi-Gradient TD: A Mean Field Perspective

**abstract/A1：** Semi-gradient temporal-difference learning is a core algorithmic component of reinforcement learning, but bootstrapping with function approximation generally induces a non-conservative vector field and can lead to instability. We analyze this effect for two-layer neural networks in the mean-field regime and identify a simple stability mechanism in which entropy-induced relaxation dominates the sensitivity of the moving bootstrap target. Under this condition, we prove exponential convergence to a unique fixed point and quantify its bias relative to the unregularized Bellman solution. We also show that the same mechanism extends to entropy-regularized off-policy control through soft Q-learning.

</details>

### 243. [Sequential Learning-Based Physics-Informed Neural Network Modeling for Parameter Estimation on a Power Converter](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20041v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20041

**作者：** Samuela Rokocakau、Panagiotis Kakosimos、Giulia Tresca、Pericle Zanchetta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20041v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20041v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出分阶段训练的 PINN，按灵敏度分析确定 L→C→rL→rC 的梯度释放顺序，从时域数据辨识 DC buck 变换器的电感、电容与寄生电阻；五阶段方案 L、C 误差在 5% 内、串联 ESR 在 10% 内，三阶段变体精度相当而快 11%。

**相关性简评：** 其「按灵敏度排序的分阶段梯度释放」可能为 LVR 中的训练阶段划分与监督/贡献分配提供线索，但该文属电力电子参数辨识的远领域应用，无内部推理状态或视觉证据，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sequential Learning-Based Physics-Informed Neural Network Modeling for Parameter Estimation on a Power Converter

**abstract/A1：** A staged physics-informed neural network (PINN) is proposed for identifying the inductance, capacitance and parasitic resistances of a DC buck converter from time-domain data. Gradients are released in the order of L to C to rL to rC, a sequence derived from sensitivity analysis. The five-stage schedule recovers L and C to within 5% and the series ESR to 10%, while a three-stage variant attains the same accuracy 11% faster. An 84 factorial sweep confirms that the trained PINN remains accurate for simultaneous 8% perturbations of all four components, achieving a waveform RMSE of 2.6 x 10-2 over 5 ms-an order-of-magnitude better than a simultaneous-training baseline.

**comment/C1：** IEEE ECCE 2026

</details>
