### 244. [GeoCloak: An Agentic Framework for Semantics-Preserving Geolocation Privacy via Peripheral Clue Manipulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20055v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20055

**作者：** Yixin Wu、Mingjie Li、Yun Shen、Michael Backes、Yang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20055v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20055v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GeoCloak，一个 agentic 框架：分析图像识别地理位置相关线索，对「视觉上边缘但具决定性」的线索施加定向语义编辑，并自动配置对抗扰动，在效用反馈下迭代抑制 LVLM 的地理定位推断，跨四个数据集与五个 LVLM 评测隐私保护效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「视觉—地理不对称」诊断与按线索定位、抑制视觉证据的规划机制，可能与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、不同区域对模型输出的贡献分配及视觉旁路问题有概念联系，可供核查；但该文面向隐私防御，并非 latent 推理或推理状态生成研究。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoCloak: An Agentic Framework for Semantics-Preserving Geolocation Privacy via Peripheral Clue Manipulation

**abstract/A1：** The rapid advancement of large vision language models (LVLMs) has made geolocation inference from images highly accurate and accessible. This poses serious privacy risks: a passive adversary with black-box LVLM access can infer a user's location from a single photo with no metadata. Existing protection methods rely almost exclusively on adversarial perturbation, which introduces perceptible visual artifacts and utility degradation. We identify the visual-geolocation asymmetry: geolocation-decisive clues, such as street signs, tend to be visually peripheral but dominate location inference. Exploiting this asymmetry, we propose GeoCloak, an agentic framework for semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection. It acts as a clue-aware planner that analyzes each image to identify geolocation-relevant clues, dispatches targeted semantic edits to directly suppress peripheral clues, and autonomously configures adversarial perturbation to further suppress residual geolocation features, all under iterative utility-aware feedback. We evaluate GeoCloak on four diverse datasets spanning 108 countries and 576 cities against five LVLMs and three inference methods. GeoCloak reduces overall city-level accuracy from 41.2% to 9.9%, approximately half of the best-performing baseline, while achieving substantially superior utility preservation both qualitatively and quantitatively. We hope this work takes a meaningful step toward practical, semantics-preserving geolocation privacy protection, giving users greater control over the location information inadvertently embedded in their shared images.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-245"></a>

### 245. [EvalBuilder: Can LLMs Build Efficient Benchmarks?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20099v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20099

**作者：** Huaisheng Zhu、Hangfan Zhang、Teng Xiao、Siyuan Xu、Shijie Zhou、Zhimeng Guo、Zhengyu Chen、Jingang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20099v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20099v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将自动基准构建形式化为元评价问题：给定任务与昂贵参考基准，考察 LLM agent 能否造出低成本代理基准，并提出 EvalBuilder 框架从保真度、效率与稳健性三个维度评估其是否保持模型排序、能否泛化到新模型与回复。

**相关性简评：** 其「代理基准保真度与稳健性」的元评价思路，可能可供核查如何以更低成本评估 LVR/VLM 推理方法的排序、泛化与实际成本，但原文不涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理，联系较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EvalBuilder: Can LLMs Build Efficient Benchmarks?

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used for long-horizon research and engineering, yet evaluating these capabilities remains expensive, time-consuming, and heavily dependent on human expertise. This motivates a natural question: can LLM agents themselves construct efficient benchmarks that preserve the conclusions of costly reference evaluations? We study this capability by formulating automatic benchmark development as a meta-evaluation problem, where an agent is given a task and an expensive reference benchmark and requested to construct a lower-cost proxy benchmark that faithfully distinguishes among candidate models and solutions. Based on this formulation, we introduce EvalBuilder, a framework for evaluating such benchmark-generation capabilities along three key dimensions: fidelity, efficiency, and robustness. Specifically, we assess whether generated benchmarks preserve model rankings, generalize to held-out models and freshly collected responses, and provide well-calibrated and discriminative scores while substantially reducing evaluation cost. We further provide an iterative environment in which agents can refine their benchmarks under a fixed time budget. Our framework provides a systematic testbed and benchmark for understanding when current LLM agents can develop useful evaluators and how benchmark construction itself can be increasingly automated.

</details>

<a id="paper-246"></a>

### 246. [Exhaustive Canonical Skeletons Generation: A Formal Dataset for Decomposable Symbolic Regression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20108v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20108

**作者：** Giorgio Morales、Anikó Ekárt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20108v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20108v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用确定性约束上下文无关文法穷举生成一元「canonical skeleton」符号回归数据集，使一个骨架概括多种具体函数形式以减少冗余，并配套采样策略与三层共 150 题的基准，显示现有 SR 方法仍有明显困难。

**相关性简评：** 其「骨架概括具体形式、消除结构重复」的思路与 LVR 中的状态抽象、latent 轨迹去冗余可能有概念类比，可作为表示压缩与覆盖度的对照线索；但对象是符号回归而非多模态推理，联系需较多核查，暂不宜优先阅读。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exhaustive Canonical Skeletons Generation: A Formal Dataset for Decomposable Symbolic Regression

**abstract/A1：** Neural symbolic regression (SR) models require diverse datasets of mathematical expressions for robust training. However, existing datasets rely predominantly on stochastic sampling methods, which suffer from high structural redundancy and incomplete functional coverage. To address this, we introduce an exhaustive dataset of univariate canonical skeletons generated via a deterministic constrained context-free grammar. A canonical skeleton acts as a generic structural representation that subsumes more specific functional forms, eliminating redundant expressions and avoiding repeated computational efforts. Although our dataset consists of univariate skeletons, it is designed to support the decomposable SR paradigm to solve multivariate SR problems without assuming linear separability. Furthermore, we pair this dataset with a sampling strategy that generates clean numerical data without triggering numerical instabilities or relying on artificial protected operators. Canonicalizing two existing neural SR datasets reveals that our dataset is substantially more compact, eliminates structural repetitions, and achieves a uniform operator distribution free of simplicity bias. Finally, we establish a benchmark of 150 SR problems across three tiers, on which state-of-the-art SR methods are shown to struggle significantly.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE Computational Intelligence Letters

</details>

<a id="paper-247"></a>

### 247. [From Spectral to Frobenius: When Muon Loses Its Spectral Implicit Bias](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20112v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20112

**作者：** Yongtao Wu、Mahdi Mansouri、Zhenyu Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20112v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20112v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究 Muon 优化器的隐式偏置：证明有限 Newton-Schulz 迭代与稳定常数使 Muon 在线性可分分类中趋向 Frobenius 而非 spectral 最大间隔方向，并在动量下保持该结论，而当迭代次数随训练对数增长时可恢复 spectral 偏置。

**相关性简评：** 隐式偏置刻画训练过程最终选择何种几何性质的解，或可供核查其对 LVR 训练中 latent 状态与表示几何、监督目标如何塑造解的类比启发，但二者联系属推测，需先确认是否具有可操作关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Spectral to Frobenius: When Muon Loses Its Spectral Implicit Bias

**abstract/A1：** Prior work on implicit bias shows that gradient descent selects the Frobenius max-margin direction on linearly separable problems, whereas Fan et al. (2025) established spectral max-margin behavior for spectral-norm steepest descent methods, including idealized Muon with exact orthogonalization. In practice, however, Muon approximates orthogonalization using finite Newton-Schulz iterations and a positive stabilization constant. We prove that these implementation choices cause Muon to select the Frobenius rather than the spectral max-margin direction in linear classification with separable data. We also establish this behavior for both exponential moving average and Nesterov-style momentum. The key technical ingredient is a summable perturbation argument showing that the resulting parameter trajectories remain at bounded distance from those of gradient descent. We then disentangle the roles of Newton-Schulz iteration count, the stabilization constant, and the Newton-Schulz polynomial, revealing distinct asymptotic behaviors, including directions that achieve neither Frobenius nor spectral max-margin. Finally, we show that Muon can recover its spectral max-margin bias with Newton-Schulz iterations growing logarithmically over training.

</details>

<a id="paper-248"></a>

### 248. [Maximising Transparency in Context Compaction Improves Retention of Facts and Commitments for Lightweight Coding Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20116v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20116

**作者：** Felix Woolford

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20116v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 transparent-compact，一种用于轻量编码智能体的确定性上下文压缩方法：逐字保留用户与助手的原文，并显式标注压缩中被丢弃的信息，在承诺遵守与事实召回基准上与默认的模型摘要式压缩及 pi-vcc 比较。

**相关性简评：** 其关于有损状态压缩下信息保留、显式标注丢失内容、以及「自信出错」与「承认未回忆」的区分，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的信息坍缩、视觉证据保留，以及回答是否真正使用 latent 内容的评价提供类比线索，具体对应关系有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Maximising Transparency in Context Compaction Improves Retention of Facts and Commitments for Lightweight Coding Agents

**abstract/A1：** Long coding agent sessions outgrow the model's context window, so agents compact: older history is replaced by something shorter, usually a model-written summary. A summary selects and rewords, and the agent cannot see what was lost. Existing work has explored a range of compaction techniques, but comparatively little attention has been paid to their effectiveness with lightweight models, including those deployed locally or used for high-volume workloads. Here, we present transparent-compact, a simple deterministic method for the open-source Pi coding agent, designed specifically for use with such models and with the purpose of maximising the transparency of the agent's view of its own compacted context. It guarantees that user and assistant prose in its context is unchanged by a model or algorithm, and explicitly flags where compaction has stripped information. We compare it with Pi's default model-written summary compaction and with pi-vcc, a popular deterministic method with similar methods but without verbatim retention of prose. Our benchmarks run on four models of varying performance levels, with emphasis on Qwen3.8-27B: a lightweight model at the top end of what consumer hardware can serve locally. On sets probing commitments (rules the user set which exist only in the conversation) and recall of facts, transparent-compact matched or outperformed both alternatives. In particular, it showed an advantage in avoiding confident blunders, i.e. assertion of false information as opposed to recognizing that something was not recalled. Even with a frontier model, its advantage was present but smaller. We argue that transparent-compact spares lightweight models from reasoning or searching their way around what compaction lost, and results in improved recall and application of information as a result, without the need for task or context-specific structuring of the session history.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-249"></a>

### 249. [A Bregman Proximal Viewpoint on Neural Operators](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20120v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20120

**作者：** Abdel-Rahim Mezidi、Jordan Patracone、Saverio Salzo、Amaury Habrard、Massimiliano Pontil、Remi Emonet、Marc Sebban

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20120v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20120v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把神经算子层的作用看作 Banach 函数空间上 Bregman 正则优化问题的极小解，将激活算子解释为对偶空间到原空间的 Bregman 邻近算子，并据此提出包含逆激活的 Bregman 神经算子变体，在 Fourier 神经算子上实验显示有利于训练更深、更准的模型。

**相关性简评：** 「层即邻近算子／优化极小解」的视角可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的迭代更新规则、循环深度与长度泛化的训练稳定性提供可核查的建模线索，但属纯数学算子学习，与多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Bregman Proximal Viewpoint on Neural Operators

**abstract/A1：** We present several advances on neural operators by viewing the action of operator layers as the minimizers of Bregman regularized optimization problems over Banach function spaces. The proposed framework allows interpreting the activation operators as Bregman proximity operators from dual to primal space. This novel viewpoint is general enough to recover classical neural operators as well as a new variant, coined Bregman neural operators, which includes the inverse activation operator and features the same expressivity of standard neural operators. Numerical experiments support the added benefits of the Bregman variant of Fourier neural operators for training deeper and more accurate models.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICML 2025

</details>

<a id="paper-250"></a>

### 250. [Seeing at the Edge: Energy Characterization of Vision-Language Model Inference on Edge Platforms](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20136v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20136

**作者：** Junlin Liao、Harshit Kondle、Di Liu、Zonghua Gu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20136v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20136v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在树莓派 5（Hailo-10H NPU）与 Jetson Orin Nano Super 上测量 Qwen2-VL-2B 及其纯文本版本的整机能耗、延迟与热行为，并把每次请求的固定能耗与随输出长度增长的能耗拆开比较。

**相关性简评：** 其「固定成本 vs 每 token 成本」的分解及约 40 token 处的能耗交叉点，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的成本权衡与长度泛化提供定量参照，但该文属部署能耗刻画，与 latent 推理机制距离较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Seeing at the Edge: Energy Characterization of Vision-Language Model Inference on Edge Platforms

**abstract/A1：** Vision-language models (VLMs) are moving onto edge devices, but little is known about how much energy they draw on single-board computers with NPU or GPU accelerators, or where that energy goes. We measure total-system energy, latency, and thermal behavior of Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct and its text-only sibling on a Raspberry Pi 5 with a Hailo-10H NPU and on a Jetson Orin Nano Super, on both CPU and accelerator, and separate the fixed energy of each request from the energy that grows with output length. Because the accelerators use different quantization and serving software, we compare deployment stacks rather than silicon. Three findings stand out. First, NPU offloading makes the Pi a much more efficient VLM platform: it cuts energy per generated token by about two-thirds, leaves the host CPU largely free, and avoids the thermal throttling that the CPU backend reaches in sustained use. Second, an image adds both fixed and per-token energy, but at typical response lengths generating the answer costs far more than processing the image. Third, the Jetson GPU favors short responses through its lower fixed cost per request, whereas the Pi NPU favors longer ones through its lower cost per output token; their energy curves cross near 40 output tokens.

</details>

<a id="paper-251"></a>

### 251. [In-Context Batch Black-box Optimization via Flow Matching](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20138

**作者：** Quoc-Anh Hoang Nguyen、Xinyu Zhang、Daolang Huang、Julien Martinelli、Samuel Kaski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20138v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FMTR：离线用条件流匹配学习一个以历史为条件、可生成变长批次的摊销式提议分布，训练时以 Thompson sampling 的 argmax 为起点，经随优化时域变化的 Langevin 映射推向高价值区域，测试时固定参数、无需重拟合代理模型或数值优化采集函数。

**相关性简评：** 该工作把「何时探索、何时利用、如何并行产生多个候选」摊销进一个历史条件的生成模型，可能为 LVR 中并行 latent 状态生成与动态计算/停止决策下的预算分配提供概念线索，但属远域迁移，需核查其时域条件设计是否可类比推理步数泛化。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** In-Context Batch Black-box Optimization via Flow Matching

**abstract/A1：** Batch black-box optimization is important for expensive systems where many candidates can be evaluated in parallel, yet only a few rounds of evaluation are affordable. In this regime, effective batch construction must identify multiple promising regions while avoiding redundant evaluations, and conventional Bayesian optimization can require increasingly difficult batch-selection procedures as the batch size grows. We introduce Flow Matching for Thompson-Sampling Refinement (FMTR), an amortized approach that learns offline a continuous, history-conditioned proposal distribution from which variable-size batches can be sampled. FMTR is trained offline by conditional flow matching on Thompson-sampling argmaxes refined toward high-value regions via a horizon-dependent Langevin pushforward map, adapting the exploration-exploitation tradeoff over the optimization horizon. At test time, the model parameters remain fixed and batch generation requires neither task-specific surrogate refitting nor numerical acquisition optimization. FMTR achieves strong performance against batch BO and in-context baselines on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, while substantially reducing the computational cost of batch selection.

</details>

<a id="paper-252"></a>

### 252. [Dataset-Level Regularization for Joint-Embedding Self-Supervised Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20158v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20158

**作者：** Konstantinos Almpanakis、Anna Kreshuk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20158v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20158v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 out-of-batch 正则化：在 CPU 内存中维护数据集级状态，用小批量训练时全局构造的实例目标替代批内估计，以求更忠实地逼近全数据集正则梯度，缓解 joint-embedding 自监督学习的表征坍缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「数据集级状态 + 防表征坍缩」的正则化视角可能与 LVR 关注的内部状态信息坍缩、表示几何问题有概念联系，可供核查；但该工作为纯视觉自监督，不涉及多模态推理状态，迁移线索较间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dataset-Level Regularization for Joint-Embedding Self-Supervised Learning

**abstract/A1：** Self-supervised joint embedding learning aligns representations of augmented views while regularizing their distribution to prevent collapse. Regularization aims to shape the overall representation distribution, but most joint embedding methods restrict its computation to the active minibatch. We propose out-of-batch regularization, which maintains a dataset-level state in CPU memory and uses globally constructed per-instance targets during minibatch training. Evaluated on natural images and biomedical datasets, these anchors approximate the full-dataset regularization gradient more faithfully than conventional minibatch estimation at small batch sizes. Applications to contrastive (SimCLR) and non-contrastive (SIGReg) approaches show consistent improvement over the corresponding in-batch objectives for image classification, minority-class performance under controlled imbalance, instance retrieval, and continued self-supervised pretraining, with the largest gains at small batch sizes. These results suggest that dataset-level regularization can be decoupled from minibatch optimization, making batch size an optimization choice that does not affect the geometry of the regularizer.

</details>

<a id="paper-253"></a>

### 253. [Drivers of LLM Consensus Formation Under Competing Incentives: A Simulation Study](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20200v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20200

**作者：** Isabel Jones、Matthew Edwards、Janina A. Hoffmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20200v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20200v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用「网络共识」博弈评估多智能体 LLM 在激励冲突下达成一一致的过程，变化通信可达性与网络结构，测试六个开源模型和 ChatGPT 5.6-sol，发现开源模型需全局广播才能达成共识，闭源模型靠更低固执度、更强跟随多数倾向和更信息丰富的消息在局部通信甚至无通信下也能共识，而高聚集网络会普遍降低共识率。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关联，但「消息中编码邻居状态的信息量」「通信拓扑如何决定集体结果」这类线索，可能为 latent 多步推理中状态传递该携带多少任务相关信息、以及聚合结构的影响提供类比参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Drivers of LLM Consensus Formation Under Competing Incentives: A Simulation Study

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments that produce unanimous agreement, both in intended interactions that need coordination and in unintended emergent interactions. Consequently, understanding how LLMs with competing incentives reach consensus is crucial to better interpret and design LLM collectives. We introduce an evaluation, based on the Network Consensus game, to assess the mechanisms driving this process. Here, a network of agents must agree on one of two colours to receive a payoff, but agents are offered different payoffs for the agreed colour. We vary components such as communication access and network structure and assess six open-source models and one closed model (ChatGPT 5.6-sol). We find that open-source LLMs reach consensus less often than humans in comparable experiments and only if messages are communicated across the global network. ChatGPT 5.6-sol, however, is able to reach consensus with communication limited to its neighbours and with no communication ability. This is achieved through lower stubbornness and higher majority-following tendencies, and by providing more information about its neighbours' states in messages. However, situating agents in a highly clustered graph reduces consensus, regardless of these traits, demonstrating that both system design and individual model traits drive collective outcomes.

</details>

<a id="paper-254"></a>

### 254. [Robust Counterfactuals for Markov Decision Processes under Statistical and Causal Uncertainties](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20210v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20210

**作者：** Jessica Lally、Milad Kazemi、Nicola Paoletti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20210v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20210v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对从轨迹数据估计的 MDP，同时处理转移的统计不确定性与未知因果模型（多个 SCM）的不确定性，提出部分反事实推断框架：给出对所有兼容 SCM 均成立的反事实概率闭式界，并通过在反事实 IMDP 上的悲观值迭代计算达成目标结果的最小鲁棒干预。

**相关性简评：** 其反事实归因、最小干预集与鲁棒界的思路，可能为 LVR 中判断哪些 latent 推理步骤或视觉证据真正导致答案变化、以及做因果抽象与贡献分配提供可核查的借鉴，但原文限于 MDP 序列决策，与 VLM 推理的关联尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Counterfactuals for Markov Decision Processes under Statistical and Causal Uncertainties

**abstract/A1：** Counterfactual reasoning (asking what would have happened, had a decision been different) is a powerful tool for identifying causes of failure and explaining the behaviour of AI systems in sequential decision-making tasks. In Markov Decision Processes (MDPs), two sources of uncertainty complicate counterfactual inference: statistical uncertainty in the MDP transitions when the MDP is estimated from trajectory data, and uncertainty about the unknown causal model, as there are multiple structural causal models (SCMs) compatible with each MDP. Existing work addresses these two challenges in isolation. We address both simultaneously by introducing a framework for partial counterfactual inference in MDPs that is robust to both statistical and causal uncertainty. Our approach provides: 1) closed-form analytical bounds for the counterfactual probabilities that are valid across all SCMs entailed by a given interval MDP (IMDP); 2) a method to compute minimal robust interventions to achieve a desired counterfactual outcome via pessimistic value iteration on the induced counterfactual IMDP. Since the latter is constructed using our analytical bounds, our approach remains efficient and scalable to realistic MDP environments. Experiments across multiple case studies demonstrate our method's robustness and practical utility for identifying minimal sets of actions that, if changed, would have led to improved outcomes.

</details>

<a id="paper-255"></a>

### 255. [Design Space Exploration of Reinforcement Learning Configurations for Post-Legalization Placement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20224v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20224

**作者：** Foteini Oikonomou、Maria Pantazi-Kypraiou、George Goudroumanis、Pavlos Stoikos、Athanasios Tziouvaras、Christos Anagnostopoulos、Georgios Karakonstantis、George Floros

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20224v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20224v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在三个 ISPD2015 基准上系统探索 GNN-PPO 框架用于 legalization 后布局精修的设计空间，比较 18 种配置（奖励函数、GNN 结构、动作空间、候选集大小、训练策略），分析其陷阱并给出一套设计分类方法来指导 RL 模型的设计决策。

**相关性简评：** 属于 EDA 布局这一较远领域，但其对 RL 动作空间、候选集规模与训练策略的系统消融与陷阱总结，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 动作空间设定、候选视觉证据筛选与训练配置选择提供可核查的方法模板。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Design Space Exploration of Reinforcement Learning Configurations for Post-Legalization Placement

**abstract/A1：** Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) have recently been explored for VLSI placement optimization, but their effectiveness for post-legalization placement refinement remains unclear. This paper presents a systematic design space exploration of a GNN-Proximal Policy Optimization (GNN-PPO) framework across three ISPD2015 benchmarks. We evaluate 18 configurations covering reward functions, GNN architectures, action spaces, candidate-set sizes, and training strategies. We analyse the results we obtain to identify pitfalls and to assess the prospects and opportunities of the RL methods for post-legalization placement. On top of that we propose a design classification approach to guide the design decisions of RL models.

</details>

<a id="paper-256"></a>

### 256. [Retrieval Over Training: Similarity search-based Model Selection for Time Series Anomaly Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20243v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20243

**作者：** Christos Panourgias、Roberto Stanzione、Adrien Petralia、Themis Palpanas、Paul Boniol

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20243v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20243v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RAMSAD：给定新时间序列，在历史实例知识库中按相似度检索 top-k 序列，迁移其检测器性能记录以完成模型选择，在分布外设置下比现有选择器更稳健，且以更低成本接近全检测器集成。

**相关性简评：** 其「相似度检索 + 历史性能迁移」的思路，可能为 LVR 中按 latent 相似度筛选证据或选择计算路径/假设提供类比；但该文面向时间序列异常检测器选择，与多模态推理状态的关联仍属推测，需核查其表示与检索粒度是否有借鉴价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Retrieval Over Training: Similarity search-based Model Selection for Time Series Anomaly Detection

**abstract/A1：** Anomaly detection is a fundamental task for time-series analytics, with important implications for the downstream performance of many applications. Despite the large number of anomaly detection methods proposed in the literature, recent benchmark studies have shown that no single detector performs best across highly heterogeneous time series. Therefore, a practical and scalable solution is to develop a model-selection method that, for a given time series, selects the anomaly detector most likely to perform well. Nevertheless, the model selection approaches proposed in the literature suffer from a significant drop in accuracy when applied in Out-of-Distribution (OOD) settings. In this paper, we tackle the aforementioned limitation and propose RAMSAD, a retrieval-based framework for model selection in time-series anomaly detection. Given a new time series, our method queries a knowledge base of previously observed instances, retrieves the top-k most similar series, and transfers detector recommendations from their performance profiles. The framework can operate with standard similarity measures as well as embedding-based representations. Our results show that RAMSAD outperforms existing baselines in In-Distribution (ID) settings and is the most robust selector in OOD, where, when selecting a small subset of detectors, it matches or exceeds ensembles that run the entire detector pool, at a fraction of their cost. Overall, we demonstrate that similarity-based retrieval constitutes a strong and efficient foundation for model selection in time-series anomaly detection.

</details>

<a id="paper-257"></a>

### 257. [FUSE: Fairness-aware Update Screening against Byzantine Exploitation in $q$-Fair Federated Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20264v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20264

**作者：** Tiantong Sun、Jinlin Liu、Xingru Wei、Zequan Fang、Wei Chen、Qiang Yang、Xiaojin Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20264v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20264v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 q-fair 联邦学习下的拜占庭鲁棒聚合，在仅可见公平缩放后客户端更新的「update-only」接口下分析标签偏斜时更新的几何结构，并提出结构感知聚合器 FUSE，借助少量可信标注数据做系数恢复、尺度与锥一致性筛选和筛后步长控制。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是区分「公平导致的合法放大」与「恶意缩放」的结构化筛选与少量可信标签校准，可能为判断 latent 推理状态是证据驱动还是虚假放大、以及贡献归因提供类比线索，但领域相距较远，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FUSE: Fairness-aware Update Screening against Byzantine Exploitation in $q$-Fair Federated Learning

**abstract/A1：** Client-level fair federated learning, in the form of $q$-fair federated learning ($q$-FFL), agnostic federated learning, or tilted empirical risk minimization, has emerged as an attractive solution to equalize utility across heterogeneous clients. This paradigm grants high-loss clients greater relative influence, while a central server aggregates their loss-weighted updates into a single shared model. Yet, the Byzantine robustness of $q$-fair federated learning has been largely neglected, leaving the server unable to tell legitimate fairness-induced amplification from malicious update scaling. To address this critical gap, we formalize the problem of Byzantine-robust $q$-fair federated learning under an \\emph{update-only interface}, in which the server observes nothing but the fairness-scaled client updates. Our analysis under shared class-conditional label skew shows that fairness scaling preserves the nonnegative class-mixture structure of honest updates, while replacing their unit-sum constraint by a client-dependent radial scale. Moving beyond magnitude-based screening, we show that this warped geometry supplies two complementary tests, one radial and one structural, that a single norm cannot provide. We then introduce FUSE, a structure-aware robust aggregator that uses a small trusted labeled dataset for coefficient recovery, scale and cone-consistency screening, and post-screening step-size control. Across four benchmarks and seven Byzantine attacks, FUSE improves seed-wise worst-attack accuracy over the strongest baseline on each benchmark by 3.9--30.1 percentage points, while largely preserving the influence of the high-loss honest clients that fairness is meant to protect.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, 12 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-258"></a>

### 258. [When Does Task-Aware Imputation Matter? An Analysis of Robustness Under Train-Test Mismatch](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20342v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20342

**作者：** Maria V. Salvador、Ana F. Sequeira、Jaime S. Cardoso

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20342v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20342v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把缺失值视为任务条件优化问题中的变量，提出统一框架并实例化为摊销式与 latent optimization 两种方式，在 98 个表格数据集（其中 36 个考察训练—测试失配）上检验其相对经典插补的鲁棒性收益。

**相关性简评：** 来自缺失数据表格学习这一较远领域，但「仅在训练不完整时用 latent optimization 做测试时适配」以及「重构误差不是下游效用的好代理」两点，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态是否被答案真正使用、以及如何评价 latent 内容与效用脱节提供可供核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Does Task-Aware Imputation Matter? An Analysis of Robustness Under Train-Test Mismatch

**abstract/A1：** Missing data is a pervasive challenge in machine learning, often degrading predictive performance and complicating model deployment. Most existing approaches treat imputation as a preprocessing step independent of the downstream task, a separation that becomes problematic under train-test mismatches in missingness mechanisms or rates. We introduce a unified framework that treats missing entries as variables in a task-conditioned optimization problem. We instantiate this framework in two settings: an amortized approach for scenarios with missing data at both training and test time, and a latent optimization approach for settings where only training data is incomplete. The evaluation is conducted on 98 tabular datasets, and robustness under train-test mismatches is assessed on 36 datasets. Results reveal that reconstruction error constitutes a poor proxy for predictive utility (Spearman rho approximately 0.16 to 0.32), and that task-aware imputation becomes critical in scenarios where classical methods fail to recover complete-data performance, achieving win rates of up to 80% in challenging train-test mismatch scenarios.

</details>

<a id="paper-259"></a>

### 259. [Fractional is Better: Learnable Derivative Orders in Neural Operator Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20363v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20363

**作者：** Fares B. Mehouachi、Saif Eddin Jabari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20363v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20363v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究神经算子是否应输入与 PDE 微分结构对齐的特征：证明提供导数特征可改进逼近界，并发现最优导数阶 β\* 严格小于 PDE 阶 m，源于谱空间的偏差—方差取舍，进而提出可学习分数阶导数特征的架构无关增强 ∂-NO。

**相关性简评：** 其「可学习连续阶数 + 闭式谱空间偏差—方差分析」可能为 LVR 中「显式特征该注入多少、latent 状态如何被自动正则化」提供类比视角，可供核查；但研究对象为 PDE 算子代理，与视觉多模态推理链路较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Fractional is Better: Learnable Derivative Orders in Neural Operator Learning

**abstract/A1：** Neural operators learn mappings between function spaces, enabling fast surrogate solutions to partial differential equations. Despite remarkable architectural diversity, these methods often share a common input representation: raw coordinate-value pairs $(x, u(x))$. We ask whether inputs aligned with PDE differential structure can improve learning. Through Picard iteration on mild solutions, we show that derivatives of the input appear explicitly in the solution operator, suggesting that providing derivative features should reduce the network's implicit differentiation burden. We prove this intuition: providing derivative features improves approximation bounds from $O(W^{-(s-m)/d})$ to $O(W^{-s/d})$, where $W$ is parameter count, $s$ is input regularity, $m$ is the PDE order, and $d$ is spatial dimension. Our central finding, however, is a surprise: the optimal derivative order $β^\*$ is strictly less than the PDE order $m$. This gap arises from a bias-variance tradeoff in spectral space that we characterize in closed form. Learning $β$ from data achieves automatic spectral regularization. We introduce $\\partial$-NO (del-NO), for derivative-augmented neural operators, an architecture-agnostic augmentation that provides learnable fractional derivative features to any neural operator backbone. Across benchmark problems and architectures, $\\partial$-NO consistently improves prediction accuracy, with learned orders that reflect a representation of known physics modulated by noise and finite-sample constraints.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at ICML 2026. 37 pages, 11 figures, 13 tables. Code: https://github.com/FaresBMehouachi/delNO

</details>

<a id="paper-260"></a>

### 260. [From Human Corrections to Learned Segmentations: A Critical Mathematical Review of Information, Learning, and Human Effort](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20396v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20396

**作者：** Jon Sporring

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20396v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20396v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 这是一篇约 40 页的批判性综述，用统一的信息论与风险评估框架比较主动学习、机器教学、交互式分割与顺序预测评估，试图回答人类纠正何时转化为有效学习、以及在同等人工投入下如何定义「更好的分割」，本身不提出新的采集算法或声称性能更优。

**相关性简评：** 其条件信息分解、超额风险分解与「同等投入」评价口径，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选的实际贡献、选中位置与标签的相对信息量、以及 latent 步骤是否真正被后续推理使用提供可核查的分析视角；但领域为分割与人机交互，暂无与 VLM 推理的直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Human Corrections to Learned Segmentations: A Critical Mathematical Review of Information, Learning, and Human Effort

**abstract/A1：** When does a human correction become useful learning, and what establishes better segmentation at matched human effort? This critical narrative review follows feedback through its selection, learner update, and evaluation. It compares active learning, machine teaching, interactive segmentation, and sequential predictive evaluation using a common mathematical account of target information, predictive error, response predictability, and human work. An exact Bayesian update converts evaluation-label information into expected log-loss improvement; an excess-risk decomposition explains what an approximate learner can lose. Conditional information decompositions separate the roles of selected locations, labels, and displayed context. Empirical comparisons show why response protocols, update mechanisms, and effort denominators matter, distinguishing current-mask refinement from reusable learning and simulated interaction from human evidence. We identify what logs can reveal, what requires independent evaluation, and what remains model-dependent. The resulting research priorities are to test what selection reveals, whether using it improves assimilation, and whether the resulting interaction policy benefits segmentation at matched effort. These are distinct claims: predicting a teacher's responses need not identify human intentions or establish better learning. No new acquisition algorithm or empirical superiority is claimed.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages, literature-selection and search record included

</details>

<a id="paper-261"></a>

### 261. [One Size Does Not Fit All: Curvature-Aware Adaptive Perturbation for SAM](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20415v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20415

**作者：** Alessia Rossi、Felipe Campelo、Gabriel L. Oliveira

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20415v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20415v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 SAM 固定扰动半径 ρ 忽略局部曲率的问题，提出 CAP-SAM：把 ρ 视为扰动信号强度，按 sharpness ratio 自适应调制，并证明其非凸随机设定下收敛率与 SAM 一致，在 CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet 上优于 SAM 及主要变体。

**相关性简评：** 其「依局部曲率自适应调节扰动强度」的机制可能为 latent 推理状态更新中的扰动/探索强度与动态计算分配提供可供核查的线索，但该工作属通用优化与泛化领域，与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，迁移需较多推敲。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** One Size Does Not Fit All: Curvature-Aware Adaptive Perturbation for SAM

**abstract/A1：** Poor generalisation in deep neural networks is frequently associated with convergence to sharp minima. Sharpness-Aware Minimisation (SAM) improves generalisation of deep neural networks by minimising a combination of the training loss and the local sharpness of the loss landscape, biasing convergence towards flat minima. However, SAM relies on a fixed perturbation radius $ρ$ that ignores local curvature, leading to inefficient behaviour: the perturbation may be too small to escape poor basins defined by sharp local minima, or too large to allow convergence to solutions with good loss-sharpness tradeoff. We reinterpret $ρ$ as the intensity of the perturbation signal and propose Curvature-Aware Adaptive Perturbation for SAM (CAP-SAM), a geometry-aware method that modulates the perturbation radius based on a sharpness ratio, enabling a more effective search for robust minima on the loss landscape. We prove that CAP-SAM preserves the same convergence rate as standard SAM in non-convex stochastic settings. Extensive experiments indicate that our approach consistently outperforms SAM and its most prominent variants across diverse network architectures on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet.

</details>

<a id="paper-262"></a>

### 262. [Tracking a Small Set of Experts in Online Convex Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20416v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20416

**作者：** Angelos Assos、Anand Brahmbhatt、Elad Hazan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20416v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20416v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究在线凸优化中与「仅在 m 个专家间切换 k 次」的比较器竞争的问题，在 hypergraphic planted-clique 猜想下证明多项式时间算法与信息论最优后悔之间存在本质的计算分离。

**相关性简评：** 其「维护少量专家并决定何时切换」的后悔—计算代价权衡，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设的选取、动态计算分配与切换/停止决策提供抽象参照，但联系较远，可供核查能否迁移到推理期状态维护。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tracking a Small Set of Experts in Online Convex Optimization

**abstract/A1：** For the fundamental problem of prediction from expert advice, Freund asked whether one could compete with a comparator that switches $k$ times among only $m$ experts. For a discrete set of experts, this problem was resolved by Bousquet and Warmuth (2020), who gave an efficient algorithm with optimal regret matching the information-theoretic limit. We study the analogous problem in online convex optimization, where the action space is continuous. An inefficient algorithm can be derived by discretizing the action space and applying the Bousquet--Warmuth algorithm, giving regret of $\\widetilde{O}(md+k)$, while the best known efficient algorithm for this problem has regret $\\widetilde{O}(dk)$. We show that this computational inefficiency is inherent, establishing a separation between the information-theoretic and polynomial-time regret achievable for this problem. Specifically, under the hypergraphic planted-clique conjecture, we show that for every polynomial-time algorithm there exist instances for which it incurs at least $(k\\sqrt d)^{1-o(1)}$ regret.

</details>

<a id="paper-263"></a>

### 263. [OrionMoE-v1.0: A Mixture-of-Experts Foundation Model for Tabular In-Context Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20418v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20418

**作者：** Mohamed Bouadi、Nassim Bouarour、Aditya Tanna、Vinay Kumar Sankarapu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20418v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20418v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OrionMoE-Small，一个面向表格上下文学习的紧凑基础模型：在双轴 Transformer 上沿特征轴做稀疏 MoE，路由同时依据单个特征表示与数据集上下文，并以结构化因果先验生成的合成分类任务预训练，下游预测不做梯度适配。

**相关性简评：** 其「数据集条件化稀疏路由」是自适应分配计算的一种实现，可能为 LVR 中动态计算与逐特征/逐位置状态路由提供可供核查的线索；但该工作面向表格数据，不涉及视觉证据或 latent 推理状态，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OrionMoE-v1.0: A Mixture-of-Experts Foundation Model for Tabular In-Context Learning

**abstract/A1：** Tabular foundation models enable in-context learning by predicting unseen observations from a labeled support table without task-specific parameter updates. Despite recent advances, most existing approaches rely on dense architectures that apply the same transformations across features and datasets. We introduce OrionMoE-Small, a compact tabular foundation model that combines distribution-aware representations with dataset-conditioned sparse mixture-of-experts computation. Built on a dual-axis Transformer architecture, OrionMoE-Small selectively activates experts along the feature axis, with routing decisions informed by both individual feature representations and the broader dataset context. This design enables adaptive allocation of computation across heterogeneous tabular problems while retaining a shared computational pathway across tasks. OrionMoE-Small is pretrained entirely on synthetic classification tasks generated from a structured causal prior and performs downstream prediction without gradient-based adaptation. Experiments across multiple tabular benchmarks demonstrate competitive performance against substantially larger foundation models, with particularly strong results on small-context and high-dimensional datasets. Further analysis reveals favorable relative performance under class imbalance and highlights the importance of feature composition.

</details>

<a id="paper-264"></a>

### 264. [Continual Learning for Reliable In-Sequence Molecular Communication](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20426v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20426

**作者：** Siddhant Setia、Junichi Suzuki、Tadashi Nakano

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20426v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20426v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究分子通信中可靠的按序信息传递，提出 Affine Memory Replay：保存过去仿真样本并用插值、外推合成回放样本，在持续学习中正则化模型更新，以增量方式估计不同实验设置下的通信性能。

**相关性简评：** 领域与 LVR 相距较远，但其「回放旧样本＋插值/外推合成样本以平衡稳定性与可塑性」的持续学习思路，可供核查是否可用于 latent 推理轨迹的顺序训练与遗忘抑制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Continual Learning for Reliable In-Sequence Molecular Communication

**abstract/A1：** This paper considers reliable in-sequence molecular communication, where bio-nanomachines exchange molecule-encoded information in a specified order, and investigates a new simulation-based method to incrementally evaluate communication protocols by gradually increasing the complexity of experimental settings. The proposed method, called Affine Memory Replay (AMR), builds a continual learning (CL) model to estimate communication performance between bio-nanomachines. AMR stores past simulation samples and synthesizes additional replay samples through interpolation and extrapolation between past samples. Through incremental performance estimation tasks, it replays both past and synthetic samples to regularize model updates, helping the model adapt to new experimental settings while retaining knowledge from earlier ones. Experimental results show that AMR significantly outperforms existing CL methods in maintaining overall estimation accuracy across sequentially learned estimation tasks. It effectively balances memory stability and learning plasticity under moderate training cost.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 7 figures. Published in 2026 20th IIAI International Congress on Advanced Applied Informatics

</details>

<a id="paper-265"></a>

### 265. [The Accuracy Well: Nonmonotone Accuracy Dynamics at Vanishing Initialization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20439v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20439

**作者：** Yaroslav Gusev、Eugene Golikov、Dmitry Yarotsky

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20439v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20439v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在小初始化条件下研究分类精度的非单调动态，提出「精度井」现象（先快速上升、随后下降、很久后恢复），在深层线性网络、MNIST/CIFAR 网络与合成任务上观察，并给出「目标驱动组织—退化瓶颈—重新招募恢复」的三阶段解释及线性网络理论。

**相关性简评：** 论文指出精度可揭示损失无法反映的训练动态，这一「指标非单调、损失近乎不变」的解耦视角可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的评价与监督提供类比线索：仅凭 loss 或状态收敛判断推理质量或许会遗漏行为变化，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Accuracy Well: Nonmonotone Accuracy Dynamics at Vanishing Initialization

**abstract/A1：** We study classification dynamics under small initialization and identify a pronounced non-monotone behavior of accuracy that we call the accuracy well: accuracy rises rapidly early in training, subsequently drops, and only much later recovers. We observe this phenomenon in deep linear networks, fully connected ReLU networks on MNIST, deep convolutional networks on CIFAR-10, and controlled synthetic tasks, across different losses and optimizers and in both training and test accuracy. We propose a three-stage explanation in terms of target-driven organization, a degeneracy bottleneck, and subsequent recruitment and recovery. We develop a rigorous theory of these dynamics for shallow and deep linear networks, showing in particular that high accuracy can emerge while the predictor and the change in loss remain asymptotically vanishing, before continued optimization causes accuracy to deteriorate. For a simple synthetic classification task, we prove an extreme instance in which accuracy asymptotically rises to 100%, collapses to 0, and later recovers permanently to 100%. Our results show that classification accuracy can reveal aspects of learning dynamics that are not captured by the training loss alone.

**comment/C1：** 124 pages, 11 figures. Submitted to ICLR-2027: https://openreview.net/forum?id=OZ6JYRP75V

</details>

<a id="paper-266"></a>

### 266. [SafeAdapt-Poker: Counterfactual Risk Calibration for Budgeted Adaptation under Opponent Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20473v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20473

**作者：** Miaobo Hu、Shuhao Hu、Xiaobo Guo、Xin Wang、Bokun Wang、Rui Chen、Daren Zha、Jun Xiao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.GT、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20473v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20473v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在扑克对手行为漂移下研究「预算受限的适应」，将其刻画为双反事实的序贯控制问题：每步分别估计偏离冻结锚点的局部下行风险，以及现在消耗预算会损失多少未来适应价值，并用累积 KL 账本限制总偏离、在配对校准会话上筛选候选控制器。

**相关性简评：** 其「当下消耗预算换取即时收益 vs 保留预算获取未来信息」的权衡、风险校准与有限候选扰动界，可能与 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的动态计算预算分配和停止决策形成类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SafeAdapt-Poker: Counterfactual Risk Calibration for Budgeted Adaptation under Opponent Shift

**abstract/A1：** Opponent histories can mislead after behavioral shifts. SafeAdapt-Poker formulates budgeted adaptation under opponent shift as a dual-counterfactual sequential-control problem using only information available before each decision. At each decision the controller asks two distinct questions: what local downside a deviation from the frozen anchor would induce under matched legal continuations, and what future adaptation value would be lost by consuming part of the remaining deviation budget now. The first question is answered by a monotone strategic-risk model over deviation intensity; the second by a paired budget-state continuation model that prices current budget consumption. The controller selects among support-preserving candidate deviations by trading immediate adaptation value against these two quantities, a pathwise cumulative-KL ledger bounds total anchor-relative deviation, and a finite family of frozen controllers is screened on complete paired calibration sessions before held-out interaction. A finite-candidate perturbation bound shows that the allocation objective degrades continuously with continuation-value estimation error. Evaluation covers exact-game exploitation and safety, four opponent shifts, local-risk calibration, delayed-opportunity allocation, direct opportunity-value estimation, value-error robustness, factorial mechanism attribution, budget sensitivity, abstract-HUNL cross-play, and player/time-held-out public-history behavior. On exact Leduc the full controller attains a matched weak-pool gain of +0.0718 with a worst-opponent delta of -0.0126, raises delayed-opportunity capture from 0.6318 under myopic allocation to 0.7841 under the same session budget, and reaches +42.7 mbb/hand in abstract-HUNL cross-play; the retained public-history screen reports 0.5820 action accuracy and 0.9277 log loss versus 0.5039 and 0.9778 for a public prior.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-267"></a>

### 267. [Average-Reward Policy Identification from a Single Continuing Trajectory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20486v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20486

**作者：** Tongxi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20486v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20486v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究平均奖励强化学习中仅凭单条持续轨迹学习近最优策略的问题，提出一种自适应安排「在哪里收集信息」的算法，给出交互次数上界并证明匹配的信息论下界，说明在最坏情况下单轨迹学习并不比无限制采样更难。

**相关性简评：** 其「按环境提供信息的方式调整信息收集需求」的思路，或可类比 LVR 中何时、何处获取视觉证据与动态计算、停止决策的成本权衡，线索较远，可供核查但需较多迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Average-Reward Policy Identification from a Single Continuing Trajectory

**abstract/A1：** In reinforcement learning, an agent must explore an unknown environment to learn how to make good decisions. Unlike methods that can freely sample any state--action pair, an agent learning from a single trajectory must spend additional time navigating between states. It is therefore natural to expect that learning from a single trajectory requires more experience. In this paper, we show that this additional cost is not always necessary. For average-reward reinforcement learning, we develop an algorithm that learns where to collect information while ensuring that the required samples can be obtained efficiently. We establish an upper bound on the number of interactions needed to identify a near-optimal policy. Furthermore, we prove a matching information theoretic lower bound, showing that, for a broad class of environments and at sufficiently high accuracy, learning from a single trajectory is no harder in the worst case than learning with unrestricted access to state action samples. Our results reveal that the additional complexity cost of navigation can be avoided by adapting information gathering requirements to how the environment provides information.

</details>

<a id="paper-268"></a>

### 268. [Graph-Enhanced Disease Trajectory Discovery and Temporal Endpoint Forecasting from Longitudinal Electronic Health Records](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20502v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20502

**作者：** Jin-Jia Zheng、Vincent S. Tseng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20502v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20502v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 MIMIC-IV 上构建图增强框架，先划分患者再建图，学习训练集内的连续入院转移并冻结端点特异的分支 motif 策略，用时间感知 Transformer 通过 cross-attention 为每个终点检索至多 24 个患者匹配 motif 来预测事件时间，并报告长程时序获益与生存、GNN 修复方面的负结果。

**相关性简评：** 「检索结构化证据并经 cross-attention 注入预测」的设定，加上证据主要支持 730 天以上长程时序、对端点判别和因果归因无稳定增益的结论，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容贡献分配的核查提供类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Graph-Enhanced Disease Trajectory Discovery and Temporal Endpoint Forecasting from Longitudinal Electronic Health Records

**abstract/A1：** Longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) can reveal disease-progression structure, while useful forecasting requires leakage-controlled discovery, censor-aware outcomes, and inspectable trajectory evidence. We developed a graph-enhanced framework in MIMIC-IV v3.1 that splits patients before graph construction, learns train-only consecutive-admission transitions, and freezes endpoint-specific branching-motif policies selected on validation data. The final catalog contained 99,527 motifs, and a time-aware Transformer retrieved at most 24 patient-compatible motifs per endpoint through endpoint-specific cross-attention. Across five training seeds, micro-AUPRC increased from 0.9330 to 0.9357, while event-time MAE decreased from 676.9 to 657.7 days (reduction 19.2 days; 95% paired patient-cluster bootstrap CI, 13.8-24.8). Timing effects were heterogeneous: MAE worsened for events within 730 days but improved by 58.9 days for events occurring after 730 days. In an exploratory internal temporal split, branch conditioning reduced conditional event-time MAE by 26.9 days (95% CI, 23.0-30.8) without evidence of better micro-AUPRC. Corrected survival evaluation over the supported 0-2,555-day interval did not establish macro C-index or integrated Brier score superiority over a matched history-only discrete-hazard model. A directed, degree-matched GNN repair substantially improved unseen-edge discovery, yet replacing GNN-derived scores within fixed motif identities did not improve downstream forecasting. These results position branching motifs as structured trajectory context whose main value is long-range timing support rather than uniformly stronger endpoint discrimination, survival prediction, or causal explanation.

</details>

<a id="paper-269"></a>

### 269. [A Two-Level Clinical Novelty Prioritization Framework for Disease Trajectories Discovered from Longitudinal Electronic Health Records](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20512v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20512

**作者：** Jin-Jia Zheng、Vincent S. Tseng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20512v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20512v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出两级框架，将从纵向 EHR 中挖掘的疾病轨迹 motif 分为语义稀疏度（对照 PubMed 文献嵌入距离）与可靠性调整的临床优先级两层，并对排序稳定性与下游预测增益做敏感性分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「语义评分与下游预测增益不单调」「代理指标需与不确定性/可靠性分离」的结论，可能启发对 LVR 中 latent 轨迹评分与真实收益脱钩、贡献分配的核查，但领域较远，仅为概念线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Two-Level Clinical Novelty Prioritization Framework for Disease Trajectories Discovered from Longitudinal Electronic Health Records

**abstract/A1：** Graph-based analysis of electronic health records (EHRs) yields disease-trajectory catalogs, but structural support does not establish semantic novelty. We present a two-level framework to prioritize 99,527 branching motifs previously discovered by an upstream patient-disjoint, train-only MIMIC-IV v3.1 pipeline and frozen after validation-based catalog selection. The framework separates path-level and node-to-endpoint semantic sparsity from reliability-adjusted clinical priority. Three deterministic textualizations per motif or node-target pair were embedded with BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5 and calibrated against 166,972 pre-2023 PubMed references using mean top-20 cosine distance. Positive recombination occurred in 8.63% of motifs and had negligible effect on global ranking, whereas specificity and support/GNN reliability terms changed clinical-priority ordering. Robustness analyses across neighbor counts, templates, encoders, generic-label taxonomies, score settings, PrimeKG, and corrected density-based RND showed that broad ordering was more stable than hard top-k membership. A semantic-dose analysis did not support a monotonic relation between score and forecasting benefit after endpoint and token-count adjustment (1.96 days per SD; 95% CI, -0.75 to 4.68). In the frozen catalog-q90 high-semantic subgroup, branch-enhanced forecasting reduced conditional event-time MAE by 20.1 days (95% CI, 13.3-27.0) and sMAPE by 2.37 percentage points (95% CI, 1.81-2.93), while discrimination and endpoint-ranking differences were inconclusive. Corrected RND showed low tail agreement with the primary score (Spearman rho = 0.430; q90 Jaccard = 0.125) despite a similar aggregate timing association. These findings support representation-aware, uncertainty-aware literature-relative prioritization of discovered trajectories rather than causal discovery or clinician-adjudicated novelty.

</details>

<a id="paper-270"></a>

### 270. [Bayesian Learning to Select Fair Classifiers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20514v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20514

**作者：** Saatvik Kher、Hyungrok Do、Judy Zhong、Padhraic Smyth

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20514v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20514v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把「在线数据流中挑选能同时平衡预测性能与敏感属性子群公平性的智能体」建模为贝叶斯多臂老虎机，用 Thompson Sampling 在允许真实标签延迟到达的条件下更新选择策略，并在表格模型、大语言模型和人类标注者等真实数据集上评估遗憾下降，同时给出兼顾准确性与公平性的性能保证。

**相关性简评：** 其老虎机式的自适应查询选择与延迟结果下的策略更新，可能为 LVR 中「何时调用视觉证据、动态分配算力、以及延迟监督下的贡献分配」提供问题框架与评价思路；但论文不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，联系较远，线索仅供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayesian Learning to Select Fair Classifiers

**abstract/A1：** Evaluations of classifiers (AI or human or agents) often assume a batch setting in which every agent produces labels on every example in a test set, which can be expensive or impractical in many contexts. This motivates online policies that adaptively select agents to query as examples arrive and update policies as outcomes are observed. We study the problem of selecting, in an online stream, the agent that best balances (i) predictive performance and (ii) subgroup fairness of a sensitive attribute, and that allows for delayed observations of true class labels. We formulate this as a Bayesian multi-armed bandit problem using Thompson Sampling, explicitly balancing accuracy and fairness. We conduct empirical evaluations on real-world datasets spanning problems involving tabular classifiers, large language models, and human labelers, demonstrating substantial reductions in regret relative to baselines. Our theoretical contributions include guarantees on the performance of the selected agent that incorporate both the accuracy and fairness aspects of the problem.

</details>

<a id="paper-271"></a>

### 271. [The Physics Is the Network: Fast, Training-Free Neural Emulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20525v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20525

**作者：** Luka Todorovic、Kaushal Gianchandani、Sai Ravela

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY、nlin.CD、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20525v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20525v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PolyNets，把多项式动力学的连续时间神经表示与简单循环网络耦合作离散时间积分，在 KdV 方程上对比数值求解器与三种已训练神经模型的精度、相位误差和 GPU 吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 其免训练、循环式状态积分与显式数值结构的组合，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的迭代生成与更新机制提供远域类比，但属物理仿真领域，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Physics Is the Network: Fast, Training-Free Neural Emulation

**abstract/A1：** Neural emulation need not require learning a substitute for known physics. We investigate PolyNets that couple a continuous-time neural representation of polynomial dynamics with a simple recurrent network for discrete-time integration. When the governing coefficients are known, the resulting tensor graph requires no training and preserves the prescribed numerical scheme while enabling efficient hardware execution and parallel simulation. Using the one-dimensional Korteweg-de Vries equation, we compare PolyNet with CPU and GPU finite-difference Runge-Kutta solvers and three trained neural models: a state-map emulator, a physics-informed predictor, and a learned-tendency integrator. Analytical soliton solutions provide a reference for amplitude and phase errors across grid resolutions, 10^6 dispersively scaled time steps, ensembles of up to 10^4 trajectories, and varying initial amplitudes. PolyNet achieves accuracy comparable to the numerical solvers, including at amplitudes outside the learned models' training conditions. It combines this fidelity with high GPU throughput, approximately constant over the tested resolution range, and favorable ensemble scaling.

</details>

<a id="paper-272"></a>

### 272. [PhySGA: A Physics-Space Grounded Agentic Framework for Generalizable and Interactive 3D Composition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20560v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20560

**作者：** Weichen Lyu、Chong Xia、Mingyu Dong、Long Xu、Shengjun Zhang、Yueqi Duan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20560v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20560v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PhySGA 提出一个物理空间接地的智能体框架用于交互式 3D 物体组合：Assembly Agent 负责几何与运动学层面的全局规划与粗调，Critic Agent 依据接触动力学诊断模拟器状态并做细粒度闭环修正，配合 Action Router 与自进化技能库，在 Isaac Sim 中处理刚体、关节体和可变形物体。

**相关性简评：** 其「规划—批判—闭环修正」结构与把历史试错蒸馏为可复用技能库的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、停止与验证决策、轨迹经验复用等问题的表述提供类比线索，但研究对象是 3D 物理组合而非 latent 视觉推理，迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PhySGA: A Physics-Space Grounded Agentic Framework for Generalizable and Interactive 3D Composition

**abstract/A1：** 3D object composition is pivotal to digital content creation and robot simulation, while also serving as a critical testbed for physical intelligence in foundation models. Previous methods either leverage large language models to directly predict object coordinates, or utilize generative diffusion priors to synthesize composite images for visual alignment, both of which rely on static priors in digital spaces. However, these methods consistently struggle with complex compositions such as precise hanging, gravity-driven settling and hidden containment, as these tasks require interactive construction processes involving global planning, fine-grained alignment, and physical simulation. To address these challenges, we propose PhySGA, a Physics-Space Grounded Agentic Framework for 3D composition via agent exploration and physical simulation, enabling the universal interactive assembly of rigid, articulated, and deformable objects. Directly applying vanilla LLMs or VLMs to this task proves insufficient, as current models inherently lack grounded physical understanding and interactive construction experience. To overcome this limitation, we design a collaborative feedforward-feedback architecture: an Assembly Agent focuses on geometric and kinematic reasoning for global step planning and coarse adjustment, while a Critic Agent reasons over contact dynamics for simulator state diagnosis and fine-grained closed-loop correction. Furthermore, Action Routers map semantic planning and analysis onto executable physical primitives within the simulator, while a task-specific Self-Evolving Skill Library distills historical trial-and-error feedback into reusable experiences, boosting success rates and minimizing exploration attempts. Extensive experiments within Isaac Sim demonstrate that PhySGA significantly surpasses existing methods in relation fidelity, physical plausibility, and visual quality.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://agenticei.github.io/PhySGA-page/

</details>

<a id="paper-273"></a>

### 273. [Scaling Discovery Requires Forgetting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20563v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20563

**作者：** Shangyin Tan、Ben Zhou、Lakshya A Agrawal、Jialin Zhang、Yilin Chen、Qiuyang Mang、Donghyun Lee、Dennis Jacob、Koushik Sen、Alex Dimakis、Matei Zaharia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20563v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20563v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 LLM 智能体自动科研的规模化瓶颈：单智能体长程迭代、并行以及并行通信三种扩展方式都会随历史积累而停滞，作者提出「遗忘」策略，即周期性丢弃搜索状态（各智能体会话与候选历史）、从当前最优候选重启，在启发式编程、内核优化与模型训练四类任务上取得提升。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 的状态更新与计算分配问题有概念关联：论文观察到历史累积会导致锚定与编辑幅度缩小，这与循环/迭代式 latent 推理中状态过度累积、信息坍缩的隐患类似，其「丢弃再重启」的做法可能为 latent 状态的清理与停止决策提供核查线索，属推测性借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Discovery Requires Forgetting

**abstract/A1：** Automated research with LLM agents has shown promising results in various domains. Through iterative refinement and scored verifiers, LLM agents are capable of discovering new ideas. There are different ways to scale automated research: let one agent iterate for longer, run several agents in parallel, or further let the parallel agents communicate and build on one another's candidates. However, we find that all three strategies eventually plateau as the agents accumulate history. Each agent becomes anchored to its own past attempts, making fewer and smaller edits. Beyond this point, additional budget brings little further improvement. In this paper, we show that the key to scaling discovery further is to forget. Periodically discarding the search state, including every agent's session and the candidate history, and restarting from the best candidate found so far, unblocks the search and pushes the same budget further. We implement forgetting in a team of agents with communication and evaluate it on four tasks spanning heuristic programming, kernel optimization and model training. The results show up to 15% improvement compared to parallel agents, and up to 39% compared to a single agent. Our results suggest that, for open-ended discovery, what an agent is allowed to remember matters as much as how much compute it is given.

</details>

<a id="paper-274"></a>

### 274. [Any Time, Any Space: A World-State-Centric Framework for Agentic Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20571v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20571

**作者：** Weijie Wang、Haomin Zhang、Ye Li、Akide Liu、Zeyu Zhang、Haoyu Zhao、Yefei He、Donny Y. Chen、Feng Chen、Bohan Zhuang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20571v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20571v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ATAS 框架，把剧本编译为带位置、相机与事件时间引用的镜头请求，生成前从静态几何的「场景记忆」和按场景/事件/时间索引的「事件记忆」检索证据，生成后重建并注册片段，且仅在评审与状态检查通过时更新记忆，以提升跨镜头的空间与时间一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其「按场景与时间索引的持久记忆＋生成前证据检索＋准入式状态更新」可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、latent 状态何时写入与停止判断提供类比线索，属于跨领域的可核查启发，非已验证结论。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Any Time, Any Space: A World-State-Centric Framework for Agentic Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Long-form agentic video generation must turn a screenplay into shots that remain spatially and temporally consistent across nonconsecutive requests. Passing prompts or recent frames between shots does not provide a shared spatial frame for revisiting a scene or a time-indexed record for replaying an earlier event. We present Any Time, Any Space (ATAS), which compiles a screenplay into shot requests with explicit location, camera, and event-time references. Before generation, it retrieves relevant evidence from a scene memory of registered static geometry and an event memory of dynamic observations indexed by scene, event, and time. After generation, ATAS reconstructs and registers the clip. It updates memory only when review and state checks accept the observation, while film delivery remains independent of memory admission. Across four video generators, ATAS improves all reported cross-shot consistency measures and achieves the highest aggregate benchmark score for each backbone. It also outperforms the compared agents and 3D-guided methods on target-image spatial revisit, leads three event-recapture criteria, and receives the highest human ratings on all six criteria. The video results and code are available on our project page: https://lhmd.top/atas/.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://lhmd.top/atas/, Code: https://github.com/ziplab/ATAS

</details>

<a id="paper-275"></a>

### 275. [Feed-Forward Multi-View Feature Coding for 3D Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20590v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20590

**作者：** Seungmi Choi、Ji-Hyoun Choi、Suhyun Jo、Tae-Gwon Park、Hui Yong Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20590v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20590v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MFGS：前馈式多视图特征编码框架，用率失真端到端训练把多视图图像编码为紧凑比特流，接收端由解码特征直接预测高斯属性，并用重要性引导的高斯选择适应不同渲染预算。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但「同一比特流下按重要性选择高斯以调节渲染预算」可能为 latent 状态或 token 的预算自适应分配与贡献筛选提供思路，属推测性借鉴线索，需核查其重要性度量是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Feed-Forward Multi-View Feature Coding for 3D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods enable efficient reconstruction of 3D scenes from sparse multi-view images in a single forward pass, without scene-specific optimization. However, existing feed-forward scene-coding pipelines first generate a complete Gaussian representation and then encode its geometry and appearance attributes separately, often involving non-differentiable operations. The resulting 'generate-then-compress' pipeline requires complex attribute-specific codec designs and limits rate-distortion performance by restricting the exploitation of cross-attribute redundancy. We propose multi-view feature coding for Gaussian splatting (MFGS), an end-to-end feed-forward framework for scene coding. Instead of compressing an explicit Gaussian representation, MFGS extracts multi-view features from the input images and encodes them into a compact bitstream. Through end-to-end rate-distortion training, MFGS learns compact multi-view features that jointly represent geometry and appearance, enabling the receiver to predict Gaussian attributes from the decoded features. We further introduce importance-guided Gaussian selection to accommodate different rendering budgets, allowing the receiver to adjust the number of Gaussians used for rendering from the same bitstream. Across RE10K, ACID, and DL3DV, MFGS achieves 99.76-99.86% bitrate savings over the generate-then-compress pipeline, as measured by BD-rate, while reducing total GMACs by 52-55% and providing an approximately 12 times end-to-end speedup.

</details>

### 276. [Who Should We Listen to More? Welfare-Aware Preference Acquisition for Pluralistic Alignment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19035v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19035

**作者：** Ruomeng Ding、Tianwei Gao、Lianrui Geng、Mohit Bansal、Zhun Deng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19035v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19035v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多元价值对齐下的人类偏好反馈分配问题，提出基于社会福利目标的 WAPA 方法，用辅助参考模型估计每条查询的边际福利增益并据此选择查询，在 Roleplay 与 OpinionQA 数据上验证监督质量与分配可控性。

**排除原因：** 纯文本偏好数据采集与对齐策略研究，不涉及视觉/多模态推理状态、证据获取或 latout 生成与使用，与 LVR 主线无实质联系，也未见可迁移的状态更新或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Should We Listen to More? Welfare-Aware Preference Acquisition for Pluralistic Alignment

**abstract/A1：** Pluralistic alignment needs to accommodate diverse preferences under limited human feedback. Using AI simulators to label preference comparisons reduces annotation costs, but variation in simulation accuracy across individuals can leave some people's preferences poorly represented in training. We study how to allocate human feedback across people to construct training data for pluralistic alignment, and how this allocation affects the trained policy. To make this allocation explicit and controllable, we define a social welfare objective over individual supervision utilities that measure label quality. Each query's marginal welfare gain depends on its expected supervision improvement, the recipient's current utility, and application-designated priority. Welfare-Aware Preference Acquisition (WAPA) uses auxiliary reference models to estimate query gains and individual utilities, then selects queries by estimated marginal welfare gain while updating recipient utilities. We prove exact optimality of the acquisition algorithm for the estimated welfare objective under a fixed query budget and model predictions. Experiments on six Roleplay collections and four OpinionQA regions demonstrate improved supervision and controllable allocation tradeoffs. Policy training on four Roleplay collections improves preference prediction for both seen and unseen individuals. When prioritizing a designated group and poorly represented individuals, acquiring human labels for 20% of comparisons recovers 75-86% of the average predictive utility gap between policies trained entirely on AI-generated labels and entirely on ground-truth labels.

</details>

<a id="paper-277"></a>

### 277. [How Inner Optimizers Shape Replica Scaling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19064v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19064

**作者：** Eugene Belilovsky、Stefan Horoi、Edouard Oyallon、Benjamin Thérien

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19064v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19064v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究分布式训练中内层优化器（Muon、AdamW 等）如何影响模型副本扩展的性能退化，提出以白化式预条件作为内层优化器设计目标。

**排除原因：** 论文属分布式训练优化与伪梯度协方差分析，未涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、视觉证据或 latent 推理，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Inner Optimizers Shape Replica Scaling

**abstract/A1：** Training large language models (LLMs) across geographically distributed and poorly interconnected compute pools or data centers has become a topic of significant interest. This has led to increased study of local optimization methods such as DiLoco for LLMs, especially those that use local (or inner) adaptive or preconditioned optimizers, which are critical for high-performance LLM training. However, a central challenge remains: it is well known that increasing the number of model replicas (replica scaling) while fixing the total training budget leads to monotonically decreasing performance. Existing work has empirically demonstrated that the choice of inner optimizer (Muon v.s. AdamW) can significantly improve this trend, but offers only limited explanation of the performance difference. In this work, we consider a range of inner-optimizers and identify two important factors that impact replica scaling: (1) the covariance structure of the pseudo-gradients and (2) the impact of batch size on the pseudogradient's noise scale. Based on these insights, we theoretically and empirically demonstrate that whitening-based pre-conditioning is a powerful inner-optimizer desideratum. Our empirical evaluation extends the results to reveal that whitening-based inner-optimizers achieve strong replica scaling (for 8-96 replicas), with performance relative to DP improving as parameters and data are scaled.

</details>

<a id="paper-278"></a>

### 278. [Towards Training and Inference of Graph Neural Networks in Graph Databases](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19142v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19142

**作者：** Victor Pekkari、Harry Wik、Alfred Clemedtson、Borun Shi、Xuan-Son Vu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DB

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19142v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19142v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究如何在图数据库中直接完成 GNN 的训练与推理，利用 Cypher 实现采样、特征检索乃至完整的 forward pass，并给出约 2.5x 的训练加速与无额外内存开销的 in-DB 推理。

**排除原因：** 属于图数据库层面的 GNN 存储与执行工程优化，既不涉及多模态/视觉推理的 latent 状态，也没有可核查的、面向 LVR 的表示、证据筛选或停止决策线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Towards Training and Inference of Graph Neural Networks in Graph Databases

**abstract/A1：** Graph Neural Networks on large graphs commonly rely on in-memory graph storage with ad hoc data processing pipelines for both training and inference. Graph databases provide scalable storage and efficient traversal for graphs that can evolve over time, but their applicability in GNN training and inference are rarely studied. We show that graph DBs can be used in GNN training and inference directly, executing sampling, feature retrieval, and even the full forward pass. We show different GNN training pipelines can perform on-the-fly sampling and retrieval in graph DBs using Cypher. We further provide optimisations that speed up the training process by \\~2.5x while maintaining the same accuracy. We illustrate that fully in-DB inference is possible, independent of how the GNNs are trained. Our inference setup can be &gt;10x faster than cold loading the graph into RAM with no extra memory overhead on real-world graphs. Our work lays the foundation for in-graph-DB GNN training and inference.

</details>
