### 279. [Agronomic Characterization and Time-Series UAV Phenotyping to Support Field Pea Breeding in Iowa](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19154v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19154

**作者：** Yanben Shen、Joscif Raigne、Asheesh Singh、Arti Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19154v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19154v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究内容为豌豆种质农艺性状评价与 UAV 时序冠层表型选育，属作物育种与遥感表型领域，与 latent visual reasoning、VLM 推理的状态表示、生成或停止决策均无实质联系，也无可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agronomic Characterization and Time-Series UAV Phenotyping to Support Field Pea Breeding in Iowa

**abstract/A1：** Field pea has gained interest in the Midwest U.S. and has the potential to benefit Iowa cropping systems. This study evaluated 17 accessions and 7 Canadian varieties for agronomic and seed yield performance. This study found that PI accession trials exhibit genetic diversity in phenology and canopy traits, whereas the Canadian elite varieties demonstrated great yield potential under Iowa conditions. The accession genotypes matured between 75 DAP (late June) and 95 DAP (late July) across six site-years. The top-yielding Canadian varieties averaged 1961 kg/ha across two site-years. To comprehensively assess field pea performance, this study extracted time-series Unmanned Aerial Vehicle(UAV)-derived traits, assessed UAV-derived traits, and evaluated their correlations with multiple agronomic traits and yield. The results found that UAV-derived time-series canopy area was the most yield-relevant trait (r = 0.63) among all UAV-derived canopy features, vegetation indices, and manually collected measurements. Compared with yield, UAV-derived canopy area showed higher selection reliability (H2 = 0.87, CV = 13.46%), making it suitable for phenotypic selection and accelerating Midwest field pea breeding programs. This study identifies useful UAV-derived features for pea breeding and demonstrates the value of integrating diverse germplasm, elite commercial varieties, and time-series UAV phenotyping. Combining genetic variation from the accession trial with the yield potential of high-performing commercial varieties provides valuable breeding materials and phenotyping strategies for developing locally adapted, high-yielding field pea varieties for future production in the Midwest.

</details>

### 280. [AgentCIScope: Grounded Contextual Integrity Evaluation for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19158v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19158

**作者：** Chengquan Guo、Zhaorun Chen、Zinan Lin、Xiaodong Liu、Bo Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19158v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19158v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 AgentCIScope，一个将公开与合成隐私案例转为可执行多步任务、并以隐私政策、角色约定和敏感属性分类为依据设定披露约束的平台与基准，用于评测 LLM agent 的上下文完整性，发现任务完成能力强并不保证信息流合规。

**排除原因：** 内容属于 agent 隐私与信息流合规评测，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也未触及表示保留、latent 生成、证据筛选或停止决策等主线问题，暂无具体的可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AgentCIScope: Grounded Contextual Integrity Evaluation for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents are becoming increasingly capable of handling daily workspace tasks by interacting with application environments through tool calls. However, as agents read and write sensitive user data across email, documents, and other applications, a central privacy question is whether they preserve contextual integrity (CI), that is, whether the information flows they produce comply with context-specific disclosure constraints. Existing work either focuses on model-level privacy leakage or evaluates agents in limited application environments with manually constructed disclosure constraints. In this work, we introduce AgentCIScope, a unified platform and benchmark for evaluating agent-level contextual integrity through executable, multi-step tasks across diverse, realistic application environments. AgentCIScope transforms public and synthetic privacy cases into executable agent tasks, with disclosure constraints grounded in three criteria: governing privacy policies, persona-based agreement, and a predefined sensitive-attribute taxonomy. Based on AgentCIScope, we evaluate 12 agents on 936 tasks and find that strong task-completion capability does not guarantee contextual-integrity preservation: single-run leakage rates range from 32.5% to 94.4%, while repeating each task five times further increases leakage by 4-19 percentage points. Leakage varies substantially across attributes: personal preferences and booking details have average leakage rates of 84.7% and 76.5%, compared with 17.1% for credit-card information and 29.4% for SSNs; environments: calendar has the highest average leakage rate as an information source, while WhatsApp ranks highest as an information destination; and governing privacy policies. AgentCIScope provides deep insights into agent privacy leakage and serves as a unified testbed for developing privacy-preserving agents.

</details>

<a id="paper-281"></a>

### 281. [Diagnosing Low-Confidence Patient Profiles via Localized Harmful Covariate Shift Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19178v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19178

**作者：** Olivier Lefebvre、Hakima Laribi、Dan Poenaru、Martin Vallières

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19178v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19178v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是临床预测模型的后验可靠性诊断，通过子群发现与协变量漂移检验定位低置信患者群体，属于分布漂移与公平性评估范畴，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、生成、训练或停止决策，缺乏可供迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diagnosing Low-Confidence Patient Profiles via Localized Harmful Covariate Shift Detection

**abstract/A1：** Before deploying clinical prediction models, their reliability must be assessed to ensure safe use. Errors may arise from distribution shifts between training and deployment or from heterogeneous performance where the model systematically underperforms for certain patient subgroups. Yet most post-hoc diagnostic methods evaluate reliability only at the population level, checking whether the deployment data resemble the training data and whether the model performs well on average, without accounting for heterogeneity that may cause some patients to be disproportionately affected. We propose a unified diagnostic pipeline that combines both perspectives. MED3pa first discovers interpretable low-confidence patient profiles, and Detectron is then applied within each profile to test whether its degradation is associated with a detectable harmful covariate shift. Across three clinical prediction tasks and three injected subgroup shifts in MIMIC-IV, we find that shift detectability itself is informative. An injected age shift, undetectable at the population level, was nonetheless localized to specific degraded profiles, whereas sex- and race-related shifts were detected broadly and therefore offered little guidance for identifying the most affected patients.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at SMASH 2026 (Symposium on Machine Learning and Applications in Healthcare). Code available at https://github.com/MEDomicsLab/study\_MED3pa\_SHIFT2026

</details>

<a id="paper-282"></a>

### 282. [FireGraphNet: Sub-daily wildfire spread prediction with directed graph neural networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19191v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19191

**作者：** Aiyshwariya Paulvannan Kanmani、Miguel Esparza、Ali Mostafavi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19191v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19191v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FireGraphNet 将 12 小时野火蔓延建模为有向 H3 图上的节点分类，边特征编码风向、地形、燃料连续性与屏障，在卫星火烧边界数据上优于距离基线，并做了消融与燃料属性干预分析。

**排除原因：** 这是地理时空预测任务，虽用了有向图消息传递与消融分析，但对象是物理蔓延而非模型内部推理状态，未涉及视觉证据筛选、latent 状态生成或停止决策，找不到可供核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FireGraphNet: Sub-daily wildfire spread prediction with directed graph neural networks

**abstract/A1：** Wildfire decisions are made in hours, yet most learned spread models predict daily on targets including burned area. We propose FireGraphNet which formulates 12-hour growth as node classification on directed H3 graphs, with candidates ahead of the front and wind alignment, terrain, fuel continuity and barriers on edges. On satellite perimeters it achieves average precision 1.7-1.9x the strongest distance baseline on test California fires and 1.62x out of state. We show this skill comes from message passing rather than cell features or the aggregator, is highest on young fires in state, is carried deep along the wind by its edges, and is hidden by pooling. A convolutional network matches this ranking once trained on new growth, at worse calibration, so candidate construction, target definition, and aggregation shape conclusions about model skill. Editing fuel and barrier attributes reduces predicted spread more under guided than random placement, extending FireGraphNet to operational intervention analysis.

</details>

<a id="paper-283"></a>

### 283. [PREFER: From Player Experience to Personalized Game Refinement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19213v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19213

**作者：** Qi Chai、Zhang Zheng、Lu Yang、Zixun Sun、Hao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19213v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19213v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 PREFER 框架，用源自心理测量问卷的体验维度，让 LLM 充当虚拟玩家评测游戏体验，并据此为不同 persona 选择改进目标、修改游戏规则并试玩验证偏好提升。

**排除原因：** 主题为 LLM 游戏体验评测与游戏设计迭代，既无视觉或多模态推理，也不涉及 latent 状态生成与使用，未提供可核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PREFER: From Player Experience to Personalized Game Refinement

**abstract/A1：** Understanding how large language models (LLMs) evaluate gameplay experience is important for using their feedback to guide game design. However, overall ratings alone provide limited insight into which aspects of experience are associated with these judgments and how they can inform concrete game changes. We introduce PREFER, a framework that uses a shared set of experience dimensions adapted from established psychometric questionnaires to benchmark how models evaluate gameplay and to guide persona-specific game refinement. Through these dimensions, we characterize the perspectives models offer as virtual playtesters by examining how their overall assessments relate to specific aspects of experience and to the gameplay they produce. To examine how the same dimensions can guide refinement for players with different preferences, we use personas to simulate distinct player profiles. For each persona, PREFER uses experience-preference relationships to select refinement objectives, revises game rules and their implementations, and playtests candidate games to assess experience changes and predicted preference gains. Experiments with five personas show that PREFER achieves 9.2% higher mean post-play preference ratings than the strongest evaluated baseline.

</details>

### 284. [A Gradient-Regularized Deep Learning Method for Restoring Image in Myopic Vision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19218

**作者：** Kebin Contreras、Luis Toscano-Palomino、Rafael Torres、Jorge Bacca

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19218v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是用 U-Net 对近视散焦（Zernike Z\_2^0）做低层图像预补偿/复原，属光学与图像复原应用，未涉及多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策，也未见可供迁移的推理期表示或监督线索，故与 LVR 主线无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Gradient-Regularized Deep Learning Method for Restoring Image in Myopic Vision

**abstract/A1：** Myopic vision degrades the quality of human vision due to spherical eye defocus aberrations that impair the perception of fine spatial details. To address this limitation, this work proposes a deep learning-based approach to computationally design transformed images that allow individuals with myopia in fixed distance to perceive scenes with enhanced clarity. The method trains a neural network to counteract defocus aberrations modeled using the second-order Zernike polynomial $Z\_2^0$. The approach employs a U-Net architecture trained with a loss function combining reconstruction error minimization with spatial difference structural preservation through $\\ell\_1$ regularization. Training is carried out exclusively on the KITTI dataset, while testing is performed on DIV2K and ImageNet datasets, demonstrating generalization across diverse image distributions. Under myopic conditions modeled by $Z\_2^0$, baseline performance shows visual degradation with PSNR values ranging from 19.47 to 27.42 dB across datasets and severity levels. The proposed gradient-regularized method achieves consistent improvements, delivering PSNR gains ranging from 1.48 to 7.34 dB and significantly lower perceptual compared to classical baselines.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19240v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19240v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建金融披露领域的图结构评测：用 11 账户节点、11 交易类型边构成的复式记账图，考察开放权重与专有 LLM 从 SEC 10-K 文本中抽取并分类证据关系的能力，并发布 483 段人工标注的关系分类基准。

**排除原因：** 属于金融文本领域的抽取与关系分类基准，既不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选，也没有生成、训练监督或停止决策方面的机制，仅「schema 引导的证据发现」一词与 LVR 表面相似，缺乏可供核查的具体迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FinGREAT: Evaluating LLMs on Financial Graph-guided Retrieval And Typing

**abstract/A1：** Financial disclosures express the underlying relationships of double-entry accounting through unstructured text. While financial LLM benchmarks predominantly emphasize question answering and classification, schema-guided evidence discovery remains relatively under-explored. We investigate whether open-weight LLMs can extract and classify evidence corresponding to predefined double-entry accounting relationships underlying the text. Using a financial accounting graph comprising 11 nodes (accounts) and 11 edges (transaction types), we evaluate mid-sized models from 5 open-weight families across 1,300 SEC 10-K Item 7 filings spanning 26 years, comparing their extraction performance against three proprietary models. Results reveal substantial differences in extraction volume, edge distributions, and cross-model agreement. Building on this study, we introduce a human-annotated relation classification benchmark comprising 483 passages across three financial accounting categories, evaluating 34 open-weight and 10 proprietary models. The strongest open-weight and proprietary models achieve 84.1\\% and 92.8\\% accuracy, respectively, with considerable variation across accounting categories. Our findings highlight the importance of evaluating evidence discovery and relation understanding separately, while investigating locally deployable models for scalable, privacy-sensitive financial analysis.

</details>

### 286. [MemeTrace: A Multimodal LLM-Based Agent for Safety-Aware Meme Provenance Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19267v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19267

**作者：** Bo Shao、Yihan Ma、Yiting Qu、Michael Backes、Yang Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CY

**内容简述：** 提出 MemeTrace，一个基于多模态 LLM 智能体的表情包溯源与风险分析系统，并在自建 Meme100 数据集上对比 VLM 基线。

**排除原因：** 属内容安全审核的任务级应用，虽为多模态智能体但依赖外部检索与分类，未涉及视觉推理内部状态、latent 生成或监督机制，与 LVR 兴趣缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MemeTrace: A Multimodal LLM-Based Agent for Safety-Aware Meme Provenance Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Online memes are often carriers of unsafe content such as disinformation and propaganda. Their rapid evolution in response to news events and strong dependence on contextual information make it difficult for platforms and moderators to detect and moderate them effectively. Moreover, existing moderation studies typically classify memes as safe or unsafe based on individual images, lacking the ability to perform broader provenance analysis, i.e., where a meme originates, how it spreads, what cultural symbolism it represents, whether it poses potential risks to society, etc. To bridge this gap, we introduce MemeTrace, a multimodal LLM-based agent that automatically traces the provenance and potential risks of emerging memes. We evaluate MemeTrace on Meme100, a dataset of 100 real-world memes, using both human annotation and automatic metrics. The results demonstrate that MemeTrace substantially outperforms the VLM baseline. More importantly, MemeTrace achieves higher accuracy in detecting unsafe memes, especially those involving disinformation and sensitive content, and remains robust under visual perturbations.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19270

**作者：** Maximilian Kirsch、Christine Preisach

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19270v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19270v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用 RNN 对 Sentinel-2 时序数据进行树种分类，并与随机森林、XGBoost 比较，再用 Integrated Gradients 分析区分纯林与混交挪威云杉像元的谱段与物候时间特征。

**排除原因：** 属于遥感地表分类应用，虽涉及时序建模与归因分析，但既非多模态推理，也未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 状态使用或证据决策，与 LVR 主线无实质可借鉴联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploring Interpretability in Deep Learning-Based Forest Classification - Separating Similar Forest Classes using Sentinel-2 Time Series

**abstract/A1：** The ecological insurance theory posits that diverse forests are more resilient and better able to adapt to changing environmental conditions. Increasing drought and heat stress in Central Europe promotes mixed forests for maintaining ecosystem services. However, a diverse species distribution complicates remote sensing-based species mapping, as distinguishing pure from mixed species compositions at the pixel level remains challenging. We evaluated the potential of recurrent neural networks (RNNs) for tree-species classification from Sentinel-2 time series and investigated the spectral-temporal features underlying class discrimination, with particular emphasis on separating pure from mixed Norway spruce pixels. We compared RNN classification models with Random Forest (RF) and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) and used Integrated Gradients (IG) to quantify feature attributions across spectral bands and time. The best-performing RNN achieved a Macro-F1 score of 0.851 on the test data, outperforming XGBoost by 0.012 and Random Forest by 0.103. Beyond classification performance, the attribution analysis revealed distinct spectral-temporal patterns associated with the separation of pure and mixed Norway spruce pixels. Attribution values increased particularly after approximately day of year (DOY) 125, while B11 showed the strongest contribution during periods of increasing spectral divergence between the two classes. These findings show that recurrent models can provide competitive classification performance while also revealing the phenological periods and spectral regions that are most relevant for distinguishing closely related forest compositions. Combining time-series classification with explainability therefore provides additional insight into the spectral-temporal mechanisms underlying tree-species discrimination and may support more detailed mapping of mixed forest structures.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19290

**作者：** Nikolay Semenov、Artem Riabinin、Sergey Bassenko、Anna Averchenkova、Julia Agafonova、Anastasia Aliaskina、Kirill Kozlov、Lev Novitskiy、Nikita Samsonov、Nikolai Gerasimenko、Denis Dimitrov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19290v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Kandinsky S2V 1.0 系列语音驱动人像视频生成模型（2B 与 21B），基于参考图、语音和文本生成说话视频，含蒸馏加速变体并开源。

**排除原因：** 属于音频驱动视频生成的技术报告，虽有多尺度视觉记忆与蒸馏加速，但均是生成保真与采样效率机制，未涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Kandinsky S2V 1.0: Audio-Driven Avatar Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** We present Kandinsky S2V 1.0, an open family of speech-to-video models for controllable audio-driven human animation, comprising Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite (2B parameters) and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro (21B parameters). From a single reference image, a speech waveform, and a text prompt, the models generate long-form 25 FPS video in which lip motion, expression, pose, and gesture follow the speech. The architecture draws text and audio conditioning from one Qwen2.5-Omni model and adds a reference-aware three-scale visual memory. Training starts from the Kandinsky 5.0 Video foundation models \[1\] and combines mixed reference-memory layouts with an area-adaptive face loss. The training corpus contains 1.17M single-speaker clips selected by active-speaker detection, with captions generated directly in English and Russian. A side-by-side human evaluation against the strongest open-source baseline and two proprietary commercial models shows that Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro performs comparably to or better than these models on average and is also preferred for lip synchronization. On common speech to video benchmarks, Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro rank among the top performing models according to mean rank over five reported metrics. Beyond the base models, we provide π-Flow distilled variants of both Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Lite and Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro. The distilled Kandinsky S2V 1.0 Pro model generates video in 16 sampling steps without classifier-free guidance, achieving a 5.4 times end-to-end speedup over the base model, while matching or exceeding it on nearly all reported benchmark metrics. To accelerate open research and deployment in speech-to-video generation, we release the code, model checkpoints, and diffusers integration under the MIT license.

**comment/C1：** Technical report on the open-source S2V model

</details>

### 289. [Generalization Analysis of Biased Stochastic Gradient Methods for Minimax Problems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19322v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19322

**作者：** Shuang Zeng、Yunwen Lei、Yiming Ying

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19322v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19322v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是 minimax 优化的有偏随机梯度稳定性与泛化理论分析（含 zeroth-order、clipped SGDA 的 excess risk 界），与 LVR 关注的多模态推理状态表示、证据使用、停止决策无实质联系，也未见可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generalization Analysis of Biased Stochastic Gradient Methods for Minimax Problems

**abstract/A1：** Minimax optimization refers to a class of optimization problems with some variables to minimize and other variables to maximize. Biased stochastic gradient methods have shown practical success in solving minimax problems to either improve robustness, enhance communication efficiency, or decrease computational costs. These successes motivate a lot of theoretical works to study the convergence of biased stochastic gradient methods, while their generalization analysis remains untouched. In this paper, we present the first framework to study the stability and generalization of biased stochastic gradient methods for minimax problems. We establish a connection between stability and generalization for minimax problems by relaxing the existing bounded gradient assumption to a bounded second moment condition. We then introduce a generalized Lipschitz-type condition on bias and gradient estimators, and derive a general stability bound to clarify the connection among bias, gradient estimators, and stability. We apply our general analysis to Zeroth-order and Clipped stochastic gradient descent ascent (SGDA), and derive stability bounds that match those of SGDA under appropriate smoothing/clipping parameters. We combine stability and convergence analyses together, and derive optimal excess risk bounds of order $1/\\sqrt{n}$, where $n$ is the sample size.

**comment/C1：** to appear in NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19330

**作者：** Hochul Hwang、Jahir Sadik Monon、Soowan Yang、Shiven Umeshbhai Patel、Anh Nhat Hong Nguyen、Kien Nguyen、Keshav Garg、Dylan Gage、Duretti Hordofaa、Anshu Anjna、Eshed Ohn-Bar、Donghyun Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19330v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19330v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NavAble 数据集与合成数据生成管线，覆盖 11 类无障碍关键物体，用于盲人导航场景的语义分割与感知可靠性提升。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于辅助导航的分割数据集与数据合成，虽含 VLM 过滤与合成资产生成，但未涉及视觉推理的状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NavAble: A Large-Scale Dataset and Synthetic Data Generation Pipeline for Blind Navigation

**abstract/A1：** Reliable recognition of accessibility-critical objects (e.g., audible pedestrian signals, door-activation buttons, and handrails) is essential for assistive navigation technologies that support safe, independent mobility for blind and low-vision (BLV) users. Yet these categories are underrepresented in existing large-scale vision datasets, and the few datasets that include them suffer from limited class coverage, inconsistent annotations, and poor diversity. We introduce NavAble, a large-scale dataset spanning 11 accessibility-critical object classes, combining 41K curated open-source images with 8K newly collected, densely annotated real-world images. To extend scale and diversity, we develop a synthetic data generation pipeline that renders high-fidelity 3D assets across 37 environments under diverse styles and viewpoints, yielding 452K frames with rich ground-truth annotations from 565 assets. The pipeline minimizes manual effort by combining automated web image crawling, VLM-based filtering, SAM 3D-based asset generation, and convenient camera trajectory configuration, enabling large-scale data generation at a diversity unattainable through manual collection. Experiments show that fine-tuning existing segmentation models on the NavAble Dataset achieves reliable accessibility-object segmentation (&gt;80% mIoU on our held-out test set). We also demonstrate the effectiveness of synthetic data augmentation, which yields up to +3.5 mIoU over real-world-only fine-tuning. Beyond benchmark evaluation, validation on egocentric data from two mobility-assistive robots demonstrates improved perception reliability in real-world navigation scenarios. Together, NavAble establishes a scalable foundation for BLV navigation perception, directly addressing the data scarcity and embodiment gaps. The dataset and pipeline are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/NavAble/NeurIPS\_2026\_BLV.

</details>

### 291. [Can We Believe AI Peer Review?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19339v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19339

**作者：** Zongrui Yang、Haoyuan Li、Zhongsheng Wang、Gillian Dobbie、Jiamou Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19339v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19339v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 AI 审稿分数的可重复性、分布估计与跨模型一致性，属于科学评审元评估，未涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策，也无可借用为 LVR 评测或训练的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can We Believe AI Peer Review?

**abstract/A1：** As large language models become widely used in scientific review and manuscript revision, AI scores are increasingly serving as feedback for assessing paper quality and guiding edits. Yet whether a single AI score reliably reflects the reviewer's judgment, and whether revision gains based on that score are endorsed by other reviewers, remain understudied. Using 200 papers from ICLR 2025 and 2026 and approximately 14,000 AI reviews, we study score reliability, score-distribution estimation, and cross-model agreement on revision gains. Repeated reviews fluctuate even under identical conditions. Finer-grained scoring protocols reduce score variance but also systematically shift mean scores, while the gains assigned to manuscript revisions depend strongly on the reviewer.

**comment/C1：** 8Pages and Long appendix.(include supplementary material)

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19369v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文比较行人检测器架构（YOLO11、RT-DETR、YOLO26）与跟踪器（ByteTrack、BoT-SORT）在拥挤场景下的 HOTA 与速度表现，属于视频多目标跟踪的工程选型评测，不涉及 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，未提供可供核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Impact of Detector Architecture, Model Scale, and Scene Density on Multi-Pedestrian Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Pedestrian tracking in video is important for applications such as surveillance and scene analysis, but it becomes more challenging in crowded scenarios with frequent occlusions and rapid motion. This paper analyzes how detector architecture, model scale, scene density, and inference regime affect the tracking of multiple pedestrians. We compare the YOLO11, RT-DETR, and YOLO26 detector families with the ByteTrack and BoT-SORT trackers on sequences from the MOT17, MOT20, and SOMPT22 benchmarks. For YOLO26, we also compare traditional overlap filtering via Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) with end-to-end inference. The RT-DETR family achieved the highest mean scores on the HOTA tracking quality metric across all three benchmarks with both trackers, with a more pronounced advantage on MOT20, albeit at a higher computational cost. On MOT17 and SOMPT22, YOLO26 variants, especially YOLO26-L with NMS, offered favorable combinations of quality and speed compared with RT-DETR. In the YOLO26 inference comparison, the end-to-end mode did not yield consistent tracking gains over the NMS-based regime. Taken together, these results provide a practical guide for selecting detectors and trackers for this task based on application priorities.

**comment/C1：** 6

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19382v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19382v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文聚焦端侧韩语小模型的词表裁剪、任务感知量化与 NPU 部署，属效率与工程落地工作，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、latent 生成或证据使用，也没有可核查的状态更新、监督或决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ixi-sLM: On-Device sLM for AI Call Agent Service

**abstract/A1：** Running an AI call agent on-device makes it private, network-independent, and free of per-query cloud cost, but shipping one as a real-world service imposes constraints a cloud deployment never faces: ~1GiB memory budget, in-call latency and thermal limits, static-integer NPU execution, Korean STT input far from sLM pretraining, and cloud-level quality expectations. We bring ixi-sLM, a 1.2B Korean sLM, to mobile NPUs through ON-DEVICE MODELING: domain-adaptive modeling trims the vocabulary by call frequency to shrink the embedding and LM head that take nearly half the model under INT4 transformer weights and refits the tokenizer to call speech (18% fewer tokens on call traffic), while task-aware quantization calibrates on real calls and assigns per-module mixed precision by activation sensitivity. A single domain-adapted model runs ixi-O's two production services, AI Call Summary and AI Call Answering, at 94% of a state-of-the-art cloud LLM's task-weighted quality; our on-device modeling carries that quality onto the NPU with only a marginal drop in overall score, in an 803MiB model (28% of FP16) at 23% of the CPU's per-call energy. The same recipe deploys across Qualcomm and Apple NPUs and serves several million ixi-O subscribers in production.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on On-Device Intelligence: Foundation Models under Real-World Constraints

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文讨论分布式 LLM 训练的并行配置投影与自动调优，目标是吞吐量工程优化，与 LVR 的视觉证据保留、latent 状态生成、推理停止决策等主线没有实质联系，其中的「低成本打分器驱动搜索」也属通用工程范式，未构成具体可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Trial-and-Error Loop: Hybrid Projection and Automated Tuning for Distributed Training

**abstract/A1：** Configuring distributed LLM training across hundreds of GPUs requires jointly optimizing tensor, pipeline, expert, context, and data parallelism alongside micro-batch size, recomputation, sharding, and overlap, a combinatorial space where memory legality can hinge on a single byte of activation accounting. Today, teams navigate it through expensive multi-node trial and error, burning GPU-hours on crashes and suboptimal recipes. We present Primus Projection, which closes this loop with two coupled components. First, a hybrid projection engine that measures what it can and simulates what it cannot: GPU microbenchmarks on as few as a single device capture compute kernels and any in-scope communication; a sub-node downscale-measure-upscale workflow analytically restores out-of-scope collectives, FSDP overlap, and the full pipeline schedule at the multi-node target. A pure simulation mode runs entirely on CPU, enabling pre-silicon planning without any GPU. Second, the Primus Tuning Agent treats the projection engine as a fast scoring oracle (seconds per candidate instead of tens of minutes) and uses it to drive an intelligent search over the configuration space. A deterministic seed planner sweeps single axes; an LLM investigation loop proposes cross-axis combinations the planner cannot reach, with an analytic memory pre-filter that rejects infeasible candidates before any GPU time is spent. Validated on Llama 3.1 (dense) and Mixtral 8x22B (MoE) across two GPU generations, all projections stay within ~10% of measured multi-node throughput, and fidelity holds at production scale: 4.7% error on DeepSeek V3 671B at 1,024 GPUs and 1.9% on Qwen3-235B-A22B at 256 GPUs. In a Mixtral 8x22B case study, the Primus Tuning Agent discovers a configuration delivering +27% throughput over the published 4-node BF16 reference at the same scale, in under 30 minutes of single-node exploration.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 22 pages, 8 figures, 25 tables

</details>

### 295. [Comprehensive Comparisons of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms in Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19429v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19429

**作者：** Dechen Xie、Mushuang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在无信号交叉口的自动驾驶决策任务上对比了四种强化学习算法（FVI、RCPI、DQN 及离散动作版 D-DDPG）的最优性、安全性、训练稳定性与训练/决策成本，并据此给出方法选择建议。

**排除原因：** 属于自动驾驶决策中 RL 算法的通用基准比较，未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成或视觉证据使用，训练/数据效率与稳定性的权衡也停留在任务层面，缺少可供 LVR 核查的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Comprehensive Comparisons of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms in Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** This paper presents a comprehensive comparison of four representative reinforcement learning (RL) methods in autonomous vehicle (AV) decision-making, including: Fitted Value Iteration (FVI), Rollout Classification Policy Iteration (RCPI), Deep Q-Network (DQN), and a discrete-action adaptation of Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (D-DDPG). The four methods are applied to the same AV decision-making problem at an unsignalized intersection and are compared in terms of optimality and safety, training stability, and training and decision cost. RCPI is the closest to a reference solution and has the most stable training process. However, it is the least data-efficient. D-DDPG reaches the second-smallest gap to a reference solution with the highest data efficiency, but has the least training stability. DQN is the most stable model-free method but has the highest collision rate. FVI achieves a lower collision rate than DQN, but is less data-efficient and less stable. Based on these results, we provide suggestions for selecting a method according to whether a model is available, the budget for training, and whether stable training is required.

</details>

### 296. [AdCoEvo: A Co-Evolving Multi-Agent Advertising Delivery System](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19454v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19454

**作者：** Tianzhu Hu、Hang Yu、Zeyuan Zhao、Zeyu Pan、Huan Luo、Chun Gan、Mingzhe Cao、Liubin Wang、Mingming Li、Jiao Dai、Jizhong Han、Songlin Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19454v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19454v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文面向广告投放的多智能体协同进化系统，关注出价、预算与策略更新的业务收益，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态与证据使用；其「诊断—规划—调整」反馈与参数更新属具体行业流程，未见可供 LVR 核查的状态表示或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AdCoEvo: A Co-Evolving Multi-Agent Advertising Delivery System

**abstract/A1：** Digital advertising is an important means for platforms to reach potential users and drive business growth. Advertising delivery is the core link between marketing objectives and actual returns. Existing methods have achieved partial automation in individual tasks such as bidding and budget allocation, but they still rely on human operators for overall coordination, making it difficult to support large-scale operations. They also often fail to achieve optimal performance, and individual experience remains difficult to turn into reusable operational capabilities. In addition, as advertising markets change, advertising delivery systems need timely strategy updates to remain effective in dynamic markets. To automate the entire advertising operations process and improve adaptability to market changes, we propose AdCoEvo, a co-evolving multi-agent advertising delivery system. Specifically, the system connects delivery decisions across different timescales and operational granularities through role specialization. Diagnostic results guide account planning and strategy adjustment in the next period. The multi-agent system updates its model parameters based on actual delivery outcomes and continually improves delivery strategies through mutual feedback among planning, adjustment, and diagnosis, thereby achieving co-evolution. Experiments show that, relative to the rule-based operational baseline (Rule), AdCoEvo increases advertiser value by 14.02% while reducing cost per acquisition by 7.07%. Compared with a multi-agent system with fixed parameters, AdCoEvo increases advertiser value by 3.96% and reduces cost per acquisition by 1.24%, indicating that co-evolution can yield greater delivery returns.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19458

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19458v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19458v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PFedMA，一个面向异构隐私需求的联邦多智能体框架，通过维护历史能力与可靠性状态引导智能体选择，并让隐私规则在本地与全局之间双向演化。

**排除原因：** 论文核心是联邦多智能体编排与隐私规则演化，围绕客户端选择和数据去中心化，未讨论视觉证据保留、latent 推理状态生成或答案对内部状态的依赖，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系，也未见可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PFedMA: Who Should Join Next as Federated Multi-Agent Orchestration Evolves?

**abstract/A1：** Federated Multi-Agent Systems (FedMAS) offer a promising paradigm for collaborative reasoning among LLM-based agents while keeping raw client data decentralized. However, reliable multi-round collaboration remains challenging. Agent selection must take into consideration evolving task requirements and the agent's historical reliability, while privacy rules must adapt to changing collaboration contexts and meet the client's personalized needs. To address these challenges, we propose PFedMA, a federated multi-agent framework for feedback driven multi-round collaboration under heterogeneous privacy requirements. Across rounds, PFedMA maintains historical capability and reliability states to guide agent selection, evaluates task responses before aggregation, and refines subsequent queries according to accumulated collaboration feedback. PFedMA enables bidirectional local to global and global to local private rule evolution across heterogeneous clients. Clients generalize locally updated privacy rules and selectively incorporate globally maintained rules, thereby facilitating cross-client privacy rule evolution while preserving personalized constraints. Extensive experiments across reasoning, privacy sensitive, and adversarial settings show that PFedMA improves task performance, reduces measured privacy leakage, and improves system robustness.

</details>

### 298. [Robust Real-Time Monocular Human Pose Estimation with Insole-Based Sensor Fusion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19494v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19494

**作者：** Liang Yu、Tao Zhu、Xiangqing Lu、Wenjian Luo、Liming Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19494v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19494v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文采集同步的鞋垫压力/加速度与视觉多模态数据，提出按身体部位解耦的时序融合框架，把足部传感特征并入下肢分支以在遮挡或短暂出画时补偿视觉缺失，并在三种实时视觉骨干上验证姿态精度与步态指标。

**排除原因：** 属于可穿戴传感器融合的姿态估计任务，虽涉及「视觉证据不完整」，但未触及 VLM 内部状态、latent 推理状态生成或证据获取与停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的实质接口。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust Real-Time Monocular Human Pose Estimation with Insole-Based Sensor Fusion

**abstract/A1：** Real-time monocular human pose estimation is an important sensing component for human-centered intelligent systems. While recent camera-based methods have achieved impressive accuracy, fundamental challenges persist, particularly when visual observations are incomplete, such as during lower-body occlusion or short out-of-frame intervals. In these challenging but common scenarios, vision-only systems often struggle to reconstruct physically plausible lower-limb motion and reliable foot-ground contact. Due to the highly unobtrusive and wearable nature of smart insoles, this paper investigates whether simple plantar pressure and acceleration sensors can effectively resolve these visually induced limitations. To systematically investigate this, we first collect a synchronized multimodal dataset and then propose a part-decoupled temporal fusion framework. The proposed model fuses foot-sensing features into the lower-body branch to compensate for incomplete visual evidence, while a lightweight coordinator exchanges information between the upper- and lower-body temporal representations to maintain whole-body consistency. The framework is evaluated with three representative real-time visual backbones based on 2D keypoints, 3D landmarks, and SMPL-based human mesh recovery. We evaluate our framework on three representative real-time visual backbones. Quantitative experiments demonstrate that incorporating simple foot sensors consistently improves pose accuracy, motion smoothness, and gait-related measurements over vision-only baselines, with the most significant gains observed under occlusion and short visual-dropout conditions. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that unobtrusive foot sensing provides vital and complementary physical observability when camera observations are temporarily unavailable.

</details>

### 299. [HankelKANet: Lightweight Path Loss Estimation via Physics-Inspired Hankel Convolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19524v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19524

**作者：** Yue Ruan、Xiaoxiao Cheng、Yunan Zhu、Wenlong Huang、Meng Hua

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.NI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19524v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19524v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 HankelKANet，用 Hankel 函数参数化的卷积 KAN 嵌入电磁传播特性，实现轻量级无线路径损耗估计，并分析其空间梯度约束带来的泛化收益。

**排除原因：** 面向无线路径损耗回归的物理启发网络设计，与视觉/多模态推理中的状态生成、证据使用或停止决策无实质联系，也未提供可迁移的 latent 推理线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HankelKANet: Lightweight Path Loss Estimation via Physics-Inspired Hankel Convolution

**abstract/A1：** Accurate path loss (PL) estimation is essential for wireless network planning and optimization. This paper proposes HankelKANet, a lightweight physics-inspired network for PL estimation that structurally embeds electromagnetic propagation characteristics into the neural architecture. Built on a single residual UNet backbone, the proposed model integrates urban layout features with a convolutional Kolmogorov-Arnold network(KAN). Specifically, the convolutional kernels are parameterized by the radial amplitudes of the zeroth- and first-order Hankel functions. This formulation acts as a physics-inspired nonlinear distance encoding, enabling the network to capture dominant attenuation characteristics directly without requiring additional physics-based loss terms. Experimental results demonstrate that HankelKANet achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency on both the simulated RadioMapSeer dataset and the real-world Huawei RSRPSet. Compared with the leading baseline, PEFNet, it reduces the parameter count by 78% and lowers the RMSE by 16.96% on the simulated data, while further exhibiting robust cross-dataset transferability and high data efficiency during target-domain adaptation. To interpret these empirical gains, we mathematically establish a parameter-conditional spatial gradient bound. Using discrete Dirichlet energy (DDE) and Jacobian sensitivity heatmaps, we empirically verify that this physical encoding intrinsically constrains the network's spatial derivatives. This structural smoothness effectively eliminates non-physical spatial ripples, preventing overfitting to simulator artifacts and serving as the fundamental driver for robust real world generalization.

</details>

### 300. [RDCompGS: Rate-Distortion Optimization for Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting with Image Codecs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19538v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19538

**作者：** Huiping Ren、Shuai Wan、Luqian Bai、Zhang Chen、Fuzheng Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19538v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19538v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 RDCompGS，将可微的编码码率估计与渲染失真联合起来，对 3D Gaussian Splatting 的表示做率失真优化，并配以码率约束调整模型，在 H.265/HEVC 与 JPEG XL 下取得显著 BD-Rate 降低。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是 3DGS 存储与传输压缩，属渲染表示编码问题，未涉及视觉推理状态生成、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策等 LVR 关注点，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RDCompGS: Rate-Distortion Optimization for Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting with Image Codecs

**abstract/A1：** 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality, real-time novel view synthesis but requires significant storage and transmission resources. Mapping Gaussian attributes to 2D representations enables efficient compression using mature image codecs. However, Gaussian representations that achieve comparable rendering quality are generally treated similarly during optimization, despite substantial differences in coding rate, leading to limited rate-distortion (R-D) performance after compression. To address this limitation, we propose RDCompGS, a 3DGS compression method that enables overall R-D optimization of Gaussian representations with respect to subsequent coding. A differentiable estimate of the coding rate is incorporated into the R-D optimization with rendering distortion. The rate constraint adjustment model further regulates the R-D trade-off across different compression conditions. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDCompGS achieves average Bjøntegaard Delta Rate reductions of 37.84% and 49.31% with H.265/HEVC and JPEG XL, respectively. The estimated rate also shows a strong correlation with the actual bit-rate under different compression conditions.

**comment/C1：** This manuscript has been submitted to IEEE Transactions on Multimedia (TMM)

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-Scenario Industrial Defect Detection via Domain-Robust Training and Metric-Aligned Selection

**abstract/A1：** Automated defect detection of industrial boards must remain highly reliable under cross-scenario variations in lighting, surface contaminants, and production line conditions. This makes cross-scenario defect detection a domain generalization problem, wherein models are required to detect, localize, and classify defects across unseen scenarios while minimizing false alarms on clean boards. For the IDA 2026 Track 1 challenge, we propose a cross-scenario defect detection method that includes four complementary modules: cross-scenario robust training, small-defect-aware learning, score calibration and inference threshold selection, and metric-aligned checkpoint selection. These four modules respectively introduce cross-scenario robust normalization and DSU-style statistic perturbation with progressive random convolution, NWD and parameter-free spatial attention, the VFL learning objective, and a custom SWAD variant driven by the Track 1-oriented proxy. Experimental results demonstrate that our model achieves a Track 1 score of 0.564 with a screening score of 0.696 on the test dataset on a single RTX 4090 GPU. The code is available at https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/Dikai.

**comment/C1：** This paper has been accepted for presentation in IEEE ICME 2026

</details>

**作者：** Jingyuan Li、Dongzhan Zhou、Minting Pan、Yujie Liu、Yuan Gao、Yankai Jiang、Xinyu Gu、Zhe Liu、Yang Li、Bo Zhang、Lei Bai、Tong Si、Weinan Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19550v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19550v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文面向自然语言实验协议到实验室工作流的编译与可验证执行，属 AI for science 的实验室自动化应用，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、latent 生成、证据获取与停止决策；其协议缺陷诊断修复与设备无关图表示虽涉及 LLM 纠错和表示分层，但与 LVR 主线没有可具体核查的借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LabCompiler: A Framework for Verifiable Human-Machine Laboratory Execution from Imperfect Protocols

**abstract/A1：** Closing the loop of AI-driven science requires reliably translating natural-language protocols into executable laboratory workflows despite protocol defects and heterogeneous hardware. Existing approaches often couple scientific interpretation with device-specific code generation, making errors difficult to localize and limiting reuse across laboratory platforms. We introduce LabCompiler, a robust protocol-to-execution framework that transforms imperfect natural-language protocols into verifiable human-machine workflows. Its protocol firewall diagnoses and repairs defects before compilation while preserving unaffected content, guided by troubleshooting knowledge accumulated through perturbation-guided self-evolution. A two-level, device-agnostic graph representation separates experimental semantics from hardware-specific implementation and supports capability-based device assignment with coordinated human execution. We further introduce a protocol recovery benchmark covering four controlled perturbation types and naturally occurring errors collected from protocols written by iGEM participants. Experiments show that LabCompiler outperforms frontier LLMs in protocol error correction while enabling coordinated human-machine execution across heterogeneous, partially automated laboratory settings.

**comment/C1：** 40 pages

</details>

### 303. [Learning Visual Speech Representations via Cross-Modal Distillation and Joint Face-Lip Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19559v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19559

**作者：** Jing-Xuan Zhang、Genshun Wan、Jianqing Gao、Jia Pan、Yumei Zhang、Lichen Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.AS

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19559v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19559v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作面向视听语音识别（唇读）任务，用 Whisper 特征做跨模态蒸馏目标并联合唇部 ROI 与人脸双流编码，属于任务型表征学习应用，未涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、latent 推理、证据获取或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Visual Speech Representations via Cross-Modal Distillation and Joint Face-Lip Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Lipreading remains challenging due to the inherent ambiguity of visual speech cues and their sensitivity to head pose and video resolution. In this work, we propose FLARE, a framework for robust visual speech representation learning via cross-modal distillation and joint face-lip modeling. FLARE leverages semantically rich Whisper representations as the acoustic teacher signal, converting them into discrete distillation targets through a random-projection quantizer and pretraining the model with a masked prediction objective. To capture complementary visual information, FLARE employs a dual-stream architecture that jointly processes lip region-of-interest (ROI) and full-face inputs, where the lip stream captures finegrained articulatory motion and the face stream encodes broader facial dynamics. During pretraining, auxiliary audio input is incorporated to facilitate cross-modal alignment, and modality dropout is applied to encourage robust visual-only representations. For downstream lipreading, FLARE serves as a visual encoder coupled with either a lightweight Transformer decoder or an LLM-based decoder. Evaluated on LRS2, LRS3, and the out-of-domain WildVSR benchmark, FLARE achieves strong lipreading performance, with word error rates of 11.7%, 13.2%, and 34.3%, respectively. A progressive design study confirms the contribution of each major component. Further analyses demonstrate that joint face-lip modeling improves robustness under degraded video resolution and large head pose variation, while yielding more phoneme-discriminative visual representations. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/jxzhanggg/FLARE.

**comment/C1：** accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 304. [SaTPO: Text-to-Image Safety Alignment via Transition Preference Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19563v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19563

**作者：** Ziyi Fang、Jingzhi Li、Xiaojun Jia、Xingxing Wei、Wenqi Ren、Haijun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19563v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19563v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究文本到图像扩散模型的安全对齐（基于去噪步的转移偏好优化），属图像生成安全领域，未涉及视觉推理的内部状态表示、证据使用或停止决策，也没有可供迁移的 latent 推理线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SaTPO: Text-to-Image Safety Alignment via Transition Preference Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Safety alignment of text-to-image diffusion models aims to suppress harmful content without compromising useful generation capabilities. However, preferences over completed images compress multidimensional generation factors into a coarse signal, potentially driving changes beyond those required for safety. To address this, we propose Safety Transition Preference Optimization (SaTPO), which enforces step-wise alignment during denoising. SaTPO leverages the insight that an unsafe prompt and its safe rewrite naturally form a tightly coupled preference pair when conditioned on a shared parent state and noise realization. By anchoring optimization on identical parent states, SaTPO eliminates historical trajectory divergence, confining safety adaptation to an incremental alignment while preserving task-relevant semantics. Multi-scale drift constraints and benign replay further limit behavioral deviation and preserve ordinary generation. We additionally construct CoProPV, a refined dataset ensuring visually verified contrasts. Experiments on Stable Diffusion v1.5 and SDXL demonstrate improved safety-quality trade-offs. Compared with AlignGuard, PerspectiveVision unsafe-detection rates on Inappropriate Image Prompts decrease from 12.70% to 6.00% and from 15.04% to 9.30%, respectively, while FID improves from 32.28 to 25.95 and from 36.50 to 27.51. Warning: this paper includes potentially offensive content.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 8 figures

</details>

### 305. [InstructPro2: Natural Language and Biological Grounding Guided De Novo Protein Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19592v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19592

**作者：** Charles Novak、Zhenqiao Song、Chuan Li、Jianwen Xie、Lei Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19592v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19592v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究以自然语言与生物学条件引导的蛋白质序列生成，属计算生物学设计任务，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取与停止决策等主线问题，也无可供核查的 latent 表示或推理期状态更新线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InstructPro2: Natural Language and Biological Grounding Guided De Novo Protein Design

**abstract/A1：** The ability to generate proteins with desired functions is a central goal of computational biology. Although natural language can express diverse design objectives, existing protein generation methods often support only narrow tasks or make limited use of biological constraints. We develop InstructPro2 to translate natural-language design objectives into protein sequences while incorporating task-specific biological context. A text encoder captures the design instructions, while dedicated ligand and antigen encoders provide biological context to the sequence-generating decoder. For pretraining, we assemble InstructPro2Bench, comprising 205.7 million &lt;function description, protein&gt; pairs from UniProtKB. We subsequently adapt the model to ligand-binding protein and antibody design. On functional protein generation benchmarks, InstructPro2 outperforms Pinal, the strongest baseline, achieving a functional recovery F1 of 74.8 versus 62.2 and lowering global RMSD from 5.33A to 3.04A. For antibody design, the model obtains a DockQ score of 0.39, while ligand-binding protein design reaches a docking success rate of 72.6%. These findings highlight how integrating language-based instructions with biological conditioning can extend protein generation to distinct design settings.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 306. [PN-Mix: Pair-Norm Guided Mixed-Precision Key Cache Quantization for Efficient Long-Output LLM Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19605v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19605

**作者：** Gil Jae Song、Sungjoo Yoo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19605v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19605v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PN-Mix，依据 RoPE 通道对上 query–key 对范数乘积估计显著性，在 prefill 阶段一次性确定通道对精度分配，用于长输出 LLM 推理的混合精度 key cache 量化。

**排除原因：** 该工作属纯文本推理的 KV cache 低比特量化与效率优化，虽涉及注意力结构与缓存，但目标是数值压缩精度分配，未触及视觉/多模态推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏具体可迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PN-Mix: Pair-Norm Guided Mixed-Precision Key Cache Quantization for Efficient Long-Output LLM Inference

**abstract/A1：** Long-output inference in large language models suffers from severe KV cache memory and bandwidth bottlenecks. While KV cache quantization alleviates this issue, low-bit quantization (e.g., 2-bit) severely degrades model accuracy. Recent mixed-precision key-cache quantization methods mitigate this degradation by allocating higher precision to important key channels, but estimate channel saliency using only key statistics. Through attention logit perturbation analysis at the RoPE channel-pair level, we show that quantization errors in channel pairs with large query--key pair-norm products contribute significantly to logit variation. Based on this insight, we propose PN-Mix, a mixed-precision key-cache quantization method for RoPE-based decoder-only LLMs, which allocates higher precision to salient channel pairs identified once during the prefill stage and reused throughout decoding. Across standard reasoning, code-generation, and AIME benchmarks, PN-Mix with 2.26-bit average precision achieves comparable or better average accuracy than prior mixed-precision methods using 2.32--2.39 bits. Furthermore, in our Pareto sweep, PN-Mix degrades gracefully down to 1.5 bits, whereas the compared baselines exhibit sharper performance drops below 2.25 bits.

**comment/C1：** EMNLP 2026

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MagicGarment: Benchmarking Single-Image Standalone 3D Garment Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Single-image 3D reconstruction has advanced rapidly, yet standalone garments remain challenging due to thin-shell geometry, open boundaries, and layered structures. Existing datasets and evaluation protocols provide limited support for systematic evaluation in this setting. We introduce MagicGarment, a benchmark for single-image standalone 3D garment reconstruction with 1,518 professionally constructed assets across 17 composite garment types, aligned multimodal annotations, and a unified evaluation protocol. We further establish a garment-aware reference baseline by adapting a pretrained image-to-3D backbone at two levels: Grounded Semantic Refinement (GSR) provides garment-focused visual conditioning, while Flow-Time-Route LoRA (FTR-LoRA) adapts the frozen 3D generative backbone using flow-timestep, token-routing, and expert-load information. Experiments demonstrate MagicGarment's benchmark utility and the effectiveness of garment-aware adaptation. Project page: https://1019538157.github.io/MagicGarment/.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19640

**作者：** Irsyaad Rijwan、Liam Brydon、Aidan Jared、Jörg Simon Wicker

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19640v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19640v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 enviGraph 框架，用环境转化规则从 PubChem 等结构生成并筛选约 77.7 万合成母体—产物对，用于 graph-to-sequence 预测器的预训练，再在观测到的生物降解反应上微调，做产物与递归通路预测。

**排除原因：** 属于化学信息学中分子图到序列的通路预测应用，合成数据仅作为领域预训练语料，与视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选或 THINK/LOOK/STOP 等研究问题无具体联系，也无可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Synthetic reaction data from transformation rules for graph-to-sequence pretraining in biodegradation pathway prediction

**abstract/A1：** The scarcity of observed biodegradation reactions constrains the training of product and pathway predictors. We present enviGraph, a framework that generates and curates synthetic reaction data, uses these data to pretrain a graph-to-sequence predictor, and fine-tunes the predictor on observed environmental reactions for product and recursive pathway prediction. Applying inherited environmental transformation rules to structures from PubChem, NORMAN SusDat and EPA DSSTox, followed by curation, produces 776,851 canonical parent-product pairs for pretraining. We compare this dataset with three United States Patent and Trademark Office (USPTO) reaction datasets: USPTO-50K, USPTO-MIT and USPTO-Stereo. We evaluate enviGraph and the sequence-based enviFormer using ten folds split by pathway on the EAWAG Biocatalysis/Biodegradation Database (BBD), regulatory pesticide biodegradation data (Soil) and activated-sludge biodegradation data (Sludge). For both predictors, synthetic pretraining gives the highest BBD mean precision-recall area under the curve (PR-AUC) among the evaluated pretraining datasets for single-generation and multi-generation prediction. enviGraph achieves BBD single-generation PR-AUC of $0.202 \\pm 0.050$ with synthetic pretraining, compared with $0.169 \\pm 0.065$ for the retrospectively selected strongest USPTO condition, USPTO-Stereo (mean $\\pm$ sample standard deviation across folds). Its exact BBD top-1 and top-5 accuracies with synthetic pretraining are 0.355 and 0.594. The strongest USPTO conditions give higher PR-AUC means than synthetic pretraining in most Soil and Sludge comparisons. enviGraph has higher Soil multi-generation PR-AUC means than enviFormer under each of the four main pretraining datasets, and with synthetic pretraining it also leads enviFormer on BBD single-generation PR-AUC and exact top-1 and top-5 accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables. Additional file 1 is provided as an ancillary file

</details>

### 309. [Mapping Arctic Polynyas from Sea Ice Concentration Data Using Hierarchical Partitioning and Differentiated Region Growing: Sensitivity to Threshold and Product Choices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19647v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19647

**作者：** Tianyu Zhang、Haiyi Ren、Zhilun Zhang、Yang Hong、Chunlei Mi、Xiao Cheng、Fengming Hui

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19647v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19647v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究海冰密集度数据的北极冰间湖制图方法及其阈值与产品敏感性，属于遥感地球科学应用，未涉及视觉推理内部状态、证据使用或停止决策等问题，也无可供核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mapping Arctic Polynyas from Sea Ice Concentration Data Using Hierarchical Partitioning and Differentiated Region Growing: Sensitivity to Threshold and Product Choices

**abstract/A1：** Accurately delineating Arctic coastal polynyas from sea ice concentration data remains challenging because of complex coastal geometry and spatially heterogeneous ice conditions. We develop a daily sea ice concentration (SIC) mapping framework based on hierarchical partitioning, connected-component analysis, and differentiated region growing for polynya and open-water regions. Applied to September-May ice seasons from 2002/03 to 2024/25, the framework achieves a mean overall accuracy of 96.74% and a mean kappa coefficient of 0.93 in the 63-scene visual assessment. Sensitivity experiments reveal a consistent pattern: the absolute magnitude of SIC-defined polynya extent is strongly configuration-dependent, whereas major temporal features are comparatively more stable. Relative to a 75% upper SIC threshold, seasonal extent integrals differ by 13.48%, 32.47%, and 65.04% at thresholds of 80%, 85%, and 90%, respectively, while the broad interannual structure remains recognizable. The response is also strongly regional and seasonal, with the largest threshold sensitivity in the Central Arctic Ocean, very weak sensitivity in the Gulf of St. Lawrence, and greater pan-Arctic curve separation from January onward. At an 80% threshold, product-comparison MAPE is 68.19% between the 6.25 and 25 km products and 11.47% between the 12.5 and 25 km products under their respective comparison periods and reference definitions. Regional product disagreement is likewise heterogeneous. Together, these results show that reproducible long-term polynya monitoring requires a fixed and explicitly documented combination of SIC product, threshold, and spatial classification rules, while broad temporal patterns can remain informative even when absolute extent differs substantially.

</details>

### 310. [On Global Joint Lower Bounds in Zeroth-Order Nonconvex Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19652

**作者：** Yuxuan Xia、Xiao-Tong Yuan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19652v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究零阶非凸优化的全局下界与极小极大最优性（维度、精度、平滑参数依赖），属纯优化理论，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用与停止决策，未发现可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On Global Joint Lower Bounds in Zeroth-Order Nonconvex Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Recent work has sought to transfer first-order nonconvex optimization guarantees to zeroth-order methods through gradient estimators, typically incurring a factor linear in $d$. \\citet{kornowski2024} obtained an $\\mathcal{O}(dδ^{-1}ε^{-3})$ oracle complexity for nonsmooth nonconvex optimization and asked whether its dependence on all three parameters is jointly optimal. Although the dimension dependence and the first-order dependence on $(δ,ε)$ are separately known to be unavoidable, a matching zeroth-order lower bound has remained open. We answer this question in the unrestricted full-space setting. For smooth stochastic nonconvex optimization, we establish a scalar-oracle lower bound of $Ω\\!\\left(dL\_0^2\\min\\{ΔH,L\_0^2\\}/ε^4\\right)$ assuming a smooth, globally lower-bounded population objective and a second-moment bound on sample Lipschitz constants. A smooth reduction then yields a lower bound for Goldstein stationarity, proving the joint minimax optimality of the dependence on $(d,δ,ε)$ under explicit high-dimensional and small-accuracy conditions. Crucially, our results allow algorithms to query arbitrary points in $\\mathbb{R}^d$, adaptively select sample-function labels, reuse the same sampled function at any number of locations, and output points without spatial restrictions. Thus, unlike lower bounds based on bounded query regions or fresh evaluation noise, our guarantees apply to a fully adaptive, reusable-sample scalar oracle. We also establish a separate noiseless exact-value lower bound against randomized algorithms, and hence also against deterministic methods. Our proof combines a globally defined sequential nonconvex construction with an exact Gaussian scalar channel, multistage random-field estimates, and an antithetic finite-sample realization that preserves information hardness under adaptive reuse.

</details>

### 311. [MicroSDF: Microfacet-Driven Hybrid Neural SDFs for Mixed-Reflectance Surface Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19681v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19681

**作者：** Lejia Ye、Yuhua Xu、Yulan Guo、Lian Xu、Xiaohu Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19681v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19681v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MicroSDF 提出基于微表面理论的混合神经隐式 SDF 框架，统一建模反射与非反射表面的几何与外观，用于高保真三维重建。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于神经隐式表面重建与逆渲染，涉及几何、材质与颜色分支，与 LVR 关注的多模态推理状态、视觉证据使用、停止决策等无实质联系，也未提供可迁移的状态更新或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MicroSDF: Microfacet-Driven Hybrid Neural SDFs for Mixed-Reflectance Surface Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Accurate 3D reconstruction in real-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the coexistence of reflective and non-reflective surfaces, which pose distinct modeling demands. Existing methods often treat these surface types separately, limiting their generalizability and physical plausibility. To bridge this gap, we propose MicroSDF, a novel neural implicit framework that facilitates geometry and reflectance modeling through microfacet theory. Our approach incorporates three core innovations: (i) a microfacet-guided geometry model that extracts multi-scale surface normals (macroscopic and microfacet) from a signed distance field (SDF), regularized by a proposed microfacet normal consistency loss to enforce physically plausible surface orientations; (ii) an enhanced dual-branch color model, where the specular branch leverages the microfacet normals to model high-frequency reflectance, and the vanilla branch, unlike prior works, uses reflection direction (instead of viewing direction) to better model diffuse and low-frequency specular components; and (iii) a detection-guided color blending strategy that adaptively fuses the color outputs based on reflection priors, providing more physically intuitive blending than implicitly learned blending weights. Combined with a tailored multi-stage optimization scheme, the proposed MicroSDF achieves robust and high-fidelity reconstruction across reflective and non-reflective surfaces. Extensive experiments on DTU, Shiny Blender, Ref-NeRF, and DeepVoxels datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, establishing a new direction for physically grounded neural reconstruction.

**comment/C1：** Published in IEEE Transactions on Image Processing, vol. 35, pp. 4106-4121, Apr. 2026. DOI: 10.1109/TIP.2026.3678096

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19693v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19693v1>)

**内容简述：** 提出 ReFormBench 基准，以模型自身无约束回答为格式基线，用 TCPR 与 EMCR 两个指标衡量模型在指令变体下纠正格式、满足约束的能力。

**排除原因：** 纯文本格式遵循与指令纠正的评测，不涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、latent 推理状态生成与更新、证据获取或停止决策，未发现可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReFormBench: Benchmarking the Stability of Response-Conditioned Format Correction in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Reliable use of large language models requires responses to satisfy formatting requirements across changing instructions. Standard compliance scores do not distinguish preserving a model's observed format from correcting it, or reveal whether that correction holds across variants. We introduce ReFormBench, a benchmark that uses each model's unconstrained response to the same question as its formatting baseline. It links targets by feature and require/forbid mode, evaluating their instantiated requirements across variants. ReFormBench comprises 5,000 difficulty-screened three-constraint test instances derived from 910 source questions and covering 66 formatting features. TCPR measures complete instruction compliance, while EMCR measures individual and joint correction of targets opposed to the annotated baseline state. Across ten models, EMCR ranges from 34.04% to 64.72%, and the highest three-target correction estimate is 50.02%. Compliance and correction scores yield different model rankings and task-level profiles. A prompting experiment on Qwen3.5-9B improves both metrics, and the catalog covers over 95% of extracted instances on four additional models. ReFormBench makes response-conditioned correction across instruction variants a measurable dimension of format following. The code and data are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReFormBench/.

</details>

### 313. [No Way To Steal My Face: Proactive Defense Against Identity-Preserving Personalized Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19717v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19717

**作者：** Lizhi Xiong、Jun Li、Ziqiang Li、Weiwei Jiang、Zhangjie Fu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19717v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19717v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究面向扩散模型身份保护（人脸隐私）的对抗扰动防御，属于图像生成安全领域，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取或训练监督，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，也无具体可借鉴的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** No Way To Steal My Face: Proactive Defense Against Identity-Preserving Personalized Generation

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in diffusion models have enabled high-fidelity, identity-preserving image generation for personalized applications such as digital avatars and virtual try-on systems. However, their reliance on sensitive facial reference images raises growing privacy concerns. Existing defense mechanisms are primarily designed for training-based personalization and struggle to generalize to emerging training-free approaches, due to fundamental differences in their identity integration paradigms. To bridge this gap, we propose IDGuardian-the first generalizable and model-agnostic identity protection framework capable of defending against both training-based and training-free personalization methods. IDGuardian abstracts the personalization process into two critical stages: identity extraction and identity injection. It then introduces crafted adversarial perturbations to simultaneously disrupt both stages. Specifically, it degrades the identity features extracted by external encoders and establishes an adversarial conceptual bridge that misdirects the generative trajectory away from the target identity. Extensive experiments show that IDGuardian effectively protects identity across various personalization pipelines and model architectures, while remaining robust to post-processing, adaptive attacks, and cross-dataset generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/OpenAscent-L/IDGuardian.git.

</details>

### 314. [A Diagnostic Study of World-Space Human Recovery Under High Camera Dynamics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19729v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19729

**作者：** Hao-Jen Chien、Wei-Lun Chao、Yu-Lun Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建了包含快速大幅相机运动的广播舞蹈视频压力测试，诊断单目世界坐标人体恢复中的轨迹漂移，并给出无需重训的闭式尺度校正。

**排除原因：** 属于三维人体网格与相机尺度标定问题，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据使用或决策停止，未提供可迁移到 latent visual reasoning 的具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Diagnostic Study of World-Space Human Recovery Under High Camera Dynamics

**abstract/A1：** Monocular world-space human recovery methods excel on current benchmarks, but these rarely include fast camera motion. We introduce the K-Pop stress test, broadcast dance videos that combine fast, large camera motion with dense multi-person choreography. Leading methods produce plausible per-frame meshes on these videos, yet their world-space trajectories drift far from the performers' stage positions. Through a systematic diagnosis, we show that the camera and human estimates each remain self-consistent, and that the drift appears when they are combined at mismatched scales. Each is recovered at its own scale, so a mismatch between the two can turn camera motion into human drift that grows with camera displacement and becomes severe in broadcast dance videos. We derive a closed-form correction that estimates this mismatch and applies one scale to all performers, removing the drift while keeping their relative positions in one shared world frame. It requires no retraining and works with existing backends. Combined with physical constraints, the correction yields stable world-space reconstruction for choreography modeling, scene editing, data generation, and virtual reshooting.

</details>

### 315. [Object Pose from 2-D to 3-D Point and Line Correspondences](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19734v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19734

**作者：** Thai Quynh Phong、Radu Horaud、Adnan Yassine、Pham Dinh Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19734v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19734v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是经典的相机与三维物体间位姿估计工作，用 dual number quaternion 构造误差函数并以信赖域方法优化，属于几何视觉与数值优化范畴，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可供核查的 latent 表示或训练监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Object Pose from 2-D to 3-D Point and Line Correspondences

**abstract/A1：** In this paper we present a method for optimally estimating the rotation and translation between a camera and a 3-D object from point and/or line correspondences. First we devise an error function and second we show how to minimize this error function. The quadratic nature of this function is made possible by representing rotation and translation with a dual number quaternion. We provide a detailed account of the computational aspects of a trust-region optimization method. This method compares favourably with Newton's method which has extensively been used to solve the problem at hand, with Faugeras-Toscani linear method \[6\] for calibrating a camera, and with the Levenberg-Marquardt non-linear optimization method. Finally we present some experimental results which demonstrate the robustness of our method with respect to image noise and matching errors.

</details>
