### 316. [ProtModernBERT: Compute-Efficient Protein Language Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19738v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19738

**作者：** Alberto Martínez、Natalia Flechas Manrique、Llorenç Espinosa-Portalés、Luc Andrea

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、q-bio.QM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19738v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19738v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究的是氨基酸序列上的蛋白语言模型预训练与低算力架构改进，与 LVR/VLM 推理中的内部状态、证据获取或停止决策无实质联系；其算力效率与归纳偏置结论虽可泛泛迁移到编码器设计，但缺少具体可核查的推理机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ProtModernBERT: Compute-Efficient Protein Language Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Large protein language models provide a powerful framework for learning sequence-function relationships and guiding experimental design. However, architectural improvements are underexplored, especially in low-compute regimes of relevance for resource-constrained research laboratories. We show that the training cost for encoder-only transformer architectures can be reduced by leveraging modern architectural refinements, offering an encouraging performance / compute-efficiency trade-off. We introduce ProtModernBERT, a modernized BERT-style model pre-trained on amino acid sequences, and compare it with ProtBert, Amplify, and ESM2 across a set of supervised structural tasks -encompassing secondary structure prediction, disorder prediction, and subcellular localization- and ProteinGym's zero-shot functional benchmark. We find that ProtModernBERT matches or closely tracks the performance of Amplify-120M and ProtBert with up to 80-90\\% reduction in pre-training FLOPs, and narrows the gap to ESM2 when controlling for pre-training. Our analysis using a cluster-based validation protocol for hyperparameter selection and randomly initialized models disentangles the contributions of architecture and data, showing that modern components provide stronger inductive biases that are more consequential than parameter count in low-compute regimes.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures

</details>

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CAST，一种基于历史自适应的谱裁剪方法：用 AdamW 二阶矩设定行列缩放、用裁剪输出谱范数的指数滑动平均设定阈值，在 MoE 预训练中降低训练与验证损失。

**排除原因：** 该工作属 LLM 预训练优化器与梯度谱裁剪，不涉及多模态内部状态、证据使用或推理控制，也无可具体核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CAST: History-Adaptive Spectral Clipping for LLM Pretraining

**abstract/A1：** Soft spectral clipping (SSC) reduces gradient singular values, but its effect depends on the gradient scale and clipping threshold. We introduce CAST (Clipping with Adaptive Scales and Thresholds), a history-adaptive extension of SSC. AdamW's previous second moment sets row and column scales. An exponential moving average of the clipped output's estimated spectral norm sets each matrix's threshold. CAST restores the original coordinates before the AdamW update. We train a mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 19.08B total parameters for 83.89B tokens. CAST achieves lower training and mean validation losses than nine gradient-processing baselines, with the fewest relative gradient spikes. It reaches the best baseline's final smoothed training loss in 7.2\\% fewer steps.

</details>

### 318. [Q-MAGIC: Quad-only Mesh Autoregressive Generation via Singularity-Aware Circular Relative Offset Tokenization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19777v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19777

**作者：** Yuming Zhao、Zezeng Li、Weimin Wang、Xiang Wu、Na Lei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19777v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19777v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究四边形网格的自回归生成与序列化/偏移 tokenization，属于 3D 几何生成任务，不涉及视觉语言模型的内部推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，其相对偏移编码等线索也难以对应 LVR 的表示与 latent 使用问题，故认为缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Q-MAGIC: Quad-only Mesh Autoregressive Generation via Singularity-Aware Circular Relative Offset Tokenization

**abstract/A1：** Autoregressive models have proven effective for triangular mesh generation by transforming it into predicting the next face, converting the non-differentiable connectivity prediction into a sequence of token predictions. However, generating quad-only meshes remains challenging because quad faces impose stricter topological constraints than triangles. The model must not only serialize quad faces into a valid token sequence, but also support unambiguous connectivity reconstruction during de-serialization. To address this, we propose Q-MAGIC, a novel autoregressive model for quad mesh generation. We first introduce a tailored serialization scheme for quad meshes, which requires at least two new vertices for the next face and is less flexible than triangles due to strict wiring constraints. To meet these requirements and reduce serialization length, we propose a singularity-aware serialization that prioritizes wiring from singular points, leading to more coherent traversals. Additionally, we propose a circular relative offset tokenization that encodes geometry mainly via local relative offsets. Built upon this representation, an autoregressive Transformer learns the underlying distribution of quad meshes via next-token prediction, enabling effective quad mesh generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Q-MAGIC produces more compact sequences while consistently improving geometric fidelity and mesh quality compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19785v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19785v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 FPV-CRASH 数据集与固定协议评测，用于无人机第一视角视频中坠机瞬间的离线时序定位；包含 1052 段片段、单帧时刻标注、自动生成字幕与热力图参考模型。

**排除原因：** 属于无人机视频的时序事件定位数据集与刷点式基准，标注目标是单一时间戳回归，未涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据筛选、latent 生成或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可供核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FPV-CRASH: A Dataset for Offline Crash Moment Localization in Drone First-Person-View Videos

**abstract/A1：** Crash Moment Localization aims to localize the major physical impact that interrupts controlled flight in an onboard first-person-view (FPV) video recorded by a drone, thereby providing a temporal reference for retrospective crash analysis in aerial robotics. However, existing crash-video datasets predominantly target surveillance or dash-camera footage, and their annotation schemes and task formulations are not designed for temporal localization of the major physical impact in UAV onboard FPV videos. To address this gap, we present FPV-CRASH, a dataset and fixed-protocol reference evaluation for offline Crash Moment Localization in drone FPV videos. FPV-CRASH contains 1,052 drone FPV clips (526 Crash and 526 Normal); each Crash clip has a single-frame reference annotation for the Crash Moment, and every clip is paired with four English captions automatically generated from sampled frames and the known Crash/Normal label. Normal clips carry no timestamp target and provide training-time background supervision. The captions support an auxiliary Text-to-Video Retrieval task, whereas localization inference remains video-only. We further provide a heatmap-based reference model that estimates a continuous Crash Moment timestamp from sparsely sampled frames. Under the fixed, non-source-disjoint clip-level protocol, the reference model reduces MAE from 1.12 s for the strongest fixed timing-prior baseline to 0.56 s (a 50% relative reduction) and achieves a median absolute error (MedAE) of 0.33 s; the fraction of clips localized within 0.5 s rises from 37.14% to 65.71%. The release includes annotations, available source video identifiers and URLs, clipping metadata, fixed split lists, and preprocessing code so that the reportable portion can be reconstructed without redistributing source footage. At submission time, all source-compilation URLs remained accessible.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 320. [LiFi: LiDAR Generation from Multi-View Images via Geometric and Semantic Collaborative Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19786v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19786

**作者：** Sizhuo Zhou、Xiaosong Jia、Fanrui Zhang、Qifeng Li、Junjie Li、Zirui Wang、Yukang Feng、Yu Hong、Shaofeng Zhang、Wenlong Liao、Tao He、Juyong Zhang、Junchi Yan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19786v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19786v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是从多视角图像生成 LiDAR 点云的跨模态数据合成工作，用几何与语义双分支引导 latent diffusion 并提出平衡的 CFG 策略，属于图像/模态生成任务，既不涉及 VLM 推理状态与视觉证据使用，也没有决策、停止或监督方面的联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LiFi: LiDAR Generation from Multi-View Images via Geometric and Semantic Collaborative Guidance

**abstract/A1：** Generating LiDAR point clouds from multi-view camera images is a valuable task with applications in controllable data synthesis, cross-modal simulation and various perception tasks. However, most existing methods are not specifically designed for this task, and the quality of their generated LiDAR remains limited. Moreover, these methods typically focus only on semantic cues in images or generate LiDAR from a monocular camera image. In this work, we introduce LiFi (LiDAR From images), a dedicated framework for generating visually and geometrically aligned LiDAR scenes from multi-view camera images. LiFi guides the latent diffusion process through two complementary branches: geometry and semantics. The geometry branch constructs scene features from images through a reverse sampling algorithm and an uncertainty-aware geometric encoder combined with DepthAnything3, whereas the semantic branch provides high-level semantic representations via a cross-view interaction module. We further introduce a dual-branch balanced classifier-free guidance (CFG) strategy, which enhances the model's conditional generation capability while preserving the independent learning and collaborative controllability of the two branches. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LiFi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in generating high-fidelity LiDAR scenes. The code will be publicly available at: https://github.com/szzhou88/LiFi.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19810

**作者：** Shiv Muthupandiyan、Tolga H. Dur、Mac Walker、Timo Flesch、Mahmoud Ghanem、Steph Suddell、Giles Harper-Donnelly、Sophie Rose、Jasper Götting、Coleman Breen、Jake Pencharz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19810v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19810v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 ReproBAIT 评测，考察 coding agent 在无网络沙箱、有限信息与算力预算下能否从已发表研究的有限描述中复现生物 AI 工具，并以留出数据上的工具指标评分，另设记忆化检查，发现部分工具有较强复现能力、最复杂工具则较弱。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是代码智能体复现生物工具的能力与记忆化，既不涉及视觉/多模态推理，也没有内部 latent 状态、证据获取或停止决策等可核查线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReproBAIT: Can coding agents build their own biological AI tools?

**abstract/A1：** Large language models are increasingly competent at leveraging scientific knowledge and existing computational tools to execute complex workflows. At present, little information exists on their ability to reconstruct tools when given limited descriptions of such tools. We introduce an evaluation titled ReproBAIT (Reproducing Biological AI Tools), which measures the ability of coding agents to design, implement, and train biological AI tools taken from published research across a range of information access conditions. The coding agent is securely sandboxed without internet access and receives training data, a fixed time and compute budget, and limited information about the tool to replicate. We then score the reproduced biological AI tool based on its performance (tool-specific metrics) on held-out data. We evaluated a set of biological AI tools of varying complexities: ProteinMPNN, an inverse protein folding tool, RFdiffusion3, an all-atom generative diffusion tool, and a closed-source molecular design tool. We institute a methodology to check for memorisation, as the open source tools are likely to be included in models' pretraining corpus. Across a range of leading closed- and open-source coding agents, we find substantial reproduction capability for some selected tools, though limited capability for a highly complex tool. We find moderate evidence consistent with memorisation, varying across coding agents. These results suggest that tool developers need to be increasingly cautious when releasing information about the tools publicly; re-creation of the tool based on such information could undermine a tool's commercial value or invalidate dual-use managed access strategies.

</details>

### 322. [VisibilitySplat: Ray-Traced Occlusion for Relighting with 3D Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19847

**作者：** Sven Kluge、Oliver Staadt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作围绕 3DGS 逆渲染，用 BVH 点光线追踪稀疏估计遮挡可见性以支持重光照与材质—光照分解，属图形学渲染管线改进；其「可见性」是几何透射率计算，不涉及 VLM 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，未见可供迁移的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VisibilitySplat: Ray-Traced Occlusion for Relighting with 3D Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** We address inverse rendering with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by introducing explicit visibility modeling for relighting as well as material and light decomposition. Our approach augments the standard rasterized 3DGS pipeline with a sparse visibility oracle that estimates transmittance-weighted occlusion for Gaussian primitives from sampled lighting directions. This oracle is implemented using a point-based ray tracer accelerated by a bounding volume hierarchy (BVH) and queried sparsely within the inverse-rendering objectives, while the original 3DGS loop is retained for training efficiency, interactive visualization, and tooling compatibility. Shading follows a physically based rendering (PBR) formulation with a principled microfacet bidirectional reflectance distribution function (BRDF) and low-resolution high-dynamic-range (HDR) environment maps. We evaluate our method on the TensoIR synthetic dataset against baselines based on neural radiance fields (NeRFs), tensor representations, and 3DGS. The results show competitive performance and improvements over several earlier baselines for novel-view synthesis (NVS), albedo reconstruction, and relighting. Ablations indicate that explicit visibility modeling is an important contributor to relighting and material-illumination disentanglement, while pure PBR supervision can favor appearance fitting in NVS. Overall, the findings suggest that sparse, point-based visibility queries effectively complement rasterized splatting for inverse rendering while preserving the practical advantages of the 3DGS ecosystem.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables. Published at WSCG 2026

</details>

**排除原因：** 属于医学多模态解析的竞赛系统与任务适配工作，仅有按问题路由适配器等工程配置，未涉及视觉细节保留于内部状态、latent 推理状态生成与更新、证据获取或停止决策，也无可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multimodal Medical Image Parsing with Task-Specific Low-Rank Adapters

**abstract/A1：** Adapting a single vision-language model to medical image parsing requires training choices that reflect the demands of each task. We study task-specific adaptation with Qwen3.5-9B for FLARE 2026 Task 3, covering six task families and seven imaging modalities. Our system couples a shared, frozen 4-bit backbone with LoRA adapters selected from the input question. We customize adapter ranks and prompts, rebalance rare classification targets, and supplement regression training with dermatology classification examples. On the fixed internal holdout, the task-specific adapters achieve a dataset-macro balanced accuracy of 0.7791 for classification, a dataset-macro micro-F1 of 0.6286 for multi-label classification, a counting mean absolute error (MAE) of 11.73, a dataset-macro detection F1 of 0.8914, a dataset-macro regression MAE of 9.78, and a report-generation CRIMSON score of 0.8204. On the organizer's 1,703-example hidden-validation set, the submitted system achieves 0.8496 classification balanced accuracy, 0.5338 sample-averaged multi-label F1, and 14.5950 regression MAE. The final 30.1 GB container averages 3.1 seconds per case, including model loading, and produces identical outputs across repeated verification runs. The benefits vary across tasks, supporting the use of task-specific adapter configurations and image budgets within a shared medical vision-language model. Code is available at https://github.com/TahaSabir0/FLARE26-Hexapus.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 4 figures. FLARE 2026 Task 3 methodology paper (2D track)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19911

**作者：** Zhi Li、Haowei Liu、Hongchen Yang、Xiaoxuan Wang、Song Gao、Zhenli He、Wei Zhou、Shaowen Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19911v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19911v1>)

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CARD 闭环对抗鲁棒性蒸馏框架：先用可靠性增强的学生反馈反向精炼鲁棒教师，再用精炼后的教师继续训练同一学生，并在 CIFAR 等数据集上验证鲁棒性提升。

**排除原因：** 该工作属对抗鲁棒性蒸馏，讨论的是图像分类下的师生训练范式与反馈可靠性，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据使用与停止决策；其师生回路属于训练期权重传递，而非推理期状态生成或更新，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CARD: Closing the Teacher-Student Loop for Adversarial Robustness Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Adversarial robustness distillation typically treats a robust teacher as a fixed knowledge source and transfers robustness to a compact student in a one-way manner. This paradigm terminates teacher-student interaction once the student is distilled, thereby discarding potentially useful feedback from the distilled student. However, exploiting such feedback is nontrivial because indiscriminate weak-to-strong transfer may propagate student errors to the teacher. To address this issue, we propose Cyclic Adversarial Robustness Distillation (CARD), a closed-loop framework that turns the distilled student into a feedback source for refining the robust teacher and subsequently transfers the teacher gains back to the same student. CARD first performs strong-to-weak adversarial distillation and then uses reliability-enhanced student feedback to refine the robust teacher. The refinement combines inverse adversarial student feedback with teacher-student discrepancy maximization, after which the refined teacher is used to continue training the same student under the unchanged base distillation objective. We further establish a sample-level sufficient condition for reliable weak-to-strong feedback transfer and derive a distribution-level upper bound on the refined teacher's risk. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet-10 across multiple teacher-student architectures and base distillation methods show that CARD improves adversarial robustness in most evaluated settings while generally preserving clean accuracy. These results show that the distilled student can provide useful feedback to the teacher, enabling robustness transfer to proceed beyond the conventional one-way teacher-to-student paradigm.

</details>

### 325. [Beyond DFT electronic band structures via dual-space orbital learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19919v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19919

**作者：** Minhyuk Kang、Beom Seok Kang、Nikolai Argatoff、Miguel Nouman、Danish Khan、William A. Goddard、Anima Anandkumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.mtrl-sci

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19919v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19919v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文用「双空间轨道学习」框架由低成本 PBE 计算预测 HSE06/G0W0 能带能量与态密度，属计算材料学与电子结构预测；其动量分辨的状态回归与 LVR 关注的多模态推理状态、证据筛选和停止决策无实质关联，也未提供可迁移的表征或训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond DFT electronic band structures via dual-space orbital learning

**abstract/A1：** Accurate electronic band structures are essential for predicting material properties, but high-fidelity calculations are computationally demanding. We introduce O-kRystal, a dual-space orbital-learning framework that uses a lower-cost Perdew-Burke-Ernzerhof (PBE) calculation as both an energy baseline and a source of structured orbital information. Combining PBE band energies with real- and reciprocal-space orbital matrices, O-kRystal predicts higher-level corrections for individual electronic states as a function of crystal momentum and spin. For states within 5 eV of the band-edge midpoint, it predicts Heyd-Scuseria-Ernzerhof (HSE06) band energies with a mean absolute error of $0.171 \\pm 0.009$ eV across nearly 6,000 three-dimensional materials spanning insulating, metallic, magnetic, and nonmagnetic systems. On a separate benchmark of six strongly correlated materials, it reduces the corresponding PBE band-energy MAE by 68.7% and resolves orbital-dependent changes in the relative placement of transition-metal d and ligand p states. Despite training only at high-symmetry points, it maintains comparable accuracy at unseen points along band paths and reconstructs HSE06 densities of states from dense Brillouin-zone predictions. Finally, training O-kRystal on $G\_0W\_0$ quasiparticle energies for two-dimensional materials demonstrates its applicability across levels of theory and the potential of orbital learning for higher-fidelity, momentum-resolved electronic-structure prediction beyond density functional theory.

**comment/C1：** 46 pages, 10 figures (including 4 supplementary figures); Supplementary Information included

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Instruction Mining From Images: Constructing a Synthetic Dataset for Multimodal Learning ILSP DCVLR Competition Submission

**abstract/A1：** This work presents a compact and reproducible pipeline for synthesizing and curating image-based instruction-response pairs for DCVLR. We draw from the Cauldron collection and use Magpie-style instruction and response synthesis with Qwen2.5-VL-7B Instruct to create concise, image-conditioned instruction pairs. The pipeline consists of instruction synthesis, conditioned on input images and controlled via a system prompt, sanitization, tagging, and filtering of the instructions based on the score obtained by a reward model (DeBERTa-v3). We retain samples with positive scores to maximize the quality of the generated instructions. We observe that score distributions have a median and mean reward score of ~ 1.53 and ~ 1.75 respectively, indicating that most generated instructions are of good quality. The resulting dataset contains diverse instructions in the math, spatial, and social reasoning domains.

</details>

### 327. [Safe-FCL: Continual Risk Awareness and Fine-Grained Safety Aggregation for Federated Continual Learning of LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19968v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.19968

**作者：** Shuaijie Ma、Yongkang Hu、Yu Cheng、Jiuan Zhou、Yi Xie、Yushuo Zhang、Zhaoxia Yin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.19968v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.19968v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Safe-FCL 框架，面向 LLM 的联邦持续微调，通过持续风险感知与细粒度安全聚合抑制恶意客户端更新，并报告了安全性评分与风险识别准确率的提升。

**排除原因：** 研究的是联邦学习中的投毒防御与聚合权重调控，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、视觉证据筛选与停止决策，也无可迁移到 LVR 的 latent 状态或训练监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Safe-FCL: Continual Risk Awareness and Fine-Grained Safety Aggregation for Federated Continual Learning of LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Federated fine-tuning enables large language models (LLMs) to learn collaboratively from distributed client data, but malicious client updates can compromise global model safety. As federated tasks evolve, changing malicious update patterns challenge the ability of existing defenses to maintain reliable risk detection over time. To address this challenge, we propose Safe-FCL, a framework integrating continual risk awareness with fine-grained safety aggregation for federated continual learning of LLMs. Specifically, the Continual Risk Awareness (CRA) mechanism learns emerging risk patterns across sequential tasks and constrains detector updates using a protective subspace derived from historical features, mitigating forgetting of previously learned malicious patterns while adapting to new risks. To translate risk awareness into aggregation-level safety control, the Fine-Grained Safety Aggregation (FGSA) mechanism combines client-level risk assessments from CRA with module-level deviations from historical update behavior to adaptively regulate aggregation contributions, suppressing potentially malicious updates while minimizing unnecessary restrictions on benign updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Safe-FCL outperforms current SOTA methods, improving global-model Rule and MD-Judge scores in federated continual learning by 0.41% and 0.64%, respectively, while increasing average risk recognition accuracy by 2.01%.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 4 figures, 11 tables

</details>

### 328. [GBTSVR: Twin Support Vector Regression via Granular Ball Computing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20053v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20053

**作者：** A. Quadir、M. Tanveer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20053v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20053v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作用粒度球（granular ball）作为区域级学习单元改造双支持向量回归，面向噪声、离群点与分位数回归，属表格/时序数据的经典回归方法，既不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态，也没有证据获取、停止决策或 latent 表示的使用线索；「以区域代替样本」与视觉 token 合并的类比过于宽泛，不足以构成可核查的借鉴点，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GBTSVR: Twin Support Vector Regression via Granular Ball Computing

**abstract/A1：** Regression models used in real-world applications are often challenged by noise, outliers, data heterogeneity, and asymmetric error characteristics, which severely limit the effectiveness of conventional point-wise learning models. While twin support vector regression (TSVR) partially addresses these issues through the use of nonparallel regression bounds, it still relies on individual samples as basic learning units. As a result, TSVR remains highly sensitive to noisy and contaminated observations and fails to leverage the underlying structural and regional information inherent in complex datasets, leading to suboptimal robustness and generalization performance. To address these limitations, we introduce granular ball twin support vector regression (GBTSVR) and its quantile extension, granular ball twin support vector quantile regression (GB-TSVQR). The proposed models integrate granular ball computing with TSVR, replacing individual data points with compact, structure-preserving granular balls as fundamental learning units. This region-level representation effectively suppresses noise and outliers while preserving intrinsic data geometry, thereby enhancing robustness, stability, and generalization. GBTSVR constructs nonparallel lower- and upper-bound regression functions over granular balls, significantly reducing sensitivity to sample-level perturbations. Building upon this framework, GB-TSVQR further incorporates quantile regression principles to simultaneously capture heterogeneous trends and asymmetric error distributions across different quantile levels. Extensive experiments on synthetic datasets, time-series benchmarks, and outlier-contaminated data demonstrate that the proposed GBTSVR and GB-TSVQR consistently outperform state-of-the-art regression models.

</details>

### 329. [GDBA-Net: A Gated Dual-Branch Adaptation of SAM2 for Robust Cross-Domain Nuclei Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20073v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20073

**作者：** Biplab Poudel、Betserai Albert Magarire、Anish Kumar Shah、Jiacheng Xie、Dong Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20073v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20073v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是病理图像细胞核分割的领域自适应工作，通过门控双分支适配器融合语义与频域结构先验来微调冻结的 SAM2 编码器，关注分割精度与跨域泛化；不涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、证据筛选、停止决策或 latent 轨迹训练，也无可迁移的状态更新或监督线索，故与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GDBA-Net: A Gated Dual-Branch Adaptation of SAM2 for Robust Cross-Domain Nuclei Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Accurate nuclei segmentation underpins quantitative computational pathology, yet remains challenging due to morphological variability, weak boundaries, nuclear crowding, and pronounced domain shifts arising from differences in tissue preparation, staining, and acquisition. While deep learning and recent foundation models have improved segmentation performance under matched distributions with the original training data, their generalization across heterogeneous histopathology domains remains limited. We introduce GDBA-Net, a parameter-efficient nuclei segmentation framework built around the proposed Gated Dual-Branch Adapter (GDBA). GDBA-Net adapts a frozen SAM2 encoder by integrating pretrained semantic representations with explicit frequency-derived structural priors. The complementary representations are independently transformed through Grid MLP branches and dynamically integrated using sample-adaptive channel-wise gating, selectively emphasizing structural cues relevant to nuclear delineation. Despite using only 4.3 million trainable parameters, GDBA-Net achieves state-of-the-art in-dataset performance and consistently superior cross-dataset generalization across six diverse nuclei segmentation benchmarks under fully supervised, cross-dataset, and five-shot adaptation protocols, outperforming competing CNN-, transformer-, and foundation model based methods. Comprehensive ablations confirm that these gains arise from the coordinated design of semantic-frequency complementarity and adaptive gating rather than increased model capacity. By uniting transferable semantic representations with explicit structural priors, GDBA-Net provides a robust, annotation-efficient solution to domain variability in nuclei segmentation. https://github.com/biplabpoudel25/GDBA\_Net

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Feasibility of a Patient-Specific Digital Twin for Ventriculoperitoneal Shunt Trajectory Planning

**abstract/A1：** Catheter obstruction is the commonest cause of shunt failure; freehand insertion at Keen's point leaves the tip free in cerebrospinal fluid in only one case in ten. We built a digital twin that reconstructs skull, scalp, ear and ventricles from preoperative CT by deterministic geometry, searches every entry around Keen's point for the perpendicular trajectory with the longest intraventricular length under safety constraints, and returns the plan as two ruler measurements from the ear and two depths. Executed freehand in 11 consecutive adults (IDEAL Stage 2a), it achieved Hayhurst Grade 1 placement in 10 of 11 against 22 of 213 historically (p = 9 x 10^-9), median entry and angular deviations 2.5 mm and 5.9 degrees, 1.4 cm more intraventricular catheter than Keen's point, and no intraoperative complication. The technique was unchanged; the gain came from choosing entry and depth per patient. Whether it lowers revision is the next stage's hypothesis.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 8 figures, 2 tables; includes Supplementary Information

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用随机矩阵理论对最小二乘联邦学习中的广义全变差最小化（GTVMin）做大维渐近分析，给出 i.i.d. 与非 i.i.d. 高斯混合下协作与局部适应的偏差—方差权衡的解析表达式。

**排除原因：** 研究的是去中心网络上的联邦优化与正则化权衡，既不涉及视觉或多模态推理，也没有关于推理状态生成、证据使用或停止决策的具体线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Large-Dimensional Analysis of Least-Squares Federated Learning via Generalized Total Variation Minimization

**abstract/A1：** Federated learning over decentralized networks inherently trades off the suppression of local finite-sample noise through collaboration against the bias that collaboration introduces between heterogeneous nodes. We study this tension by performing a large-dimensional analysis of Generalized Total Variation Minimization with least-squares models. Using tools from random matrix theory, we show that Lipschitz observations of the global GTVMin solution concentrate in large dimensions, universally for any underlying network structure. We provide exact analytic expressions for the asymptotically precise performance under both i.i.d. and non-i.i.d. Gaussian mixture distributions on a fixed network. For the latter, these expressions quantify how regularization balances local adaptation against collaboration. Our theoretical results are confirmed by experiments on synthetic and real-world data.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 9 figures

</details>

**abstract/A1：** KANLab is a browser-based visualization tool designed to interactively teach the core concepts of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN), forked from the widely successful TensorFlow Playground. To maximize educational clarity, KANLab omits residual connections and learnable scaling parameters found in modern KAN implementations, ensuring that displayed B-spline activation curves directly correspond to their underlying trainable control points. Users can inspect and modify activation functions, initialization schemes and observe gradient descent correcting manual disruptions. Furthermore, user can explore phenomena such as activation variance, interactive pruning, and re\\-/training within a compact 2D machine learning setting. KANLab is open-source and available at https://kanlab.app/

**title/T1：** Attention-Based Spatio-Temporal Approach for Fire and Smoke Detection

**abstract/A1：** In various industries, smoke and fire are two of the most important threats in the workplace. One of the common methods for detecting smoke and fire is the use of infrared thermal and smoke sensors, which cannot be used in outdoor applications. Therefore, the use of vision-based methods seems necessary. The problem of smoke and fire detection is spatiotemporal and requires spatiotemporal solutions. This paper presents a method that uses spatial features along with temporal-based features to detect smoke and fire in the scene. It consists of three main parts; the task of each part is to reduce the error of the previous part so that the final model has a robust performance. This method also uses transformer modules to increase the accuracy of the model. The results of our model show the proper performance of the proposed approach in solving the problem of smoke and fire detection and can be used to increase workplace safety.

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20155v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20155v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是贷款申请与内部政策的 LLM 合规核查原型，属金融文本抽取与规则匹配的应用落地，不涉及视觉或多模态表示、latent 推理状态生成、证据获取与停止决策等主线问题，也未见可供迁移的状态更新或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM-Based Policy Compliance Verification of Loan Applications

**abstract/A1：** Banks rely on extensive internal policy frameworks to ensure that corporate lending decisions comply with regulatory requirements and internal risk standards. Reviewing loan applications against these policies is a largely manual and knowledge-intensive process, which can lead to overlooked policy violations or exceptions. This report presents a prototype of an LLM-based policy checker that automatically verifies loan applications against internal lending policies. The proposed approach follows a two-step architecture. First, compliance-relevant rules are automatically extracted from policy documents and converted into structured, machine-readable representations. Second, loan applications are evaluated against the extracted rules to identify policy exceptions, non-compliant findings, missing information, and cases requiring manual review. Evaluation results demonstrate that the rule extraction process is highly stable across multiple runs and consistently captures rules identified as critical by a domain expert. When evaluated on 11 real loan applications with known policy exceptions, the system successfully detected the majority of these exceptions and identified additional compliance issues that had previously been overlooked during manual reviews. While the approach produces false positives and cannot fully replace human judgement, the results indicate that LLM-based policy compliance verification can provide valuable support in the loan application verification process by highlighting potential compliance risks.

</details>

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究用 MILP 训练稀疏 DNF 布尔规则网络以做可解释二分类，属离散结构优化的符号学习，与 LVR 关注的多模态推理状态生成、视觉证据使用与动态计算无实质联系，也未提供可核查的状态或监督方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Optimizing Sparse Boolean Rule Networks using Mixed Integer Linear Programming

**abstract/A1：** We study the problem of learning accurate and cognitively simple binary classifiers that are directly interpretable as Boolean rules. We propose mixed integer linear programming (MILP) formulations for training sparse disjunctive normal form (DNF) networks, viewed as small networks with a conjunctive hidden layer and a disjunctive output layer. Contrary to previous work that uses MILP for inductive rule learning, we start with a fixed network structure, for which we then find optimal weights. A regularized objective explicitly trades off misclassification costs against sparsity, yielding globally optimized rule sets. Our main technical contribution is an enhanced formulation that substantially reduces the number of integer decision variables and eliminates redundant constraints, while preserving exact Boolean semantics for all feasible solutions. Furthermore, we develop a combined approach based on initialization and dropout to break symmetries in the weights between rules, mitigating redundancy, and encouraging more diverse and stable rule representations. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and UCI datasets show that the advanced formulation improves computational efficiency and achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to established interpretable baselines, including SAT-based DNF learning and well-known rule- and tree-based learners. Overall, our approach provides a principled optimization framework for learning compact Boolean rule sets with explicit control over accuracy and sparsity trade-offs.

**comment/C1：** Preprint (submitted version, prior to peer review) of a paper accepted at RuleML+RR 2026, to appear in LNCS

</details>

### 336. [HGRP: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Routability Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20209v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20209

**作者：** Yule Sheng、Yue Yuan、Shan Shen、Daying Sun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20209v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20209v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 面向 EDA 布局可布线性预测，用异构图网络建模 cell/net/布线栅格以回归拥塞，属于电路物理设计任务，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取与停止决策，也没有可供借鉴的具体 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HGRP: Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Routability Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Routability prediction enables rapid evaluation of placement quality without routing, providing effective feedback for routability-driven placement optimization. Recent machine learning methods have achieved efficient routability prediction through data-driven approach. However, their prediction performance highly depends on the capability of input representations to capture essential circuit information. To address the limitations of existing circuit representations in simultaneously preserving logical topology, physical layout and routing resource information, this paper proposes HGRP, a heterogeneous graph neural network for routability prediction. HGRP models the cells, nets, and routing grids as distinct types of nodes and explicitly represents topological connectivity, spatial mapping, and grid adjacency, thereby constructing a heterogeneous graph representation that preserves multi-level circuit semantics. Based on the representation, a heterogeneous message passing mechanism is developed to enable information interaction and feature fusion among distinct types of nodes, effectively learning the complex correlations between circuit structures and routing behaviors. Experimental results on the CircuitNet dataset show that HGRP improves F1-score by 18.69\\% relative improvements compared with the state-of-the-art in the congestion prediction task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/ShenShan123/HGPR.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by ASP-DAC2027. Camera-Ready version

</details>

### 337. [Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Robust Planetary Landing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20252v1>)

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20252v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20252v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究用对抗强化学习为行星着陆控制器生成物理约束下的困难扰动条件，通过控制器与扰动生成器交替优化的三阶段训练提升鲁棒性。

**排除原因：** 属航天器制导控制领域的鲁棒性训练，不涉及视觉或多模态推理、内部状态表示、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可核查的状态生成或监督借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Robust Planetary Landing

**abstract/A1：** Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) policies for planetary landing and other aerospace guidance, navigation, and control tasks are commonly trained under randomized disturbances to improve robustness. However, typical stochastic perturbation sampling assumes independent variations, whereas real mission disturbances often exhibit spatial and temporal correlation and cannot be reproduced through independent random draws. This work investigates adversarial reinforcement learning to discover challenging off-nominal conditions. A secondary agent generates physically constrained perturbations that degrade the performance of a landing controller, forming an asymmetric sequential zero-sum game. Training proceeds in three stages: baseline controller learning, robustness enhancement with stochastic perturbations, and an iterative adversarial phase in which the controller and disturbance generator are alternately optimized. Preliminary experiments in a three-degree-of-freedom landing environment show that adversarial disturbances reveal vulnerabilities missed by conventional randomization and provide early indications that, with a more extensive training campaign, the approach can yield improved robustness and stronger generalization under previously unseen perturbation regimes.

</details>

### 338. [Resolution Allocation with Learnable Resizing and Spectral Filter Parameterization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20274v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20274

**作者：** Wei Wu、Eric C. Larson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20274v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20274v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文系统比较了空间域与频谱域学习的卷积核在可学习下采样比例下的表现差异，在三个图像分类数据集和两种模型规模上验证频谱核对分辨率分配更敏感。

**排除原因：** 研究问题是 CNN 卷积核参数化与下采样比例的图像分类精度，不涉及多模态推理、latent 推理状态或证据获取与停止决策，未见可核查的迁移线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Resolution Allocation with Learnable Resizing and Spectral Filter Parameterization

**abstract/A1：** Learnable downsampling and spectral-domain filter parameterizations have each made convolutional networks more flexible, but their interaction has not been systematically examined. We bridge this gap by examining convolutional kernels learned in the spatial and spectral domains, together with learned downsampling ratios under a cropped resizing implementation. We find that spectral and spatial kernels respond differently to learned resolution allocation, with larger gains for the spectral kernel. We observe this consistently on three datasets (EuroSAT, CIFAR-10 and STL-10) and at two model sizes (differences in accuracy gain of +0.013 to +0.028 on held-out test sets). Part of this difference reflects the greater gain of the spectral kernel from retaining more spatial area, but the difference remains positive when total retained area is matched. This pattern is observed across datasets and model sizes, although its magnitude and dependence on the complexity budget vary with model size and the resizing implementation.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables. Submitted to ICASSP 2027

</details>

### 339. [TANGO: RNA Topology and Geometry Co-Design](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20277v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20277

**作者：** Tianmeng Hu、Biao Luo、Ke Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20277v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20277v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究的是 RNA 序列逆设计，用多智能体强化学习联合满足二级拓扑与三级几何目标，不涉及 VLM 的 latent 推理状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策；其中的分级课程与多目标微调虽属通用训练技巧，但未指向 LVR 的具体问题，缺乏可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TANGO: RNA Topology and Geometry Co-Design

**abstract/A1：** Designing RNA sequences that satisfy target tertiary-structure objectives is an important problem in RNA engineering, with broad applications in synthetic biology and therapeutics. Existing 3D RNA design methods have made substantial progress, but they largely treat the target fold as a geometry-only objective. This is incomplete for RNA, whose functional folds are specified not only by tertiary geometry but also by secondary base-pairing topology. We therefore formulate RNA inverse design as a topology-and-geometry co-design problem and propose TANGO, a multi-objective multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for this task. TANGO adopts a staged curriculum: it first learns a topology-aware policy from secondary-structure targets, then transfers this policy to tertiary-structure targets to train a general co-design model, and finally applies frontier-guided fine-tuning to refine target-specific trade-offs among secondary-structure fidelity, tertiary-structure fidelity, and sequence diversity. Experiments show that TANGO generates diverse and novel designs, improving tertiary- and secondary-structure performance by 18.3% and 52.7%, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20278

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20278v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20278v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一种在保留人脸水印的前提下实施的黑盒人脸规避攻击（PGD-L\_2），通过空间与频谱保持项让扰动降低人脸识别相似度，同时维持水印可恢复性与视觉质量。

**排除原因：** 研究的是生物特征认证中水印有效性与身份可靠性的脱节，涉及对抗扰动与频谱保持，但与 VLM 内部推理状态、视觉证据使用或 latent 生成方式没有可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Valid Watermark Is Not a Valid Identity: Spatial-Spectral Adversarial Face Evasion with Watermark Preservation

**abstract/A1：** Facial image watermarking can support traceability and certification in biometric pipelines, but provenance or manipulation detection alone does not guarantee reliable biometric identity. We introduce a watermark-preserving face evasion attack that targets this gap under a black-box watermarking setting, without access to the watermarking components. The proposed PGD-L\_2 attack guides adversarial perturbations with spatial and spectral preservation terms to reduce face-recognition similarity while limiting pixel-level distortion and high-frequency changes that may interfere with hidden watermark cues. Evaluated on controlled facial images with two watermarking schemes, two face-recognition models, and two datasets, the attack achieves evasion rates above 92% at operational thresholds while preserving watermark recovery above 93% for StegaFormer and above 99.7% for FaceSigns, with visual quality above 47dB PSNR at epsilon\_2=2.0. These results expose a structural gap in watermark-based facial certification: a face image can remain watermark-valid and visually faithful while becoming biometrically unreliable.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE International Joint Conference on Biometrics (IJCB 2026)

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20309

**作者：** Jakob Hinterholzer、Mohamed Youssef、Oliver Bimber

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20309v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20309v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文用仿真对比事件相机与帧相机在合成孔径成像中去除树叶等遮挡物的效果，比较不同速度、照度与数据预算下的重建质量。

**排除原因：** 研究主题是多传感器成像仿真与遮挡去除，既不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、latent 生成与训练监督，也没有证据筛选或停止决策层面的可迁移线索，与 LVR 主线无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Event Cameras versus Frame Cameras for Synthetic-Aperture Imaging: A Simulation Study

**abstract/A1：** Synthetic-aperture imaging (SAI) removes occluders such as foliage by integrating many views of a scene along a camera path. It has so far been used mainly with frame cameras. We ask whether an event camera has advantages for SAI, and answer the question in simulation, where both sensors see exactly the same scene and the same camera movement. A densely rendered Blender sequence is turned into frames with an EMVA~1288 noise model of an industrial global-shutter camera, and into an event stream with the v2e simulator parameterized like a Sony IMX636. The frames are integrated with the existing airborne optical sectioning renderer. For the events we add a new integration path that projects every single event onto the focal plane at its own interpolated pose, so no events have to be binned into frames. We compare the two sensors in three main runs and an additional dark high-speed test. In a bright scene at 25,m/s the frame camera records only three images on the path and cannot remove the occluder, while the event camera reconstructs the target, even after an event-rate controller drops most of its events to fit the camera link. At the same speed in a 5,lux scene the event camera no longer resolves the target either. In a 5,lux scene at 5,m/s both sensors reconstruct the target at similar quality. We also compare the data one traversal of the aperture costs. At high speed the capped event stream still shows the target at the same data volume as the three frames. At 1,m/s we compare both cameras at two matched data budgets, and there the event camera has no advantage over the frame camera in reconstruction quality. The data rate of the event camera depends on the scene and is highest exactly in the fast bright scenes where it is needed most.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20326

**作者：** Anjali K. S.、Manoj Kumar Panda、Chandramani Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20326v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20326v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 ISAC 波束成形流程中统一评测经典与深度学习 DoA 估计器（含集成架构），比较其估计精度、推理时延与可达频谱效率。

**排除原因：** 属通信感知领域的到达角估计与波束成形评测，虽涉及精度-时延权衡与集成，但未触及视觉/多模态内部状态、证据筛选或 latent 推理生成，缺乏可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Comparative Evaluation of Deep-Learning based DoA Estimators for Real-Time ISAC Beamforming

**abstract/A1：** Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) systems rely on accurate Direction-of-Arrival (DoA) estimation for effective beamforming and spectral-efficiency enhancement. While numerous classical and deep-learning-based DoA estimation methods have been reported in the literature, their suitability for real-time ISAC operation remains insufficiently understood because practical deployment requires consideration of both estimation accuracy and computational latency. This paper presents a unified evaluation framework for systematically comparing classical and learning-based DoA estimators within an ISAC beamforming pipeline. Representative classical and learning-based DoA estimators, together with an ensemble architecture, are integrated with Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based beamforming and evaluated under identical operating conditions. The impact of DoA estimation accuracy, inference latency, beamforming-weight generation time, and resulting effective spectral efficiency is investigated. Results show that different estimators occupy distinct operating points in the accuracy-latency-performance design space. Although learning-based approaches substantially reduce estimation time compared with MUSIC, the overall processing latency increasingly becomes dominated by the computational cost of SVD-based beamforming. Among the evaluated methods, the Regression Convolutional Neural Network (RCNN) exhibits a favorable balance between estimation accuracy, computational efficiency, and beamforming performance, making it a promising candidate for real-time ISAC deployment. The findings further suggest that future low-latency ISAC systems may benefit from beamforming strategies that reduce or avoid computationally intensive SVD-based beamforming-weight generation.

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InfiniSplat v2: Analyzing and Improving Feed-Forward 3DGS from Any Views

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction builds 3D scenes directly from input images for novel view rendering, but learning Gaussian attributes from predicted geometry for high-quality rendering under large viewpoint changes remains challenging. We analyze limitations in Gaussian attribute learning and introduce InfiniSplat v2, which improves rendering quality through architectural and training changes. Our method decouples geometry and attribute prediction with a frozen geometry branch, aligns predicted Gaussians using ground-truth camera poses during training, and adopts large-angle target view supervision. Across six datasets, the feed-forward outputs of the same trained model achieve state-of-the-art mean LPIPS and DISTS among the evaluated methods in both pose-conditioned and pose-free settings. In the pose-conditioned setting, mean LPIPS and DISTS decrease by 25.8% and 32.9%, respectively, compared with the strongest evaluated baselines. We further find that opacity learned with only image reconstruction supervision suppresses geometrically inconsistent points. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/InfiniSplat2/

### 344. [Beyond Expert Rankings: Contrast Calibration for Mixed-Precision MoE Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20353v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20353

**作者：** Renjie Xie、Juncheng Yang、Aoting Hu、Yan Liang、Mingxi Zhang、Zheheng Hong、Wei Xu、Yanzhi Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20353v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20353v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究 MoE 语言模型的混合精度量化分配（按专家敏感度分配位宽以降低存储与 KL 散度），属于推理效率压缩工作，未涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺乏可核查的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Expert Rankings: Contrast Calibration for Mixed-Precision MoE Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models activate only a few experts per token, yet all expert weights must be stored. Mixed-precision quantization reduces this storage cost by assigning higher bit-widths to sensitive experts and lower bit-widths to resilient ones, but expert rankings alone do not determine how strongly to weight their errors. To address this gap, we propose NatScale, a contrast-calibrated allocation framework for mixed-precision MoE quantization. It uses a model-specific power transformation to calibrate gradient-based expert weights to an information-theoretic contrast target while preserving rankings. We jointly evaluate each expert's gate, up and down configurations to estimate its output error. We then combine these errors and calibrated weights with an existing prior based on weight spectra, variance and precision decay to select configurations under a storage budget. Across three models, NatScale achieves the lowest native-to-quantized KL divergence and the highest top-token agreement in all nine settings against five allocation baselines. It also yields the lowest perplexity in seven settings. These findings highlight the importance of objective-aware precision allocation.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, including references and appendices

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20412v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20412v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 研究的是实例分割中混合监督（框标注+像素标注）与伪标签噪声抑制，属于纯视觉分割的训练数据质量问题，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据获取、latent 生成或停止决策，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hybrid supervised instance segmentation by learning label noise suppression

**abstract/A1：** To reach top accuracy, current fully supervised instance segmentation methods severely rely on large-scale pixel-wise labeled datasets. They are usually expensive and time-consuming to obtain. Though weakly or semi-supervised methods utilize cheap bounding box labeled, image-level labeled or unlabeled samples to save the labeling cost, their performance is largely sacrificed. To save labeling cost without losing much performance, in this paper, we present a pipeline that can utilize economical bounding box labels and accurate pixel-wise labels in a hybrid way. Specifically, we design two ancillary models to learn label noise suppression and obtain accurate pseudo pixel-wise labels from bounding box labels for training. One is designed to suppress mislabeling between foreground and background, and the other is designed to suppress noise from mislabeling of instances. Moreover, we exploit category-aware spatial attention module, category constraint module, instance constraint module, and self-learning training approach to improve the accuracy of pseudo labels. Experiments on the PASCAL VOC 2012 and the Cityscapes datasets show that our method can achieve competitive performance with much less labeling cost.

</details>

### 346. [Infant Fingerprints: Quality Assessment &amp; Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20432v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20432

**作者：** João L. H. Dall Agnol、Dalcimar Casanova、Rodrigo Minetto、Luiz F. P. Southier、Marcelo Teixeira、Marcelo Filipak、Anil K. Jain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20432v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20432v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** null

**排除原因：** 该文研究婴儿指纹质量评估与识别，属生物特征识别领域，未涉及视觉语言模型内部状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 推理生成，与 LVR 主线无实质联系，也未见可迁移的状态或决策线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Infant Fingerprints: Quality Assessment &amp; Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Infant fingerprint recognition is hindered by a combination of physiological characteristics, such as small ridge width and spacing, and acquisition challenges that include involuntary motion, skin residue, and uneven fingertip pressure. These factors undermine fingerprint quality standards originally designed for adults, which assume feature distributions that do not hold for infants. To address this gap, this paper evaluates ten fingerprint quality assessment methods on a longitudinal dataset that spans six age groups from newborns to three months, alongside a parameter tuning routine that uncovers age-specific fingerprint characteristics to recalibrate methods with domain-dependent assumptions. Both default and tuned configurations are compared through recognition experiments assessing the effectiveness of quality-guided gallery selection. Template-fusion strategies and latent fingerprint recognition technologies are further examined to identify the best possible matching rates achievable by the top-performing method. Results show that tuned parameter sets outperform default configurations for the majority of methods and age groups, indicating that targeted recalibration of existing frameworks improves gallery utility and contributes to more reliable infant fingerprint recognition.

</details>

### 347. [Learning by Examples: Power Grid Steady State Analysis with a Tabular Foundation Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20462v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20462

**作者：** Olayiwola Arowolo、Oluwadara Adedeji、Peter Palensky、Jochen L. Cremer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20462v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20462v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究将预训练表格基础模型 TabPFN 直接用于电力系统稳态分析，在潮流预测、N-k 故障筛选和电压稳定裕度预测三类任务上与专用神经基线比较。

**排除原因：** 属特定工程领域的表格基础模型应用与性能对比，不涉及内部推理状态、视觉证据、停止决策或轨迹训练等机制，也未提出可供核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning by Examples: Power Grid Steady State Analysis with a Tabular Foundation Model

**abstract/A1：** Foundation models have shown strong generalization across scientific domains, but their use in power systems remains largely task-specific. We investigate whether a pretrained tabular foundation model (TFM), TabPFN, can serve as a general-purpose neural solver for power grid steady-state analysis. We evaluate the TFM on three tasks: power flow (PF) prediction, N-k contingency screening (NCS), and voltage stability margin (VSM) prediction. We performed experiments on 118- and 2000-bus systems, comparing the TFM against task-specific neural baselines. Without retraining or fine-tuning, the TFM matches or outperforms the best task-specific model in several settings across the three tasks. For 118-bus PF prediction, a variant of the TFM achieves errors of $1\\times 10^{-3}$ pu for voltage magnitudes and $0.30^\\circ$ for voltage angles, outperforming all baselines. On unseen N-1 contingency and close-to-infeasible PF cases, the TFM is competitive with the DCPF approximation. For millions of unseen N-2 contingencies, the TFM achieves AUCs of 0.92 and 0.95 for branch and voltage violation screening, respectively. The TFM also attains accurate VSM prediction performance for the 2000-bus system with limited labelled data. The TFM attains strong results across the tested tasks despite pretraining without any power-system-specific dataset and requiring no fine-tuning step. These results offer a glimpse into the potential of pretrained TFMs as a neural solver for a range of power grid analysis tasks.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 8 figures, under review

</details>

### 348. [OLiVES: An Outdoor Low-Light Video Benchmark for Enhancement and Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20501v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20501

**作者：** Yini Li、Joanne Lin、David Blackham、David Bull、Nantheera Anantrasirichai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20501v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20501v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OLiVES：一个使用专业电影摄影机拍摄的户外低光视频基准数据集，含 2.5 万余对对齐的常规光/低光帧和 4.6 万余条视频目标分割标注，用于联合评测低光视频增强与视频目标分割。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于低光视频增强与分割的数据集和基准构建，未涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未见可供核查的表示或训练线索，与 LVR 主线缺乏实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OLiVES: An Outdoor Low-Light Video Benchmark for Enhancement and Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Low-light video processing remains an underexplored problem in computer vision, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality ground-truth data. Existing public datasets exhibit several limitations, including restricted motion diversity, limited scene coverage, weak degradations, reliance on auxiliary modalities such as event data, and misalignment between low-light inputs and ground truth. Furthermore, current datasets typically address either low-light enhancement or scene understanding, whereas these tasks should be considered jointly. We introduce \\textbf{OLiVES}, a benchmark dataset for outdoor low-light videos with dynamic scenes, designed to support both low-light video enhancement (LLVE) and video object segmentation (VOS). The dataset addresses key content gaps by incorporating a diverse range of natural textures, animals, occlusions, dynamic activities, and close-up scenes. To the best of our knowledge, OLiVES is the first outdoor low-light video dataset captured using professional cinema-grade cameras. It provides over 25,000 aligned normal- and low-light frame pairs, along with over 46,000 VOS annotations. We benchmark state-of-the-art LLVE and VOS methods on OLiVES and show that training on it improves performance on both tasks. The OLiVES project page is available at: https://anonymous-OLiVES.github.io/.

</details>

### 349. [The Wealth of Agents: Scaling Agentic AI Across Developers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20518v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20518

**作者：** Austin W. Hanjie、Carlos E. Jimenez、Zirui Wang、Karthik Narasimhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20518v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20518v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究「多开发者智能体系统」（MDA），即由不同开发者分别构建的专业化智能体协作组成的系统，通过经验与理论建模比较 MDA 与单开发者系统，并在 GAIA 基准上验证协作系统在准确率与资源效率上的优势。

**排除原因：** 研究焦点是开发者协作的组织与资源分配经济学，智能体之间是外部消息协作而非内部 latent 状态生成与更新，也未涉及视觉证据筛选、推理状态、停止决策或监督机制，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Wealth of Agents: Scaling Agentic AI Across Developers

**abstract/A1：** Today's agentic systems are typically designed, built, and deployed by single developers with full control over all participating agents. While this simplifies development, it limits the potential scale of agentic systems. We study multi-developer agentic systems (MDAs) which enable developers to collaboratively build agentic systems to push frontier capability beyond what any individual developer could achieve alone. We construct empirical and theoretical models of developers to compare MDAs with single-developer agentic systems (SDAs). On GAIA, a general assistant benchmark, a population of specialized developers whose individual agents do not exceed 35% accuracy reaches 70% when their agents are combined in an MDA. This MDA performs 12 percentage points above the median general-purpose SDA built with several times each specialist's development budget. Our theoretical models show how MDAs can incentivize specialization and encourage more efficient use of aggregated developer resources. Put together, our results make a preliminary case for MDAs as a new scaling axis by enabling developers with diverse capabilities and resources to advance agentic AI collaboratively.

</details>

### 350. [Detection of Potato Plant Leaf Diseases using Convolutional Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20544v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20544

**作者：** Haimanot Bitew Alemayehu、Simachew Melaku Ayalew、Chilotie Desalew Mengesha、Ayisheshim Almaw Bogale、Anteneh Birhan Yenew、Abrham Tegegne Bogale

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20544v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20544v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究开发一个轻量级 CNN，将马铃薯叶片分为健康、早疫病和晚疫病三类，并在 PlantVillage 与埃塞俄比亚田间数据集上评估，同时与 VGG16、MobileNetV2 比较精度与计算效率。

**排除原因：** 属于农业图像分类的常规应用与轻量模型对比，不涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选、生成方式或训练监督等 LVR 关注点，也无具体可借鉴的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Detection of Potato Plant Leaf Diseases using Convolutional Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Potato is an important food-security crop in Ethiopia, but its production is affected by foliar diseases, particularly early blight and late blight. This study develops a lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) to classify potato leaves into three categories: healthy, early blight, and late blight. The proposed model was trained using 2,215 images from the PlantVillage dataset and evaluated on an independent field dataset containing 247 images collected from potato farms in Banja, Awi Zone, Ethiopia. On PlantVillage, the model achieved 99.04% training accuracy, 98.21% validation accuracy, and 98.66% test accuracy. On the field dataset, it achieved 90.28% accuracy. The study also compares the proposed CNN with VGG16 and MobileNetV2 in terms of classification performance and computational efficiency. The proposed model contains 446,787 trainable parameters, occupies approximately 1.70 MB, and requires an average of 5.58 seconds per training epoch in the reported experimental setup, compared with 9.96 seconds for VGG16 and 7.68 seconds for MobileNetV2. The results demonstrate the potential of a compact CNN for potato leaf disease classification and highlight the importance of independent field evaluation, where image conditions are more variable than in controlled benchmark datasets. The proposed approach may support preliminary disease identification for smallholder farmers in Ethiopia and similar agricultural settings.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables. The study presents a lightweight CNN for three-class potato leaf disease classification and evaluates it on PlantVillage and an independent field dataset collected in Banja, Awi Zone, Ethiopia

</details>

### 351. [A$^3$PreCore: Robust Rigid Registration for Native FLAIR-Space Brain-Mask Transfer](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20561

**作者：** Elmira Mirzabeigi、Zahra Khalilzad、Taylor Snowden-Richardson、Jesus Eduardo Lugo Arce、Jodie R. Gawryluk、Brian R. Christie、Jocelyn Faubert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、eess.IV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20561v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作研究 T1 到 FLAIR 的刚性配准与脑掩膜迁移及质控指标，属于医学图像配准与分割质控，不涉及视觉推理的内部状态、生成或证据使用，也没有可供借鉴的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A$^3$PreCore: Robust Rigid Registration for Native FLAIR-Space Brain-Mask Transfer

**abstract/A1：** Reliable brain-mask transfer into native fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) space can fail when T1-to-FLAIR rigid registration is unstable. We developed A$^3$PreCore, a six-degree-of-freedom rigid-registration strategy that aligns the full bias-corrected T1-weighted image to native FLAIR using normalized mutual information while preserving the established T1-space anatomical mask. We also introduced extended quantitative quality control (E-QC), which characterizes mask extent, field-of-view-relative position, FLAIR intensity support, boundary support, inferior morphology, and pairwise geometric disagreement. In 78 participants with paired T1-weighted and FLAIR imaging, A$^3$PreCore was compared with fsl\_anat and the Brain Extraction Tool (BET). Gross rigid-body misregistration occurred in 21/78 fsl\_anat outputs (26.9%) and 0/78 A$^3$PreCore outputs (exact two-sided McNemar p=9.54 x 10^-7). Among 57 participants without gross fsl\_anat misregistration, A$^3$PreCore preserved close agreement (median Dice=0.993; median paired Delta NMI=-0.00007, p=0.429). A$^3$PreCore remained broadly concordant with BET (median Dice=0.955), with less inferior and extracranial extension in representative comparisons. Method-blinded neuroscientists ranked A$^3$PreCore first in 35/40 participants (87.5%). E-QC additionally exposed severe orientation-related disagreement not evident from global descriptors or centroid displacement alone. These findings support A$^3$PreCore as a robust native-FLAIR brain-mask transfer strategy and E-QC as a complementary framework for multidimensional mask characterization and participant-specific geometric quality control.

</details>

### 352. [CommerceGen-Studio: An End-to-End Framework for Commercial-Grade Main-Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20603v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.20603

**作者：** Xiaoli Xu、Zhengtao Wu、Zhengze Xu、Tao Liu、Yongchao Du、Zuan Gao、Xingjian Wang、Hao Yan、Mengting Chen、Wei Li、Bo Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20603v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20603v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作是电商主图生成的工业化三阶段流水线（VLM/LLM 迭代改写、布局优化、DiT 多条件生成），目标是图像质量与商用可发布性，与 LVR 关注的内部视觉状态保留、推理证据筛选、latent 状态生成与停止决策无实质联系，其中的 agent 循环与策略优化也未针对 VLM 推理状态，未见可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CommerceGen-Studio: An End-to-End Framework for Commercial-Grade Main-Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** We present CommerceGen-Studio, an end-to-end framework for generating commercial-grade main images for e-commerce Stock Keeping Units (SKUs). Our framework is organized along a three-stage pipeline of information extraction, semantic rewriting, and multi-condition generation: Prompt-Eval-Refine-Loop Agent (PERL-Agent) distills heterogeneous SKU metadata into concise selling-point text lists through an iterative closed loop of VLM and LLM roles scored by a reference-free rubric. Layout Thinker Optimization (LTO) aligns the rewriter with both caption and rendered-image quality via alternating domain-specific policy optimization. SKU-DiT, trained under Reinforce-Distill-Reinforce (RDR) progressive training, conditions jointly on the white-background product image, the trademark crop, and the structured layout to hold subject fidelity, trademark integrity, and character-level text accuracy together. The framework runs in production at a scale of millions of main images per day with a latency of a few seconds per image, which we believe makes it among the first systems of this kind to operate at commercial scale. We also release CommerceGen-Bench, a benchmark of hard cases collected from live traffic across all product categories. On it, our framework achieves the highest overall scores among the evaluated open-source baselines on both editing tracks, with and without prompt enhancement. It also matches much larger closed-source models, and beats several of them at Instruction Following and Product Integration - the two properties that determine whether an image can be published.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, system report

</details>
